- `[state][algorithm]` A cursor/read-position into a buffer trimmed from the far end (bounded undo history, ring buffer, windowed log, capped scrollback) is ONE coupled invariant — evicting the oldest with `shift()` must decrement the cursor in the same operation, or the cursor drifts past the end and a later read dereferences undefined and crashes. Two variables bound by a relationship must be updated together atomically; editing one is a silent half-applied invariant. → projects/local__sandbox-undo-redo.md#E1
- `[state][editor][input]` When coalescing rapid edits into one undo step, gate the merge on same-KIND and spatial ADJACENCY, not just a time window (else type+delete or a caret-jump collapse into one step whose undo deletes text the user never grouped), and make the merged undo data span BEFORE-the-first-subedit → AFTER-the-last — the classic bug is the newest sub-edit overwriting the burst-start anchor so undo removes the wrong range (typing "hello" undid to "hell"). Test byte-equality vs the pre-burst doc. → projects/local__sandbox-undo-redo.md#E2
- `[state][editor]` Every undo/history entry (state snapshot AND captured undo-data) must be a DEEP, private, unshared copy — a stored reference, a shallow copy, or a reused command instance lets a later mutation silently rewrite the past so undo restores the CURRENT state, not the historical one. Shallow copies are the trap: top-level scalars restore while nested arrays/objects corrupt. Applies to any time-travel/state-versioning store. → projects/local__sandbox-undo-redo.md#E3
- `[state][editor]` An undo inverse (or history delta) must derive from command args PLUS state captured DURING apply — never from the forward args alone. Destructive ops (delete/clear/set) don't preserve what they destroy, so an args-only inverse restores a hole/default/empty (delete→`null`, clear→`[]`) and one lossy delta poisons the whole chain after it. Capture the pre-image at apply time; full snapshots are immune at a memory cost. Test byte-equality of doc before-do vs after-undo. → projects/local__sandbox-undo-redo.md#E4
- `[state][editor][testing]` In a linear undo/redo, a NEW edit after undo branches the timeline — the do/commit path MUST clear the redo stack, or `redo()` replays a stale command against a since-diverged document and silently corrupts it (`["a","X"]`→redo→`["a","b","X"]`). This is the core invariant, not an optimization; coalescing corollary: merge only into the live top-of-undo, never an already-undone command. Test it (undo → different edit → assert redo is a no-op). → projects/local__sandbox-undo-redo.md#E5
- `[gamedev][camera][state]` A progress-driven camera schedule alone isn't enough for procedurally-positioned content — an outer node placed at its FINAL layout radius can already be far off-frame on day one while the camera is still at max zoom; hard-clamp scale every frame to the real extent of what's currently visible, and test across the full timeline on more than one aspect ratio (matching your design's base resolution exactly hides this bug) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E4
- `[animation][state][testing]` Never gate animation/timeline completion on an exact `>=`/`==` against accumulated float time — `30×(1/30)=0.999…9 < 1`, so an `elapsed >= duration` tween hangs a hair short and its onComplete never fires; and the error DIRECTION is dt-dependent (`60×(1/60) > 1` fires fine), so it silently fails at some frame rates and not others (the "stuck at 99% / callback never runs" bug). Use a tolerance and SNAP to the target on completion; tolerate ±1 tick at frame boundaries. → projects/local__sandbox-anim.md#E1
- `[animation][gamedev][state]` A dt-driven frame stepper must DRAIN the accumulator in a while-loop (capping at one step/tick makes animation lag behind real time under any frame drop — ~0.6s behind after 1s of dt=0.25) AND SUBTRACT the frame duration, never reset to 0 (resetting discards the sub-frame remainder and drifts even in exact arithmetic — ~15% of frames dropped over 10s). `while(acc>=frameDur){acc-=frameDur; step()}`. Same pattern as physics substepping. → projects/local__sandbox-anim.md#E2
- `[animation][gamedev]` Motion written as a fixed per-frame factor (`value += (to-value)*0.1` each frame) is frame-rate DEPENDENT — a geometric decay in frames, not seconds, so it converges faster on faster hardware (99.82 @60fps vs 95.76 @30fps for the same 1s move). Drive from elapsed/duration easing, or make the factor time-aware (`1 - exp(-rate*dt)`); every per-frame increment must be a function of dt. → projects/local__sandbox-anim.md#E3
- `[animation][state][gamedev]` An animation FSM's per-clip state (frame index, time accumulator, `done` flag) must reset on every transition — sharing the animator's fields leaks the previous clip's frame/timing/completion, so a new clip starts on the old clip's frame, and one played after a finished one-shot is born "already done" showing an undefined frame. Reset all three in `play()`, guard with a same-clip no-op so a held state doesn't pop, and make one-shot completion an edge (clamp last frame + latch once), not a per-tick level. → projects/local__sandbox-anim.md#E4
- `[animation][state]` Fire looping-timeline events as CROSSINGS in raw, unwrapped monotonic time — never wrap the playhead endpoints first, which makes a seam-crossing interval (`from=0.6, to=wrap(1.4)=0.4` → `>0.6 && <=0.4`) unsatisfiable and silently drops the event, and a multi-loop dt drops all of them. Enumerate each event's loop-copies in `(lo,hi]`, fire sorted by absolute time, and match the unwrap to the play mode (sawtooth vs triangle). Invisible until a frame straddles the seam — test with large dt. → projects/local__sandbox-anim.md#E5
- `[gamedev][animation]` A flat time-budget per batch of simultaneous events makes every batch feel the same size regardless of real magnitude — a batch 200x bigger than another can spawn in the same ~2s window, reading as a flash instead of a felt crescendo; grow the budget sub-linearly (sqrt(n)) instead, and verify a "scale with size" pacing fix against small/medium cases too since a shrink-with-size variant of the same fix can silently regress them → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E5
- `[determinism][gamedev][state]` A compounding DBZ-style power-level stat crosses float64's safe-integer boundary (2^53) after only ~21 representative multiplicative story-arc jumps, though true overflow needs ~416 — exact-integer-sensitive ops (hashing, diffing, tie-detection) need BigInt/fixed-point past that point even though display/combat math stays accurate far longer → projects/local__sandbox-power-scaling.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][gamedev][state]` No damage-formula curve (linear/ratio/capped-ratio/log/sqrt) prevents one-shot kills once attacker:defender power ratio exceeds ~5-10x — only capping damage as a percentage of the DEFENDER's own max HP produced ratio-independent fight length (flat 5 turns from 1x to 100,000x) → projects/local__sandbox-power-scaling.md#E2
- `[gamedesign][gamedev][state]` A percent-of-defender-max-HP damage cap verified fair in 1v1 becomes deterministically lopsided in 3v1 team fights (boss loses 30/30 seeds) — neither scaling the cap by attacker count nor matching action economy fixes it, because the cap structurally rewards attacking high-max-HP "tank" targets with bigger absolute hits → projects/local__sandbox-dbz-team-combat.md#E1
- `[state][gamedev]` A multi-axis stat system can silently reduce to fewer real axes than intended: `defense` was fully modeled (base value, tier multiplier, present in the stats object) but never referenced in the damage formula, so it had zero effect on any outcome despite looking complete → projects/local__sandbox-dbz-team-combat.md#E2
- `[ai][gamedev][algorithm]` An urgent-override that fires correctly (score>=threshold, not stolen by a competing action) can still give zero functional lead time if its saturation distance is nested inside a separately-verified capture-lock radius sharing the same metric — always solve both thresholds and compare, don't assume "it fires" means "it helps" → projects/local__sandbox-combined-integration.md#E4
- `[architecture][state][testing]` In any undo/redo or command-sourced authority, route ALL structural mutation through the ONE history stack — including entity ALLOCATION (add/remove), not just the obvious edits. Partial command-sourcing is worse than none: acyclicity, reference-identity (generational handles), and cache-coherence guarantees each assume state is a pure function of the linear log, so an out-of-band mutation that interleaves with an undo silently violates whichever invariant that path assumed — and it passes every single-rule test until then. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-graph-editor.md#E1
- `[testing][process][architecture]` Independently-verified components are NOT a verified system: unit-verifying each in isolation structurally cannot find interaction-seam bugs, because each test fixes away the enlarged scope the others impose (a delete's cascade an undo must capture, the extra transitions a cache must invalidate on, the identity a deep-copy must preserve). Run an explicit combined pass aimed at the BOUNDARIES — scope-growth, cache-invalidation, single-source-of-identity are the recurring seams — and write each "when combined with X, do Y" caveat back onto the rule. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-graph-editor.md#E2
- `[architecture][determinism][state]` Reference-for-freshness (saves resolve definitions live by id so patches flow in) vs snapshot-for-replay (bit-identical fingerprint) is NOT an irreducible conflict — version the shared CONTENT (immutable, revision-addressed, a HEAD pointer) and stamp each save with the `contentVersion` it was recorded against: replays pin that revision (deterministic), live play resolves at HEAD (fresh), one mechanism. Don't choose globally or snapshot content into every save. The only residual is definitional (one replay can't be both faithful and patched). → projects/local__sandbox-combined-save-system.md#E1
- `[state][architecture][testing]` A save that stores a REFERENCE into a reshapeable space (definition id, enum stage, cutscene PC, node index) silently repoints to the WRONG target when a migration inserts/reorders/renames in that space — unless the migration remaps every stored reference with an explicit old→new map (guarded for idempotency, or a re-run double-shifts). ORDINAL/positional refs fail SILENT (valid-but-wrong); SYMBOLIC ids fail LOUD (detectable dangling) — so persist stable symbolic ids, not positions. Each reference rule looks correct alone because nothing reshaped its space in its own test. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-save-system.md#E2
- `[gamedesign][gamedev][state]` A percent-max-HP damage cap that punishes high-HP "tank" targets (companion E1) is fixed by wiring a genuinely unused `defense` stat into a PRE-CAP mitigation formula — the two mechanisms are complementary, and the fix has a real, narrow transition zone (K=3 to K=6 in this test) that must be found by sweeping, not guessed → projects/local__sandbox-dbz-team-combat.md#E3
- `[gamedesign][gamedev][state]` A defense-mitigation constant tuned to be competitive at one attacker-count ratio (3v1, K~5) does not transfer to a different ratio (2v1: same K collapses to 5/30 boss-deaths, needs K~3 instead) — balance parameters need re-verification per encounter shape, not one global constant → projects/local__sandbox-dbz-team-combat.md#E4
- `[testing][gamedev][state]` A symmetric-roster control test (identical stats both sides) caught a real ~62.5%/37.5% win-rate skew from stable-sort tie-breaking on equal speed values — always run a symmetric control before trusting an asymmetric multi-agent simulation's balance results → projects/local__sandbox-dbz-team-combat.md#E5
- `[determinism][architecture][testing]` In a deterministic real-time loop, the discrete/continuous split is the load-bearing decision: keep authoritative (fingerprinted) state on the INTEGER LATTICE and make all float/feel state presentation-only — that alone resolves float-tolerance, frame-rate-independence, and completion-edge conflicts. Enforce it with a canonical serializer that THROWS on non-integer core state, fire discrete events on integer tick counts, quantize unavoidable transcendentals (or use squared-distance), and fingerprint PER TICK (end-state agreement hides a mid-run desync). → projects/local__sandbox-combined-game-loop.md#E1
- `[determinism][gamedev][state]` For a fixed-timestep loop to run the same tick COUNT across different frame-rate schedules (deterministic replay/lockstep), drive the accumulator from an INTEGER/fixed-point time source with the tick duration an exact integer of base units — a float-dt accumulator strands a ULP-short tick so equal-total schedules differ by ±1, and a drain-test tolerance is a band-aid that just moves the ±1 boundary and makes the count depend on the chosen epsilon (fuzz: 0/400 wrong with integer time vs tolerance's adversarial over-counts). Per-tick content already keys on the integer tick index. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-game-loop.md#E2
- `[algorithm][physics][gamedev]` A grid-occupancy pathfinder and a continuous swept-collision system composed over the same grid silently disagree on "safe": the pathfinder only forbids entering a flagged cell (no clearance from merely-adjacent cells) and a planner's legitimate "never reject my own start cell" exception also means "never collision-check my own cell" by implication — verify both explicitly, don't infer one system's guarantee from the other's → projects/local__sandbox-combined-movement-stack.md#E1
- `[state][architecture][testing]` Composing an undo/redo command system with any other system that mutates a shared structure in place (a weighted loot table, a pool, a cache) reintroduces the aliasing bug undo-redo's own "deep-copy every preimage" rule already fixed — the shared field looks like global system state, not per-command data, so it's exactly the field the undo system's own tests never had reason to copy: 20/20 seeds diverged from ground truth with reference capture, 0/20 with deep copy → projects/local__sandbox-combined-rpg-progression.md#E1
- `[state][architecture][testing]` Merging two independently-satisfied groups by folding one's fix directly onto the other's proven sandbox (not rebuilding) found the group-merge itself clean in 2/2 stress configs -- because the original aliasing fix was a BLANKET structural-clone copy strategy, not a per-field allowlist, it generalized to a new mutable field for free; verify this generalization explicitly on every group merge rather than assuming a fix "still works" → projects/local__sandbox-combined-rpg-progression.md#E2
- `[gamedesign][gamedev][testing]` A percent-max-HP damage cap (needed for team-fight balance) and a Pareto-diverse skill menu (needed to avoid one dominant pick) individually verified sound — combined, the cap can eliminate a high-variance skill's entire niche (any threshold above the cap fraction becomes unclearable by ANYONE) while the aggregate "ever-optimal" metric stays unchanged, since the skill still wins elsewhere → projects/local__sandbox-dbz-merged.md#E1
- `[state][testing][gamedev]` A live replanner's fix (never reject the agent's own position) removes the only check that could catch a non-atomic save/load desyncing position from the occupancy grid — realistic untargeted obstacle churn produced this 0.58% of the time, and it was silent 100% of the time by construction → projects/local__sandbox-seam-replan-save.md#E1
- `[determinism][architecture][gamedev]` A damage formula verified correct in isolation (power*rate, capped at %maxHp) produces non-integer results 96% of the time with realistic power values — composed with a fixed-timestep/replay system enforcing an integer-lattice authority (game-loop#E1's canonical serializer), every one of those throws; quantize (floor) only at the authoritative-state write boundary, not throughout the formula → projects/local__sandbox-combined-loop-combat.md#E1
- `[animation][architecture][state]` Folding an animation FSM into the same blanket-deep-copied state an undo/redo history already safely generalizes to (rpg-progression#E1/E2) is still wrong: presentation/feel state (frame index, accumulator, one-shot latch) must live OUTSIDE the undo-tracked snapshot (game-loop#E1's split), or every undo silently rewinds the animation clock even on commands that never touched it — 109/218 undo/redo ops corrupted the clock until split out, 0/218 after → projects/local__sandbox-combined-rpg-progression.md#E3
- `[architecture][state][testing]` "Route all mutation through the ONE history stack" (graph-editor#E1) under-specifies granularity for a cascading deallocation: routing it as its OWN separate command measurably regressed correctness versus not routing it at all (10/20 -> 20/20 divergent seeds) because one player action became two undo-stack entries — it must share the SAME transaction as its trigger (0/20) → projects/local__sandbox-combined-rpg-progression.md#E4
- `[state][testing][architecture]` A pathfinder's occupancy grid modeled as a `Set` for fast lookup silently becomes `{}` on `JSON.stringify` — total, not per-field, loss of the same kind savemigrate#E3 already found for `undefined` — every previously-blocked cell reads as open post-reload with zero error; convert explicitly to an array only at the save/load boundary → projects/local__sandbox-combined-movement-stack.md#E2
- `[determinism][physics][gamedev]` Composing an integer-lattice determinism architecture with continuous-substep collision detection shows the two fixes trade off along a single dial (lattice resolution vs. collision half-extent) — fine resolution is safe (<5% drift), coarse resolution is wildly unreliable (both over- and under-detection, an aliasing artifact collapsing two different half-extents to the same count) → projects/local__sandbox-seam-loop-movement.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][algorithm][procgen]` A local-constraint generator's 0% contradiction rate (procgen-wfc#E1) says nothing about global connectivity of the walkable area — composed with keylock#E1's reachability-gated key placement, the fix held perfectly (0 softlocks) only because 96% of maps had unreachable floor regions the fix never had to consider; global connectivity needs its own explicit fixup pass, not a byproduct of local satisfaction → projects/local__sandbox-combined-wfc-keylock.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][algorithm][procgen]` A min-distance-rejection scatter algorithm verified correct on an open plane (scatter#E1) placed props on wall tiles in ~4.5/6 draws when composed with a walled WFC+keylock dungeon — its spacing logic was never wrong, its candidate space (every cell, not just walkable ones) was; filter to valid positions before scattering, don't assume placement correctness implies candidate-space safety → projects/local__sandbox-combined-wfc-keylock.md#E2
- `[determinism][gamedev][state]` A verified sandbox failure mode (float-dt accumulator produces frame-rate-dependent tick counts) reproduces at real, significant magnitude (~20% movement-speed difference, 30fps vs 144fps) in the-game-the-recursion's live explore.js step-cadence gate — untested by the project's own determinism suite, which never drives this code path → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-the-recursion.md#E16
- `[algorithm][procgen][gamedev]` A cell-based pathfinder (astar-replan: is the neighbor cell non-wall?) silently ignores EDGE-based connectivity from a socket/tile generator (WFC: are the facing sockets compatible?) — 500/500 found paths crossed a disconnected edge until moves were gated by edge compatibility, not just destination-cell walkability → projects/local__sandbox-combined-wfc-keylock.md#E3
- `[process][gamedev][saga]` The saga's deliberate independent-repos tradeoff (E13) explicitly accepted pattern drift as a cost, but never turned "check siblings before rebuilding a subsystem" into a Stage-0 process step — E15's concrete drift instance is exactly the predicted cost materializing, and the missing mitigation is a roadmap-doc gap, not a per-project lesson → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-prologue.md#E16
- `[algorithm][architecture][state]` A dependency-ordered formula system (toposort schedules by DECLARED deps; expr#E1 fails loud on undefined variables) never silently corrupts data, but an undeclared-yet-referenced dependency can "accidentally work" via incidental topological-round alignment — a later, unrelated edit elsewhere in the graph can flip it from a returned value to a thrown error with the error pointing at the symptom, not the missing edge → projects/local__sandbox-combined-skilltree-formula.md#E1
- `[algorithm][state][process]` Making a state-recomputing replanner undoable: the undo snapshot must cover the FULL state the replanner reads (position+occupancy+caches+rng) — a replanner that trusts current state unconditionally turns any missing snapshot field into a silent wrong/absent path (sandbox-verified: position-only undo → no path, no error). → projects/local__sandbox-combined-undo-replan.md#E1
- `[browser][tooling][process]` A deferred cache-bust gap (E8) got fixed in two siblings by a rule that explicitly said "fix it in all of them, not just the one under investigation" — but the origin project (where the gap was first catalogued) was never rechecked and still has it live today → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-prologue.md#E17
- `[algorithm][determinism][state]` Amortizing a variable-cost replan across a deterministic fixed-timestep loop: budget per tick in WORK UNITS (node expansions), not milliseconds — a wall-clock budget makes the finish tick machine-dependent and silently breaks tick-count determinism (sandbox: ms→11 vs 32, work→11 vs 11). → projects/local__sandbox-combined-replan-gameloop.md#E1
- `[algorithm][process][harness]` Diffing two toposort orderings of the SAME dependency graph reports spurious changes (4/6 nodes falsely "removed and re-added") whenever only the declaration/tie-break order differs — neither system is buggy (myers-diff#E3 already knew edit scripts aren't unique; here the INPUTS aren't canonical), so canonicalize order-non-unique output with a deterministic tie-break before diffing, don't rely on the diff to compensate → projects/local__sandbox-combined-skilltree-formula.md#E2
- `[algorithm][state][gamedesign]` shuffle#E1's sort-comparator bias, connected to a concrete consumer: if a scatter/placement algorithm randomizes candidate try-order via `sort(random)` instead of Fisher-Yates, the first-tried (most-likely-to-succeed) candidate is skewed 91.7% from uniform across 50k trials — an ~2x over/under-representation of specific cells, game-visible at scale, not just a statistical curiosity → projects/local__sandbox-scatter.md#E2
- `[algorithm][gamedev][state]` flowfield#E1's own flagged scope gap ("a dynamic scenario would need its own check") is now checked: forcing the flow field to rebuild every tick (worst case for amortization) still leaves the flow-field-beats-per-agent-A* crossover intact — it only shifts absolute cost, never reverses direction, so the "5+ agents" rule of thumb safely extends to replanning/dynamic-obstacle scenarios → projects/local__sandbox-pathfinding-flowfield.md#E2
- `[algo][state][architecture]` A lazy-refill token bucket (ratelimit#E1's own verified fix, untouched and not at fault) goes stale when its CAPACITY is a dynamic formula output (expr+toposort-derived stats) rather than static config — raw tokens stayed above a newly-shrunk capacity in 200/200 trials until the next request happened to trigger refill(); fix with an explicit re-clamp hook fired at the capacity-change event itself, not deferred to the bucket's own lazy path → projects/local__sandbox-combined-skilltree-formula.md#E3
- `[determinism][testing][gamedev]` Constant-RNG-roll-count-per-decision (waiting-city#E9) is necessary but not sufficient for probabilistic-test stability — answering-deep#E3 shows the discipline followed correctly while an unrelated content change (shifting active-entity population, not any decision's branch) still broke a smoke test via shared-stream entanglement; tests need their own isolation discipline too → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-the-answering-deep.md#E12
- `[input][ui][gamedev]` A UI-hint-source-of-truth fix in one code path (prologue#E3) didn't prevent the identical bug class reappearing in a NEW code path in the sequel that copied the architecture (wrong-sky#E6) — two instances of an untestable-by-unit-test bug class is the signal to centralize the computation, not keep fixing instances by hand → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-wrong-sky.md#E14
- `[process][gamedev][saga]` Comparing a successful cross-sibling fix (same session, both repos already open) against a failed one (deferred, rediscovered in a different session) shows propagation succeeds by favorable circumstance, not by an enforced process — the checklist for "which siblings to check" must come from memory's project index, not from which repos happen to be open → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-wrong-sky.md#E15
- `[tooling][process]` brain train edge/group/combined rank purely by shared-tag count with no accessibility filter — across 7 invocations this session, the top pick was an inaccessible repo the majority of the time, forcing a manual rescan of the ranked list each call → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E6
- `[gamedesign][algorithm][testing]` Sharing one "distance from spawn" value (Euclidean) between an A* heuristic (correct: 100/100 optimal paths, underestimation is REQUIRED) and a difficulty/loot pacing gradient (wrong: 14.6% of cells off by >50%, max 86% error, underestimation is HARMFUL) means the working pathfinder gives zero signal the difficulty system is broken — the two consumers need separate distance sources, not one shared value → projects/local__sandbox-combined-pathmetrics.md#E1
- `[algorithm][gamedev][testing]` A corner-cutting guard applied at candidate-move GENERATION time (before cost/heuristic computation) is structurally weight-independent — composed with weighted A* (weights 1-10), it held at 0/95 corner-cuts across densities, while a guard-off control reproduced the known ~100% baseline; guards gating candidate generation compose safely with search optimizations by construction → projects/local__sandbox-combined-pathmetrics.md#E2
- `[algorithm][graph][state]` A stable force-directed layout (graph-viz#E3, no NaN/drift) has NO reliable spatial correlation with an externally-meaningful order (toposort rank) — correlation ranged -0.95 to +0.95 across 30 seeds of the identical graph, sign determined by arbitrary initial positions, not structure; fixed with an explicit rank-pull force (0.523 -> 0.977 mean correlation, consistently signed) → projects/local__sandbox-combined-skilltree-formula.md#E4
- `[gamedesign][algorithm][procgen]` A connectivity-fixup pass built and verified for WFC's disconnection mechanism (wfc-keylock#E1) required zero changes to repair a completely different generator's disconnection (density-rolled maze, maze#E1) — 1000/1000 disconnected before, 0/1000 after — because it operates on the final grid's region topology alone, independent of how the grid was produced → projects/local__sandbox-combined-wfc-keylock.md#E4
- `[procgen][algorithm][state]` Rasterizing an L-system's continuous turtle-graphics segments by ENDPOINTS ONLY fragments a single, fully-connected path into 14/62/306 spurious disconnected components at iterations 2/3/4 — worsening exactly as lsystem#E1's own "sparser at higher iterations" finding predicts — because segments longer than ~1 grid cell skip intermediate cells; full-line rasterization gives 1/1/1 components → projects/local__sandbox-combined-wfc-keylock.md#E5
- `[testing][determinism][input][gamedev]` Frame-perfect scripted input testing on a tick-based system independently exposes two non-overlapping bug classes — sub-frame press invisibility and same-tick gate-update ordering — composed from sandbox-dbz-combat#E1 + the-game-prologue#E2 → projects/pappydapimp69__test.md#E14
- `[algorithm][physics][gamedev][testing]` A shared spatial lattice needs two independent checks — brute-force resolution-vs-smallest-extent validation, and an explicit audit of every second system's assumed guarantees — composed from sandbox-combined-movement-stack#E1 + dog#E13 → projects/pappydapimp69__test.md#E15
- `[mobile][testing][accessibility][gamedev]` "Tested on mobile" is not one property — layout, real-input-API response, device/OS-geometry occlusion, and cross-feature overlap during co-active states are four independently-invisible failure classes, composed from dog#E25 + dog#E27 + the-game-the-recursion#E10 + the-game-the-recursion#E11 → projects/pappydapimp69__test.md#E16
- `[algorithm][gamedesign][gamedev][procgen]` A procgen connectivity algorithm's guarantee is either global-by-construction (a spanning tree — trust it) or local (adjacent-constraint satisfaction — needs an explicit separate connectivity pass), composed from wfc-keylock#E1 + wfc-keylock#E3 + the-game-the-recursion#E6 → projects/pappydapimp69__test.md#E17
- `[gamedesign][gamedev]` the-game-prologue's archetype skill blocks (rotational {2,1,1} across melee/aura/perception) independently match the exact Pareto-diversity structure sandbox-dbz-combat#E2 later found necessary to prevent monotonic-power-axis collapse — structural match confirmed, in-practice balance not measured → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-prologue.md#E18
- `[gamedev][state][determinism]` A gate-bypass bug (recursion#E2) and an RNG-drift-risk discipline (waiting-city#E9) are the same higher-order rule from two angles: any value a reducer trusts to gate or perturb an outcome must be fixed in state before the dependent command exists, never sourced from that command's own payload → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-the-recursion.md#E17
- `[parsing][algorithm][testing]` expr's fail-loud undefined-variable fix (reliable against a graph-ordering composition) does NOT protect against a data-corruption composition: csv#E1's row-splitting bug fed a malformed formula that silently evaluated to NaN and propagated through the whole skill tree, 0/3 loud errors, because the corruption lands on expr#E2's separate, already-known, unchecked parseFloat path — a parser's fail-loud guarantee is only as strong as its weakest unchecked path → projects/local__sandbox-combined-skilltree-formula.md#E5
- `[architecture][determinism][state]` save-system#E1's freshness-vs-determinism reconciliation (pin snapshots to captured content, never re-resolve retroactively) is a GENERAL pattern, not save/load-specific: undoing a "learn skill" action after its formula is patched silently returns the NEW patched value if undo re-derives instead of restores (tier1=50 instead of undefined) — apply the same pin-at-capture-time fix to any capture-and-restore mechanism composed with a live-patchable definition source → projects/local__sandbox-combined-skilltree-formula.md#E6
- `[determinism][gamedev][testing]` "Redo" a combat sequence by re-running the simulation on a continuing live RNG stream (instead of replaying the captured log) matched the original WINNER in 300/300 trials — a structural coincidence of the mechanic's damage asymmetry — while the full turn-order log diverged 300/300 and final HP diverged 135/300; verify replay against the full trajectory, not a summary statistic, or a passing test can hide a completely different replayed fight → projects/local__sandbox-combined-rpg-progression.md#E5
- `[gamedesign][algorithm][procgen]` A connectivity-fixup and a scatter-candidate-filter, each already verified generator-agnostic across WFC and maze, held with zero changes against a THIRD structurally-different generator (CA, multiple roughly-equal regions instead of one-dominant-plus-islands): 237/500 -> 0/500 disconnected, 0/3000 wall placements — both fixes operate on final grid representation, not generation mechanism → projects/local__sandbox-combined-wfc-keylock.md#E6
- `[gamedev][state][determinism][testing]` A reducer being fully deterministic gives zero evidence an FSM state can actually be exited — sandbox-verified: a 100%-reproducible liveness bug survives inside a byte-identical-replay reducer until an unconditional max-dwell timeout is added, composed from dog#E11 + the-game-the-waiting-city#E9 → projects/pappydapimp69__test.md#E18
- `[determinism][gamedev][state][testing]` "No RNG, no wall-clock" is necessary but not sufficient for a frame-rate-independent authoritative sim — an accumulator that resets to 0 on fire is 20% frame-rate-dependent by construction (sandbox-reproduced, matching a real ~20% case), carrying the overshoot forward drops it to <1%, composed from test#E2 + the-game-the-recursion#E16 → projects/pappydapimp69__test.md#E19
- `[algorithm][gamedev][state]` A distance field for placement/difficulty must match the agent's move model: for diagonal (8-dir) movement use an octile 8-connected Dijkstra field — Euclidean underestimates (ignores walls) and 4-connected BFS overestimates (ignores diagonal shortcuts), erring in opposite directions (sandbox: euclid 6.0 < octile 13.66 < bfs 16.0). → projects/local__sandbox-combined-diagonal-distance.md#E1
- `[state][gamedev][testing]` NaN-poisons-shared-state prevention (audit constructor initializers) and containment (isFinite-guard at consumption boundaries) are complementary, not redundant — sandbox-confirmed: prevention alone leaves a real gap for any non-constructor injection channel, composed from dog#E3 + the-game-the-recursion#E1 → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E28
- `[state][gamedev][physics]` A full-session-revert respawn is a teleport in dog#E15's sense even though nobody calls it one — sandbox-confirmed: an unreset respawn injects a false one-frame velocity spike 30x steady-state into any position-differencing tracker, eliminated by resetting prev-position at the respawn call site, composed from dog#E15 + the-game-the-waiting-city#E1 → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E29
- `[algo][state][determinism]` A Bloom filter seeded at construction from an ambient source (Date.now()) and persisted without its seed — only its bit array — produces false negatives on every reload with a new ambient seed (1000/1000); persist the seed, not just the resulting bits → projects/local__sandbox-combined-bloom-audio.md#E1
- `[audio][dsp][testing]` psg#E1's actual measured noise-LFSR period (57337, not a power of 2) fed through mix#E1's naive radix-2 FFT silently discarded 43% of the buffer with zero error — a real project value, not a synthetic edge case, reproduces the exact "silent garbage" failure; zero-pad to the next power of 2, don't truncate → projects/local__sandbox-combined-bloom-audio.md#E2
- `[audio][state][determinism]` Same pattern as loop 1's Bloom-filter-seed bug, new generator: an LFSR noise channel reloaded from its DEFAULT seed instead of its persisted CURRENT register produces an audible discontinuity (7/20 mismatched samples at resume); persist the generator's live internal state, not its construction parameters → projects/local__sandbox-combined-bloom-audio.md#E3
- `[determinism][gamedev][state]` An input-gated cadence must count integer ticks, not `cooldownMs -= dt` — the float form makes the discrete-event RATE frame-rate-dependent (live fairness bug), the tick lattice makes it frame-independent (sandbox: float 20 vs 16, tick 19 vs 19). → projects/local__sandbox-combined-cadence-frametick.md#E1
- `[determinism][gamedev][input]` Intra-tick op order (read-input vs advance-phase/set-flag) is authoritative discrete state, not presentation — a gate flag set after the input sample opens its window a tick late (same window+input, hit vs miss); pin the order on the integer lattice, set gate flags before the read (sandbox: on-tick cancel misses vs hits by order alone). → projects/local__sandbox-combined-tickorder-cancel.md#E1
- `[determinism][algorithm][state]` A fixed seed isn't reproducibility: if incidental/cosmetic code draws a variable count from the same prng stream before a gameplay draw, the outcome shifts — give gameplay its own stream and treat a branch-dependent draw COUNT as a leak (sandbox: same seed → 0.5823 vs 0.7770 shared, invariant when separated). → projects/local__sandbox-combined-seed-drawstream.md#E1
- `[browser][tooling][process]` A per-deploy cache-bust applied only to the entry URL (dog#E5's fix as literally described) is sandbox-confirmed insufficient — a nested import keeps a stable, stale cache key across deploys unless it's versioned too, composed from dog#E5 + the-game-prologue#E8 → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E30
- `[accessibility][gamedev][testing]` An eased color/luminance transition between two individually CVD-safe states can pass through a mid-transition state that's fully confusable with a third, unrelated entity — verified sustained 50-74% of a 300ms transition, affecting normal vision too. Endpoint-only verification (t=0/t=1) is not sufficient for perceptual-safety properties; sample the full trajectory at ≤1-frame granularity. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-accessibility-render.md#E1
- `[accessibility][gamedev]` A locked-hue palette retrofit approved via a flat "15 raw lightness points" heuristic can pass cases the real CVD-simulation check rejects — the true required separation is hue-dependent (measured ~25-30 points for some hues) since lightness doesn't map linearly onto CVD-simulated luminance across hues. Always validate retrofit candidates against the real simulation, never the flat threshold alone; also verify feasibility exists before locking a hue. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-accessibility-render.md#E2
- `[dsp][realtime][testing]` A lock-free SPSC consumer that "drains until empty" each callback is only real-time-safe if the producer's burst size is bounded — iteration COUNT is a budget dimension independent of per-message cost, and a bursty cross-thread producer can hand the callback orders of magnitude more iterations than expected (measured ~1300x overrun). Cap messages drained per callback with carryover; don't trust "each iteration is cheap" to bound the total. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-audio-realtime.md#E1
- `[dsp][state][testing]` A decay/feedback loop assuming repeated multiplication by a constant < 1 eventually reaches exact zero is wrong for some decay rates in IEEE-754 — rounding can land on a stable nonzero subnormal fixed point (verified: `0.9 * Number.MIN_VALUE === Number.MIN_VALUE`) that the multiplier never steps below. Flush-to-zero is a TERMINATION correctness requirement for decay loops, not just a CPU-performance optimization. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-audio-realtime.md#E2
- `[gamedesign][state][testing]` A quest/narrative stage gated on an event TYPE alone (e.g. "a monster died") silently advances on any causally-unrelated event of the same type (a different monster's death) once more than one possible source exists — reproduced directly. Mint a causal token at the specific triggering outcome and require it downstream; never infer causality from type-plus-coincidental-timing on an event bus. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-narrative-flags.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][state]` An event bus's `emit()` iterating the LIVE listener array while a handler unsubscribes mid-dispatch (e.g. a standard "unsubscribe on completion" pattern) silently skips whichever listener now occupies the shifted index — reproduced directly. `emit()` must dispatch against an immutable snapshot of listeners taken before the loop starts; this is a hard requirement, not optional hardening. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-narrative-flags.md#E2
- `[ui][gamedev][architecture]` A single action-resolver (dog#E20) is sandbox-confirmed to prevent the-game-wrong-sky#E14's recurring hint/handler drift by construction — the same availability-logic change that desyncs two independently-authored surfaces cannot desync two callers of one resolver, composed from dog#E20 + the-game-wrong-sky#E14 → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E31
- `[determinism][architecture][state]` Pinning a reducer gate threshold once (recursion#E17's fix for command-supplied bypass) is necessary but not sufficient when that threshold is itself a live-formula output (skilltree#E3's territory) — sweep confirmed the resulting staleness bug 48/48 trials under pin-once-only, 0/48 with an added eager invalidation hook → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-the-recursion.md#E18
- `[architecture][state][security]` "Trust the agent's position unconditionally" (astar-replan#E2) and "never trust a command-supplied gate threshold" (recursion#E17) looked contradictory but are the same rule keyed on VALUE SOURCE not VALUE TYPE — re-sourcing a "trustworthy" position from a command payload produces a real, sandbox-demonstrated wall-clip exploit → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-the-recursion.md#E19
- `[ui][event-ordering][gamedev]` the-game-the-answering-deep#E1's modal-overwrite-race bug class reproduces live and unfixed in the-game-wrong-sky's actual shipping code — the player's first-ever NPC conversation shows an empty-flavor-text modal, confirmed by running the real reducer, not a reimplementation; the already-verified sibling fix was never ported → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-wrong-sky.md#E16
- `[determinism][algo][testing]` A Bloom filter seed-persistence fix (verified for correctness after reload) also meets a much stricter bar — bit-identical canonical fingerprint across independent replays — without modification: 0/200 mismatches once seeded from a deterministic game seed → projects/local__sandbox-combined-bloom-audio.md#E4
- `[audio][dsp][determinism]` audio-realtime#E2's stuck-nonzero-subnormal decay finding (proven on a simple `y*=0.9`) also traps genesis-fm#E1's two-term feedback-averaging recurrence — never reaches exact 0 even after 30,000 samples without flush-to-zero, correctly reaches 0 at sample 9857 with it; the fixed point is a property of float64 decay in general, not the specific formula → projects/local__sandbox-combined-bloom-audio.md#E5
- `[determinism][gamedesign][testing]` A resource-costed decision's own AFFORDABILITY branch needs waiting-city#E9's constant-roll-count discipline applied specifically at that branch, not just at the top-level action choice — a routine cost rebalance caused an unrelated entity's decision sequence to diverge in 157-167/200 ticks when the sub-roll was skipped-when-unaffordable, 0/200 when always-drawn-but-conditionally-used → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-the-waiting-city.md#E14
- `[testing][harness][process]` A phantom E2E failure that "looks like a game bug" can come from two orthogonal, sandbox-confirmed-independent causes — shared process-level resources across concurrent workers (not auto-isolated by Playwright per its own docs) and stale scripted coordinates against autonomous movement — fixing one gives zero protection against the other, composed from test#E9 + the-game-the-waiting-city#E2 → projects/pappydapimp69__test.md#E20
- `[gamedev][state][process]` Derived state going stale against a changed content source is one shape at two boundaries — a runtime reload (test#E8) and a save persisting across a hotfix (the-game-prologue#E10) — sandbox-confirmed both need the same fix: one shared, explicit resync path, not per-consumer ad-hoc updates, composed from test#E8 + the-game-prologue#E10 → projects/pappydapimp69__test.md#E21
- `[architecture][state][testing]` Stress-testing the whole mega group at multi-agent scale (6-10 NPCs sharing one command history, every prior fix composed simultaneously) found a bug invisible to every single-agent test in this project: an "undo my own last action" implementation that jumps to a per-agent recorded stack index, instead of always popping the shared stack's actual top, corrupts state in 20/20 seeds the moment a second agent acts — invisible at N=1 by mathematical necessity → projects/local__sandbox-combined-rpg-progression.md#E6
- `[determinism][algorithm][state]` An amortized computation inside a deterministic loop must survive a save/restore of its IN-FLIGHT state — verify a mid-computation checkpoint resumes to the no-save baseline (correct frontier serialization matches; a frontier dropped by naive JSON diverges); saving settled state misses it (sandbox: correct==baseline, naive→no path). → projects/local__sandbox-combined-replan-save.md#E1
- `[algorithm][gamedev][testing]` A pathfinding stack (distance + diagonal cost + heuristic) verified pairwise still needs an END-TO-END check on one map — assert the distance field, A* g-cost, and heuristic admissibility agree simultaneously; pairwise agreement doesn't prove the triple consistent (sandbox: field==A* cost, octile admissible, 25.66 vs Manhattan 16). → projects/local__sandbox-combined-pathfinding-stack.md#E1
- `[determinism][gamedev][state]` A real-time loop stacking cadence + combat + fixed-timestep is frame-independent only if EVERY discrete gate is on the tick lattice — fingerprint the whole loop's discrete events across frame schedules, not each timer alone (sandbox: 6ms==33ms ticks/steps/cancels). → projects/local__sandbox-combined-realtime-loop.md#E1
- `[determinism][gamedesign][testing][architecture]` A formula system's documented "throws after an unrelated graph edit flips topo order" fragility (skilltree-formula#E1) composes with waiting-city#E9's constant-roll-count discipline via a THIRD trigger beyond affordability branches (E14): a naive catch-and-return-early on formula error skips a roll and desyncs every other entity on the shared RNG stream — 50/50 ticks diverged under catch-early, 0/50 under always-draw-then-default, across 4 seeds → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-the-waiting-city.md#E15
- `[architecture][gamedesign][ai][testing]` movement-stack#E1's commit-time occupancy veto (trust the destination cell, but recheck at commit) is correctly implemented in waiting-city's real shipped code (0/48 overlaps, live-verified) — but the veto silently converts into a wasted AI tick when the FSM's first-ever transition assumes the move succeeds: an always-aggro enemy adjacent on tick 1 goes patrol->chase->attack instead of straight to attack, reproduced 48/48, absent in the gradual-approach control → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-the-waiting-city.md#E16
- `[testing][determinism][architecture][security]` astar-replan#E1's "silent precondition violation is invisible in normal-shaped output" mechanism confirmed live in wrong-sky's real reducer: a broken dodge-gate (918 strikes-during-dodge, 2758 leaked dmg/60 seeds) fingerprints byte-identical to a correct one under replay — the repo's own smoke suite never exercises dodge timing, a verified test-coverage gap (real gate is currently correct) mirroring wrong-sky's own E14 unthreaded-gate bug shape → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-wrong-sky.md#E17
- `[architecture][save][gamedesign]` wrong-sky#E2's real snapshot-over-reconstruct idiom (session-local, free at respawn scope) is reused for cross-deploy save persistence, where it silently inherits save-system#E1's named freshness cost — live-verified: a content-only patch (no WORLD_VERSION bump) never reaches a continued save (reward 4 vs fresh 999, objectives 1 vs 2), and the only remedy is a full wholesale save wipe, not a scoped fix → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-wrong-sky.md#E18
- `[state][determinism][testing]` V8 (dog's runtime) really does randomize an internal hash seed per process, same as Python's PYTHONHASHSEED that broke sandbox-bloom#E2's filter — but sandbox-confirmed (two independent process runs, diffed byte-identical) it does NOT leak into native Map/Set/Object iteration order; a hand-rolled hash-seeded structure would still be vulnerable, composed from sandbox-bloom#E2 + dog#E12 → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E32
- `[physics][state][determinism]` dog#E10's lerp-follow formula never reaches exact convergence — sandbox-confirmed via a magnitude-scaling check to be a DIFFERENT IEEE754 mechanism (large-accumulator addition-precision loss) than sandbox-combined-bloom-audio#E5's subnormal-rounding fixed point, but the same practical fix applies to both: epsilon threshold, never exact equality, composed from sandbox-combined-bloom-audio#E5 + dog#E10 → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E33
- `[determinism][gamedesign][testing]` A pity/dup-protection pool's own COLLAPSE BOUNDARY (loot-pity#E1's territory, where the eligible set narrows to exactly one candidate) is a third, distinct trigger for violating waiting-city#E9's constant-roll-count discipline, beyond E14's affordability branch and E15's error branch — "no need to roll, it's forced" skips a roll and desyncs an unrelated entity on 290+/300 ticks; onset delay is content-tunable (8 to 178 ticks), letting a short regression fixture miss it entirely → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-the-waiting-city.md#E17
- `[determinism][architecture][state]` recursion#E17's "pin the gate value in state" rule is necessary but not sufficient when that value is a power-scaling-style compounding formula (sandbox-power-scaling#E1's territory) — logically-equivalent computation orders (live-sequential vs. fold/replay reconstruction) diverge in float64 from arc 2 (fingerprint-only) and actually flip the gate boolean near E1's own ~21-arc safe-integer crossover (23-29 arcs, live-verified); no current live bug (real gates are plain integers, confirmed via grep) → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-the-recursion.md#E20
- `[determinism][architecture][testing]` prologue#E9's existence-gated quest spawning (a deliberately correct fix for pre-acceptance independence) is ALSO a population-timing perturbation of the shared RNG stream, live-verified in this repo's own real code: accepting a quest before an unrelated `stalker1` fight changes that fight's damage roll in 6/8 seeds (RNG state diverges 8/8), the exact mechanism answering-deep#E3 already documented, now confirmed in a second, structurally different AI-dispatch architecture (adjacency-gated, not per-tick) → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-prologue.md#E19
- `[rendering][gamedev][algorithm]` Researched claim "slope-scaled shadow bias is more robust than constant bias" verified numerically: it eliminates acne (0/90 swept angles vs constant bias's 2/90) but overcorrects into an ~11x larger peter-panning gap at the same grazing angles unless clamped or paired with normal-offset bias — adopting half a two-part recommendation relocates the tradeoff, doesn't solve it → projects/local__sandbox-shadowbias.md#E1
- `[determinism][framerate][architecture]` prologue's real tick scheduler (src/app/game.js:326-329, `nextTickAt = now + TICK_MS`) loses ticks at low frame rate — 0/400 jittery schedules matched the ideal tick count (vs 400/400 for a drain-and-subtract accumulator), and steady 30fps needs 33s more real time than 60fps to reach the same game-tick over ~8.3min (~6.6% drift) — live-verified by running the real unmodified game.js; the project's own golden-fingerprint test can't catch this (replays a fixed command script, never wall-clock scheduling); third live instance of this bug family this session (recursion#E16, wrong-sky#E9) → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-prologue.md#E20
- `[testing][algorithm][process]` A coverage/completability report has two independent blind spots — a no-winner sentinel silently pollutes the aggregation tally (dbz-combat#E3), and a fixed enumeration bounds coverage to a tiny fraction of the true space regardless (the-game-the-recursion#E14) — sandbox-confirmed fixing either leaves the other fully open, composed from dbz-combat#E3 + the-game-the-recursion#E14 → projects/local__sandbox-dbz-combat.md#E5
- `[state][testing][algorithm]` savemigrate#E2's idempotency discipline, tested against a save payload carrying an UNRELATED system's fields (Bloom filter seed/bits) alongside the versioned data being reshaped: byte-identical output after 1 and 2 migrations, fields untouched — a version-gated migrator using non-destructive spread is safe for any unrelated payload data by construction, not by luck → projects/local__sandbox-combined-bloom-audio.md#E6
- `[orchestration][routing][knowledge-capture]` classify a mined finding by target repository type before choosing its output format → projects/local__scabby-the-spoon.md#E1
- `[orchestration][schema][submission]` derive repository proposals from the live intake contract rather than a generic remembered format → projects/local__scabby-the-spoon.md#E2
- `[determinism][gamedev][testing]` game-loop#E2's "fingerprint per tick, end-state hides a mid-run desync" principle is load-bearing, not a nice-to-have, for cadence-frametick#E1's float-cooldown bug: two frame schedules (16ms/20ms steps) over an identical 400ms run reported IDENTICAL end-state fire counts (20 vs 20, a false pass) while per-tick comparison found real divergence at 4/24 sampled instants → projects/local__sandbox-combined-cadence-frametick.md#E2
- `[algorithm][gamedev][state]` diagonal#E1's corner-cutting guard, verified only for live A* search, was never re-applied to diagonal-distance#E1's own full-field octile Dijkstra — the unguarded distance field underestimated distance at 21/98 (21%) of reachable cells, up to 31% at a deliberate wall-pinch — a validity guard belongs to the movement model, not the specific search algorithm asking about it → projects/local__sandbox-combined-diagonal-distance.md#E2
- `[algorithm][pathfinding][gamedev][testing]` A pathfinding module verified against synthetic obstacle layouts "matching" a real game's density is not fully verified until run against the ACTUAL obstacle/waypoint data — two real bugs (a blocked-goal fallback that reintroduces the original clipping; a point-sampled grid missing a real-sized obstacle that doesn't cover any cell's exact center) only surfaced once specific real coordinates were used, not from synthetic layouts of matching density. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E34
- `[ui][browser]` A `position:fixed;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%)` control bar with no `flex-wrap`/`max-width` clips equally off BOTH screen edges once content width exceeds viewport width (measured: 57px/edge at 500px, 92px/edge at 380px) — fix with `flex-wrap:wrap` + `max-width:95vw` + shrinkable children; test at narrow viewports deliberately, not just the design resolution → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E7
- `[audio][dsp][testing]` genesis-fm#E1's own prescribed fix for peak-meter blindness (a full-render FFT) is not self-sufficient — sandbox-confirmed it silently inherits genesis-mix#E1's non-power-of-2-length bug (identical result for artifact-present vs. clean render), realistically triggered by genesis-psg#E1's actual documented hardware period (57337), composed from genesis-fm#E1 + genesis-mix#E1 + genesis-psg#E1 → projects/local__sandbox-genesis-fm.md#E2
- `[determinism][algorithm][state]` replan-save#E1's "frontier dropped by naive JSON diverges" IS savemigrate#E3's undefined-key-drop mechanism, arising naturally in a BFS start-node's parent field — sandbox-confirmed ordinary property reads can't observe it at all (obj.parent===undefined either way), only a structural fingerprint comparison can, composed from sandbox-combined-replan-save#E1 + sandbox-savemigrate#E3 → projects/local__sandbox-combined-replan-save.md#E2
- `[audio][dsp][testing]` A short-window FFT spectral comparison (absolute OR normalized) cannot verify LFSR tap correctness — 3 of 6 tested wrong taps, with periods wildly different from the correct hardware tap (57337 vs 200000+/23622/16383), produced spectra nearly indistinguishable from correct (energy ratio ~1.0, normalized L2 ~0.017); periodicity is a time-domain property a short frequency-domain window cannot see → projects/local__sandbox-combined-bloom-audio.md#E7
- `[determinism][architecture][state]` loop-combat#E1's "quantize damage to integer at the boundary" fix is sufficient only while both the raw damage AND the cap threshold stay within float64-safe-integer range — with attacker power and defender maxHp compounding together (power-scaling#E1's own 5.5x/arc rate), two mathematically-equivalent damage formulas diverge in their quantized output starting at arc 25 (diff 32768, growing to 134M+ by arc 30) → projects/local__sandbox-combined-loop-combat.md#E2
- `[physics][dsp][testing]` The "decaying signal gets stuck at a nonzero subnormal, never reaches exact zero" finding is domain-independent (verified identically for a physics restitution velocity, no audio involved) — an exact-zero rest check never trips, but a conventional epsilon-threshold check trips ~140x earlier than the subnormal range, normally masking the hazard. Composed with swept-collision: a subnormal velocity overflows `t=(boundary-x)/v` to `+Infinity`, so any time-of-impact formula needs an explicit finite/range check independent of whether upstream velocity is floored. → projects/local__sandbox-brain-loop1-denormal-swept.md#E1
- `[animation][dsp][testing]` A fixed-timestep frame-accumulator's own "drain in a while loop, step() per sub-step" fix is required for discrete EVENT counting too, not just numeric drift — checking a threshold-crossing event (e.g. an audio loop-seam) once per frame off the aggregate advance undercounts the true crossing count by up to N-1 when a lag spike drains N sub-steps, even though final position stays correct. Detect/count events per sub-step inside the drain loop. → projects/local__sandbox-brain-loop2-frameacc-loopseam.md#E1
- `[algorithm][gamedev][state]` "Octile 8-connected Dijkstra field" (diagonal-distance#E1) is underspecified without corner-cut prevention (pathfinding-diagonal#E1) — sandbox-confirmed an unguarded field underestimates true walkable distance by 41% through a diagonal wall gap, composed from sandbox-combined-diagonal-distance#E1 + sandbox-pathfinding-diagonal#E1 → projects/local__sandbox-combined-diagonal-distance.md#E3
- `[algorithm][pathfinding][gamedev][testing]` Path SMOOTHING (string-pulling a zigzagging grid-A* path into a few waypoints) is a hazard separate from the pathfinding search itself — a shortcut check against grid-cell walkability instead of real obstacle geometry can cut a corner directly through an obstacle the search correctly routed around (reproduced: naive string-pull collapsed a 21-point verified-clear path to 2 points that cut straight through the obstacle). String-pulling needs its own circle/geometry-vs-segment line-of-sight check, independent of the search's own correctness. → projects/local__sandbox-dog-pathfinding.md#E1
- `[algorithm][graph][state][testing]` A rank-pull force (pulling each node toward its own category's fixed sector) gives the SAME drift-protection as a generic centering force for disconnected force-directed-layout components — verified initial-condition-invariant (48.828/35.591 without → 42.289/42.294 with, from two different starts) — but does NOT protect against the orthogonal coincident-node NaN hazard (`nanHit: true` either way): a new force added for an unrelated reason can fix one failure mode of a layout and do nothing for another in the same kernel. → projects/local__sandbox-rankpull-forcelayout.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][input][state]` A hold-to-charge meter's rate formula (the-game-prologue#E7, "shaped by current fill percent") inherits skilltree-formula#E3's stale-capacity bug with a worse consequence — sandbox-confirmed fill-percent exceeding 100% drives the rate NEGATIVE, draining the meter while held, composed from sandbox-combined-skilltree-formula#E3 + the-game-prologue#E7 → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-prologue.md#E21
- `[gamedev][state][testing][process]` A shared-resource stress test that only asserts on position ("exactly one succeeds, the rest revert cleanly" — dog#E16's own recipe) is blind to an FSM-driven agent silently advancing its decision state before a commit-time veto confirms it won — sandbox-confirmed the literal recipe passes while 2/3 agents sit reverted-but-"arrived", composed from dog#E16 + the-game-the-waiting-city#E16 → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E35
- `[architecture][testing][gamedesign]` prologue#E9's existence-gating (quests spawn targets only on acceptance) solves only the TEMPORAL half of causal correctness — the real kill-objective match (`obj.target === e.kind` in src/sim/reduce.js) checks kind not instance, inheriting narrative-flags#E1's type-only fragility; live-verified exploit completes "Clear the Road" without ever touching the real target, masked only by today's one-entity-per-kind content scope; sibling `arcObserve` system already does this correctly by pinning entity id → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-prologue.md#E22
- `[testing][determinism][gamedesign]` A command-withholding i-frame architecture (test#E4, the-game-wrong-sky#E9 — independently verified twice) is not test-covered by a general determinism fingerprint — sandbox-confirmed a wrong window length is invisible unless the script specifically probes the disputed boundary tick, composed from test#E4 + the-game-wrong-sky#E9 → projects/pappydapimp69__test.md#E22
- `[algo][determinism][gamedev][state][testing]` Integer time removes frame-rate nondeterminism, not process-seed nondeterminism. If a hash-based structure participates in authoritative state, its seed is part of the replay state and must be deterministic or persisted. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-game-loop.md#E3
- `[audio][dsp][determinism][realtime][state][testing]` For audio resume paths, verify continuity and callback budget separately. "State resumes correctly" is not evidence that the resume repair queue is real-time-safe. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-audio-realtime.md#E3
- `[audio][dsp][realtime][state][determinism][testing]` State reload bugs that create producer bursts are realtime bugs when callbacks drain until empty. Persist deterministic seeds/state, and still cap callback queue work because producer correctness should not be the only thing enforcing the realtime budget. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-bloom-audio.md#E8
- `[viz][accessibility][animation][web][testing]` Pacing is not control. Any Brain/Viz animation that auto-runs longer than five seconds beside other content needs a user control, and non-essential motion should honor reduced-motion preference. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-brain-viz-growth.md#E1
- `[viz][canvas][animation][growth-visualization][performance][testing]` For Brain/Viz growth batches, pace by batch size and composite expensive effects by pass count. Do not let per-mark filters reintroduce a linear cost spike inside a fixed reveal budget. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-brain-viz-growth.md#E2
- `[viz][accessibility][visualdesign][web][testing]` For Brain/Viz category marks, color can decorate or reinforce meaning but cannot be the sole key. Every category needs a non-color identity path. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-brain-viz-growth.md#E3
- `[viz][accessibility][animation][visualdesign][testing]` In Brain/Viz, palette accessibility is temporal when colors animate. Validate the in-between states, not only the designed swatches. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-brain-viz-growth.md#E4
- `[viz][git][growth-visualization][tooling][testing]` History is a narrative source, not an accounting source. For Brain/Viz growth charts, schedule from a history stream only after it is reconciled to the current canonical total. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-brain-viz-growth.md#E5
- `[viz][git][tooling][testing]` For Brain Viz growth timelines, event streams are animation input, not truth. Clamp them to a separately parsed current total. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-brain-viz-growth.md#E6
- `[viz][animation][sync][growth-visualization][testing]` If a Brain/Viz timeline is warped for readability, the warp is part of the data contract. Labels, marks, and motion all consume the same pins. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-brain-viz-growth.md#E7
- `[viz][animation][state][testing]` Never gate Brain Viz playback completion on exact accumulated time. Complete within a frame tolerance and snap to the semantic endpoint. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-brain-viz-growth.md#E8
- `[viz][animation][state][timeline][testing]` If a visualization has both authored narrative beats and data-sized motion, anchor the narrative first and warp the motion between anchors. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-brain-viz-growth.md#E9
- `[quest][state][security][gamedev][testing]` If a reducer command can benefit from omitting or changing a policy value, that value belongs in state before the command exists. For exclusive choices, the accept command should identify the offer; state should identify the batch. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-quest-offer-batch.md#E1
- `[audio][dsp][realtime][harness][state][testing]` For bounded DSP operators, meter silence is an output property, not a lifecycle property. Terminate realtime voices from internal state thresholds, and flush subnormal decay state explicitly. → projects/local__sandbox-genesis-fm.md#E3
- `[viz][growth-visualization][graph][camera][testing]` A growth visualization camera should follow live extents, not narrative progress. Compute center and scale from finite visible content every frame. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-garden-viz.md#E1
- `[viz][graph][process][testing][knowledge-management]` In a knowledge graph visualization, edge count is not confidence. Show independence and untested seams beside it or the biggest cluster will look more proven than it is. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-garden-viz.md#E2
- `[viz][tooling][parsing][schedule][testing]` Before debugging garden group pacing, prove the group parser is complete. A schedule over missing groups can look orderly while silently hiding content. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-garden-viz.md#E3
- `[viz][graph][layout][growth-visualization][testing]` Stable layout is not ordered layout. If a visual schedule has semantic group order, encode that order as a layout constraint. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-garden-viz.md#E4
- `[viz][camera][growth-visualization][mobile][testing]` In a procedural growth visualization, progress can suggest a camera, but live geometry must veto it. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-garden-viz.md#E5
- `[viz][graph][layout][readability][testing]` Do not ask the growth algorithm to also be the label layout algorithm. Semantic placement first; readability repair second. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-garden-viz.md#E6
- `[viz][growth-visualization][garden][layout][testing]` If the visual claim is "knowledge grows," make growth order spatially meaningful. Use golden-angle phyllotaxis for the birth-order field, but reserve collision freedom for a separate readability pass. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-garden-viz.md#E7
- `[viz][animation][growth-visualization][pacing][testing]` When a visualization animates bursts, duration should communicate magnitude without charging one second per item. Start with square-root scaling and test the small, medium, and large cases. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-garden-viz.md#E8
- `[viz][growth-visualization][determinism][animation][testing]` For growth visualizations, use integer time for "how many events should exist by now" and size-aware pacing for "how should the batch feel." A beautiful garden layout still needs a deterministic reveal clock. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-garden-viz.md#E9
- `[input][ui][gamedev][mobile][testing]` For a new player action, "works on keyboard" is not a shipped interaction. Audit keyboard, touch, and gamepad separately, especially when the action is meant to replace a dangerous shared control. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-hold-dismiss.md#E1
- `[audio][dsp][determinism][harness][state][testing]` For bounded audio generators, a peak meter answers "did output stay in range," not "did the generator resume the same signal." Save live generator state and test replay/resume with content fingerprints. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-lfsr-peak-meter.md#E1
- `[mobile][ui][browser][testing][gamedev]` For mobile game controls, "the right cluster is not hidden" is not enough. Assert that the active cluster's bounding box intersects the visual viewport, especially for bottom-anchored UI and `vh`-sized canvases. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-mobile-visibility.md#E1
- `[harness][gamedev][browser][testing][process]` Polling solves "when"; rereading solves "where." A live browser game harness usually needs both once autonomous movement exists. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-polling-click.md#E1
- `[save][state][gamedev][process][testing]` Save/import and restart are different lifecycle surfaces. Audit both whenever a function gains a new caller: imports must not replay once-only side effects, and restarts must restore the exact run-start snapshot. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-save-lifecycle.md#E1
- `[ui][gamedesign][input][state][architecture][testing]` One action in the UI is not the same as one effect in the reducer. If an entity can have multiple roles, apply the action resolver at execution time too, or treat shared side-effect targets as requiring explicit coordination. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-action-resolver.md#E1
- `[state][gamedev][testing][security]` For sparse numeric maps, never use truthiness as presence. Presence, zero magnitude, and numeric corruption are three different states, and reducers/renderers need to keep them distinct. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-sparse-map.md#E1
- `[visualization][browser][testing][tooling]` For generated visual artifacts, update snapshots only after a browser render pass, and write baselines to a project-owned writable directory — treat tool-owned folders like `.codex` as read-only unless a write succeeds. → projects/local__codex-visual-snapshots.md#E1
- `[gamedev][state][process]` When a position-differenced speed/velocity gates behavior and you can't cheaply enumerate/trust every teleport-reset call site (contrast dog#E15's per-site reset), bound the derived value on BOTH sides — exclude near-zero AND implausibly-large per-frame deltas — filtering the teleport at the point of use instead of depending on every future mutation site remembering to reset tracking state. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E36
- `[browser][input][process]` A controller's Start/Guide/Home button often triggers a SYSTEM-level overlay that steals window focus before your own input polling sees the press — rAF throttles on an unfocused tab and looks exactly like "paused" with zero errors, so hook `blur` + `visibilitychange` to your own pause state as a safety net, or players get stuck on a frozen screen with no way back in; this bug reports as "it freezes," not "pause is broken," and won't surface from testing pause triggers in isolation. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E37
- `[gamedev][state][process]` An agent meant to CONGREGATE must dwell on arrival (mill within the gathering radius until a timer expires), not treat reaching the point as task-complete — an arrival-exit makes shared gather spots self-defeating (everyone pings and leaves, nothing accumulates); measure avg concurrent members, not "did a cluster ever form." → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E38
- `[ui][gamedesign][input]` A single "resolve the one relevant action/button" pattern (dog#E20) is only correct while there's truly ONE selectable target — the moment a sibling item is added to that same menu/surface, replace the hardcoded resolver with real selection state (focus index + navigation + highlight), not a smarter guess; audit every existing single-target resolver when a sibling item is added to what it resolves over. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E39
- `[harness][gamedev][process]` A headless driver must mirror the real loop's full sibling-update call-set and order (a subsystem owned by an update fn your test never calls silently never ticks → false "nothing happened") AND advance past one interval of any throttled cadence before asserting — suspect harness/loop divergence before the feature when a result is "zero." → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E40
- `[gamedev][state][gamedesign]` To make emergent clusters breathe (form→peak→disperse→reform) with no central manager, drive them by DECAY not balance: saturating gain + member-scaled decay (caps size) + age-scaled decay (guarantees death) + post-death cooldown (forces migration) — a balanced inflow/outflow reaches a fixed point and the crowd freezes forever. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E41
- `[gamedev][input][state]` To detect a GESTURE (circle, swipe, consistent turn) from accumulated per-frame deltas, sum the SIGNED delta and threshold on the total's magnitude — never abs() each step before summing, which measures total activity/noise and can't tell a deliberate consistent motion from directionless jitter of the same total magnitude (rapid back-and-forth zigzag passed the same threshold as a real circle until fixed). → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E42
- `[algorithm][ui][stats]` An aggregated preference/profile vector built as a raw weighted SUM grows unbounded and pins any fixed-range (clamped) display to the edge after one strong input — reading as "it never updates" though the number is still climbing off-screen; aggregate as a normalized weighted AVERAGE (÷ total |weight|) so it stays in the items' own trait space, moves with every input, and gives distance-match a fixed honest denominator. Corollary: "display doesn't update" on a clamped viz → suspect out-of-range before a missing re-render → projects/pappydapimp69__collective.md#E6
- `[browser][animation][ui][testing]` `position:fixed` is relative to the nearest ancestor with a non-`none` `transform` — including one that's only transiently transformed via a CSS `animation` (e.g. a `.3s` fade-in) — so a fixed element nested inside an animating container gets wildly wrong coordinates for the animation's duration; keep fixed elements as siblings of anything that animates `transform`, and measure `getBoundingClientRect()` in tests both mid- and post-animation → projects/pappydapimp69__collective.md#E7
- `[gamedesign][balance]` A damage source several multiples larger than the "normal" hit against a discretely-regenerating shared HP pool will eventually read as an instant kill even at modest absolute values — gate the first lethal crossing per encounter (not per run, not unlimited) to a visible 1-hp save instead of removing/capping the damage itself. → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-prologue.md#E23
- `[browser][process][ui]` A class in markup guarantees nothing if the stylesheet defining it isn't actually `<link>`ed by the page — grep the class name across ALL CSS files and check each hit against the HTML's real `<link>` tags before debugging further; separately, verify positioned/overlay elements with getBoundingClientRect() or a screenshot, not just classList.contains("hidden") — a DOM-state check proves toggling works, never that the element actually renders where expected. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E43
- `[browser][mobile][ui][testing]` A normal-flow primary CTA can sit entirely below the fold on a realistic constrained-HEIGHT mobile/in-app viewport even when the page scrolls fine — width-only viewport sweeps (e.g. 412px wide × 844px+ tall) never catch this; test height as deliberately as width, and prefer `position:fixed` for any action that must be reachable on first paint → projects/pappydapimp69__collective.md#E8
- `[javascript][state][gamedev]` In a JS object literal that both shorthands/spreads a source object AND sets a key derived from it under the SAME name, the later (derived) key silently wins and the reference vanishes — it passes every test that reads only the scalar and throws only where the lost reference is dereferenced (here `unit.def` became the defense number, so `e.def.ai.length` crashed the first enemy turn and soft-locked the battle); name the reference and the derived value apart (`src` vs `def`), and grep for any key appearing twice in one literal. → projects/pappydapimp69__draft.md#E1
- `[browser][state][ui]` localStorage in sandboxed/embedded contexts (artifacts, iframes, in-app webviews) can THROW on the access itself, not just the parse — wrap every get/set in try/catch, load before first paint, and validate every restored field (known keys, clamped ranges, length-capped arrays) so unavailable-or-corrupt storage degrades to a clean first-run instead of a white screen → projects/pappydapimp69__collective.md#E9
- `[ui][ux][design]` A computed user-model (preference vector / taste profile) shown as bars-and-axes reads "cold and clinical" — narrate its dominant dimensions back in SECOND PERSON ("you like to dive in first…"), generated from the same values the system acts on and gated on a magnitude threshold so a thin profile admits it, with the raw chart kept as optional detail underneath; warmth is a framing fix, not a data-completeness one → projects/pappydapimp69__collective.md#E10
- `[process][testing][tooling]` A local pre-push hash/diff gate verifies the staged file, not the separate tool-call generation that performs the push — at large payload sizes (~300KB+) those two can silently diverge with no error, producing a fabricated push despite a "passing" gate; the real fix is a tool that reads bytes from disk (real git) instead of requiring content to be re-emitted as a call argument → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E44
- `[data][web][process]` For factual data ABOUT a live product, reconstruct from the product's OWN data surface (embedded bundle JSON / internal API / page data-island) — third-party build-guide/wiki summaries of a live system rot silently and misattribute one entity's data to another; if only summaries are reachable, flag it low-confidence and resync from source ASAP, and asset-hash-check that you pulled the current build → projects/pappydapimp69__collective.md#E11
- `[ui][process]` A new visual variant (secondary button, muted card, etc.) must have EVERY inherited property re-checked for legibility against the SPECIFIC container it renders in, not just the properties it explicitly overrides — a base color choice tuned for the component's common background is not guaranteed correct for a new container with different characteristics; verify with a real screenshot, a code read alone won't catch it. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E45
- `[gamedev][state][process]` Before using a "distance-like" helper whose name could mean real OR squared distance (dist2/distSq/etc.), grep existing call sites of THAT function in the file you're in and copy their comparison shape — don't assume its convention from a similarly-named helper in a different file of the same project; one squared a radius against a real-distance dist2() and turned an intended 8-unit crowd radius into 64. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E46
- `[browser][css][flexbox]` A flex-item label with no `white-space: nowrap` wraps onto multiple lines on a narrow viewport without its container growing to match — the overflow lines render on top of the next sibling instead of pushing it down. Give any single-line flex-row label explicit `nowrap` + `overflow: hidden` + `text-overflow: ellipsis` + `min-width: 0`. → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-pitch.md#E1
- `[process][tooling][ci][state]` In a promote-else-hold batch pipeline, a "quarantine this item" flag (a STEWARD HOLD block) must be honored by EVERY downstream gate — here the post-promotion schema validator didn't skip held files, so one permanently-held malformed proposal kept the whole steward run non-zero, skipped the commit step, and discarded every other proposal's promotion; the jam is stable (the held item is never removed or fixed) and re-fails identically every run (223 red runs).
