Test the deadlock input directly: one held + several passing must still commit the passing ones. → projects/pappydapimp69__memory.md#E5
- `[browser][audio][input][gamedev]` A "Start Game" click unlocks the Web Audio autoplay gate (any user activation) but NOT the Gamepad API's gate (a gesture on the gamepad specifically, per W3C spec) — sandbox- and source-confirmed independent gates, don't collapse them into one readiness flag, composed from dog#E1 + test#E3 → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E47
- `[testing][harness][determinism][gamedev]` Headless Chrome's SwiftShader path produces two OPPOSITE-shaped, sandbox-confirmed-orthogonal test hazards — a false FAIL from real-time-based timing assertions (dog#E2) and a false PASS from shallow "didn't throw" 3D-load checks (test#E7) — plus a real, easily-confused flag-naming gotcha (`--enable-` vs `--allow-unsafe-swiftshader`), composed from dog#E2 + test#E7 → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E48
- `[gamedev][physics][state][testing]` When a behaviour-FSM "approach" state's exit requires the agent to be settled/at-rest at its target (not just to have touched it — an NPC must stop to begin milling), a missing arrival/slowing radius (dog#E7) LIVELOCKS the state (dog#E11): a full-speed seek crosses the target but is never near-AND-slow, so the settle guard never trips (sandbox: naive seek stays in "approach" all 4000 ticks at minDist 0.033). Add the arrival radius to fix the cause AND a max-dwell exit as the safety net, and treat a dwell-triggered exit as a flag the agent never settled, not as normal completion; assert BOTH exited AND settled, since a liveness-only check passes on the dwell-masked case. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E49
- `[gamedesign][state][gamedev]` When an ambient diffusion system (gossip/reputation/morale spread) writes to a score that ALSO gates goals, band its contribution to a range strictly INSIDE the gating thresholds and freeze any value the player set firsthand (outside the band) as the diffusion's authoritative source — else the one knob is over- and under-powered at once: strong enough to feel alive means strong enough to auto-complete or silently undo goal-gating state, weak enough to be safe means too weak to matter. Verified in-game: banded gossip primes a stranger to the 0.55 cap but never crosses the 0.7 friend line, and never moves a firsthand-earned bond. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E50
- `[testing][harness][state][gamedev]` A NaN fed into a `<`/`>` threshold gate never throws — it collapses the gate to a constant set by the OPERATOR, not correctness: an exclude-if-far gate (`if d>R continue`) then includes everything, an include-if-near gate (`d<R`) includes nothing, so a proximity/threshold test on a NaN input reads as a false PASS or false FAIL purely by comparison direction. A wrong test-only accessor (`obj.x` when the value is at `obj.pos.x`) is a classic silent NaN source (`undefined+n`). Assert the harness's OWN computed metric is finite and in-range for both the near and far case before trusting the feature's output. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E51
- `[input][gamedesign][gamedev]` When a QTE/minigame remaps buttons that already carry world actions, don't rely on each world action's own incidental guard to stay quiet during the window — a partially-guarded set leaves exactly the UNguarded action double-firing (here two of A/B/X were neutralised by an interact self-guard and a movement freeze, so only B leaked: inputting "spin" also barked), and it reads as "mostly works." Suppress ALL overloaded actions at one seam gated on the QTE-active flag, and audit per input surface — the collision exists only where the QTE reuses a physical control that also has a world binding (the gamepad, not the keyboard/touch on separate controls). → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E52
- `[browser][tooling][process]` On a build-step-free static multi-page site, repurposing a SHARED file's load contract (classic `<script>` ↔ ES `type="module"`, or any type change) silently breaks every OTHER entry page still loading it the old way — no error until someone opens that page, and the working main page is no evidence the others load (here runner.html `<script src>`'d game.js after it became an ES module, so the bonus game couldn't run yet stayed advertised). When a shared file's module type changes, grep every script/link/import referencing it and fix-or-delete each consumer in the same commit; treat each .html as its own untested load path. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E53
- `[testing][harness][determinism][gamedev]` To verify a change moves the TARGET of a continuously-eased value (lerp/`1-pow` follow — HUD meters, camera, morale drift), don't assert on the converged value or elapsed time: under a headless/variable clock the ease travels an unknown distance per frame (dog#E22), so both conflate "target changed" with "clock advanced" (a real feature read as "no effect": 0.60→0.585 over 150 frames). Seed the value BETWEEN the two candidate targets and assert the SIGN of its movement — direction is sign(target−value), dt-invariant and decisive in a few frames (seeded at 0.30: rose to 0.39 without the feature, fell to 0.30 with it). → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E54
- `[gamedev][state][determinism][testing]` dog#E36's bounded-velocity gate is fed an input (a locally position-differenced velocity) that is itself observer-cadence-dependent even though the underlying authoritative position stream is schedule-independent — sandbox-verified: two observers differencing the SAME closed-form trajectory at different local dt disagree on the gate in 77.5% of 120 swept configs (5.8% of 3600 checkpoint comparisons), fully closed (0/900) by tracking velocity as authoritative state instead of deriving it locally → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E55
- `[ui][browser]` A control's dynamically-overridden label can be individually accurate yet still collide with a NEIGHBORING control's own static label once a particular state is reached (Sprout's Play button auto-relabeled to the exact text its sibling Replay button always shows) — check a state-based label override against every other visible label in the same cluster, not just whether the override text alone is correct → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E8
- `[gamedev][camera][state]` A scripted camera override (a cutscene punch-in) that computes its target as `safeCurrentValue * effectStrength` bypasses an existing safe-frame clamp entirely, even though the clamp still runs on the normal flow — taper the override's strength against how crowded the scene currently is (not the schedule), and test new time-based effects across the whole timeline's range of scene states, not each occurrence in isolation, composed from brain/E4 → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E9
- `[gamedesign][state]` Deriving crit cut-scene presentation (physical lunge vs ki beam) from the existing `move.beam` combat-balance flag instead of a new field avoids any drift between balance data and presentation for every move authored so far — but it silently couples the two, so budget for a fallback-defaulted override field (not a field rename) the day a move needs them to disagree. → projects/pappydapimp69__draft.md#E2
- `[gamedesign][architecture]` Choosing fully-procedural (canvas-drawn sprites + synthesized Web Audio) over authored assets for a single-file, CSP-safe, zero-build-step game buys portability but re-opens quality problems (e.g. emoji-glyph terrain contrast) that authored art wouldn't have — fix by adding a per-category bespoke-procedural escape hatch (a lookup table), never by relaxing the no-external-assets constraint. → projects/pappydapimp69__draft.md#E3
- `[gamedesign][state]` Gate story-driven party unlocks and boss availability off the SAME counter (storyIdx) so roster and fiction can never disagree; accept the real cost that a fixed-size rotating slot silently retires earlier recruits with no in-fiction explanation, rather than introducing a second unlock flag. → projects/pappydapimp69__draft.md#E4
- `[tooling][git][state]` Clamping a git-history-derived event stream to its true final total (brain/E2, sandbox-combined-brain-viz-growth/E5,E6) is necessary but not sufficient once the timeline tracks multiple independent categories — a naive "trim from whatever day is latest" correction silently zeroed a real, unrelated day's contribution to absorb a different category's much-earlier dip; scope the retroactive shrink to that SAME category's own prior contributions, verified via a per-category (not just aggregate) executable assertion → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E10
- `[tooling][process][observability]` brain analyze's co-verification graph misses real edges when an entry cites `slug.md#E<n>` instead of the two patterns its prose-mining fallback recognizes → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E11
- `[architecture][process]` a knowledge node needs a downstream reader before it earns a standalone home — a populated single-log beat three unproven, better-argued alternatives → projects/pappydapimp69__creativity.md#E1
- `[process][tooling]` Brain's project-local incoming folder (`<project>/.brain/memory/incoming/`) and its machine-wide shared-cache incoming folder (`~/.brain/memory/incoming/`) have identical relative paths and both accept and schema-validate writes — a proposal written to the cache side instead of the project side makes `brain sync` correctly report "0 pushed, 0 held," indistinguishable from genuinely having nothing to sync. Confirm the ABSOLUTE write target, and treat any "nothing to sync" result as ambiguous until checked against `git status` in the real source directory. → projects/pappydapimp69__draft.md#E5
- `[process][gamedesign]` A build spec's hard data-integrity rule ("never fabricate") and its own worked-example scale assumption ("expect 20-35 days") can disagree once real data is reconstructed (here: only 4 real days existed) — run the real reconstruction FIRST, before any creative/structural decision locks in, treat the hard rule as load-bearing and the scale assumption as illustrative, re-derive its guidance as a ratio against the REAL count instead of forcing the absolute numbers or padding with invented content, and report the mismatch rather than silently absorbing it. → projects/pappydapimp69__draft.md#E6
- `[gamedesign][state]` When a canvas/animation build must support scrubbing to arbitrary timestamps (a transport bar, or a test harness that seeks straight to a moment), make ONLY the specific state that needs bit-identical output under both scrub and continuous-playback (e.g. ambient rotation) a pure function of elapsed time — leave everything that merely needs to look reasonable by the target time (camera ease, fades) as ordinary per-frame accumulators recomputed fresh on seek; conflating the two costs real implementation complexity for no benefit on the values that didn't need it. → projects/pappydapimp69__draft.md#E7
- `[gamedesign][algorithm]` A two-arm radial/spiral layout with asymmetric population counts (20 nodes vs 8) let the larger arm's spiral wrap past 180 degrees and visually collide with the smaller arm — invisible until the FINAL frame (every node revealed) was actually rendered and checked. Fix: give the smaller arm a radius FLOOR set from the larger arm's real outer reach, so arms are separated by radius band (collision-proof by construction), not by angle alone (which breaks once one category's own spacing needs more than its allotted angular share). → projects/pappydapimp69__draft.md#E8
- `[ui][gamedesign][input]` A dog#E20-shaped single-action-resolver's "new verb = new branch, no new UI wiring" claim only covers the resolver itself — sandbox-confirmed that a the-game-prologue#E6-shaped fixed-vocabulary input-capture layer beneath it silently drops the new zone's raw click before the resolver ever sees a press, leaving a fully-correct new branch permanently inert with zero error; invisible to an isolated resolver-only test, visible only end-to-end. A third failure axis distinct from dog#E39's "which of N simultaneous targets" (this fails even at N=1) — confirm the capture layer is open-set whenever a single-resolver pattern gains a new verb. → projects/pappydapimp69__draft.md#E9
- `[process][tooling]` A project's own local clone of a Brain node repo (e.g. `/home/user/memory`) can silently sit dozens of commits behind the machine-wide cache (`~/.brain/memory`) that `brain update`/`brain sync` actually keep current — reading the local clone for "or read their project files" (a fallback `brain train`/`brain mine` explicitly allow) can miss real, needed entries with zero signal that anything is stale. Default to the `~/.brain/<repo>` cache path for any direct file read; if using a project's own clone, diff its `git log --oneline` against the cache first. → projects/pappydapimp69__draft.md#E10
- `[gamedev][webgl][performance][testing]` Mesh count is NOT draw-call count in three.js — frustum culling means only in-view meshes draw (measured: 1,350 meshes → ~240–340 `renderer.info.render.calls`/frame, not ~2,100). Expose `renderer` and MEASURE before optimizing. Three distinct costs: draw-CALL count falls only from merging/instancing IN-VIEW geometry; shadow-map FILL falls from fewer/smaller casters (a prune cut casters 776→507 but calls by only ~6 — still worth it for GPU fill); state/memory falls from sharing materials — but never dedupe a material mutated per-instance at runtime (flicker/fades) or you couple independent animations. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E56
- `[security][data-integrity][rls][postgres]` In a client-direct PostgREST/Supabase app, a table with `insert ... with check (auth.uid() is not null)` accepts any body an authed attacker POSTs directly — client-side field sanitization is decorative; whitelist fields/values in a BEFORE-INSERT trigger or sole-writer RPC (revoke direct insert), especially for shared aggregate tables → projects/pappydapimp69__garden.md#E1
- `[security][llm][proxy][api]` A key-hiding LLM proxy that forwards the client's raw request body lets an authed caller spend your key on any model/params or repurpose the endpoint — pin model + clamp tokens + allow-list fields server-side; metering call COUNT doesn't bound per-call COST or SCOPE → projects/pappydapimp69__garden.md#E2
- `[security][architecture][serverless][concurrency]` A per-user quota enforced as SELECT-count→compare→INSERT (worse if the insert is fire-and-forget) is bypassable by concurrent requests — enforce it atomically at the counter (DB advisory-lock/count-guarded insert or Redis INCR), await it, and refuse rather than forward; RLS blocking client deletes stops resets, not races → projects/pappydapimp69__garden.md#E3
- `[testing][verification][javascript][browser]` To verify a REAL ES module in a headless browser you must serve over http:// — file:// has an opaque ('null') origin and Chromium blocks module imports from it ("CORS … origin null"), though classic scripts still load, so it only breaks on `type="module"`; use a static server (+ NO_PROXY localhost in a proxied sandbox), and never inline-copy the module to dodge it (that tests a copy) → projects/pappydapimp69__garden.md#E4
- `[algorithm][pathfinding][testing][process]` A pass that SIMPLIFIES a correct output (path smoothing/string-pull) must be validated against an INDEPENDENT continuous ground truth at the planner's real padded tolerance — not the raw footprint the smoother reused (dog-pathfinding/E1) nor the path's own vertices (test/E13); either shares the smoother's blind spot, so a green collision check certifies a clipping path → projects/local__sandbox-path-smoothing-validation.md#E1
- `[testing][verification][supabase][postgres]` Verify Supabase RLS/trigger/DEFINER-RPC security locally without Docker/cloud: initdb a scratch PG, stub the Supabase surface (auth schema + auth.uid() via a current_setting GUC + authenticated/anon roles), apply the real migration, and assert the ADVERSARIAL cases with SET LOCAL role-play — don't ship security-critical DB objects reviewed-but-unrun → projects/pappydapimp69__garden.md#E5
- `[algorithm][gamedesign]` dog#E19's scatter-rejection fix (reject a candidate on distance < sum of BOTH real radii, checked against every already-placed point) sandbox-confirmed as a general alternative to draft#E8's spiral-arm-collision fix (radius-band segregation) — applied globally across both arms in biggest-first order, it eliminates the same real collision using the ORIGINAL unshrunk footprint sizing and preserves 392px of genuine radius-range overlap between categories, where the segregation fix produces 0px by construction; prefer reject-and-nudge over structural segregation whenever categories must stay visually interleaved. → projects/pappydapimp69__draft.md#E11
- `[testing][harness][determinism]` dog#E22's polling-based headless-test methodology ("poll for the real state transition, don't compute a fixed wait") correctly eliminates timing-ratio flakiness but sandbox-confirmed gives a FALSE ALL-CLEAR for the-game-prologue#E20's exact bug class (a tick scheduler silently losing sync with real elapsed time) — the buggy scheduler still eventually reaches the target tick, just measurably late (16.7% drift observed), and a poll with no elapsed-time comparison cannot see that. Add an explicit real-elapsed-time-vs-ideal-tick-count assertion alongside polling, never as a replacement for it, whenever a real-time scheduler drives authoritative game state. → projects/pappydapimp69__draft.md#E12
- `[physics][testing][determinism]` A decaying velocity pins at a nonzero float32 subnormal fixed point (never exact 0) across three independent decay/boundary/sign parameterizations, and the stuck value always overflows a naive swept-collision `t=(boundary-x)/v` to `Infinity` — confirms E1's finding generalizes beyond its original parameters; an explicit finite/range guard on `t` holds regardless of how large the epsilon-vs-stuck gap happens to be → projects/local__sandbox-brain-loop1-denormal-swept.md#E2
- `[process][tooling]` ground "has state X already occurred" checks in an artifact only reaching X can produce (a structural marker) — never a bare keyword/substring match, which meaning-bearing prose about X can innocently reproduce → projects/pappydapimp69__memory.md#E6
- `[procgen][algorithm]` independent per-source ring/contour families crossing over shared 2D space can be wrong to assume "worse the closer sources are" — verify the crossing-count curve empirically (it can peak mid-range); a single shared distance field never crosses, by construction → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E12
- `[gamedesign][narrative]` Tension T19's externally-cited reconciliation (fixed authored spine + salience-steered presentation over a never-pruned variant pool achieves both coherence and agency) sandbox-confirmed first-hand: committed playstyles land real tag-satisfied callbacks 75% of the time, genuinely mixed playstyles correctly land fewer (50-62%, never collapsing to a disguised binary bucket — verified via deliberately partial-match playthroughs, not just the two extremes), and contrasting playthroughs diverge on 5-6 of 8 beats with zero branches ever pruned. Aggregate steering rate observed 65.6%, same ballpark as the cited 70-74% but likely a content-density property, not a mechanism constant. → projects/pappydapimp69__draft.md#E13
- `[architecture][data-integrity][privacy][security]` Privacy-preserving crowdsourced aggregation: publish confidence from per-observation QUALITY not agreement COUNT (real study: ~25% of consensus "research-grade" obs were wrong); coarse-location + no user_id + no media beats coordinate-obscuration for geoprivacy; but full anonymity structurally defeats sybil/flood detection — reintroduce minimum linkage as a one-way hashed per-user-per-cell-per-day dedup key → projects/pappydapimp69__garden.md#E6
- `[motion][gamedesign][architecture]` A hard "avoid this region" constraint computed as a MIN across N independently-rotating/varying entities and applied to a value they all SHARE is bounded by whichever single entity is worst-positioned at that instant — measure the constraint's actual worst case across the entities' full range of variation before shipping, and prefer scoping the fix to the offending entities over tightening the shared parameter everything else depends on → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-pitch.md#E2
- `[viz][state][data]` Merging two independently-built `{id,...}` arrays into one shared id-keyed object: a generic fallback bucket name (`"other"`) chosen independently by both sources collides silently and double-counts into a plausible-looking wrong total, no error, no visual glitch — verify every fallback token is genuinely unreachable or explicitly namespaced per source before merging → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-pitch.md#E3
- `[audio][webaudio][gamedev]` No crossfade curve is universally safe — match it to signal correlation. Uncorrelated sources (different music/ambience layers): use EQUAL-POWER (cos/sin gains, g1²+g2²=1) — a linear fade dips −3 dB at the midpoint (powers add: √0.5). Correlated sources (same signal in phase): use LINEAR — equal-power swells +3 dB (amplitudes add: cos+sin=√2). Sandbox reproduced both (−3.01/+3.01 dB). Default equal-power for music layers, but never on a same-signal fade. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E57
- `[ui][gamedesign][input]` A single-action-resolver (dog#E20: exactly one chosen action per target) composed with an entity-pinned onboarding trigger (the-game-prologue#E15) can silently starve the tutorial step — sandbox-confirmed across 3 scenarios: if a higher-priority verb is simultaneously valid on the same entity, the resolver never surfaces the tutorial's target verb, so the step pinned to that (entity, verb) never fires, with no error from either system; the tutorial needs visibility into "available but outranked," not just "executed" → projects/local__sandbox-resolver-starves-tutorial.md#E1
- `[process][tooling][testing]` A keyword-proximity pre-commit gate guarding an open tension flags work that meaningfully engages with the tension's subject matter, including work squarely CONSISTENT with the tension's own recorded resolution, not just adversarial commits — 3-for-3 this session on benign, on-topic composed edges; check the tension's current `Lean:` line before acknowledging, treat a hold as "check consistency," not "something is wrong" → projects/pappydapimp69__memory.md#E7
- `[process][git][tooling]` A `git stash` taken before rebasing onto a fast-moving shared repo can conflict on `stash pop` even when the rebase itself was clean — if the rebase target legitimately promoted/deleted files the stash still references, the pop conflict is about someone ELSE's unrelated work, not the current commit; `git reset --hard HEAD` and leave the stash saved (never drop it) rather than attempt to resolve content you have no context for → projects/pappydapimp69__memory.md#E8
- `[ui][gamedesign][input]` draft#E9's closed-vocabulary capture-layer bug (a new UI zone's click silently dropped before the resolver sees it) also silently starves any onboarding step built on the same dispatched-event stream (the-game-prologue#E15) — sandbox-confirmed one root cause, two independent-looking symptoms ("the new zone is dead" and "the tutorial never completes") that would plausibly be investigated separately; audit every downstream consumer of a capture layer's vocabulary, not just the one symptom found first → projects/local__sandbox-capture-layer-starves-tutorial.md#E1
- `[dsp][realtime][testing]` A decaying voice terminated by an exact-zero check (never true, per the subnormal-fixed-point finding) becomes a permanently-alive phantom entry in a bounded per-callback drain pool — sandbox-confirmed it silently taxes the shared budget by one unit every callback forever, and compounds one-for-one with a genuine burst rather than being independent of it; audit bounded-budget systems for stuck entries, not just bursty producers → projects/local__sandbox-phantom-voice-eats-budget.md#E1
- `[gamedev][determinism][architecture]` prologue#E20's accumulator fix for tick loss (drain-and-subtract, 400/400 vs 0/400 jittery) is incomplete without a clamp — externally-verified (Gaffer On Games) the accumulator alone risks the "Spiral of Death" under a real stall; cap catch-up steps per frame, same technique lockstep#E6 independently used for missed collisions → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-prologue.md#E24
- `[input][gamedev][ui][testing]` dog#E27's device-surface input-coverage checklist gives a false all-clear for draft#E9's closed-vocabulary capture-layer bug — sandbox-confirmed the checklist passes 3/3 devices while the action reaches the resolver on 0/3, because device coverage and vocabulary coverage are orthogonal audits, composed from dog#E27 + draft#E9 → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E58
- `[gamedesign][difficulty][balance]` prologue#E23's per-encounter lethal-crossing save (gate, don't remove the damage) matches the named "Last Chance Hit Point" pattern — but every real shipped example (Darktide, No More Heroes 2) also caps it ACROSS encounters via a cooldown or use-count, a second gate prologue#E23 doesn't record either way → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-prologue.md#E25
- `[gamedesign][ux][onboarding]` prologue#E5's fix for two unrelated event-driven UI systems reading as one requirement ("audit adjacent text") only detects the Gestalt Law of Proximity after the fact — externally-verified (NN/g, Laws of UX) that the Law of Common Region (a container boundary, or deliberate whitespace) is the standard structural prevention: default to visual separation between independently-triggered UI at authoring time, not as a post-hoc audit → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-prologue.md#E26
- `[accessibility][visualdesign][gamedev][testing]` A CVD palette audit that checks only STATIC entity-color pairs is unsound if any entity ANIMATES between colors — an eased transition sweeps through intermediate colors deuteranopia-confusable with a THIRD entity even when both endpoints are safe (shown on a real palette: a midpoint at deutan-distance 0.008 vs endpoints 0.19-0.30); sample the interpolated trajectory and test it with the Vienot-Brettel-Mollon model + WCAG luminance, not a gamma-space luma heuristic → projects/local__sandbox-cvd-transition-audit.md#E1
- `[ui][gamedev][algorithm]` A SPATIAL UI marker (ideas/UI-UX/four-ui-types' "floating waypoint marker" example) projected naively — raw signed-z perspective divide, independent X/Y screen clamping — silently points to the WRONG screen side once the target passes behind the camera, and distorts the indicator's angle under two-axis clamping; sandbox-confirmed fix: divide by |z| only (no extra flip), clamp with one uniform scale factor across both axes → projects/local__sandbox-offscreen-marker-projection.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][map-design][algorithm]` sandbox-maze#E1's spanning-tree carve correctly guarantees map connectivity but is, by construction, the zero-loop/single-path worst case for backtracking — sandbox-confirmed adding back a deterministic 12.5% of discarded candidate edges preserves full connectivity while adding real loops (cycles 0→3), matching real dungeon-gen practice (Delaunay+MST+loop-restore) → projects/local__sandbox-maze.md#E2
- `[tutorial][testing][gamedev]` An entity-pinned tutorial trigger behind a multi-layer input pipeline has one silent-starvation candidate PER LAYER (capture-vocabulary and resolver-priority are two already-real ones) — sandbox-confirmed an endpoint-only "input fires" check passes while the tutorial never fires, because the failure is internal to the pipeline; audit every layer, not just the two ends, composed from local/sandbox-resolver-starves-tutorial#E1 + local/sandbox-capture-layer-starves-tutorial#E1 → projects/local__sandbox-resolver-starves-tutorial.md#E2
- `[gamedesign][map-design][algorithm]` A WFC-style "no contradiction" result (sandbox-procgen-wfc#E1) is a purely local guarantee and gives zero evidence of global connectivity (sandbox-maze#E1's territory) — sandbox-confirmed a fixed, contradiction-free tile strip can still leave its open cells disconnected; run maze#E1's flood-fill as its own explicit post-generation check, composed from local/sandbox-maze#E1 + local/sandbox-procgen-wfc#E1 → projects/local__sandbox-procgen-wfc.md#E2
- `[ui][gamedesign][input]` Re-verified from a new angle: a closed-vocabulary capture layer's "silently starves every downstream consumer" property (draft#E9 → capture-layer-starves-tutorial#E1) generalizes to a THIRD, structurally different consumer type (an aggregate achievement counter, not just a resolver or a pinned tutorial step) under a realistic mixed event stream — and a mid-session vocabulary patch does NOT retroactively recover already-dropped events (7/15 permanently lost pre-patch vs. 8/15 recovered post-patch in this run); a shipped fix protects only events going forward, so affected session state needs its own audit. → projects/local__sandbox-capture-layer-starves-tutorial.md#E2
- `[gamedev][determinism][testing][animation]` draft#E7's "pure function of elapsed time" scrub-safety guarantee silently breaks when the elapsed-time SOURCE diverges from the real per-frame dt accumulator (dog#E22's exact gap) — sandbox-confirmed identical formula, identical step count, different elapsed time (0.16 vs 0.128) produces non-bit-identical output (14.4° vs 11.52°), composed from draft#E7 + dog#E22 → projects/pappydapimp69__draft.md#E14
- `[testing][harness][determinism]` dog#E54's eased-value seed-and-check-sign verification technique injects a state no real replay could reach when applied literally inside a draft#E7-style scrub-safe (replay-from-a-fixed-start) system, silently violating the replay-determinism guarantee — sandbox-confirmed a compatible adaptation: two parallel replays from the same fixed start, compared by sign at a shared sample time, preserves the original technique's dt-invariant decisiveness without injecting anything → projects/local__sandbox-seed-vs-replay-verification.md#E1
- `[algorithm][gamedev]` A from-scratch point-in-polygon ray-cast using a symmetric inclusive-both-endpoints y-range check misclassifies a test point whose ray passes exactly through a genuine pass-through polygon vertex (sandbox-confirmed: reports "inside" when two independent ground-truth derivations both say "outside") — use the standard half-open interval form (`(yi > y) != (yj > y)`, PNPOLY convention) instead; the bug is silent on ordinary points and only manifests for vertex-aligned rays, common with grid-snapped or deliberately-placed coordinates. → projects/local__sandbox-geometry-pip.md#E1
- `[testing][animation][determinism][gamedev]` test#E13's "full seek-sweep" verification, implemented as a sweep over discrete frame indices, can pass with zero mismatches while draft#E7's continuous bit-identical guarantee still fails at a fractional-frame scrub position — sandbox-confirmed a quantization bug invisible at every tested frame boundary (12.0 vs 10.5 at t≈0.117), composed from test#E13 + draft#E7 → projects/pappydapimp69__test.md#E23
- `[testing][tooling][gamedev]` A `{...source, key: derivedValue}` construction where `key` already exists on `source` (draft#E1) is invisible to BOTH a data-driven content validation ladder (test#E1: schema + referential-integrity + smoke test — unless the smoke test happens to dereference exactly that key) AND standard `no-dupe-keys`-style lint (which cannot see inside a spread's runtime contents, confirmed against real ESLint's documented limitation) — only a dedicated spread-vs-explicit-key diff check catches it deterministically → projects/local__sandbox-validation-ladder-vs-key-collision.md#E1
- `[algorithm][geometry][gamedev]` A PNPOLY-convention point-in-polygon test (sandbox-geometry-pip#E1's exact fix) classifies boundary points asymmetrically by design — sandbox-confirmed the same algorithm returns `True` for one edge-midpoint of a symmetric square and `False` for the opposite edge-midpoint; never infer "on the boundary" from inside/outside alone, check distance-to-edge explicitly → projects/local__sandbox-geometry-pip.md#E2
- `[ui][browser][mobile][viewport][testing]` Real mobile browser chrome (status bar, address bar, host-page/app UI, bottom tab-strip/nav) eats a large, non-obvious fraction of a device's vertical space a raw resolution never reveals — test viewport HEIGHT as realistically as width, derived from an actual device screenshot's measured chrome, and assert element-vs-element overlap directly, not just off-screen-edge overflow → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E13
- `[ui][css][layout][browser]` Two `position:fixed` elements each carrying an independently hand-tuned pixel offset meant to clear the other will overlap the moment either one's content height changes — merge into one flow container (flexbox column) so overlap is structurally impossible, or measure the real dependency's height live via ResizeObserver instead of guessing a second magic number → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E14
- `[determinism][state][testing]` A compounding value built from an identical, perfectly-reproduced RNG draw sequence (same seed, same count, per test#E2) still diverges between live-sequential accumulation and a differently-grouped "logically equivalent" fold/replay reconstruction — sandbox-measured 507,904x growth in the divergence from 50 to 200,000 operations, flipping a threshold gate boolean. Distinct mechanism from dog#E55's sampling-CADENCE dependence: this is float64 ARITHMETIC-ORDER (associativity) dependence, present even at fixed cadence with byte-identical inputs (the-game-the-recursion#E20's territory) → projects/local__sandbox-rng-determinism-not-sufficient-for-replay.md#E1
- `[browser][resizeobserver][testing]` A ResizeObserver whose callback mutates the element it observes fires a real, repeated "ResizeObserver loop completed with undelivered notifications." error via `window.onerror` — but that diagnostic is invisible to `console`/`pageerror` checks, the standard "zero console errors" headless-verification pattern; listen for `window.onerror` explicitly whenever ResizeObserver is in play → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E15
- `[algorithm][gamedesign][procgen]` draft#E11's reject-and-nudge scatter (cheap and reliable at its tested ~28-node scale) carries the same scale-dependent character recursion#E6 already names for a different layout algorithm — sandbox-confirmed rejection cost rises from 0.07/node at n=28 to 5.26/node at n=200, with 53/200 outright placement failures at fixed area, composed from the-game-the-recursion#E6 + draft#E11 → projects/pappydapimp69__draft.md#E15
- `[process][brainstorming][collaboration]` The well-documented critique of brainstorming (production blocking, the dominant mechanism per a cited 50-study meta-analysis) targets INTERACTIVE GROUPS taking turns to speak — it doesn't automatically indict a single generator producing ideas without a turn-taking bottleneck (matching Brain's own `brain stance brainstorm` shape), which structurally resembles the research-favored "nominal group" condition instead → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E16
- `[process][brainstorming][collaboration]` "Quantity breeds quality" (Osborn 1953, tested by Diehl & Stroebe 1987) is only half supported — more generated output reliably means more good ideas exist in the pool (13 vs. 3 high-quality in a cited comparison), but the SELECTION/cull step doesn't get better just because the pool got bigger; generation and selection need separate rigor, not one relying on the other → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E17
- `[process][research]` A naive "how many sources assert this claim" corroboration count can rank a FALSE claim as better-confirmed than a TRUE one (sandbox: 5 sources vs 2) when most of those sources form a citation echo chain tracing back to one shared origin — count DISTINCT ROOT origins (trace each source's own derivation chain), not distinct source names; a claim repeated by many derivative sources is a visibility signal, not a reliability signal. → projects/local__sandbox-research-methodology.md#E1
- `[process][prompt-writing][agent-instructions]` Brain's `brain train` CLI restates the identical mandatory-sandbox-verification rule (tension T22) across every mode rather than stating it once — externally-verified this is a documented, deliberate prompt-engineering practice (restating critical constraints reduces forgetting across long agent workflows), not duplication to deduplicate → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E18
- `[gamedesign][algorithm]` A Metroidvania one-way drop with no ability requirement to fall can lead to a structural soft-lock if the room it lands in only escapes via an ability acquired elsewhere — sandbox-confirmed detectable by pure BFS reachability assuming ZERO abilities held (not "should have by now"), no playtesting or player-skill simulation required. Verify every one-way edge's landing room can either return ability-free or reach the specific gating ability before shipping. → projects/local__sandbox-leveldesign-softlock.md#E1
- `[browser][rendering]` Estimating UI text width as `avgCharWidth * length` instead of measuring the real string is wildly wrong for a proportional font — first-hand headless-Chromium measurement (`measureText`) found errors from -38% to +163% depending on character composition, and even ordinary words ("Illinois" +68%, "Settings" +29%) were off by double digits. Always measure the actual string with the real renderer; there is no single correction factor since the error is per-string letter-composition-dependent. → projects/local__sandbox-typography-measure.md#E1
- `[algorithm][physics]` Explicit (forward) Euler integration of any oscillatory/restoring-force system (spring, pendulum, orbit) systematically injects energy every step and diverges — sandbox-confirmed a 1D spring reached ~1.2×10^34x its starting energy over 2000 steps at dt=0.1 — while semi-implicit (symplectic) Euler, the identical formula with only the update ORDER swapped (velocity before position, using the new velocity), stayed within 1.04x. Verify any long-running oscillatory sim against its own conserved quantity (energy), not just visual plausibility over a short test. → projects/local__sandbox-physics-integration.md#E1
- `[algorithm][determinism]` A PRNG consumed at a player-triggered roll, seeded purely by frame count, is fully manipulable — sandbox-confirmed all 6 outcomes of a modeled roll are individually selectable via a specific frame-delay found by brute-force search (the real speedrunner "RNG manipulation" technique). Mixing in a per-session salt the player can't derive from timing alone broke 4/6 of the same frame-delay mappings; the fix is session-scoped entropy, not a "better" PRNG algorithm — the vulnerability is in the observable/controllable STATE fed in, not the generator. → projects/local__sandbox-speedrun-rng.md#E1
- `[browser][rendering]` German translations of real English UI labels measured first-hand averaging +31.23% wider (range -28.28% to +97.56% across 8 real word pairs), matching commonly-cited i18n guidance (~20-35%) — and overflowed a plausible fixed 90px button for at least one real label ("Settings"→"Einstellungen," 96.07px). Per-string variance is too wide for a flat "+30% padding" budget; measure the actual translated strings per locale, or use containers that genuinely reflow. → projects/local__sandbox-localization-expansion.md#E1
- `[security][algorithm]` Score/leaderboard tamper protection needs TWO independent properties, sandbox-confirmed with real HMAC-SHA256: (1) signing over the full payload — an unsigned submission is always "valid," a signed one correctly rejects a replayed old signature paired with a new score; (2) constant-time comparison of the submitted signature — a naive early-exit comparison's step count grows monotonically with matching prefix length (1→64 steps across 0→64 correct leading chars), a real measurable timing side-channel, while constant-time comparison stays flat. Neither substitutes for the other. → projects/local__sandbox-crypto-hmac.md#E1
- `[rendering][algorithm]` Alpha-blending gamma-encoded (sRGB) color values directly — the common shortcut — produces a visibly too-dark result versus the physically correct blend: sandbox-confirmed a 50% white/black blend measures true linear luminance 0.214 (should be 0.5, a 0.286 error) when done naively in sRGB space, vs. an exact 0.500 when converted to linear light, blended, then re-encoded. Any color arithmetic meant to model real light mixing (blending, mip-mapping, filtering) must operate in linear space. → projects/local__sandbox-shaders-gamma.md#E1
- `[browser][rendering][performance]` First-hand measured (headless Chromium, identical particle physics both ways): DOM-per-particle weather effects cost ~9-20x more per frame than a single canvas past ~150 simultaneous particles (mean per-frame ms; DOM 3.02ms vs canvas 0.23ms at n=1200), confirming common practice's DIRECTION with real numbers — but canvas showed a real one-off ~42ms warmup spike at small particle counts DOM did not, and headless/software rendering is a known atypical-timing environment (dog#E48), so treat exact magnitudes as directional, not portable constants. → projects/local__sandbox-weather-vfx.md#E1
- `[algorithm][determinism][gamedev]` Float64 summation order-dependence (the shared justification behind the-game-wrong-sky#E12's exact-integer FOV and the-game-the-recursion#E6's fully-deterministic all-pairs+MST) is sandbox-confirmed real, not folklore — the same value set sums to 3 different float64 results across 3 fixed evaluation orders, exact-integer summation gives 1, composed from the-game-wrong-sky#E12 + the-game-the-recursion#E6 → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-the-recursion.md#E21
- `[testing][determinism][gamedev][harness]` A Playwright-driven per-frame test must distrust BOTH frame-boundary edges at once — an instant keypress invisible to held-state sampling, and a virtual clock that may not drive the engine's own dt — composed from dog#E22 + the-game-prologue#E2 → projects/pappydapimp69__test.md#E24
- `[determinism][architecture][testing]` A shared single-stream RNG's linear draw sequence is an ordinal/positional space in save-system#E2's exact sense (array-index-like, fails silently under reshape) — E3's own shipped fix (isolate one flipped smoke test) only patches the one instance; the general fix (symbolically-keyed substreams per RNG consumer, E2's cure for ordinal references) closes the whole class, live-verified across all 3 reshape ops and routine at ~29% of ordinary configs in a 30k-config scan → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-the-answering-deep.md#E13
- `[determinism][framerate][gamedesign][architecture]` recursion#E16's movement-cadence overshoot-discard bug and wrong-sky#E9's `enemyCd[id]=now+ENEMY_CD_MS` attack gate are the SAME bug pattern verified live in both real repos, but severity scales with avg_frame_time/period, not fixed — 20% bias at recursion's 108ms period vs 1.5% at wrong-sky's real 900ms period (live-verified, low-priority), sweep maps the curve from 0% to 20% across 108-3600ms → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-wrong-sky.md#E19
- `[gamedesign][balance][pacing]` A flat floor/minimum layered on top of an existing magnitude-proportional signal (wave size, batch duration, anything meant to communicate "this is bigger") silently replaces that signal for most of the content rather than merely bounding it — measure the floor-driven fraction across the FULL input range (not the average case) before shipping one, and when a "feels flat/off" report follows adding a floor, that measurement is the first thing to check. → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-prologue.md#E27
- `[testing][tooling][browser]` Playwright's `fullPage:true` screenshot stitches multiple scrolled captures together, so any `position:fixed`/`sticky` element appears to repeat/float across the composite image even when it renders correctly — this is a stitching artifact, not a real overlap bug; verify fixed/sticky elements with a scrolled non-fullPage screenshot or `getBoundingClientRect()` instead → projects/pappydapimp69__collective.md#E12
- `[algorithm][testing][narrative]` a reversal-counting shape classifier over a noisy per-step signal (sentence sentiment) fails at ties (an undefined leading direction silently defaults to a sign) and at lexicon-morphology gaps (inflected forms score 0) — both manufacture phantom reversals; smoothing or lemmatizing before shape-classifying is earned, not decorative → projects/local__sandbox-story-arc-classifier.md#E1
- `[gamedev] [testing] [balance]` Reachability/outcome is a distinct test axis from step-correctness — simulate whole runs to a terminal state -> projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E1 → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E1
- `[gamedev] [3d] [threejs]` A blank render only at certain camera distances is usually fog `far` (or clip planes), not the scene graph — make fog per-view -> projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E2 → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E2
- `[devops] [github-actions] [github-pages]` GitHub Pages via Actions deploys only from the default branch (blocks at deploy setup, not authoring) — merge to default, and verify the served URL -> projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E3 → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E3
- `[process][tooling]` `brain query` term-matching is per-argument literal substring (hyphens stripped, spaces kept) — a quoted multi-word phrase silently never matches a hyphenated kernel title. Always pass separate unquoted keywords, or real coverage reads as a false "gap". → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E59
- `[audio][audio-engine][dsp]` genesis-psg#E1's SN76489 noise tap (0x0009) and measured period (57337) are externally confirmed exact against SMS Power's hardware documentation — which also reveals Sega's Genesis-era PSG WIDENS the LFSR from the original 15-bit design to 16 bits, a detail worth recording before reusing this tap/period against a different chip revision or platform → projects/local__sandbox-genesis-psg.md#E2
- `[testing][determinism][gamedev][harness]` test#E24's composed claim (a Playwright-driven test needs BOTH input-queueing and clock-probing, neither substitutes for the other) is sandbox-confirmed, settling its prior [assumed] provenance — a partial fix on either axis leaves the other axis's bug fully live (dt_accum wrong under queue-only, action_fired wrong under probe-only) → projects/pappydapimp69__test.md#E25
- `[gamedev][input][state]` A state driven by BOTH a per-frame polled input and a scripted override needs an explicit precedence (keyView + force + one apply(), effective = force ?? key) — let both write the same variable and the per-frame poll silently clobbers the override next tick. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E60
- `[gamedev][state][simulation]` A stationary/persistent emitter added to a travel-gated pheromone/scent field deposits NOTHING while it isn't moving — the travel gate (deposit only after N units of motion) is right for trails, wrong for a fixed aura; force the persistent emitter's deposits and test the idle case, since any test that keeps it moving hides the bug. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E61
- `[gamedev] [ui] [input] [gamepad]` A flat single-axis focus list over visually distinct control groups lets any direction leak into the wrong group — model real rows/columns/groups, keep each axis scoped to its own group -> projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E4 → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E4
- `[gamedesign][input][gamedev]` A no-fail "playable cutscene" gate that waits for a player action must make a wrong input a pure NO-OP (never a fail/advance — copying a wrong-input-loses minigame's resolver silently reintroduces the fail), accept the broadest input that still requires agency (an unprompted bow accepts ANY trick so you can't fail to bow), and guarantee discoverability with a timed hint; test the wrong-input and never-input cases, not just success. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E62
- `[gamedev][save][state]` A selectable save-slot/profile chosen AFTER the game object is constructed makes construction-time state init stale — per-profile fields set only in the constructor (NPC bonds, an achievements Set) silently keep the FIRST slot's values on a slot switch, with no error. The single play-session entry point must reset-then-reload EVERY per-profile field (default as baseline); grep the constructor and mirror each one, and test by switching profiles and asserting the second's values. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E63
- `[gamedev][save][ui]` Adding multi-slot saves to a game with an existing scattered "shown once ever" flag (a raw localStorage key outside the save payload, e.g. a tutorial/prologue-seen bit) turns it into a cross-slot leak — completing the gated content in ANY slot disables it for every future new game in every slot. Grep the WHOLE codebase for direct localStorage calls, not just the save/load module, when adding slots; and don't trust a test suite that skip-shortcuts through the same flag — it gives zero signal on this bug. A human on a real used browser found it in minutes; 130+ passing automated tests didn't. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E64
- `[gamedev] [ai] [algorithm]` A stall/no-progress detector must gate its reset on "better than the best-ever observed," not "better than the immediately prior observation" — a turn-over-turn delta is blind to slow oscillation and can leave an agent genuinely stuck forever while reporting constant progress -> projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E6 → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E5
- `[gamedev] [pathfinding] [ai]` A tile whose interaction toggles/redirects instead of relocating the mover must be excluded from EVERY "is this passable" check (pathfinder, connectivity guarantee, AI planning), not just the move resolver that already handles it correctly -> projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E5 → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E6
- `[git] [process] [workflow]` A branch that had an earlier PR squash-merged still carries its own pre-squash commits (different SHAs, identical content) — opening a second PR from it re-applies those already-merged diffs and GitHub reports a real merge conflict even though nothing actually overlaps; reset the branch onto the base and cherry-pick/rebase only the genuinely new commits before every subsequent PR, not just once. -> projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E7 → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E7
- `[testing][gamedev][process]` A headless test that `waitForFunction`s directly on a FINAL derived state (`level >= N`) deadlocks when that transition needs an intermediate player-confirm step (a card whose OWN button callback performs the increment) — the wait and the action that would satisfy it are on opposite sides of the same line, so no timeout helps. Wait on the nearest state reachable WITHOUT further test action (`phase === 'complete' || level >= N`), take the action (dismiss the card), then assert. Ask "can this become true without my script doing anything else first?" before every waitForFunction. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E65
- `[devops] [github-actions] [github-pages]` A GitHub Pages Actions deploy reporting success does not mean the live URL is serving yet — curl-verify with a cache-buster after every deploy, and treat a 404 right after a reported-success run as an expected propagation window to retry through (re-dispatch + re-check), not a config problem to debug -> projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E8 → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E8
- `[gamedesign] [ui] [security]` Fixing an information leak on one presentation surface (a HUD stat) does not mean a SECOND surface reading the same event stream (a text log, replay, summary screen) is also safe — audit every consumer of shared state against the same "who's allowed to see this" question, and test against the secret's presence in the rendered output, not just the surface where the bug was first found. -> projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E9 → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E9
- `[process][compliance]` A repo's checked-in Brain retrieval/write-back protocol (query before, mine+sync after) is not enforced by the commit-time tension gate — an agent can pass every gate check while never running query/mine/sync at all. Treat the protocol itself as a required task-loop step, not optional scaffolding that task pressure silently drops. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E66
- `[orchestration][verification]` A multi-agent blind-verification design (author/key/grade split across subagents) is only as strong as the message-passing substrate's isolation — nested subagent completions can leak to the orchestrator via the same notification channel used for top-level agents, silently breaking the "blind" boundary. If a downstream verifier refuses input because it matches the thing it's checking too exactly (the actual sign of a leaked key), treat that refusal as correct and halt — don't route around it by grading again elsewhere with the same compromised data. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E67
- `[gamedev] [architecture] [multi-agent]` Generalizing a role from one controllable entity to N means a "does the human control this" check must become instance-aware everywhere it's asked, not just at the obvious input gate — a turn/state machine with multiple handoff points needs the SAME audit applied at every one, since a stale singular assumption at any of them silently stalls the game waiting on input that will never come. -> projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E10 → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E10
- `[gamedesign][input][ux]` For a strictly linear, gamepad-navigable multi-step flow, one persistent state-driven confirm button beats one button per screen — it collapses gamepad-focus wiring to a single target, but shifts all risk onto that button's listeners staying idempotent-safe as its handler grows more stateful. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E68
- `[gamedev][camera][state]` Fixing a mis-scaled 3D character with a single `.scale.setScalar(k)` is not done at the model — every camera offset and mouth/attachment offset tuned against its old size must be multiplied by the same `k`, or the model looks right while the camera and carried objects still look subtly wrong. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E69
- `[rendering][camera][multi-entity]` a shared "whose turn" field can silently split into "who may act" vs "whose viewpoint this renders from" once an entity scales 1→N — audit both → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E11
- `[process][git][squash-merge]` checking a fact (the post-merge tip SHA) is not the same as acting on it — a "check X then do Y" habit can silently regress to "check X, forget Y" even right after Y was last done correctly; prefer folding the check into the action mechanically → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E12
- `[deploy][verification][cdn]` one clean check against a CDN-fronted deploy target is a single edge node's state, not proof of convergence — require a short streak of consecutive successes across real time before reporting "live" → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E13
- `[testing][input][regression]` a global "any input skips this" interceptor can silently eat an existing test's first post-transition action — a green suite only proves the SPECIFIC assertions in it survived, not that the swallowed action was harmless → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E14
- `[architecture][gamedesign]` A documented architectural boundary (an open tension someone already deliberately scoped) is better bent with a narrow, optional, no-op-by-default hook for the one caller that needs an exception than reopened/widened for every caller — verify the no-op path is byte-identical with an explicit test, not just claimed. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E70
- `[gamedesign][ui][ai]` a cooldown ability needs TWO gates — "recharged" and "has a valid target now" — shared by UI and AI alike, with the cooldown assigned only after every precondition passes so a refused use is free → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E15
- `[gamedesign][procgen][content]` content that depends on specific world features must be validated against the FINISHED procedural map, not the generator config — probabilistic placement makes "the map has doors" a per-seed fact, so declare each dependency and keep some content dependency-free → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E16
- `[ai][gamedesign][balance]` in a turn-based game tempo is usually the scarce resource, so an AI detour for a "free" pickup can be a net loss — A/B the radius against a whole-run sim INCLUDING the zero case to separate a bad feature from bad tuning, and record the numbers next to the constant → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E17
- `[gamedev][balance][testing][process]` A balance constant transfers across neither encounter shape nor STAT SCALE nor sample size — a filed "K=3 to K=6" range swept as-is reported EMPTY bands at every encounter shape because the real regime sat at 55-90 on our stat scale; instrument the clamp (cap-binding rate) so a wrong constant is distinguishable from an irrelevant one → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-builder.md#E1
- `[gamedev][balance][gamedesign][architecture]` A BOUNDED stat lever (acting on a fraction that saturates at 1.0, e.g. armour penetration) cannot be balanced against an UNBOUNDED one (a raw damage multiplier) by tuning coefficients — measured r=-0.70 for the bounded axis with every strongest build at its minimum; change the lever's shape, and diagnose axis balance by round robin over equal-total builds with per-axis correlation, never hand-picked matchups → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-builder.md#E2
- `[browser][rendering][canvas][ui][testing]` `position: fixed; inset: 0` does NOT stretch a canvas — a REPLACED element with `width:auto` keeps its intrinsic 300x150 and silently ignores over-constrained insets, so the game draws as if the viewport were 300x150 and renders tiny in the top-left with zero errors and a fully green suite; set width/height explicitly, and measure the element's used size before suspecting the camera → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-builder.md#E3
- `[gamedev][balance][testing][gamedesign]` A clamp is only free where it does not bind: a %-max-HP damage cap that binds in ordinary play erases every stat difference and all crit variance, collapsing fight length onto exact constants (7.0/13.0/19.0, zero spread) — keep the cap a backstop for extreme ratios, keep it UNIFORM (a pierceable cap surrenders its own guarantee), and instrument the binding RATE since a round constant in an outcome metric reads as coincidence → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-builder.md#E4
- `[gamedev][balance][gamedesign][simulation]` A single-target boss makes N-vs-1 difficulty a STEP FUNCTION in N (measured 1.00/1.00/0.00/0.00 by party size) because party damage scales linearly while boss output is fixed — give the boss AoE so its output scales with N too (became 1.00/1.00/0.79/0.16/0.01); and assert SMOOTHNESS not just monotonicity, since a step function is monotonic → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-builder.md#E5
- `[gamedesign][asymmetric][difficulty]` in an asymmetric game, a difficulty setting that tunes a SHARED rule rather than the human's opponent inverts its label for whichever role that rule favours — separate "opponent skill" from "world harshness" and route per role → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E18
- `[ai][balance][method]` "the better agent is the more careful agent" is a hypothesis, not a premise — with a shared clock, caution is priced in opponent turns, so measure any competence/difficulty knob against a whole-run score and confirm the metric moves the way the LABEL claims → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E19
- `[testing][simulation][method]` never make a sim deterministic with a CONSTANT rng — it turns every `rng() < p` into a fixed branch, silently switching off probability parameters so tiers that differ only in probability become one code path and the output reads "weak effect" rather than "error"; use a seeded PRNG and assert tiers differ in intermediate behaviour → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E20
- `[gamedev][rendering][ui][gamedesign]` A feedback channel that fires on EVERY event stops carrying information and starts destroying it: a per-hit full-screen flash refreshed faster than it decayed (170ms decay vs 115ms beats) and washed the frame to flat grey; ambient particles on the neutral stance buried the cast; one floating marker per changed field stacked four labels illegibly. Gate the loudest channels on events that earn them, clamp the peak, assert the bound over a whole sequence, and float ONE headline marker per change → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-builder.md#E6
- `[gamedev][architecture][rendering][determinism][testing]` Animate combat by resolving the fight deterministically, emitting a TRACE, and making the visuals a pure replay — display HP derived from the trace means the animation cannot disagree with the outcome, skip parity becomes two lines, and the measured balance model is the one played. Convert trace granularity to VISUAL granularity: group simultaneous same-source hits into one beat (ungrouped AoE saturated the screen flash to grey), and never apply timeline-pausing hitstop per mechanical event (+30s on a 150-beat fight) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-builder.md#E7
- `[state][save][determinism][testing]` "The restored stream is identical" does NOT imply "the serialization is canonical": a PRNG whose step ends `| 0` stores SIGNED words while the restore reads them `>>> 0`, so behaviour was byte-identical while `serialize(load(serialize(x))) !== serialize(x)` — invisible to any stream or behaviour test, fatal to save hashing, diffing and byte comparison. Normalize at the WRITE boundary and assert canonical form as its own test → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-builder.md#E8
- `[gamedev][animation][state]` a frame-rate-correct exponential smoother is still asymptotic, so a tween that declares its own start point pops by the leftover residual (~0.85 tile at a short idle) — the tween must read its origin from the object's actual transform; correct smoothing and a safe handoff are different bugs → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E21
- `[gamedev][performance][pathfinding][caching]` A path cache that treats an EMPTY result as "uncached" re-runs BFS every tick (measured 6x whole-sim slowdown, zero visible symptom) — use `null` for not-computed, and never set a rate-limit timer to 0 as "retry soon" → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E22
- `[testing][harness][gamedev][3d]` A headless screenshot timing out over a live WebGL canvas is usually SLOW, not HUNG (measured ~5s vs ~0.6s captures under SwiftShader) — prove liveness with an rAF counter + draw-call count before hunting a runaway loop, and set a real timeout instead of shrinking the test → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E23
- `[gamedev][3d][input][testing]` A camera-relative movement sign error passes every displacement-magnitude test — inverted controls still "moved" — so assert the SIGN of the delta projected on the camera's forward basis, and look at one rendered frame → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E24
- `[gamedev][cutscene][state][determinism]` Composing "use a float tolerance" (anim#E1) with "catch up after a dt gap" (prologue#E20) in a cutscene marker loop trades a skipped-marker bug for a DOUBLE-FIRED one (measured: uncapped single-fire skipped 4/5 markers; capped range-rescan with tolerance fired [1,1,2,3,4,5,5] — 7 dispatches for 5 markers; a monotonic pointer fired each exactly once) — a re-fired `cmd` marker can re-apply authoritative state, so dispatch must consume a marker INDEX, not re-evaluate a tolerance predicate every frame → projects/local__sandbox-combined-cutscene-resume.md#E1
- `[testing][harness][gamedev][playwright]` `page.waitForFunction`'s default rAF-based polling can be starved for its whole timeout by a preceding heavy synchronous `evaluate()` call — the condition was already true, only unobserved; poll from Node with `evaluate()`+`waitForTimeout()` instead, or pass `{polling: N}` → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E25
- `[gamedev][simulation][refactor][combat]` A per-fighter flag whose NAME reads as cosmetic ("is this the boss") but whose read site is actually the side-targeting mechanism will silently misroute a new fighter type's attacks onto its own side instead of the opponent — a fight still runs to a normal-looking completion, so trace-exists/outcome-resolves tests miss it; audit every fighter-construction site for a flag like this whenever you add a new actor kind, and test WHO hit WHOM, not just that a fight happened → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-builder.md#E9
- `[ui][input][browser][gamedev]` "Any key to start" must exclude keys the PLATFORM owns — modifiers, function keys, anything chorded with Ctrl/Meta/Alt — matched against both e.key and e.code, since a pre-game screen is exactly when a player is still operating the browser. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E71
- `[gamedev][3d][scaling][art-direction]` "The scenery looks wrong" is often the CHARACTER off the world's implicit unit grid, not the scenery — tune a character's scale against an in-world reference object (a car, a person), never in isolation, and check the character first when a report blames the surroundings. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E72
- `[gamedev][camera][cutscene][animation]` If a shot/beat resolves to a single stored VALUE its type vocabulary can only select between values, never express behavior — give it a start, an end and a duration, and verify motion across the beat's OWN interval, not just whether the camera is technically moving. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E73
- `[gamedev][procgen][layout][scaling]` Reusing a region's full extent as a FEATURE's width couples the feature's size to the container's, and the coupling hides until the region is resized — give the feature its own fixed dimension and derive dependents from that, not from the region. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E74
- `[animation][state][dsp][realtime]` Two independently-correct 'recover dropped events under a large dt' fixes compose into a burst amplifier — check the per-frame DISTRIBUTION a fix produces, not just its total count, and distinguish a bounded-integral transient (survivable) from a sustained over-cap rate (unbounded divergence) before trusting a drain cap. → projects/local__sandbox-anim.md#E6
- `[determinism][gamedev][state][realtime]` Integer time guarantees an identical tick COUNT across schedules, not an identical per-frame ARRIVAL distribution — audit every consumer for whether it drops/throttles/samples on frame boundaries, and test lumpy vs steady schedules of equal total time, not just equal totals. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-game-loop.md#E4
- `[gamedev][state][scripting][verification]` A scripted scene that forces ONE environmental system (weather) but not its siblings (the day/night clock) inherits the others' live state, invisibly to every state assertion — enumerate every environmental sibling a scene's prose implies and pin each from the same condition, and verify with a screenshot, not just state coverage. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E75
- `[input][ui][gamedev][architecture]` When several surfaces each claim 'any input dismisses this', the app's real exposure is the UNION of their guards, not the average — independently-written guards have non-overlapping blind spots, so fixing them one at a time yields zero app-level protection until every surface shares one predicate. → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E26
- `[input][ui][architecture][gamedev]` A safety guard IS a capture layer — placed at a shared entry point it silently starves every OTHER consumer of that event stream while the test that motivated it passes identically either way; scope the guard to the consumer that needs it, and write a starvation assertion on a DIFFERENT consumer, not just the one you fixed. → projects/local__sandbox-capture-layer-starves-tutorial.md#E3
- `[testing][harness][gamedev][process]` A verification wait-time budget calibrated against wall-clock is a function of CURRENT render cost, which only grows as a project adds geometry — treat any hardcoded wall-clock wait as suspect the moment scene content changes, and poll the game's own advancing state instead, every time a timing script is reused. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E76
- `[physics][dsp][realtime][gamedev]` Sub-stepping (the tunneling fix) makes collision-event burst a function of CONTACT DURATION, not collision count — a sustained sliding contact bursts every sub-step of every frame, and a drain cap doesn't save you, it relocates the overrun into unbounded queue latency; deduplicate at the producer on the enter-transition. → projects/local__sandbox-collision.md#E2
- `[gamedev][cutscene][ui][state]` A scrub-bar `input` handler that force-renders the sought frame only `if (!playing)` loses the race to a still-running rAF loop, which advances past the sought position on its very next tick — pause unconditionally as part of the SAME handler before seeking, not merely skip re-rendering while already paused → projects/pappydapimp69__animation.md#E1
- `[gamedev][input][browser][javascript]` A synthetic DOM event dispatched on the wrong target (`document` vs. `window`, or any two "global-seeming" nodes) with a default-`bubbles:false` event type is a SILENT no-op — `dispatchEvent` never errors when nothing catches it — so a device-input bridge built via synthetic events must be fired against a live page and checked for an observable effect before shipping; reasoning from the code alone cannot distinguish "works" from "silently does nothing" → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-builder.md#E10
- `[tooling][process][graph]` `brain`'s co-verification graph builder mined prose citations for `slug/E<n>` and `slug's ... (E<n>)` but missed the equally common `slug.md#E<n>` style (the system's own index-line convention) — a real, promoted incompatibility-precedent citation produced zero graph edge as a result; fixed with a third pattern, real-corpus impact 253→268 edges (+15), sandbox-verified byte-identical. → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E19
- `[devops][deployment][process]` a verified "just retry it" remedy needs an explicit ceiling AND a discriminator or it generalises into an outage — record at write-time how many retries is too many, and what observation separates the failure it fixes from a different failure with the same symptom → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E27
- `[gamedev][animation][state][cutscene][gamedesign]` When authored direction says "hold... do not cut early," a fixed-duration animation is the wrong primitive regardless of how long the duration is — it needs to be a persistent STATE with an explicit cancel condition, because the correct hold length is authored per-scene and can't be guessed from inside the system playing the pose → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E77
- `[gamedev][balance][gamedesign][simulation]` Scale an encounter off the PLAYER with any accumulating meter (heat/notoriety) as a bounded MULTIPLIER, never off the meter alone — meter-derived difficulty gave a heat-60 raider ~91 axis total against a day-10 player's ~24, so the aggressive stance lost every fight and ended weaker than hiding; the symptom presents as "this stance is underpowered", which invites tuning the wrong thing → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-builder.md#E11
- `[gamedev][procgen][architecture][process][worldgen]` Fixing a road-placement bug for ONE consumer of shared world geometry doesn't touch a second consumer that independently re-derived the same boundary — grep the whole codebase for other reads of the same raw geometry (mid/outer/half-extents) and promote the boundary to a shared constant, not a function-local one, the moment a second consumer is found → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E78
- `[audio][animation][state][testing][realtime][gamedev]` A pooled audio voice is the SAME per-clip FSM anim/E4 already warned about — reacquiring one and resetting only "the obvious fields" starts the new note on the old note's envelope (18/18 wrong, swept), and a mixer-level continuity check cannot see it (audio-realtime/E3's warning, now confirmed on this bug too) → projects/local__sandbox-voice-pool-reset.md#E1
- `[gamedev][nlp][process][testing][content-pipeline]` A keyword-mined staging cue that requires an exact verb tense misses the SPECIFIC shot it was written for, while still firing later on an unrelated word — so the bug reads as "the character appears late," not "the cue is broken"; enumerate the verb's full paradigm (hold/holds/held/holding) and test fire TIME against the described shot, not just presence → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E79
- `[gamedev][ai][game-design][state-lifecycle][debugging]` a "bluff" field cleared at end-of-turn was always null by the time the next turn's AI danger-check read it, making the whole branch dead code since it shipped — per-turn "public claim" fields read by a LATER turn need an explicit snapshot taken before the clear, not a live reference across the turn boundary → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E28
- `[gamedev][cutscene][camera][architecture][testing][state]` A subject resolved once at cutscene-compile time can't see a character a staging cue builds LATER in the same beat — the two-shot framing it was written for silently never engages; store the resolver as a function re-invoked per-frame (like the dog's own live position already is), never a cached one-time result → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E80
- `[gamedev][cutscene][process][testing][architecture]` Two staging cues mined from the SAME shot at the SAME timestamp fire in mining-list declaration order, not narrative order — that's a real bug whenever the physical actions genuinely have to happen in sequence; merge them into a compound cue at MINE time rather than relying on list position → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E81
- `[tooling][process][data-integrity]` `steward.py`'s `promote()` (and `parse()`'s index-line detector) end an entry's body the moment ANY line contains the substring "PROPOSED INDEX LINE" rather than checking it's the actual `## `-prefixed header — a real, already-promoted entry that merely discussed this system's own index-line convention in prose got silently truncated, losing 6 fields with no error from any check; fixed by requiring the `##` prefix, sandbox-verified byte-identical, real corpus damage found and repaired → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E20
- `[process][tooling]` `brain sync` only pushes the `memory` node's incoming/ — an `ideas` (or other non-memory node) proposal needs its own direct `git push origin main`; "0 pushed" from sync says nothing about ideas-node proposals → projects/pappydapimp69__collective.md#E13
- `[testing][playwright]` a second open Playwright page throttles rAF-driven timing on the others → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E29
- `[gamedev][input]` enum growth outran a hardcoded modulo, hiding the newest gamepad-cycled item forever → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E30
- `[gamedev][input]` a standard gamepad's face buttons can't cover 4 directions AND a universal confirm → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E31
- `[determinism][gamedesign][procgen][testing][pathfinding]` A connectivity check being GLOBAL BY CONSTRUCTION (test#E17) only clears the algorithm — its walkability/edge PREDICATE still needs its own audit against real move-resolution (opticon#E6's interact-in-place bug), sandbox-reproduced on textbook flood-fill → projects/local__sandbox-combined-opticon-test-connectivity.md#E1
- `[gamedev][state][events]` A "base fields + `...opts`" emit/event helper must spread `opts` BEFORE its own reserved fields (kind/type/id), not after — a caller's opts field that happens to share a reserved field's name silently wins and the event's own type never reaches its readers. → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E1
- `[state] [scripting]` a coarse phase/mode flag that force-holds ambient state for its whole duration needs a narrow boolean escape hatch for the ONE beat inside it that must override the forcing early -> projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E82
- `[ui] [touch]` hover-only-reveal (opacity:0 + :hover) hides a control from touch users entirely — give touch-relevant controls a nonzero base opacity instead -> projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E83
- `[testing][harness]` A bare `text.length > 0` assertion on a persistent/history UI element (subtitle feed, log, toast stack) can stay green even when the specific event under test was silently dropped, if older unrelated content is still sitting in the same element — assert the expected text, not just non-emptiness. → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E2
- `[gamedev][physics][state][process][testing]` A new collision obstacle's blocking radius can numerically coincide with an unrelated existing arrival/interaction threshold at the same point, making the trigger angle-dependent instead of reliable — size new collision radii with explicit margin against every known proximity threshold at that point, not from visual size alone. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E84
- `[gamedev][state][events]` A periodic tick function that clears a shared event queue at ITS OWN start will silently destroy anything an earlier-in-the-same-frame caller (e.g. an input handler) already wrote to that queue — pass the earlier write through as a local, one-shot capture to the reader instead of restoring it into the shared queue (which causes an infinite-repeat regression instead). → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E3
- `[gamedev][naming]` a helper named dist2 returns plain, not squared, distance — a familiar-convention name lied about its own contract → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E85
- `[gamedev][state-ownership]` a semantically-apt field can already be owned by an unrelated system that silently overpowers a new writer → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E86
- `[three.js] [geometry]` a decorative plane placed inside its parent box's own depth extent is invisible from every angle with no error — compute the box's real z-range before trusting an eyeballed offset -> projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E87
- `[gamedesign][ai][testing]` A tuning change that measures identical to a flat/unchanged control isn't proof the lever is weak — run the control deliberately, and if they match, grep whether the AI decision code even invokes the changed mechanic before trusting either number → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E32
- `[browser][svg][animation]` A CSS `transform` on an SVG element REPLACES, not composes with, a static `transform` attribute already on that same element (e.g. a rotate for a diamond marker) — give the animation its own wrapping `<g>` instead → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E33
- `[state][input][gamedev][architecture]` opticon#E28's snapshot-before-clear and prologue#E2's event-time pending queue are ONE primitive — a latch only the consumer's drain clears — sandbox-confirmed fixing both recorded bug shapes; any discrete datum crossing a lifecycle reset needs it, live reads stay correct only for continuously-held state → projects/local__sandbox-combined-opticon-prologue-latch.md#E1
- `[state][animation][gamedev][determinism]` A scrubbable timeline is only as scrub-exact as its least pure pipeline stage — draft#E7's pure-function term survived seek bit-identically while one downstream stateful chase stage broke the composite by 17x its convergence gap; every per-frame-stateful stage needs an on-seek re-derivation hook → projects/local__sandbox-edge-draft-opticon-scrub.md#E1
