- `[ui][gamedesign][turn-based]` "Whose turn is it" (`game.turn`) answers neither "can this viewer see X" nor "can this viewer act now" — a Watcher-role HUD taught Prisoner move controls during the AI Prisoner's own turn because both questions were collapsed onto one turn variable instead of the codebase's existing separate screen-secrecy and input-authority gates. → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E34
- `[orchestration][capability][truthfulness]` report the actual state transition performed rather than claiming execution of an unavailable named command → projects/local__scabby-the-spoon.md#E3
- `[gamedev][state][turn-based][lifecycle]` A game-over check placed BEFORE the call that ends the game never fires — the round-advance/next-actor bookkeeping at the end of a turn owns the timeout and last-capture endings, so a human Watcher hitting the round limit got a finished game with no result screen; check terminal conditions after every mutation that can create one, and latch the side effects so the check is safely idempotent. → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E35
- `[process][git][testing][deploy]` Verifying a merge only against the files named in the merge output shipped conflict markers plus a duplicated `<script type="module">` (loading the whole app twice) — the summary was truncated, markers are inert to an HTML parser, and the suite had been run BEFORE the merge; scan the whole tree, re-run tests on the merge PRODUCT, and assert structural invariants (each entry point referenced once, every cache-buster equal to BUILD) that behavioural tests can't see. → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E36
- `[testing][ui][layout][harness]` A geometry test passed against verbatim-broken CSS twice: it measured before the rAF-driven ability bars rendered (nothing present, so "no overlap" was vacuously true) and it measured chip boxes rather than the overflowing labels that actually collided — absence-assertions must first assert PRESENCE (count what you measured), wait for render not just state, measure the node that moves, and be proven by re-injecting the real defect. → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E37
- `[gamedev][cutscene][ui][state]` A cutscene inherits the HUD of whatever phase it plays in — code that only ever ran inside a dedicated phase can be silently relying on that phase's setup (the prologue hid the gameplay HUD itself, so no cutscene ever had to), and running it during normal play leaves the friends panel, objective, coach hint and in-flight toasts sitting on top of the letterbox with no error; give the cinematic explicit ownership of the screen (snapshot visible elements, restore exactly that set — never blanket-show, or rule-hidden elements like pre-level-1 meters surface early). → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E88
- `[gamedev][state][input]` A boolean set ONCE at the start of a sub-phase and read elsewhere as "are we in that sub-phase" silently outlives it if nothing clears it — two input-routing call sites kept treating the whole rest of a multi-stage sequence as the one scripted stage the flag was written for, so a later free-form stage's trash cans became completely uninteractable with no error and the correct objective on screen throughout; before trusting a "we are in X" flag at a new call site, grep everywhere it is assigned — if it is only ever set true it needs an explicit "and X hasn't ended" companion at every read site. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E89
- `[gamedev][worldgen][geometry]` A placement algorithm built from FIXED absolute offsets is implicitly coupled to the geometry it was tuned against — a later, independently correct fix that shortened the distance between its two endpoints left the offset nearly as long as the route itself, collapsing four intended stops into a cluster a few units apart; express such offsets as a fraction of the live distance between the endpoints, and separately audit any "search within maxD" helper for a squared-vs-plain-distance units mismatch, which silently matches the globally nearest candidate regardless of the stated radius. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E90
- `[gamedev][worldgen][gamedesign]` If waypoint spacing is bounded by a straight line between two fixed endpoints, tuning the PERPENDICULAR offset cannot fix "too close together" — it widens the zig-zag without lengthening the walk, yielding a real numeric gain (3.6-14.5u to 17.2-23.2u) the player still experiences as no travel; lengthen the route instead by authoring a wander against the world's real walkable bands, decoupling path length from endpoint distance, and convert the metric into player-felt units (seconds at walk speed, not world units) before concluding a spacing fix worked. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E91
- `[browser][svg][animation]` `transform-box` decides which space `transform-origin` is measured in — `fill-box` re-bases it to the element's OWN bbox, so passing viewBox/user coordinates under it puts the pivot far outside the shape (a 14px marker at (200,172) pivots about (393,337)) and any scale/rotate flings it across the frame; use `view-box` for an explicit point in the drawing and reserve `fill-box` for `center`/percentages. Pure-translate animations are immune, so the same bug hides at other call sites — audit by the origin's units, not by what currently looks wrong. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E92
- `[gamedesign][gamedev][playtest]` An objective is not shipped until the thing it names is FINDABLE — a separate question from whether it is implemented: "find food" survived two correct mechanical fixes (bins spawning in the road; a stale flag making them uninteractable) and still read as broken, because nothing in the world pointed at food (unlit props, a 2.4-unit interact radius, at night) while the beat's OTHER half had an affordance (an emissive glow and a point light on the warmth vent) — that asymmetry is the tell, so when a "doesn't work" report outlives a provably correct fix, stop fixing the mechanic and screenshot the beat's first frame from the player's own camera. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E93
- `[gamedev][worldgen][geometry]` Moving an anchor prop silently moves anything whose transform DERIVES from it — relocating a door recomputed a set piece's `heading = atan2(door - start)`, swinging a 26x13 underpass deck ~100 degrees until it intersected two buildings, with no error and nothing visible at ground level (the player stands under the deck, where the clipping hides); grep for derived transforms before relocating an anchor, check the rotated footprint's AABB against world geometry, and express the resolution as a clearance/`avoid` rule rather than tuned coordinates. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E94
- `[process][tooling][verification]` A sync/submit step reporting "N pushed, 0 held" is not a promotion — acceptance ran later, asynchronously, and wrote its verdict into the artifact where nobody looked, so seven lessons sat held for a whole session while being reported as filed; close that loop by reading the destination once per session, and note that a rejection written back as the artifact's own `- key:` bullets can SHADOW the very field it complains about (a stale hold block's `- provenance: ... assumed-only ...` line overwrote the real provenance and made the entry permanently unpassable), so strip stale verdicts before re-validating and check with the gate's own predicates, never the template. → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E21
- `[browser][tooling][process][debugging]` Entry-point-only `?v=` stamping leaves nested ES-module imports on stale cache keys (third recurrence) and a PARTIAL bust is worse than none — a fresh entry against a stale dep turns "old content" into an unresolved import and a black screen the moment a release adds an export, while uniformly-unstamped degrades to a consistent old set that still runs; stamp every specifier IN SOURCE via a script and assert it in the suite (no behavioural test can catch this — the source on disk is always right, so the suite is green precisely while users run old code), and treat "shipped fix had no effect" as a deploy-integrity signal before re-diagnosing the symptom. → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E4
- `[process][tooling]` An automated promoter that DELETES its input on success silently destroys whatever it only partially parses — ideas' steward reads `entries[0]` from each incoming/ file then unlinks it, so a 4-kernel proposal promoted 1 and lost 3 while validate.py (which checks every entry) and CI both went green; keep one entry per file, make the validator and promoter agree on the unit of submission, and always `--dry-run` the promoter against your own proposal first → projects/pappydapimp69__ideas.md#E3
- `[gamedesign][simulation][ai][narrative-design]` To grow an antagonist out of a social sim rather than assigning one, split the opinion scalar into an ambient hearsay channel hard-banded strictly INSIDE the behaviour gate (0.25 under a 0.3 confront threshold) plus an uncapped firsthand channel, because one scalar fed by both cannot be tuned — weak enough that gossip never starts a brawl is weak enough that nothing ever crosses, which is how it shipped at -0.178 against a -0.30 gate; source the crossing from an incident the utility scorer already produces incidentally (agents repeat a rumour to the person it is ABOUT, since being the subject is not one of juicy/present/novel), tag it pivotal and non-decaying, and give the resulting confrontation a DISCHARGE term or the pair re-enacts the identical scene every hour — measured over 20 seeds this split reach and drama into independent axes (biggest gossip: 4.45/8 reached, subject learns 4/20; housebound widow: 2.55/8 reached, 13/20 and 53 rows). → projects/pappydapimp69__smallmouth.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][simulation][testing][gamedev]` A threshold-gated mechanic can be implemented, documented and unit-tested and still NEVER fire in the running system, because unit tests hand-build inputs the system cannot produce — measured 0 mutations and 0 confrontations across 10 seeded weeks with max distortion 0.36 against a 0.50 gate and the most-negative opinion edge -0.178 against a -0.30 gate, invisible in every aggregate metric because a beat that never fires subtracts nothing; for each gate log the maximum value its quantity actually reaches under real dynamics and assert in an integration test that the mechanic fires at least once, and when it cannot, suspect the quantity is bounded by an unrelated correct feature (here a 20h news half-life capping chains at 2 hops) and change what it is a function of instead of moving the threshold. → projects/pappydapimp69__smallmouth.md#E2
- `[gamedesign][ai][asymmetric-hidden-info][testing]` An AI reading the hidden state doesn't just play strong — it deletes the mechanic that exists to manipulate the opponent's ESTIMATE of that state (bluffing was provably inert vs a hard prisoner reading the true gaze), and the damage presents as "that lever measures weak," inviting tuning instead of a leak hunt; give the AI a belief fed by the same observations the human gets, and enforce it by trapping the property with a recording getter (0 reads after, 42,079 before) rather than grepping source. → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E38
- `[process][agent][verification][testing]` A fluent, internally consistent, confidently-reported answer is the characteristic SYMPTOM of an unchecked frame rather than evidence against one — the same defect fired three times in one session at three altitudes (spacing optimised in world units when the player feels seconds; mechanics called covered because unit tests passed while firing 0 times in 10 seeded weeks; a full costed valuation produced for a currency that turned out to be a joke), and in every case the work INSIDE the frame was sound, which is exactly why nothing flagged it; the error is silent by construction and more internal rigour only makes it more convincing, so restate every frame in the recipient's units before optimising in it (seconds felt not units measured; does this gate ever fire under real dynamics not does a test supply a value past it; what is this answer denominated in) and spend the one sentence asking rather than the whole wrong deliverable. → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E22
- `[gamedev][ai][pathfinding][balance][method]` A "soft" avoid-set that falls back to ignoring itself when no clean route exists is a hard constraint that switches OFF under pressure — it made every caution/bluff/skill knob upstream measure ~3pt and get filed as a weak lever; a cluster of independent levers all measuring the same small effect should point at a shared downstream bottleneck, and the fix is to price risk and progress in one unit (cost search, no fallback) then verify with a behaviour metric AND a tempo-cost metric, since safety that is free is not caution. → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E39
- `[process][ci][deployment]` The dangerous deploy end-states are wrong-but-plausible, not red — a fallback that silently downgrades the artifact, a "cancelled" (not failed) run from a cross-branch shared concurrency group — so verify the SERVED artifact against intended bytes, refuse downgrade fallbacks, scope concurrency by ref -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E1
- `[process][testing]` An exact-match field parser that returns "" on a miss yields plausible answers, not errors — a saturated derived metric (all values pinned at 1.00) is the tell; prefix-match real label spellings, join wrapped lines, pin the corpus shape in a fixture -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E2
- `[algorithm][graph][determinism]` Hysteresis needs oscillation as a precondition — on an already-convergent loop a switching margin just freezes visit order into the answer (stability 0.99->0.61 measured); find the axis that actually oscillates (canon growth between runs) and put the margin there -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E3
- `[algorithm][graph][testing]` One deterministic run of an order-dependent algorithm is one witness, not the answer — run several decorrelated orders, publish only their agreement, print the disagreement rate (single traversal self-agreed on just 0.51 of cluster pairs) -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E4
- `[gamedesign][simulation][testing]` Branching a root-sourced rumour makes the crowd MORE right (the root's version owns the largest subtree; mutations only infect their own) — to make consensus a losing play, sweep for the regime where per-hop corruption outruns root reach, then pin the strategy win-rate as a test -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E5
- `[gamedesign][ux][onboarding][gamedev]` A system that exists but that nothing on screen points at scores the same as one that does not exist — five independent playtest failures (name reveal in a routine modal, goal-stated-not-next-action objective, catcher rules living in undrawn constants under HUD bands that contradicted the real night-scaled trigger, night as uniform darkening with zero lit places, achievement unlocks in the shared toast channel) were one defect five times, every mechanism intact and every suite green because presentation absence subtracts nothing from any test; audit each mechanic with "what visible thing signals this, and does it read the LIVE value the rule tests or a copy that can drift", bind signals to the exact variables the rules test so drift is structural nonsense, and treat a wrong signal as worse than none. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E95
- `[harness][testing][process][gamedev]` The second time a verification wall stops you, build the driver instead of working around it — six abandoned headless attempts in one session each cost ten minutes, were paid from scratch because nothing was written down, and each shipped a change marked "not verified in-engine"; the committed driver took under an hour, and the obstacles turned out to be mundane boot details (a three-step wizard behind one button, a keydown handler that started the run so a "does the dog move" probe was reading a legitimately frozen cutscene, an overlay eating pointer events) rather than the focus and event plumbing I blamed each time — poll the app's own state for transitions rather than sleeping computed durations, assert distance travelled rather than elapsed time so "input did not arrive" is distinguishable from "correctly ignored", and expect the payoff immediately: first use proved a HUD fix had shipped DEAD, overwritten every frame by a second writer, which is invisible to both source review and unit tests. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E96
- `[ai][gamedev][state]` A second independent instance of additive-bonus-defeats-a-veto: role/personality bonuses added OUTSIDE an on/off gate left a gated-off option scoring above idle (banked 1224 of a resource capped at 34) — write `gated ? 0 : (everything)`, never `(gated ? 0 : base) + extras`, because a veto has to be unconditional -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E6
- `[gamedesign][process][testing]` Content can be unreachable BY ARITHMETIC while presenting as merely slow — a tech tree costing ~67 stone per faction in a world holding 48 total, shared three ways, so four of five buildings could never appear at any seed; sum total demand vs total supply before tuning, and re-sum whenever a new consumer is attached to an existing resource -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E7
- `[ai][gamedev][simulation]` Utility-AI agents sharing faction state read identical inputs, and identical inputs + a switching margin locks the whole group onto one action forever (one agent replicated N times, no division of labour) — add a seeded per-agent bias AND stockpile-responsive urgency; the tell is a resource pinned at a constant for many cycles, which reads as a slow economy -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E8
- `[gamedesign][simulation][state]` A termination predicate keyed on a resource level goes degenerate once that level becomes the steady state — wars ended on the tick they were declared for 90 of 100 days once forests went extinct, silently starving every behaviour needing time inside the event (an arsonist reached a target 0 times in 251 assigned ticks); re-audit threshold predicates whenever an equilibrium shifts -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E9
- `[testing][harness][process]` A poll predicate evaluated in a browser/worker/remote context does not close over the caller's scope: a captured host variable reads as undefined IN-CONTEXT rather than throwing, so the wait never satisfies and surfaces as a timeout whose state dump shows a perfectly healthy target — pass host values through the explicit argument channel, and read "timed out but the state looks fine" as evidence against the predicate, not the target. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E97
- `[save][architecture][state][gamedesign]` A restored COUNT is not restored state: persisting the aggregate of one-shot rewards without their identities re-opens all of them on load — the number comes back right, the world does not, and the same reward banks twice while the HUD agrees with the rules and both disagree with the world; persist the identities (indices into a seeded deterministic list are compact and exact) and DERIVE the aggregate from what actually restored. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E98
- `[gamedev][ui][input][gamedesign]` One contextual-action slot fed by candidates with DIFFERENT reach radii must be arbitrated by distance divided by each candidate's OWN reach — category priority hands the slot to whatever was written first (a bin 1.9u from a lamp post beat the post you were standing on, killing a new verb across half the city with green tests and correct source) and raw distance hands it to the largest radius; the bug is invisible to tests of either side because every source is individually correct and only the arbitration is wrong. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E99
- `[gamedev][ui][cutscene][state]` A one-shot "hide the HUD for this cinematic" is undone on the next frame by the per-frame writers that own those elements — they guard on phase/cutscene and do not know a beat is running, so the hide is present, correct and reverted in 16ms (a name-reveal beat shipped with a coach line and an action button over it, beside a comment claiming the screen was empty); make the snapshot handle itself the ownership flag that every per-frame writer consults, and assert a "nothing else is on screen" claim against computed styles or a screenshot, never against the code that requests it. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E100
- `[gamedesign][gamedev][ux][playtest]` Drawing ONE term of a conjunctive rule is worse than drawing nothing — the player collects counterexamples against the only signal they can see (safe inside the drawn sight ring at a low profile; safe outside it at a high one) and concludes the system is arbitrary, which is how a truthful live-bound signal scored the same 1/4 as no signal at all; fold the other terms into the appearance of the one glyph they already watch so it means the whole proposition, and give an instantaneous state change a visible ramp so the moment cause becomes effect lasts long enough to observe. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E101
- `[ui][mobile][browser][testing]` A hand-picked `top` on an absolutely-positioned panel meant to sit under a sibling is a hard-coded copy of that sibling's current WRAP HEIGHT — it degrades progressively, so a 34% overlap on desktop reads as tight styling while the identical rule puts the panel 100% inside its sibling on a phone; put such panels in one flow container instead of two absolute anchors, and verify by measuring element-vs-element intersection at a phone-shaped AND phone-height viewport rather than by looking. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E102
- `[testing][harness][input][browser][mobile]` `dispatchEvent(new TouchEvent(...))` does not synthesize the POINTER events most controls listen on — browsers generate those from real hardware input, so a script-dispatched touch fires cleanly, changes nothing, and reads as "touch is broken" in innocent application code; drive touch at the protocol layer (CDP Input.dispatchTouchEvent) instead, and check which event family a control actually listens on before writing input for it. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E103
- `[ui][accessibility][design][process]` Name design tokens by ROLE-AGAINST-A-BACKGROUND, not by hue: one accent used both as text on a dark surface and as a fill under white text is arithmetically unable to satisfy both contrast requirements, so whichever use came second is failing and no local care fixes it (white on the fill measured 2.35:1 across every button in the game, while darkening the token would have broken the HUD text it also served) — split into two tokens and measure the rendered pairs, since a code read shows two plausible variables and nothing wrong. → projects/pappydapimp69__dog.md#E104
- `[gamedesign][security][ui][testing]` In any system with hidden state a REFUSAL IS AN OBSERVATION — gating an AFFORDANCE on the secret makes its absence a perfect readout of the secret (a "strike this false entry" prompt hidden while hallucinating is a lucidity meter with extra steps), and failing SILENTLY once the affordance is restored leaks it again one layer down (press, observe nothing, conclude); split what the screen shows from what the rules allow, emit a byte-identical acknowledgement for the refused case, record the refusal as a BELIEF on the observer rather than a fact on the object, and let only the effect differ — and note the test wrote the bug down first, because a suite authored from the feature's own mental model certifies that model's blind spot. → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E5
- `[gamedev][ai][gamedesign][camera]` "The AI companions scatter" is usually a VISIBILITY claim, not a behaviour one — measure on-screen fraction per companion first (measured 0.0% on screen while `goal=follow` 100% of the time, from a slot function using `+(sin,cos)` against a camera forward of `-(sin,cos)`); three faults hide under it, each invisible until the one above is fixed: a follower capped near the leader's own speed can chase a MOVING station but never reach it (60-89 degrees of bearing error vs 11 for the rear guard, reachable for free) so followers need a gap-proportional catch-up gear, a station ahead of the leader sits inside geometry 31-38% of the time and must be collision-resolved before assignment, and a YAW-anchored formation orbits the camera in first person so the rear guard can never be looked at — anchor to a smoothed direction of TRAVEL, and separate "behind you" from "gone" with a stationary 360-degree sweep in which every member must enter frame. → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E6
- `[gamedev][testing][turn-based][state]` A rule phrased in ONE actor's turns ("you cannot be caught again before your next turn") stored against a GLOBAL clock silently bakes in the actor count — a "1 round" reprieve where `round` ticks once per prisoner turn expired on the scan immediately BEFORE the freed prisoner's own next turn, so they never got the promised turn, a bug that scaled with party size and vanished at one actor (the configuration manual checks use); store durations in the units the RULE is written in and decrement on that actor's own event, adding one extra when the grant lands during their own turn, and note its test could not catch it because it asserted the formula and moved the clock by hand instead of advancing turns. → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E40
- `[testing][gamedesign][verification][ai-agents]` A scripted policy reading ground truth is not a weak oracle for a mechanic whose cost is an information gap, it is a ZERO-SENSITIVITY one — sweeping deception strength 0-2 moved the truth-reading policy 0.0pp (flat 100%) and the percept-reading policy 77.0pp (100%->23%) in the same world; so a constant tuned until the oracle is pressured is lethal to a deceived player (oracle-tuned budget leaves the deceived at 0.0%, honestly-tuned budget leaves the oracle at 100%), and because a zero-sensitivity metric looks exactly like a stable valid one it passes review and a green suite — ask what your automated player cannot be subjected to, and if that names the mechanic, build a handicapped agent on the same derived view the human sees. → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E7
- `[serialization][testing][gamedev]` A round-trip equality check proves a serialiser agrees with ITSELF, not that it preserved meaning — mapping pathfinding nodes as `{x, z}` when they are grid cells `{cx, cz}` wrote `undefined` for every node, resumed agents aimed at NaN and stood wedged until their next repath, and the round-trip test stayed green because both sides held the same corrupted array; for any field that IS a coordinate system (cell vs world, local vs global, index vs id) assert on the restored VALUES (finite, in range, resolves to a real entity), and treat a determinism failure appearing far downstream as a signal of a silently degraded value upstream rather than of the rng. → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E8
- `[refactor][state][testing][gamedev]` When a rewrite changes a system's MODEL, delete the old model's fields in the same change rather than just ceasing to write them — after two rewrites a pylon's aliveness lived in three fields (`spent`, `charge`, `live`), only ONE code path updated all three, and `charge` (believed vestigial) was still deciding what percept and render showed, so a spent pylon could still glow and still be offered; behavioural tests all passed because they only drove the one synchronising path, while an invariant sweep asserting structural facts about any reachable state under hostile input found it on 10 of 12 seeds immediately — grep for READERS not writers before calling a field dead, and when N fields encode one fact the fix is one field, because "these must agree" is a bug waiting for the next code path. → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E9
- `[browser][ui][input][rendering]` A CSS pixel is not a physical unit, and OS display scaling (Windows at 125%) resizes the view OUTSIDE the app — shrinking the CSS viewport 1920x1080 to 1536x864 while the monitor stays put, which is NOT an aspect-ratio bug (measured hfov identical at 100/125/150%) but three unit errors around the 3D view: fixed-px HUD becomes 25% physically larger AND takes 25% more of a smaller viewport (reads exactly as "zoomed in"), pointer-lock `movementX` arrives in CSS px so the same physical sweep turns the camera 20% slower, and `devicePixelRatio` cached at construction goes stale on scaling/zoom/monitor changes; size UI in viewport-relative units, convert pointer deltas by devicePixelRatio, re-read DPR on resize plus a re-arming `(resolution: Ndppx)` query, and test the SAME physical screen at several deviceScaleFactor values asserting on FRACTIONS of the viewport. → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E10
- `[process][tooling][cli]` A documented trap that readers of the document keep hitting is evidence about the enforcement point, not the reader — move any precondition the tool can decide itself (cwd vs cache, 0 results vs term count) into the tool as a refusal, and leave the prose to explain why; test the guard against the real failing invocation, since a guard written from the argv shape silently never fires on a quoted phrase → projects/pappydapimp69__collective.md#E14
- `[gamedev][state][testing][browser]` cross-frame state written onto an object a per-frame accessor rebuilds is a silent no-op — and a shared shallow-copied default makes it look like it works in fresh-state tests → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E11
- `[tooling][process][observability]` `brain query` matches substrings with no word boundary, so a short term inflates its own coverage (`raft` hits `draft`/`craft`, reporting 23+44 for a topic with none) — a high count is not evidence of coverage; open `--verbose` and read the headers before concluding a topic is done, since the false-positive direction silently suppresses work and has no automatic guard → projects/pappydapimp69__collective.md#E15
- `[ui][input][gamedesign][onboarding]` a verb with no direct-target form inherits its aim from a SEPARATE binding, so leaving that selection control out of one scheme's legend silently makes the verb unaimable while every functional test passes → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E12
- `[deploy][verification][cdn][process]` A streak of successful deploy checks means more than one check ONLY if the checks are independent — under HTTP keep-alive all K hit the same CDN edge node and the false-'live' rate exactly equals a single check's, for every K and p (at p=0.8: one check 0.80, independent K=5 0.33, sticky K=5 0.80). Force a new connection per check, space them in real time, and log which node answered; a cache-buster changes what the node is asked for, not which node answers -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E10
- `[tooling][process][git]` A commit gate that scans the MESSAGE as well as the diff will match on the acknowledgement text it just demanded — acking five tensions with written reasons produced two new matches (T3, T11) from the ack prose alone, whose keywords appear nowhere in the diff, forcing a third attempt. Any validator whose input includes the response to its own output needs that response excluded from the scan, or a round ceiling -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E11
- `[ai][testing][simulation]` A no-progress detector's improvement threshold must be a RATE (min gain per patience window), not a per-observation margin — best-ever gating catches oscillation but ambient autonomous drift of 0.01/tick mints a fresh best-ever every tick and the detector never fires in 60 ticks while the task gap never closes; a fixed epsilon is defeated by any drift rate above eps/patience -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E12
- `[save][determinism][process][testing]` Fixing a non-canonical serialization is a FORMAT CHANGE — normalize at the write boundary AND bump the encoding version in the same commit. brain-builder#E8's fix alone rewrote 105 of 200 stored save hashes while behaviour stayed identical for 200/200, and the affected subset is arbitrary (it depends on one word's sign bit), so it reads as random corruption rather than a format change and every stored golden rejects the fix as the regression -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E13
- `[knowledge-management][verification][process]` A co-verification edge's `div` counts distinct SOURCES, not distinct ASSUMPTIONS — an edge at weight 5 / div 4 had min-surviving-support 0 (all five shared one fixture) and outranked a weight 2 / div 2 edge that survives any single fault. Counting distinct assumption SETS fails too (4/4 distinct, still 0). Report worst-case surviving support under per-assumption fault injection; bears on open tension T20 -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E14
- `[dsp][realtime][testing]` A realtime drain cap with carryover preserves an event TOTAL but redistributes it across callbacks (11/20 callbacks differ, peak 7->2) — assert cumulative, never per-callback; and the cap MASKS an aggregate-vs-per-sub-step counting bug (8/21 -> 17/21 correct) by shrinking each advance, so install the accuracy fix and its test BEFORE the cap -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E15
- `[testing][determinism][input]` A golden replay fingerprint is blind to any DETERMINISTIC upstream drop — record and replay drop identically so the hash is stable and green while the feature is 0/4 dead and score is 71% understated; and a golden recorded after the defect bakes it in, so the FIX fails the test. Pair fingerprints with an input-arrival assertion and track each golden's provenance -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E16
- `[gamedev][testing][method][observability]` An outcome counter at 0 reads identically for "correctly rare" and "structurally impossible" — instrument each CONJUNCT's marginal rate and multiply: a shipped dilemma fired once in 120 sim days (6.5% x 3.1%) while arson's arrival conjunct was a hard 0/121 torch ticks. Also: measuring the DECISION is not measuring the ACTION — a clean monotonic willingness curve was cited as proof the mechanic worked while the completed action fired on 1 of 5 seeds -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E17
- `[input][gamedev][architecture][testing]` An event-time pending queue IS a capture layer, so capture-layer properties apply to the FIX — hold a LIST, not one slot per key: a per-key slot is the direct port of the sampled boolean and re-imposes the same one-event-per-key-per-frame limit, losing a sub-frame double-tap (5 presses: sampling 1, per-key slot 4, list 5) while the original bug's regression test still passes. A destructive first drain starves the second consumer 5 vs 0 with the first's tests identical either way -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E18
- `[simulation][testing][balance][method]` A live, correct seeded PRNG buys variance in EVENTS, not in OUTCOMES — across 5 seeds of a shipped sim the flavour metrics varied healthily (blessings 77-83, respawns 619-893) while pop/tech/buildings/food/births/techsResearched were bit-identical, because every progression ceiling is reachable inside the run horizon. Unlike opticon#E20's constant-RNG failure the tell is the PARTITION; unlike brain-builder#E4 the bound need not be a clamp with a binding rate (finite content simply gets consumed) and the loss is invisible within any single run; sweep >=3 seeds and check each invariant metric against its ceiling -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E19
- `[testing][harness][observability]` "Timed out but the target looks healthy" does not name a layer — a broken poll predicate (dog#E97) and a silently starved capture layer both time out with no exception, ready=true, errors=[]; under a liveness-style dump the two reports are byte-identical, and the diagnosis only ever worked when the dump held the awaited quantity showing it already satisfied. Discriminate with an in-context positive control: run the predicate against a synthetic state built to satisfy it -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E20
- `[gamedesign][state][testing][simulation]` a bounded window predicate degenerates exactly like a resource threshold (august-10#E9) when the arrival distribution it was calibrated against moves — the event still opens, its interior is never reached, nothing errors; derive such constants from the mechanism they wait on and size them to the upper tail, not the mean → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E13
- `[ui][gamedesign][state][algorithm]` a first-eligible-and-HOLD selector inverts when its predicate widens from rare to common — it latches onto a trivial early candidate and stays fully operational pointed at the wrong thing; fix the ACQUIRE ordering (worth 95%) not just preemption (worth 0% alone) → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E14
- `[testing][input][browser][mobile][touch]` A hand-built TouchEvent and a real CDP touch dispatch fail mobile input testing in observably different ways — zero Pointer Events ever (dog#E103: the dispatch itself is fake) vs. pointerdown/pointermove firing then cut short by pointercancel (touch-action still permits native panning, shadow#E3's territory) — read which signature you got before choosing a fix, they don't share one. → projects/local__sandbox-touch-cancel-signature.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][algorithm][state][simulation]` chain cohesion ("in range of ANYONE") is not proximity — legal spread is (N-1)*R so recruiting widens it for free, a perceived-but-absent member is a valid link that can bridge a party really in two pieces, and single losses are positional: drop the lead-adjacent member and 1/5 remain, drop the tail and 5/5 do → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E15
- `[testing][process][verification]` a test sharing its feature's assumption passes on both correct and defective code, so a green suite cannot identify which guards are load-bearing — negative-control every guard by reverting the defect and watching it fail, and model a caller's real conditions (object lifetime, call order, how state is fetched) rather than the convenient shape → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E16
- `[backoff][retry][distributed][performance]` Exponential backoff de-synchronises nothing — clients sharing an outage share the retry index, so 2^k keeps 200 clients in lockstep (peak 200/200); jitter is the entire mechanism (steady-state peak 16 vs 200). And the CAP converts a self-limiting retry storm into a permanent one: uncapped sheds load as the outage lengthens (0 retries in the final window), capped converges to a constant CLIENTS/CAP rate forever (400, 2.4x volume) -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E21
- `[backpressure][performance][observability][architecture]` A bounded queue cannot smooth SUSTAINED overload — past a knee at depth ~= service rate every depth serves the same count and differs only in steady latency, which parks at depth/service_rate (49 ticks at depth 100, 499 at 1000). Every component-local metric reads healthy; only end-to-end age sees it. A big enough buffer drives drops to 0 while the backlog grows unbounded. Size queues in latency, alarm on depth and item age -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E22
- `[sorting][algorithm][correctness][testing]` Chained stable sorts must run LEAST significant key first — the reverse of how the requirement is spoken — so "sort by A then by B" silently yields B-major output that is still correctly sorted and passes is_sorted(). Prefer one composite key. And on a fixture with distinct secondary values the correct and incorrect implementations produce identical output, so the test cannot cover it: require a primary-key tie with differing secondary values -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E23
- `[compression][performance][streaming][measurement]` Compression ratio on small similar messages is decided by DICTIONARY SCOPE, not algorithm or level — per-message zlib gave 1.15x where batching the identical bytes gave 14.06x, and below ~128 bytes/message it expands (0.80x at 32B). The redundancy lives BETWEEN records and per-message framing discards it before the compressor sees it; a streaming compressor with a sync-flush per message recovered 91% of the gap at 7.03x -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E24
- `[timezone][scheduling][correctness][process]` "Daily at 7am" and "every 24 hours" are different programs — over a year in a DST zone, +24h arithmetic put 238 of 365 runs at the wrong local hour; a calendar rebuild held the wall time and moved the UTC instant. Neither errors. Store local time + zone id and rebuild per day for wall-time jobs, never a precomputed UTC "next run". A nonexistent local time resolves silently; an ambiguous one has two instants that format identically, so key idempotency on UTC -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E25
- `[regex][security][validation][testing]` `$` is a LINE anchor with a special case ("end, or just before a final newline"), so `^[a-z]+$` is not a whole-string validator — it accepts a trailing newline while correctly rejecting embedded/leading/CR/CRLF, and that one hole is what input(), readline() and a textarea append for free. An accepted value produced 2 header lines instead of 1. Use re.fullmatch or \A...\Z; adding re.M inverts it, accepting embedded-newline payloads -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E26
- `[floating-point][correctness][algorithm][determinism]` `abs(a-b) < eps` is reflexive and symmetric but NOT transitive, so it is illegal in any dedupe/group/cluster/key operation — 5 values gave 4 different dedupe results (sizes 2-3) across their 120 orderings with no error raised. Sorting first makes it deterministic, not correct. Quantise instead: round(v/eps) is transitive by construction and identical for every ordering -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E27
- `[memoization][caching][determinism][testing]` Memoization changes CALL COUNT, and for a function touching a shared sequence (PRNG, id allocator, counter, log) call count is behaviour — 5 of 13 draws skipped and five unrelated downstream values diverged while a unit test of the cached function passed and got MORE stable. If repeated calls with the same argument return different values the function was never pure; the cache freezes the first result. Promote the hidden dependency to an argument -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E28
- `[pagination][database][testing][correctness]` OFFSET pagination corrupts as an exact function of NET shift before the cursor — dups = max(0, ins-del), skipped = max(0, del-ins) — not of churn volume, so BALANCED insert/delete traffic is always perfectly correct and realistic mixed-load testing is the test that passes; reproduce with SKEWED mutations. The lost row sits at a page boundary, not at the mutation (deleting row 0 lost row 30). Use keyset; never assert with a row count -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E29
- `[streaming][unicode][encoding][testing]` Per-chunk `bytes.decode()` corrupted at 34 of 37 chunk sizes and was correct at 3 unremarkable ones (13, 26, 1024) — an I/O buffer size silently decides correctness. Use an incremental decoder (0 of 37 corrupt) with a final=True flush. `errors='replace'` is a data-LOSS policy not a safe one: 0 exceptions, 21 replacement chars, no longer round-trips. An ASCII corpus corrupts at 0 of 37, so it cannot cover this -> projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__august-10.md#E30
- `[process][verification][deploy][method]` A pipeline step's own self-reported success is not proof the destination changed, and a single read of that destination isn't proof either — require a short streak of consecutive independent reads (with a ceiling, so real failure still terminates as FAILED) before trusting convergence; sandbox-confirmed composing brain#E21's async-report gap with opticon#E8/#E13's flap-and-streak requirement into one mechanism. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-brain-opticon-verification-streak.md#E1
- `[testing][harness][method][debugging]` august-10#E20's positive-control discriminator (run a poll predicate against a synthetic state to tell "broken predicate" from "starved upstream layer") correctly resolves draft#E9's capture-layer-starvation bug ONLY when the synthetic state matches the real upstream layer's actual output fidelity — sandbox-confirmed a lazily-built control (missing fields the predicate checks) gives the SAME false verdict as a genuinely broken predicate, misdirecting the fix. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-august-10-draft-positive-control-fidelity.md#E1
- `[save][determinism][testing][process]` A WORLD_VERSION-bump decision (wrong-sky#E18) made by reviewing whether a patch is behaviourally "content-only" is structurally blind to brain-builder#E8's non-canonical-serialization bug class — sandbox-confirmed 2 of 6 fixed seeds pass behaviour review as content-only while an old save re-saved under the patched code produces different bytes; the decision needs a state-swept canonical-form diff, not behaviour equality alone. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-brain-builder-wrong-sky-version-bump-blind-spot.md#E1
- `[procgen][testing][gamedesign][method]` opticon#E16's per-seed finished-map dependency check run over recursion#E14's fixed 0..N seed suite gives false confidence in inverse proportion to the failure rate — sandbox-confirmed a clean 100-seed pass at three placement probabilities while 64/30/17 genuinely unreachable-content seeds existed over 20,000; size the seed sample from the dependency's own analytic failure rate, not a round enumeration count. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-opticon-recursion-per-seed-dependency-under-fixed-suite.md#E1
- `[determinism][testing][architecture][simulation]` brain-builder#E7's trace-and-replay guarantee covers the sim/presentation seam only — sandbox-confirmed its replay-parity check passes clean on a sim carrying dog#E8's in-place iteration-order bug (3.99 unit forward-vs-reversed divergence at 12 ticks), faithfully animating a wrong outcome; replay parity is not evidence of sim correctness and never retires the order-independence regression. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-brain-builder-dog-trace-replay-parity-vs-order-dependence.md#E1
- `[testing][method][balance][verification]` mirage#E16's negative control reports on the FIXTURE'S regime, not the guard — sandbox-confirmed a damage cap reads "not load-bearing" (identical 48.0/48.0 totals) at binding rate 0.000 while the same guard is load-bearing at rates 0.333 and 1.000; instrument brain-builder#E4's binding rate alongside every negative control and treat rate 0 as uninformative, never as a pass. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-mirage-brain-builder-negative-control-needs-binding-rate.md#E1
- `[testing][determinism][method][process]` wrong-sky#E17's fingerprint-invisible defect and august-10#E16's fix-rejecting golden are the two exits from ONE dilemma controlled by fingerprint SCOPE — sandbox-confirmed identical hashes under cadence-only scope while 3 strikes leaked (hp 50 vs 80), and defect-era golden rejection once the affected field is included; no single fingerprint both catches the bug and accepts the fix. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-august-10-wrong-sky-fingerprint-scope-dilemma.md#E1
- `[balance][testing][gamedesign][method]` brain-builder#E5's smoothness assertion structurally rewards flatness, so waiting-city#E5's zero-variance dead region scores as an IMPROVEMENT not a defect — sandbox-confirmed a curve extended with a flat tail keeps monotonic True and max_step 0.63 unchanged while the new region's variance is 0.000000; assert per-region variance separately whenever a curve gains a segment. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-brain-builder-waiting-city-smoothness-blind-to-zero-variance.md#E1
- `[testing][method][harness][algorithm]` august-10#E4's decorrelated-order sweep does NOT subsume test#E8's baseline-adversarial input — sandbox-confirmed the sweep's mean advantage (24.280) is under half the adversarial case's (60), 70 of 200 orders tie outright, and E4's own agreement criterion returns False despite 0 losses; report the distribution and baseline-worst tail plus a purpose-built adversarial case. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-test-august-10-order-sweep-does-not-replace-adversarial-input.md#E1
- `[architecture][ai][state][testing]` waiting-city#E19's commit-time veto is a bounded one-tick cost only while the selector re-evaluates — sandbox-confirmed that composing it with mirage#E14's first-eligible-and-HOLD selector produces a permanent livelock (0 work, 20/20 ticks wasted, still latched) versus 15 work for a re-evaluating selector, while 0 overlaps and a healthy FSM keep both lessons' own invariants green. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-mirage-waiting-city-hold-selector-turns-veto-into-livelock.md#E1
- `[determinism][animation][architecture][gamedesign]` draft#E7's purity partition and recursion#E8's clock split are different cuts, and purity is only meaningful relative to a named clock — sandbox-confirmed an unchanged pure-function-of-elapsed-time term diverges between players at identical wall times (180.0 vs 0.0, 90.0 vs 180.0) once keyed to an input-driven advance pointer; split clocks first, then partition purity inside each. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-draft-recursion-purity-is-relative-to-a-named-clock.md#E1
- `[observability][architecture][performance][testing]` dog#E11's max-dwell liveness guarantee gives zero coverage of august-10#E22's sustained-overload backlog — sandbox-confirmed the timeout fires 20 times while served count, drops and end-to-end age stay byte-identical (4000/0/66), so "no agent stuck" plus "no drops" reads as corroborated health over an unbounded queue; alarm on item age and depth instead. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-august-10-dog-liveness-guarantee-is-orthogonal-to-backlog.md#E1
- `[determinism][testing][state][method]` waiting-city#E9's constant-roll-count audit, written with fresh inputs per call, measures a cold path and certifies the property exactly where an august-10#E28 cache breaks it — sandbox-confirmed a perfect [3,3,3,3,3] audit alongside a replayed loop consuming 36 rolls instead of 90 and diverging on 10 of 30 decisions; audit repeated decisions for one agent within a single stream. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-august-10-waiting-city-roll-count-audit-measures-a-cold-path.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][state][determinism]` Scrub-safety as "state = pure function of elapsed time" (draft#E7) silently depends on constant RNG-rolls-per-tick (waiting-city#E9) — violate the roll-count discipline and closed-form time-based scrubbing diverges from ground truth, while full tick replay always stays correct → projects/local__sandbox-combined-scrub-vs-roll-count.md#E1
- `[state][testing][determinism][method]` One NaN drives two opposite-polarity threshold gates to OPPOSITE failures from a single root cause — sandbox-confirmed exclude-if-far admits 4/4 while include-if-near admits 0/4 on identical poisoned input, neither throwing; a NaN regression test on one gate cannot express the other's failure direction, so assert both polarities. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-dog-recursion-nan-gate-polarity.md#E1
- `[algorithm][gamedev][testing][performance]` dog#E19's radius-aware rejection widens the spatial query and silently invalidates dog#E13's cell-size precondition — sandbox-confirmed a hash still sized to the old flat minDist missed 3 of 4 real overlaps, all on the large-radius points E19 was added to protect; re-derive cell size from the new max query radius in the same change. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-dog-spatial-hash-cell-size-vs-rejection-radius.md#E1
- `[security][gamedesign][ui][testing]` opticon#E9's every-consumer leak audit is defeated by its own natural remedy — sandbox-confirmed redacting each enumerated surface leaves all three leaking perfectly, since absence appears exactly when the secret holds (mirage#E5); a valid patch replaces the value with a secret-independent one or refuses on use, it never hides. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-mirage-opticon-redaction-is-itself-an-observation.md#E1
- `[testing][balance][simulation][method]` opticon#E20's constant-RNG stub makes brain-builder#E4's binding-rate instrument inverted rather than merely unavailable — sandbox-confirmed the rate collapses to exactly 1.000/0.000/0.000 under three constant stubs against a true 0.298 under a seeded PRNG, each reading as a confident balance finding; a rate at a hard 0 or 1 boundary is a live-RNG check, not a result. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-opticon-brain-builder-constant-rng-fakes-the-binding-rate.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][simulation][state][testing]` dog#E40's dwell-on-arrival requirement generalizes into the repair for august-10#E9's level-keyed termination degeneracy — sandbox-confirmed a collapsed economy opens 1 event with 0 reachable interiors under a pure resource predicate versus 34 opened / 33 interiors on the identical trace at a 3-tick minimum dwell; give event lifetimes their own units. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-august-10-dog-dwell-repairs-a-degenerate-level-keyed-predicate.md#E1
- `[process][verification][tooling][testing]` A pre-push hash gate reading the STAGED artifact verifies intent, never delivery — sandbox-confirmed it reports "pushed, 0 held" at 300KB and 500KB where the transport silently truncated while passing honestly at small sizes, so small test payloads build false confidence; hash the destination after the push per brain#E21. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-dog-brain-staged-gate-verifies-the-wrong-artifact.md#E1
- `[state][events][gamedev][testing]` mirage#E1's emit spread-order collision and wrong-sky#E5's truthy zero-magnitude read compose into a silent double misroute in which each single fix makes the other appear fixed — sandbox-confirmed the event routes to the wrong handler AND its real 0 reads as absent; repair both together, fixturing on a zero-magnitude colliding-key event. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-mirage-wrong-sky-emit-collision-plus-zero-magnitude.md#E1
- `[ai][testing][method][observability]` opticon#E5's best-ever stall gate and august-10#E12's minimum-rate gate form a strict ladder where the earlier fix is necessary and insufficient — sandbox-confirmed best-ever rescues oscillation (t=11 vs never) but is itself defeated by 0.01/tick drift (never fires; rate gate t=10), all three symptoms presenting identically as continuous progress. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-opticon-august-10-stall-detector-fix-ladder.md#E1
- `[algorithm][gamedev][correctness][testing]` Applying dog#E19's `+ otherPoint.radius` threshold on top of dog#E45's unresolved squared-distance ambiguity is a net regression — sandbox-confirmed a linear radius term against a squared comparison places 3 points where the correct predicate places 2, failing open on a real overlap, whereas the pre-E19 code was at least dimensionally consistent. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-dog-e45-e19-radius-term-on-a-squared-distance.md#E1
- `[security][architecture][state][determinism]` wrong-sky#E9's withheld-command i-frame is a gate with no value to pin, placing it outside recursion#E2's "fix the threshold in state" discipline — sandbox-confirmed a forged stream that never withholds lands every strike (HP 40 vs 70) against a fully E2-compliant reducer; E9 trades a state gate for an unstated trust assumption about the command source. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-recursion-wrong-sky-withheld-command-is-an-unpinnable-gate.md#E1
- `[process][ci][deployment][verification]` august-10#E1's silent downgrade and opticon#E3's default-branch-only constraint make "not red" and "not deployed" independent properties — sandbox-confirmed a green run serving 60 of 100 intended bytes and a cancelled run that is neither green nor red and served nothing; no predicate over run state separates deployed-fine, deployed-wrong and never-deployed, only served bytes do. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-august-10-opticon-not-red-and-not-deployed-are-independent.md#E1
- `[testing][harness][state][gamedev]` dog#E21's discarded setup write and opticon#E14's eaten first action are independent silent losses that both fall inside a directional assertion's accepted range — sandbox-confirmed an intended end state of 30 arriving as 80 and 90 while "HP decreased" stays true throughout, so the assertion gets likelier to pass as more of the scenario is lost. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-dog-opticon-two-silent-losses-inside-one-coarse-assertion.md#E1
- `[determinism][performance][state][testing]` opticon#E22's empty-result cache miss is a DETERMINISM defect, not the performance one it is filed as, whenever the cached call touches a shared sequence (august-10#E28) — sandbox-confirmed 21 BFS calls vs 2 AND divergent final shared RNG state; the "slowdown, zero visible symptom" framing is exactly what makes it look deferrable. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-opticon-august-10-empty-cache-miss-is-a-determinism-defect.md#E1
- `[process][architecture][saga][method]` wrong-sky#E14's "two instances means centralize" measures the within-repo axis while its own cited evidence (a sequel recurrence) sits on waiting-city#E8's cross-repo axis — sandbox-confirmed centralizing in one repo cuts 12 residual buggy paths only to 8, all remainder in siblings; count the two axes separately, centralization and propagation are not substitutes. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-wrong-sky-waiting-city-centralizing-does-not-cross-repos.md#E1
- `[tooling][process][verification][method]` A round ceiling bounds august-10#E11's self-feeding commit gate without explaining it — sandbox-confirmed message scanning escalates a 2-round commit to 4 rounds / 5 acked tensions, and a 3-round ceiling then reports "CEILING" identically for the self-feed and a genuine unacked tension; opticon#E27's discriminator here is free: record whether each match came from diff or message. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-august-10-opticon-ceiling-without-discriminator-on-a-self-feeding-gate.md#E1
- `[testing][harness][gamedev][determinism]` dog#E22's poll-for-the-state discipline, filed narrowly as a headless-timing fix, is also the structural remedy for waiting-city#E2's fixed-coordinate staleness — sandbox-confirmed a scripted driver dropping 6/6 to 1/6 when an NPC gains patrol movement while polling holds 6/6; both hazards are one error, asserting WHEN instead of waiting for WHAT. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-waiting-city-dog-polling-fixes-coordinate-staleness-too.md#E1
- `[observability][testing][simulation][method]` august-10#E17's per-conjunct marginal-rate instrumentation measures reachability, never correctness of effect — sandbox-confirmed a brain-builder#E9 side-targeting misroute leaves every conjunct rate healthy and the fire count identical at 4 while hits-on-enemy drops 4 to 0; E17's "decision is not action" needs a third rung: action is not target. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-august-10-brain-builder-conjunct-rates-cannot-see-a-routing-defect.md#E1
- `[balance][gamedesign][simulation][method]` brain-builder#E11's meter-driven encounter blowout is a domain instance of brain-builder#E2's bounded-vs-unbounded lever rule, not an independent lesson — sandbox-confirmed meter-only power ratios spanning 0.29x-3.75x against 0.70x-1.50x once anchored to player power and capped, so E2's shape-change remedy and its per-axis diagnostic both transfer. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-brain-builder-e11-is-a-domain-instance-of-e2.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][testing][state][method]` opticon#E40's global-clock reprieve bug is a STEP over party size — sandbox-confirmed correct at N=1 and broken at every N>=2, so it passes both a single-actor manual check and a monotonicity-only sweep; brain-builder#E5's smoothness assertion transfers from difficulty curves to correctness sweeps and is what catches the one-step collapse. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-opticon-brain-builder-party-size-correctness-is-a-step-function.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][testing][process][balance]` august-10#E7's total-demand-vs-total-supply sum is necessary and NOT sufficient once content is staged with an uneven resource mix — sandbox-confirmed an aggregate PASS at exactly 60/60 per resource while 2 of 3 stages were unaffordable under an even-split quota; recursion#E7's tag-derived per-stage quota is what makes each stage reachable. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-august-10-recursion-aggregate-supply-does-not-imply-per-stage-supply.md#E1
- `[determinism][state][testing][save]` dog#E12's same-seed-twice byte-identity check is sensitive to a leaked source's VARIABILITY, not to the leak — sandbox-confirmed it passes cleanly while an ambient draw bypasses the seeded stream, whereas test#E2's stored roll count catches it immediately (7 against 8 expected); verify stream integrity by roll-count accounting. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-dog-test-same-seed-twice-is-blind-to-a-session-stable-leak.md#E1
- `[testing][state][gamedev][architecture]` dog#E16's per-action re-validation is unverifiable by the position-only stress test its own recipe implies — sandbox-confirmed "exactly one succeeds" passes identically with and without the fix while 3 non-owners silently advance their FSM to "using"; dog#E43's non-owner-FSM assertion is the only one whose result changes. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-dog-e16-e43-position-assertion-cannot-verify-its-own-fix.md#E1
- `[process][saga][architecture][method]` recursion#E10's grep-the-prior-phase step is the concrete mechanism that pays waiting-city#E12's explicitly-accepted hand-propagation price — sandbox-confirmed a game-0 fix reaching 1 of 4 saga games under handoff-code-only versus 4 of 4 once the prior phase is grepped, matching a shared runtime without adopting one. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-recursion-waiting-city-grep-prior-phase-pays-the-handoff-price.md#E1
- `[state][refactor][testing][gamedev]` mirage#E9's model consolidation naturally picks the numeric field as the survivor, which is precisely wrong-sky#E5's zero-magnitude hazard — sandbox-confirmed a truthy read of the surviving `charge` disagrees with authoritative aliveness on the same 2 of 4 entities, trading a staleness bug for a harder-to-spot one on a model that now looks clean. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-mirage-wrong-sky-model-consolidation-picks-the-hazardous-field.md#E1
- `[verification][process][security][method]` test#E14's branch-protection claim and brain#E21's "N pushed, 0 held" are one error at two layers — reading a system's own declaration in place of observing an outcome; sandbox-confirmed a rule reading as configured, genuinely refusing one named actor and silently bypassed for another, separable only by attempting the action with each. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-test-brain-configuration-is-a-claim-not-an-observation.md#E1
- `[ui][testing][layout][harness]` opticon#E37's "measure the node that MOVES" is the precondition for detecting recursion#E9's missing final-line cursor advance, not a general nicety — sandbox-confirmed a no-overlap assertion returning True against fixed-height chip boxes and False against the real multi-line labels on the identical broken layout, so the wrong node certifies the bug. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-recursion-opticon-measuring-the-wrong-node-certifies-the-layout-bug.md#E1
- `[observability][state][testing][method]` august-10#E17's conjunct instrumentation reaches opticon#E28's clear-before-read dead branch ONLY through the predicted-vs-observed contradiction — sandbox-confirmed both marginals reading fully healthy (0.250, 0.400) at an outcome of 0 while the snapshot fix fires 22 times on the identical schedule; the multiply-and-compare is the detector, not the gauges. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-opticon-august-10-predicted-vs-observed-is-the-detection.md#E1
- `[mobile][input][testing][accessibility]` waiting-city#E6's touch-detection false-negative and recursion#E11's safe-area reachability failure hit disjoint device sets and produce the identical "buttons don't work" report — sandbox-confirmed neither fix reduces the other's failing set, while a headless-emulated profile passes both weak checks and so evidences neither. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-waiting-city-recursion-touch-detection-and-reachability-are-independent.md#E1
- `[testing][database][method][correctness]` august-10#E29 supplies the half test#E8 leaves to judgement — WHICH axis to make adversarial; sandbox-confirmed balanced pagination churn stays perfectly correct at both normal and 10x volume (0 dups, 0 skipped) while insert-skew yields 12 dups and 15 skipped, so derive the axis from the function the failure follows, not from realism or volume. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-august-10-test-e29-names-the-axis-e8-leaves-to-judgement.md#E1
- `[procgen][algorithm][testing][gamedev]` dog#E17's fixed-prop seeding and dog#E18's sub-zone exclusion close disjoint axes — sandbox-confirmed over 40 seeds that prop-seeding alone still intrudes the zone on 18 and zone-exclusion alone still overlaps props on 26, only both reaching 0/0; and per opticon#E16 the honest measure is failing-seed count, never one map inspection. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-dog-opticon-scatter-fixes-are-per-axis-and-per-seed.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][testing][algorithm][method]` brain-builder#E5's smoothness assertion is only as good as the axis swept — sandbox-confirmed a party-SIZE sweep of mirage#E15's chain cohesion reads uniformly healthy at every size (spread reaching exactly (N-1)*R) while cohesion-by-which-member-is-lost is a hard step [1,0,0,0,1]; choose the axis the failure varies on before choosing the assertion. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-mirage-brain-builder-sweep-the-axis-the-failure-varies-on.md#E1
- `[gamedev][state][architecture][rendering]` waiting-city#E4's sim-side spawn delay is an instance of wrong-sky#E13's renderer-derives-never-owns rule, not a separate gameplay-stakes judgement — sandbox-confirmed a renderer-only fade leaves the entity hittable while invisible for 3 ticks; "if a renderer-held value can change an outcome it is sim state" decides the case mechanically. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-waiting-city-wrong-sky-renderer-owned-existence-is-an-ownership-violation.md#E1
- `[compression][performance][architecture][measurement]` august-10#E24's batching-for-dictionary-scope IS buffering and so is bounded by august-10#E22's knee law — sandbox-confirmed with real zlib that per-message compression expands at 0.92x and batch-64 reaches 5.74x, but the curve knees at batch 8 and only reaches 6.17x at 128 while buffering 127 messages; size the batch at the measured knee. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-august-10-batching-for-ratio-is-buffering-for-latency.md#E1
- `[timezone][scheduling][correctness][process]` august-10#E25 holds two independent requirements and its loud statistic hides the quiet one — sandbox-confirmed a per-day rebuild fixes all 220 of 365 misplaced runs while two distinct UTC instants at the fall-back hour still derive the identical local key "day300 01:00", collapsing a local-keyed idempotency check and suppressing a real run. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-august-10-e25-holds-two-independent-requirements.md#E1
- `[determinism][framerate][gamedev][testing]` dog#E10's smoothing formula and recursion#E16's accumulator overshoot are independent frame-rate defects behind one symptom — sandbox-confirmed the dt-corrected smoother reaches 0.0000% spread across 30/60/144fps while the resetting tick accumulator stays 16.67% dependent, so fixing the visible half removes the tell and leaves the authoritative-state half. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-dog-recursion-two-frame-rate-defects-one-symptom.md#E1
- `[process][ci][tooling]` Exclude generated artifacts from any keyword gate that asks a human to confirm intent — a rendering of the corpus the gate draws keywords from trips every rule at once, and the only workable response is to acknowledge blindly, which is the exact reflex the gate exists to prevent → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E23
- `[process][harness][tooling]` A hook on the prompt path must fail open in its outermost wrapper, not inside the program — a hardcoded path that fails to start blocks every prompt including the one that would fix it, so search candidate paths and exit 0 unconditionally → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E24
- `[browser][webview][auth]` A provider's 400 disallowed_useragent in an embedded browser is an anti-phishing control, not a compatibility bug — the host app can read what is typed into its own WebView, so route to an auth method that permits embedding rather than escalating user-agent impersonation → projects/pappydapimp69__chat.md#E1
- `[process][retrieval][tooling]` Sharding a corpus for a reader that ingests whole files is a pessimisation until a router resolves the shard WITHOUT reading the shards — absence questions force reading every shard, so an unrouted split costs the same bytes plus N round trips (measured 1m20s vs 48s) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E25
- `[browser][webview][auth]` In an embedded WebView only a user-gesture, non-redirect navigation may be sent to the system browser — intercepting a redirect ejects multi-hop sign-in into a different cookie jar and it dies on a blank page, and no host allowlist can be complete enough to fix it → projects/pappydapimp69__chat.md#E2
- `[narrative-design][voice][oral-history][disfluency]` never make polished prose sound oral by damaging it afterward — construct remembering in front of a listener so every interruption reveals retrieval, repair, relationship, or resistance -> projects/local__b.md#E? → projects/local__b.md#E1
- `[narrative-design][causality][character][setup-payoff]` an encounter introduction is a causal runway, not a biography — every detail must pay off through placement, perception, action, stake, or testimony -> projects/local__b.md#E? → projects/local__b.md#E2
- `[rng][determinism][api-design]` The constant-roll-count rule binds a function's TOTAL consumption, not just its own visible draws: a CALLBACK parameter that touches the generator, invoked a data-dependent number of times, makes a scrupulously unconditional function variable-cost anyway, and every return value stays correct while resumed runs fork. Hoist such draws to the caller and pass a finished table instead of a callback — and sweep the DATA SHAPE as well as the seed when measuring a draw count, because holding the shape fixed is precisely what hides it. → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E1
- `[browser][webgl][gamedev]` A per-run WebGL context (or AudioContext, worker, socket) acquired at mount and never released accumulates silently, and browsers hard-cap live contexts and reclaim by force-losing the OLDEST — so the symptom is an earlier canvas going black with nothing thrown and nothing logged, appearing several runs after the cause. Release in the function that ACQUIRES, as its first statement, never in each caller: two mount paths where only one disposed is exactly how this survives for a project's whole life until a "play again" button turns three mounts a session into six. → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E2
- `[testing][process][tooling]` A KNOWN-RED check sitting mid-file in a `set -e` test runner silently deletes every check below it — here one accepted difficulty assertion had been switching off the entire browser tier (eleven Playwright suites) for as long as it had been red, and the loss was invisible because a suite that stops early looks exactly like a suite that ran and the expected failure message is exactly what a skimmer expects to see. Put any tolerated failure LAST, capture its status instead of letting `set -e` throw, and carry it to the exit. → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E3
- `[tooling][deploy][testing]` A checker that identifies its subject with a literal naming the PROJECT stops matching after a rename or a fork, and every check of the form "for each X found, assert P" is then vacuously satisfied — so the tool prints its success line over a graph it verified nothing in, and keeps doing so on every run. Derive the identifier from the document at run time, and make an EMPTY result set an explicit failure: a "found nothing" pass is the one outcome such a tool cannot distinguish from success on its own. → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E4
- `[ui][defaults][testing]` A default that is ALSO rendered as a pre-selected control (a `sel` class on one option, a `checked` radio, a highlighted preset) is declared twice — once in code, once in static markup — and changing the constant alone ships a first-launch-only lie: the menu names one value while the runtime uses another, with no error, invisible to anyone whose profile already stores a choice. Move the option ladder with the default, and assert the two copies against EACH OTHER on a profile with nothing stored, not each against a literal, so the guard survives the next retune. → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E17
- `[browser][state][deploy][save]` GitHub Pages (and any shared host domain) serves every project of one account from ONE ORIGIN — the project name is a path, and localStorage/IndexedDB/cookies are scoped to the origin, not the path — so two games deployed under one account SHARE a save slot and settings unless their keys are namespaced. With a schema-version guard in place the collision presents as "no save" to a player who had one, which reads as data loss rather than as a conflict; namespace storage keys per project from the first commit, and treat a fork as a new project for this even though its code says otherwise. → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E5
- `[save][state][worldgen]` A reserved sentinel value that means "this subject is special" is inert until some consumer BRANCHES on it — and a save path that reconstructs its subject from a key will happily hand the sentinel to the reconstructor and get back something valid, complete and wrong, with no error at any layer (an authored map's reserved seed fed to the procedural generator produced a real, walkable, different map with the saved positions pasted on). Grep for the branch that reads a sentinel before believing it is handled, and assert a restored subject IDENTICALLY rather than asserting that one came back, because coming back is what the defect does. → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E6
- `[testing][process][tooling]` A test that reads its subject's SOURCE and slices a fixed number of characters forward from an anchor is a guard with an expiry date: growing the subject pushes the content it asserts on out of the window, and it then fails blaming the subject rather than its own reach ("the parse broke", "a bound verb with no explanation" about text sitting right there). Bound source windows by a structural delimiter — the next declaration at that indentation, the closing tag — never by a character count. → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E7
- `[browser][animation][timing]` The timestamp `requestAnimationFrame` passes its callback is the frame's SCHEDULED time, not the current time, and in a throttled or backgrounded page it arrives behind the real clock (measured ~200ms persistently) — so a duration computed as `rafTimestamp - performance.now()-sampled-start` goes NEGATIVE and the effect never ends, with the loop running and nothing thrown. Read `performance.now()` inside the update for wall-clock durations; and never accumulate a CAPPED simulation delta for a UI effect, because the cap that protects the simulation stretches the effect. → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E8
- `[gamedev][state][testing]` In a decorated update loop, a base pause guard does not stop later wrapper timers; guard the final assembled entry point and test downstream clocks together. -> projects/pappydapimp69__od75.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__od75.md#E1
- `[gamedev][testing]` Prove procedural audio upgrades through deterministic production-graph renders, not scheduling state alone → projects/pappydapimp69__od75.md#E2
- `[tooling][process][observability][verification]` Cross-session Claude Code messaging works via a poke-only trigger bound with `persistent_session_id` (NOT `ListAgents`/`SendMessage`, which is container-local and correctly empty between cloud sessions) — but `fire_trigger` returns only an echo plus a `cse_`-prefixed fire-record id, never a delivery receipt, so every send is unacknowledged: carry `from:`/`sent:`/`reply:` in the payload and treat a reply that quotes observed fields as the only real ack. → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E26
- `[gamedev][coop][architecture][state]` Retrofit local co-op by having player two POSSESS an existing AI actor, not by spawning a new body — no balance rule keyed to actor count changes, and the AI is already the correct drop-out handler; but carry the new per-player identity through every payload that survives a world rebuild or the joined pad goes silently dead. → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E18
- `[process][prompt-engineering][verification]` Before answering "what does a newly-learned API/model change mean for us," grep the live codebase AND the recorded canon for actual usage — a verified "nothing's affected" is as valuable as verified breakage, and unchecked assertions of risk erode trust. → projects/pappydapimp69__collective.md#E16
- `[gamedev][gamedesign][ui][testing]` A hallucination or tell that is STABLE is not the same as one that is INERT: four MIRAGE hallucination kinds each set one value at onset and were each solved by the player in seconds (notice the compass is 90 degrees out, subtract 90) — tie each to a verb the player performs and keep the onset value as the opening position; and size the tell against THE RESOLUTION OF THE WIDGET THAT SHOWS IT rather than the internal quantity, since a compass drift tuned in radians was invisible against an eight-letter HUD (only 13% of 20s episodes produced a visible change until the threshold became one full compass point), and gating its release on the feet being still fired it mid-look-around, where 4.1 releases/min yielded 1.9 witnessable ones. → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E19
