- `[gamedev][gamedesign][testing][ux]` A feature whose point is to be PERCEIVED needs a test for its OBSERVED rate, not just its mechanism: MIRAGE's monster-flicker had five passing unit tests that forced the roll and hand-placed the actor, and shipped at one visible occurrence per ~110s of a state whose median episode is 7s, so the player saw none — measure the trigger window's real length and each gate's open fraction (episode ~7s x companion-in-range ~40% x companion-on-screen ~20% = never), tune to that product, and assert the observed rate from BOTH sides, a floor against "never fires" and a ceiling against a strobe. → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E20
- `[testing][process][ci]` A capture that asserts nothing (review screenshot, trace, profile dump) must never be able to fail a suite — attempt it, note the skip, carry on, since every real assertion around it still runs; and before blaming recent commits for a slow capture, read the renderer's own draw-call and triangle counters and the machine load first, which turned a 177s screenshot timeout from "which change made rendering slow" into "the compositor cannot do this here" (identical 21 calls / 21546 tris) and, on an earlier occurrence, into "a parallel agent has the box at load 13.7". → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E21
- `[gamedev][balance][gamedesign][process]` A requested "calm opening" window sized without measuring actual run length can delete the systems behind it — a flat 300s no-drain window against a ~200s basin made every difficulty tier return byte-identical balance rows (a multiplier zeroed, not mistuned) and cut companion hallucination episodes 0.88 to 0.21/run, causing the same player's other bug report; fix with a dead-calm window plus a linear ramp, prove causation with a zero-window control run rather than assuming, and assert tier ordering so the collapse cannot recur silently. → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E22
- `[gamedev][state][save][determinism][testing]` In a seeded-deterministic sim, anything that gates WHEN an rng draw fires is save state — throttle countdowns for sighting sweeps, ambient chatter, repathing and cached paths look purely cosmetic but re-phase every later draw when restored to zero, producing a resume with byte-identical positions and meters and a silently forked stream that only surfaces hours later; catch it by ticking the original AND the restored sim forward and comparing a fingerprint that includes the rng word, plus a negative control with a corrupted word that must diverge, since round-trip field equality can only check the fields you already thought to save. → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E23
- `[testing][gamedev][process][debugging]` A screenshot of a live simulation is not a snapshot — capture takes seconds of wall time while rAF keeps running, so a cleared flag can re-fire and turn the control frame into the experimental one; freeze the MECHANISM (stub its rng gate off, push its expiry far out so it takes the existing mid-effect hold path) rather than the world, since the game's own pause draws its menu over the subject, and pay any warm-up (slot memory, seeded anchors) in sim time before triggering or the probe reports a working feature as broken. → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E24
- `[process][debugging][gamedev][ui]` Take a user's SENSORY words literally before mapping them onto a same-named system — "the world feels distorted" was read as the game's hallucination-distortion feature and cost three rounds of real-but-irrelevant fixes, when it meant the IMAGE: a vertical-fov camera set to 72 rendering 105 degrees horizontal at 16:9 (fisheye, bows straight lines, present in every frame, invisible to tests because a wrong-but-consistent projection renders perfectly); also treat a player's mislocated CAUSE ("the movement controls") as a clue rather than an error, since a wide lens makes lateral motion swing and reads as locomotion, and assert the shipping invariant (horizontal FOV constant across aspect) because nothing throws when it is wrong. → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E25
- `[testing][gamedesign][verification][ai-agents]` A bot that reads ground truth cannot price a mechanic whose whole cost is an information gap — its win rate is a COMPLETABILITY oracle only, and tuning difficulty against it sets the rate for a player who cannot be deceived (measured: it justified a 1.05 to 1.9 drain increase that a percept-driven bot proved was never needed, scoring 67%/25% at the original rate); build a handicapped agent that consumes the same derived view the human does, then check its first green twice, that it survived long enough to MEET the mechanism (the omniscient bot finished in 96s, inside a 90s grace window, and hallucinated zero times) and that it is not branching on the state flag that NAMES its own handicap (`player.hallucinating` made it seek relief instantly and never walk into a single lie). → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E26
- `[testing][process][verification]` An assertion that restates the implementation cannot fail and reading a green suite will never tell you which ones those are: `ap === GUARD_AP - CAPTURE_COST` holds for every value of the constant including zero, and `beliefMoved || positionMoved` stayed green across 120 games where belief moved 30/30 turns and the prisoner moved 4/30 because two gates severed belief from routing — assert the OUTER effect and print the inner one separately, pin constants with a literal plus the property that makes them matter, and make a harness that cannot set itself up fail in its own voice with a distinct exit code, since a setup failure dressed as a feature failure caused three misdiagnoses and was scored as "mutation caught" by the audit meant to check all this. → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E41
- `[gamedev][input][camera][gamedesign][testing]` A camera-relative control fix was invisible to a full suite because at the DEFAULT camera pose the correct mapping and the hardcoded up=North one are the same function — whenever a transform has an identity case, that is where all fixtures live, so test deliberately off it, and assert the chosen cardinal is the argmax rather than merely sign-correct, since at a diagonal camera no cardinal is dominantly "up"; separately, when turning a terminal state into a playable one, write down the COMPOUNDED odds, because a 0.3 per-turn escape over a 3-turn window is a 66% escape and it lifted AI-vs-AI escape 66/52/29% to 81/61/40% before anyone noticed, and adding a state to an entity (custody) silently reinterprets every predicate that read `alive` as "can act". → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E42
- `[gamedev][balance][measurement][architecture]` When a tuning lever measures inert no matter how well it is built, attribute OUTCOMES before iterating on the lever — five successive fixes to Opticon's gaze-based caution all measured ~3pt because the gaze decided only 17% of games while dispatched guards made 40/75/83% of captures; and when demoting the mechanic that had taken over, a resource bar (action points, with a burst window and a per-capture cost) preserved the threat's shape, counterplay and difficulty ordering where a nerf dial would only have made it uniformly worse, and dimming the bar on screen turned hidden variance into something the opponent can play around. → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E43
- `[testing][process][architecture]` The same suite scored 1 survivor/12 on code written that week and 5/16 on the code written earliest — coverage tracks RECENCY, not importance, so aim a mutation audit at the OLDEST code; and three separate ways the exercise lied: assertions written against the constant they check (`ap === MAX - COST` holds for any COST including zero), a harness whose uncaught timeout crashed and got scored as "mutation caught", and a mutation silently skipped on a non-matching search string that read like a caught one — fix with literals plus a property and distinct exit codes for setup-failure vs honest-red, then re-run each mutation to prove the new assertion fails; also, not every survivor is a hole, one was dead code whose hazard a later change had removed and pinning it with a test would only have frozen it. → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E44
- `[gamedev][architecture][testing][ux]` Two defects that 1854 green assertions could not see: scaling one prisoner to three silently flipped `some(escaped)` from "you escaped" to "anybody escaped" — any predicate quantifying over a newly-pluralised actor changes meaning at N>1 while staying type-correct, and the rules layer had no handle for "which one is the player" at all, since that lived only in the UI — and automatic item pickup left players carrying icons whose rules lived only in a collapsed menu panel, fixed by making the explanatory copy a REQUIRED FIELD of the item definition so a schema test fails a new item that ships without it. → projects/pappydapimp69__opticon.md#E45
- `[gamedev][ux][progression]` Clear copy explains an upgrade; it does not make it feel important—spend repeated interruption budget in proportion to immediate gameplay consequence, and consolidate or defer lower-impact progression until its effect is visibly earned. → projects/pappydapimp69__od75.md#E3
- `[narrative][procedural-generation][ux][state]` Adaptive story wizards should secure the causal core (who acts, what they want, what blocks them, what is at stake, what choice follows) and then ask only for the structural slots the earlier answers did not already supply, instead of marching through a fixed metadata checklist — and because the route is now a function of the answers, recompute it after every committed answer and clear all downstream answers and derived labels when an upstream one changes, or a contracted route leaves stale setting and antagonist values invisibly in state. → projects/pappydapimp69__couchbound-story-engine.md#E1
- `[procedural-generation][coop][narrative][testing]` When blank player answers are auto-filled from pools, any contrast the later synthesis template asserts ("both", "different", "opposite", "combined") must be enforced at fill time — independent draws gave two players the identical risky action while the climax claimed they combined different plans, grammatically valid and structurally false; track authored vs auto-filled and apply distinctness only to the generated side, so deliberate player agreement still survives. → projects/pappydapimp69__couchbound-story-engine.md#E2
- `[communication][ux][collaboration][defaults]` Default agent replies to short, direct, plainly-worded conversation and treat build/edit/search/explain as explicit scope changes rather than inferred permission — an accurate mini-essay in answer to a narrow question reads as inattentive, and its accuracy is what makes the defect hard to see; expand only on request, for safety or accuracy, or where the task genuinely cannot be done otherwise, since a restrained default is easy to broaden and an overlong first reply cannot be unread. → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E27
- `[process][testing][architecture][deployment]` Blueprint intended and protected behavior and preflight the current implementation — authoritative state, competing overrides, duplicate listeners, invariants, regression risks and machine-checkable seams — before editing a working system, because a locally plausible patch can pass syntax checks and still be overridden in the assembled product (a CSS rule beat the semantic `hidden` state; generated fallbacks duplicated values the later prose assumed differed); verify the assembled artifact rather than the edited source, and keep what only a person can judge separate from what the automation actually proved. → projects/pappydapimp69__couchbound-story-engine.md#E3
- `[process][gamedev][deployment][testing][browser]` Local tests, assembled-bundle checks, deployment success and the public build are four distinct states, and a build closes only on the last one — B58 passed source and assembly checks and its workflow reported success while GitHub Pages was still serving B57, so fetch the public URL and require the expected build stamp and versioned cache key; likewise poll for timing and reread live geometry before browser interaction instead of reusing coordinates that reflow or autonomous movement has already invalidated. → projects/pappydapimp69__od75.md#E4
- `[process][method]` A field recording what WENT wrong cannot index what you are ABOUT to do — build a precondition index from `What:` (44.5%) and `Rule of thumb:` (41.9%), not `Where/why it failed:` (19.4%, 0 of 12 wins). → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E27
- `[state][algorithm]` A module-level scratch buffer hoisted out of a hot loop silently caps that function's domain — size it from the argument and grow on demand, never from the constant that was true when you wrote it. → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E28
- `[architecture][state]` A per-instance field nothing READS is a second copy of a constant, not plumbing — grep for readers before sizing work off "it is already there"; zero readers means its correctness is no evidence. → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E29
- `[testing][gamedev]` When a world constant turns tests red, ask what each was passing BECAUSE of — a rim, a default facing, a spawn distance is a silent precondition, and one assertion here was measuring the map boundary rather than the party. → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E30
- `[state][testing]` A serialiser whose output is always JSON-encoded has an untested contract — encoding hides live references; assert that mutating the source after packing does not move the payload. → projects/pappydapimp69__mirage.md#E31
- `[tooling][debugging][method]` In a polyglot single-file document a find-and-replace anchor is unique only within its own language region — a block inserted into the wrong one is silent, since the wrong parser discards it and an extracted-script syntax check still passes → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E28
- `[ui][gamedev][canvas][debugging]` Control chrome floating over a canvas that is also the input surface steals clicks aimed at the world and presents as a state-machine bug — make the regions disjoint by constraining the world's geometry, not by hit-testing → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E29
- `[simulation][state][determinism]` A pure logistic term is pinned at x=1 whatever the sign of its rate — a saturated population can never decline; add a LINEAR decay term or the ceiling is absorbing → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E30
- `[gamedev][rendering][state]` A pooled entity that lerps alive→dead has no state for "never existed", so an empty world renders as a world full of corpses — add a sticky spawned flag, not a fudged fade start → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E31
- `[gamedesign][simulation][determinism]` A player-paced scene that advances a live sim needs a simulated-time BUDGET, not just a rate — rate alone makes the ending a function of how long the viewer reads → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E32
- `[state][ui][gamedev]` Unlocking an input gate and scheduling its auto-advance as two statements lets a user click race the timer into a silent DOUBLE advance that skips a whole scene — unlock inside the timeout callback → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E33
- `[rendering][canvas][mobile]` A projection/camera constant derived from viewport HEIGHT alone is a landscape-only assumption — in portrait H is the long axis and the framing over-zooms; take min(W,H) and check a real portrait viewport → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E34
- `[ui][architecture][method]` Porting a standalone piece into a longer work brings chrome whose only job was to say "this is its own work" — title, transport, progress, reading fallback — and the host already has all of it; keep the renderer, delete the furniture → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E35
- `[browser][animation][debugging]` A rAF loop whose re-schedule is the last statement dies permanently on the first throw and reads as frozen CONTENT, not a crash — re-schedule first, and animate over a snapshot rather than live shared state → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E36
- `[tooling][process][deployment][verification]` In a remote container `$HOME` (`/root`) is not the parent of the repo checkouts (`/home/user`), so a `~`-anchored cache built by inferring the root from the working directory lands where the tool never looks — and an installer that prints the symlink it wrote while resolving its data root separately reports success with the halves disagreeing; echo `$HOME` before creating any `~`-anchored store and verify with the tool's status command, not its install message. → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E1
- `[simulation][determinism][method]` A counterfactual replay must spend the simulated time the real run ACTUALLY consumed, not its full budget — anything player-paced under-spends, so a schedule replay races ahead and the comparison measures pacing rather than decisions → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E37
- `[simulation][testing][method]` A harness that only checks a simulation's ENDING is blind to the whole middle — rebalance the model and every narrated beat in between can go wrong while every outcome still looks sensible; assert on the trajectory at the beats that make claims → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E38
- `[state][save]` An `if (!isPlayer)` block in a party game's save path attracts fields that are not companion-only — anything the hallucination system touches is shared, and a round-trip test that checks a companion cannot see the bug. → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E9
- `[gamedesign][ui]` A hidden-state game may lie about the state of a visible thing but must never conjure the thing — an affordance that appears only while deceived is a readout; assert the UI is IDENTICAL across the hidden flip. → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E10
- `[security][state][tooling]` A control resource read through `path.read_text() if path.exists() else ""` fails OPEN — ZaneGPT's defensive protocols, hacker-flag rules and post-turn-5 behavior all resolved to missing paths and the runtime still announced the control order to the model with their text empty; track absence explicitly and surface it in the model's own context block, because a default value for a control resource is a silent grant of permission → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E2
- `[tooling][process][verification]` A summary count taken as `len(notes)` over a log appended on both the success and failure paths inverts into a success report — `brain update` announced "4 node(s) refreshed" with exit 0 on a cache where all four nodes were missing; count successes in their own variable and make total failure exit non-zero, since the non-verbose line is the only one most callers read → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E39
- `[procgen][gamedesign][algorithm]` A binary reachability guarantee has no stopping rule for any pass that makes ground expensive — re-derive it as a distance budget reported beside the unchanged boolean, and make unreachable fail the budget as infinite distance → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E11
- `[tooling][process][testing]` A status mined from prose by severity-ordered substring scan means a resolution that narrates its own transition ("open -> resolved") re-asserts the old state and reports as open forever — fix the prose, and measure a parser change's reclassification count before preferring it → projects/pappydapimp69__tension.md#E1
- `[tooling][documentation][process][architecture]` A tool that generates docs telling readers how to invoke it must build every path from its own resolved config, not from its usual default — `brain link` records the real cache in `config.json` yet injects a static `$HOME/.brain/...` fallback, so any project linked with `--cache`/`BRAIN_CACHE` gets a pointer to a nonexistent path, and it only fails on the fallback branch, where nothing is left to mask it. → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E3
- `[testing][gamedesign][verification][method]` A test of ONE term of a conjunctive rule must pin every other term TRUE, or the conjunction supplies the pass and the test stays green for every value of the parameter it protects — a band guarding `AMBIENT_CAP < HEAT_GATE` was exercised in the dark and would have passed at 10x the gate, looking like a real end-to-end property test the whole time; mutate the constant and require a NAMED test to go red, and treat a mutation the harness could not apply as an error, never a catch. → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E4
- `[testing][gamedev][procgen][method]` A test that places an agent at a computed point must assert the point is legal, or it measures the agent's inability to act and calls it behaviour — and when a behavioural pass rate tracks a world-generation knob, suspect the harness's coupling to that knob before concluding the world changed the behaviour → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E12
- `[procgen][gamedesign][balance]` Turning a density knob up produced fewer obstacles, because filled ground is the raw material the placement pass consumes — sweep the downstream passes; and when a terrain knob appears to regress an unrelated NPC test, check the harness's coupling to that knob first, because a blind agent placement fails at exactly the rate the knob moves → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E13
- `[tooling][process][testing]` A validator that writes its rejection reasons back into the rejected file using the same bullet syntax it parses will read its own complaint as the field on the next pass — the rejection latches and no author edit can clear it; annotate in a grammar the parser ignores → projects/pappydapimp69__memory.md#E9
- `[process][tooling][cli][distribution]` A paste-and-go installer is a function of repository VISIBILITY, not installer design — Brain's `curl -fsSL <raw-url>/install.sh | sh` placeholder reads as an unfinished detail but is unfillable, because the repo is private and the anonymous raw fetch 404s under every casing (extending collective#E1, only a complete raw URL returns 200 to an anonymous client); probe the first URL as a stranger before designing the flow, treat a bootstrap placeholder as evidence about visibility rather than a missing string, and either publish the one bootstrap artifact or delete the line instead of advertising a one-liner that cannot exist. → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E40
- `[distribution][knowledge-management][retrieval]` Exporting canon to a reader that cannot open files is not a choice between a dead-pointer index and a 9.8x self-contained dump — measured over 746 entries, the index line already carries mean 0.53 recall of its own entry's rule of thumb and only 4.4% fall below 0.2, so it is a carrier rather than a summary; index-line LENGTH does not predict the loss (shortest quartile 0.556 vs second 0.438, non-monotonic), so select the tail by measured per-entry retention and ship full text for just the worst 5% at 15% of the full size — and rotate the pairings as a negative control before trusting any metric that decides a design. → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E41
- `[simulation][state][determinism]` Rating a process on a resource that correlates with it (decay on dissolved oxygen) leaves a convenience floor that keeps the process running in exactly the terminal state the design says is impossible — gate on the AGENT that performs the work, and the stopped state falls out of the terms instead of needing a special case → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E42
- `[simulation][state][gamedesign]` Growth and mortality sharing one signed number means a population at equilibrium produces ZERO deaths downstream — the trajectories look right while every consumer of death (detritus, decomposers, body counts) starves; split gross death from net change and feed the downstream pool from the gross figure → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E43
- `[cli][tooling]` try/except around a subprocess catches failure but never BLOCKING — a convenience `git pull` with prompts enabled hangs forever on a TTY while every non-interactive test passes, so disable terminal prompts and set a timeout on any decorative step → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E44
- `[cli][tooling]` A flag meaning "do nothing" (`--help`) must be parsed ABOVE the handler that acts — a subcommand that tolerantly ignores unknown tokens silently turns `--help` into consent, and `brain link --help` performed a real link → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E45
- `[tooling][agent]` Two install paths for one capability have two different load clocks — test each rather than generalising from one, and treat an agent's confident account of its OWN runtime as the least-checked claim in the room → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E5
- `[gamedev][gamedesign][testing][feedback]` A comment requiring places be DISTINGUISHABLE was discharged by a test asserting they were far APART — a necessary-but-insufficient proxy reads as full coverage forever, and anything the player must tell apart needs its assertion in the tier that can see what is drawn → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E14
- `[gamedev][rendering][process]` A filmic tone curve's toe darkens shadows to buy highlight headroom, so ACES flattens a scene that is entirely dark — check whether missing range is in the curve or in content authored near-black, at roughness 1, under smooth normals → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E15
- `[tooling][process][git]` A keyword gate over prose (brain's open-tension pre-commit check) fires on ordinary English, not terms of art — a game whose cast includes a "Builder" tripped a tension about the graph builder on 6 generic nouns; score matches by term RARITY (tf-idf / corpus stop-list) rather than raw overlap, because raising the threshold just rewards longer diffs, and a gate that fires on unrelated work trains a reflex BRAIN_ACK that is indistinguishable from no gate → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E46
- `[testing][gamedesign][simulation]` A bot used to judge whether a design's content is reachable is code under test: an argmax "return to the most-visited" focus rule is a ratchet that locks onto the first entity met, and it reported 0% completion of the headline chain where the true figure was 12% (secondary chain 16% vs 62%, same seeds, same content) — treat an argmax in a behavioural model as a defect on sight, and distrust an instrument that returns exactly the failure it was built to detect → projects/pappydapimp69__dark-goblin.md#E1
- `[tooling][process][harness]` `pkill -f <pattern>` matches the full command line of the shell invoking it, because the pattern is an argument inside that line — it kills itself, exits 128+n, and every `&&` step after it silently never runs; kill by port or saved PID instead, and never chain real work behind a `pkill -f` → projects/pappydapimp69__dark-goblin.md#E2
- `[testing][architecture][gamedev][harness]` A pure core does not remove bugs, it relocates them to the core-to-shell seam where unit tests are blind by construction — 91 green engine tests missed all three bugs a single scripted browser playthrough found (characters placed at a location string the renderer's private list lacked, so they were in play and drawn nowhere; an "A replaces B" rule written as two guards, leaving the before-case open; an overlay you could read through) — keep a screenshotting smoke driver over the BUILT artifact from the first UI commit, exit non-zero on console errors, and push each finding down into a content validator → projects/pappydapimp69__dark-goblin.md#E3
- `[testing][harness][gamedev][determinism]` Second live instance of dog#E22 in a different engine: Phaser clamps frame delta to 50ms, so a slow headless renderer (~150ms frames) runs every `delayedCall`/`addEvent` at a THIRD of real time while `Clock.now` still reads wall-clock correctly — timed text looked dead though the game was fine; diagnose by comparing a pending timer's `elapsed` against `now` via a `globalThis` handle to the live scene, and never sleep a fixed duration in a harness → projects/pappydapimp69__dark-goblin.md#E4
- `[testing][harness][process][gamedev]` Three instruments broke in one build for the same reason — a harness encodes a snapshot while the system keeps moving: an argmax in a behavioural bot (ratchet, reported 0% where truth was 12%), fixed waits and coordinates in a browser driver (clamped frame delta ran time at a third speed; a randomly dealt cast moved every target), and a top-level-only tree walk (nesting for an animation hid the controls) — audit harnesses for constants, prefer observation over assumption, and distrust a suspiciously clean result that confirms exactly what the instrument was built to detect → projects/pappydapimp69__dark-goblin.md#E5
- `[phaser][gamedev][browser][rendering]` Phaser's `load.svg` calls `atob` on a data URI, so a percent-encoded SVG kills the game before its first scene with a blank canvas and an error naming a builtin you never called — skip the loader entirely and rasterise via `Image` + canvas into `textures.addCanvas`, which is encoding-agnostic and lets you pick the raster size (which vector art with heavy outlines needs) → projects/pappydapimp69__dark-goblin.md#E6
- `[gamedev][visualdesign][testing][content]` A composed-art system needs a CONTACT SHEET rendering the whole generated population, emitted by the same tool that emits the assets — nine of forty-two figures had no face (head coverings drawn as one shape over the skull painted out the eyes) while thirteen per-item tests passed, because well-formed is not the same question as right; also split every occluder into behind/front around the identifying feature rather than trusting draw order → projects/pappydapimp69__dark-goblin.md#E7
- `[hitbox][gamedev][physics][determinism]` A hitbox is a volume ANDed with an active time window, so sandbox-collision#E1's tunneling fix does not transfer: sub-stepping POSITION while reading the active gate once per frame flips the error's sign, turning missed hits into phantom hits that land after the attack ended (8/16 probe cases false-positive) — and no fixed sub-step count is correct, because what n must resolve is the window INTERSECT contact interval (15/15 vs 8/15 for the window-length rule; a 1.0-tick window at v=3 needs n>=1003), which grazing shrinks without bound; solve that intersection analytically instead. → projects/local__sandbox-hitbox-window.md#E1
- `[hitbox][gamedev][state][events]` sandbox-collision#E2's producer-side enter-transition dedup does not give "one hit per swing": its lifetime is the CONTACT, so a gap in the active window re-enters and fires twice, while a set scoped to a per-frame-respawned hitbox entity expires with the frame and fires 6 times on one sustained contact — and each failure makes the other look fixed; key the dedup set on the swing id, whose lifetime actually matches the invariant. → projects/local__sandbox-hitbox-window.md#E2
- `[gamedev][input][mobile][phaser][process]` "Tap does nothing" on a real phone was THREE faults in series — chrome-collapsed viewport units, a cached `displayScale` that only `refresh()` recomputes (and refresh per-tap drops WebGL textures), and Phaser's Container hit test refusing a point provably inside its own hit area — so the symptom stayed identical after each necessary fix; when a control is confirmed under the pointer and the framework still says no, take the hit test into your own code (containment is four comparisons and does not vary by device), and never read an unchanged symptom as evidence a fix did nothing → projects/pappydapimp69__dark-goblin.md#E8
- `[testing][verification][process]` make each negative control a broken INPUT fed to the guard inside the test file, so it re-runs every suite run — and read what each control failed ON, since failing for the wrong reason looks exactly like a live guard → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E16
- `[testing][rendering][browser][verification]` a WebGL readback in a test judges the frame the page's LOOP last composed, not the state just set — set state, await two rAFs, then render+readPixels in one task; and never await frames inside a wall-clock-timed sequence → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E17
- `[ui][input][tutorial][testing]` an instruction that says "press" must name the button in the player's CURRENT scheme and what it acts on — resolve {key} tokens at display time, repaint on scheme change, test the names against the real bindings, and grep CSS `content:` for hard-coded key glyphs → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E18
- `[process][git][agents]` Worktree sub-agents branch from the last COMMITTED tree, not your uncommitted scaffold, and `git add -A` stages their dirs as embedded gitlinks — commit+push the scaffold and exclude `.claude/worktrees/` before fanning out → projects/pappydapimp69__od76.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][balance][method]` A playtester's "after wave 3" meant the HUD's per-stage wave counter, not the run-global wave — measured pressure was flat for waves 1-9 and stepped at stage 4; translate player numbers through the HUD and measure per-step before tuning → projects/pappydapimp69__od76.md#E2
- `[process][git][agents]` Worktree sub-agents start on the repo's DEFAULT branch, not your pushed feature branch — make their first step `git fetch origin <branch> && git checkout --detach FETCH_HEAD`, and merge their shared-test diffs with `git apply --3way` → projects/pappydapimp69__od76.md#E3
- `[process][tooling][ci][testing]` A quarantine hides every defect except the one that triggered it — `validate.py` skips held `incoming/` files (correctly, per memory#E5, which records the 223-red-run jam caused by NOT skipping them), so four proposals held on schema grounds had never once been measured against the filename rule and went red on the first push after their holds were cleared; a hold block records the first gate that refused, not a complete diagnosis, so treat a lifted hold as an item that has never been checked at all and expect a second, older failure. → projects/pappydapimp69__memory.md#E10
- `[songwriting][rhyme][testing]` Implementing a stored kernel, verify your implementation can DIFFER from what it replaces — a "two-pass" rhyme-scheme labeller returned the identical scheme as one pass on every fixture including one built to separate them, because pass two still merged near-rhymes into the perfect group and so only reordered identical merges; the kernel's mechanism is a DISTINCT letter, not an earlier one (truck/mud/blood/flood goes AAAA under merge-in, BAAA under the rule), and a test over an implementation that cannot differ cannot fail. → projects/pappydapimp69__suno-builder.md#E1
- `[songwriting][rhyme][determinism]` A rhyme checker that reads pronunciation[0] is silently choosing a MEANING — `read` is R EH1 D and R IY1 D, and only the second shares a vowel with `cheap`, so a heteronym's rhyme verdict depends on dictionary ordering with no test failing; CMUdict has 8,447 multi-pronunciation words and 2,343 where the last vowel differs, and first-entry versus all-entries implementations disagreed on 124 pairs from 400 words, so take the best classification over every pronunciation and report ambiguity rather than picking a sense for the writer. → projects/pappydapimp69__suno-builder.md#E2
- `[process][verification][testing]` CORRECTS suno-builder#E1 — I built my own labeller from a kernel's description and tested it against itself, while the tool the kernel was mined from sat in `ideas/reference/` with a headless harness I never ran; on E1's own fixture the real builder returns AABB, not the AAAA/BAAA E1 claims, and it folds near-rhymes INTO the uppercase group, so E1's mechanism claim is backwards — a reimplementation tested against your own reading of a kernel cannot disagree with you, so check whether the artifact already exists before building it. → projects/pappydapimp69__suno-builder.md#E3
- `[songwriting][rhyme][testing][tooling]` The suno-builder judges rhyme from SPELLING, so mud/blood, one/sun, said/bed, heart/part and through/blue are all "no rhyme" — 3 of 15 common pairs recognised, 62.9% recall over 1,500 phonetically perfect pairs — and it shows in the output, where truck/mud/blood/flood letters as AABB though all four are one perfect rhyme in speech; the 89-test suite stays green because its fixtures are dove/love and night/light, pairs whose spelling and sound agree, so when a tool judges a property of SPEECH from WRITTEN input, choose fixtures where the two representations DISAGREE. → projects/pappydapimp69__suno-builder.md#E4
- `[lyrical-composition][state][algorithm]` The dedup-key-lifetime kernel (sandbox-hitbox-window/E2, previously single-source from a physics/timing domain) transfers unchanged to lyrical composition, a domain sharing no code with it: an exact-text hook dedup undercounts an evolving hook (3 true instances, 2 distinct strings) and collides on an alternating two-variant chorus (5 true, 2 distinct), while an ambiguous rhyme-group match forks to different groups under two equally-reasonable tie-break defaults — same two-sided failure shape, zero shared implementation, second independent source for the kernel. → projects/local__sandbox-lyrical-composition.md#E1
- `[lyrical-composition][rhyme][testing]` A fixed slant-rhyme edit-distance threshold (K=2) accepts a short pair that's 67% different in phonemes while rejecting a long pair that's only 43% different — the pair judged more similar by proportion is the one a fixed absolute threshold refuses; scale the threshold by word length instead. → projects/local__sandbox-lyrical-composition.md#E2
- `[lyrical-composition][rhyme][tension]` T1 probe (not a resolution): across 105 consonant-coda pairs, the manner-based rhyme-type chart and the strict voiced/unvoiced-pair chart disagree on 27 (26%) — not a rare edge case — and the ledger's own cited mud/truck (D/K) and read/cheap (D/P) pairs are confirmed structurally identical under both charts, exactly as T1 states; the fork stays open pending the primary source. → projects/local__sandbox-lyrical-composition.md#E3
- `[lyrical-composition][testing][state]` A scanner's per-token logic can be correct while its SCOPE is wrong: a POV checker scanning raw tokens flags a false shift from reported speech (quote-aware scanning removes it but correctly leaves a genuine unquoted narrator-voice flag standing) and an end-rhyme-only scanner misses 2 of 3 true rhymes placed internally rather than at line-end — check what the scanner looks at before trusting what it concludes. → projects/local__sandbox-lyrical-composition.md#E4
- `[lyrical-composition][meter][rhyme][testing]` Four independent sandboxes confirm one shape: a coarse boolean/count proxy for a structural match (syllable count for stress pattern; last-syllable for stressed-tail rhyme; boolean for trailing-match depth; undifferentiated density for rhyme-type quality) agrees with the full check only where they coincide, and silently conflates cases that differ exactly on the discarded dimension (order, stress position, depth, type) — restore the discarded dimension rather than tuning the proxy. → projects/local__sandbox-lyrical-composition.md#E5
- `[lyrical-composition][rhyme][testing]` A spelling-suffix rhyme matcher run on through/rough/though/cough (all spelled "-ough") flags all 6 pairs as rhyming; phonetically none of the four shares a vowel — a 100% false-positive rate, not a tail case — so spelling-suffix matching needs a phonetic-transcription layer before it can be trusted as a rhyme signal at all. → projects/local__sandbox-lyrical-composition.md#E6
- `[lyrical-composition][rhyme-tooling][algorithm]` A rhyme-scheme letter-assigner's "first match found" search has an implicit, unbounded scope that silently conflates what should stay separate: sandbox-confirms the existing two-pass-assignment kernel (single-pass vowel-only matching collapses a perfect pair and an assonance-only line into one letter, AAA; two-pass keeps them apart, AAB) and finds a second scope bug — an assigner with no notion of song SECTION links a chorus line to an unrelated verse line sharing its rhyme_class by coincidence (ABABACAC vs the intended ABABCDCD). → projects/local__sandbox-lyrical-composition.md#E7
- `[lyrical-composition][rhyme][testing]` Operationalized "AABB stops, ABBA stays open" as closure distance (lines between a rhyme and its most recent same-letter partner): confirms AABB caps at 1, ABBA reaches 3 — but the one case that separates closure distance from the more obvious "repeat count" proxy is AAAA, which despite maximum letter repetition still caps at 1 (each A closes on the previous A, not the first), so the two axes are independent and the aesthetic claim's structural correlate must be checked past its own cited examples. → projects/local__sandbox-lyrical-composition.md#E8
- `[lyrical-composition][rhyme][testing]` A phoneme-only rhyme detector says MATCH for both night/knight (rich rhyme, different words) and night/night (repetition, same word) — craft treats these oppositely, and telling them apart needs an explicit word-identity check the phonetic comparison alone can't provide. → projects/local__sandbox-lyrical-composition.md#E9
- `[tooling][process][testing]` The memory steward's `NEG_PROV_RE` holds a Provenance field opening with a genuine "Verified first-hand" claim if the SAME field later, honestly, hedges a separate narrower point using a negation word near a verification-marker word — the 15-character proximity window has no anchor to which marker occurrence the negation actually attaches to, so writing a MORE careful, honest hedge makes an entry MORE likely to be wrongly held (5 of 9 real proposals this session, all genuinely first-hand-verified, tripped this on first submission — including, recursively, an earlier draft of this very entry). → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E47
- `[testing][architecture][audio][method]` A single-cursor look-ahead scheduler is a capture layer, so its usual endpoint check "every event fired exactly once" is an aggregate over that layer and passes at 20/20 while one consumer is audible 0/5 — any filter applied after the cursor advances still counts as a fire, so assert an audible/authored ratio per consumer kind and prefer filtering at enqueue → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E48
- `[testing][verification][method][simulation]` A negative control read at the OUTCOME returns "guard not load-bearing" in two indistinguishable regimes — the guard never fired, or it fired and a downstream clamp reconverged the number — sandbox-confirmed at binding rate 0.083 with absorbed damage identical at 100/100 while first-death moved from step 3 to step 8, so report the binding rate to rule out the first and diff the trajectory to rule out the second → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E49
- `[compression][performance][architecture][measurement]` Re-verified batching-for-ratio IS buffering (each doubling 2x wait, zlib gain/doubling decays 1.54->1.12) but the knee is corpus+codec-dependent — batch 8 elsewhere, ~128 here, bz2/lzma later still; measure gain-per-doubling per stream and prefer a sync-flushed streaming dictionary (6.45x at zero wait = zlib batch ~16-32) → projects/local__sandbox-reverify-batch-compression-knee.md#E1
- `[tooling][testing][ux]` A label reading "syllables / line <= 9" while the code tests `> sylBudget + 3` is a hidden second setting — the first warning lands at 13, and it compounds with a syllable counter that under-counts 2.7:1 (476 to 177 over 4,000 CMUdict words), so two individually defensible adjustments stack because nobody chose them together; show the threshold you actually test, and look for a second bias pointing the same way before calling slack safe. → projects/pappydapimp69__suno-builder.md#E5
- `[tooling][ux][state]` `readShareCode` returns the same `null` for a truncated code, a one-character flip, an empty string and plain garbage, so the most likely real failure — a share link clipped in transit, which is recoverable — is indistinguishable from one that was never a code, and the writer is told nothing; keep the failure reason where you detect it, and give a versioned blob a "newer than I understand" path that reads differently from "not mine", since a future `v: 2` currently fails exactly like garbage. → projects/pappydapimp69__suno-builder.md#E6
- `[process][method][verification]` A character-count substance check is an empty-field guard, not a quality gate — measured against real corpus idf over 189 canon files its verdict is uncorrelated with density, with 31 chars of boilerplate passing at 0.42 exactly as a real canon field passes at 1.03, so name it for what it catches and score mean corpus-idf per token if substance actually matters → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E50
- `[lyrics][state][algorithm]` The dedup-key-lifetime kernel confirms a third time, in a third mechanism: a repeat-marker expander ("[Chorus] x2") that mechanically duplicates a section's first occurrence has no way to know a separately-authored, evolved variant right after it ("[Chorus - final]") was meant to REPLACE its second duplicate rather than add to it — naive concatenation produces 3 chorus plays where 2 were intended, the same count-vs-content-authority conflict as the kernel's first two confirmations, now on a count+template mechanism instead of repeated literal text. → projects/local__sandbox-lyrics-structure.md#E1
- `[lyrics][duration][testing]` A lyric-sheet duration estimator breaks three independent ways depending which aggregate it counts instead of the per-instance signal it actually needs: distinct section TYPES instead of occurrences undercounts by (occurrences-types)*avg_seconds (60s of 140s here); visible lines instead of repeat-marker-expanded lines undercounts whenever "[Chorus] x2" replaces writing a section out twice; and one global syllables/sec rate misestimates a mixed-pace section by 75% even while the SONG-level total error cancels to zero, hiding the per-section error from any total-only check. → projects/local__sandbox-lyrics-structure.md#E2
- `[lyrics][testing]` A "title appears in the lyrics" checker using exact substring match catches only 1 of 3 constructed near-title-drops (missing a function-word substitution and a tense shift, both ordinary songwriting choices, not edge cases) — an order-preserving subsequence match over the title's content words only, excluding stopwords, catches all 3 with no false positive. → projects/local__sandbox-lyrics-structure.md#E3
- `[lyrics][rhyme-tooling][state]` A line-based scheme/gap scanner that doesn't EXCLUDE non-scheme-bearing lines (ad-libs; `None`-tagged spoken bridge lines) before scanning corrupts two different ways: counting an ad-lib as a real line inflates a true rhyme pair's measured gap from 1 to 2, and treating "no rhyme intended" with the same `None` sentinel used for "not yet computed" makes two unrelated spoken lines match each other via `None == None` — exclude before scanning, don't rely on a sentinel that also satisfies default equality. → projects/local__sandbox-lyrics-structure.md#E4
- `[lyrics][bracket-tags][tooling]` A bracket-tag section parser's awareness stops at the brackets in both directions: a loose keyword regex misfires on ordinary lyric content ("universe" contains "verse"), while a tag-only renumbering tool leaves a REORDERED verse's own in-lyric self-reference ("verse one") stale and undetected, because renumbering touches labels but never reads what the content claims about them. → projects/local__sandbox-lyrics-structure.md#E5
- `[lyrics][testing]` A word-frequency-only "topic drift" detector scores an extended metaphor's vehicle words (storm/lightning/flood) as MORE off-topic (0.66) than the tenor words they run alongside (0.33), despite both being equally deliberate, equally recurring vocabulary tracks — a single-frequency-profile check can't distinguish a genuine one-off outlier from a second, internally consistent vocabulary cluster. → projects/local__sandbox-lyrics-structure.md#E6
- `[ui][state][architecture][testing]` sandbox-combined-action-resolver#E1's "or explicit coordination" alternative fails when done as a don't-overwrite guard on waiting-city#E3's multi-event talk — sandbox-confirmed the guard mismatches the resolver 78/252 (same as no guard) yet scores 0/24 in a fixture where handler order equals priority, vs 0/252 for an execution-time resolver; coordination must consult the resolver's priority, not handler order. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-action-resolver-waiting-city-first-writer-guard.md#E1
- `[ui][state][architecture][testing]` Re-verified first-writer-guard#E1 over all 24 handler orders: don't-overwrite guard and no guard are mirror images (154/264 each; reverse-priority fixture flips 0/11 vs 11/11), while an in-handler guard that compares priority scores 0/264 — coordination works only when invariant to handler reordering, so sweep registration order in the test. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-action-resolver-waiting-city-priority-consulting-guard.md#E1
- `[ui][gamedesign][input][testing]` Re-verified draft#E9 x the-game-prologue#E15 from a new angle: an UNPINNED (generic-event) tutorial trigger masks the closed-vocabulary capture-layer bug — sandbox-confirmed it "completes" in 64/65 click sequences while the new zone reaches the resolver 0 times, vs 0/65 completions for a pinned trigger; entity-pinning doubles as a free end-to-end detector for a dead new zone. → projects/local__sandbox-capture-layer-starves-tutorial.md#E4
- `[browser][webgl][debugging][process]` Never feature-detect a capped resource by acquiring one — a WebGL or AudioContext probe holds exactly the resource it asks about and can be why the real acquisition then fails, so construct the real thing in a try/catch and let the throw be the answer, because the exception message is also a better diagnostic than a boolean → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E51
- `[tooling][process][ci][debugging]` A committed `__pycache__` makes running the repo's own script dirty the tree and blocks every later `git pull`, and a cache-refreshing tool that reports pull failures only under `--verbose` then prints a success tick over canon six commits stale — untrack build artifacts, and surface a failed refresh on the default output → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E52
- `[animation][state][determinism][testing]` A scale factor between an authored clock and a playback clock creates a SECOND float boundary at every lookup that converts back — `(12*1.75)/1.75` is `11.999999999999998`, so an exact `>=` resolves every seek into the previous segment while ordinary playback crosses from below and looks fine; tolerance the comparison, not the seek → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E53
- `[testing][verification][method][viz]` A test suite drifts toward whatever has a cheap oracle, so 18 green assertions covered a visualisation's clock while every shape in it was PRNG-generated and the narration argued from the invented picture — enumerate the claims the artefact makes and assert against those, which is also the only way a category error (155 systems drawn as 154 lessons) becomes visible → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E54
- `[ui][state][architecture][testing]` A priority-consulting handler guard fixes the final view (0/264) but not the path: lower-priority handlers registered first still flash a wrong modal (154/264) and fire their own side effects (264/264 if guard wraps only the view write, 154/264 if it wraps the whole handler) — guards aren't retroactive; resolve the role before dispatch (0/0/0). → projects/local__sandbox-combined-action-resolver-waiting-city-guard-cannot-be-retroactive.md#E1
- `[observability][testing][simulation][method]` Predicted-vs-observed only certifies the rung it is computed at — sandbox-confirmed fire-count multiply-and-compare catches a dead branch (0 vs 50) but passes a brain-builder#E9 target misroute exactly (50 vs 50, enemy hits 0); compute the prediction at the intended-effect rung to catch both. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-august-10-predicted-vs-observed-blind-to-misroute.md#E1
- `[ui][state][architecture][testing]` Batched render hides a priority guard's flicker (0/264) but not its stray side effects (prio 264/264, prio_full 154/264) — a green visual test proves nothing; assert effects, and split handlers into pure bid + single commit (0/0/0, same as resolve-before-dispatch). → projects/local__sandbox-combined-action-resolver-waiting-city-batched-render-hides-flicker-not-effects.md#E1
- `[process][method][verification][tooling]` An absent schema field is not an absent capability — a relation implemented as a first-class RECORD is invisible to both a schema inspection and a field-name grep, which then agree with each other and confirm nothing; one `git log` over the node showed five `connection-*` entries added that same day, so read history before reporting that something cannot be done → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E55
- `[process][method][verification][debugging]` A commit subject describes what the author thought they were doing, not what the artifact is — `add connection-<slug> connection` added an ordinary kernel whose prose starts with "Connect", and believing the subject line caused a correct claim to be retracted; diff a suspect artifact against an ordinary one of the same kind, and hold a retraction to the same standard as the claim it overturns → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E56
- `[tooling][cli]` `brain doctor` only diagnoses the MEMORY intake queue (why held proposals are stuck) — asked to "run brain doctor" to verify a set of IDEA kernels, it ran clean and produced an unrelated report (a different, real queue) with no error, because it has no awareness of any node but memory; a scoped tool's generic-sounding name is not its scope, check the source before citing a clean run as coverage. → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E57
- `[tooling][process][provenance]` A content claim's own Source/Provenance text can't be trusted to verify itself — checking WHO committed a canon entry (the git author) rather than what it says about itself settled a real provenance question in one command: every legitimate kernel syncs as `Claude <noreply@anthropic.com>`, so a kernel introduced by the owner's own account directly is a different, auditable signal than anything written in the kernel's body. → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E58
- `[tooling][cli][process]` `brain gate`'s keyword-overlap check false-positives on generic English words that happen to sit in a short tension title (construction/boundary/builder/composition), not just on genuine topic overlap — hit twice this session on commits with zero actual relation to the flagged tensions' real subjects; read the tension's full body before acking, since the gate is a designed fail-safe, not a relevance classifier. → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E59
- `[tooling][process][provenance]` Atypical delivery (an owner push bypassing `brain sync`) is not evidence a content label is false — asserted "the SONGWRITING tag is false" with confidence from provenance evidence alone, and was wrong: the owner had directed that exact conversation-data mining for that exact songwriting purpose. Delivery-path evidence and content-truth evidence are independent; when the missing piece is intent, ask before concluding. → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E60
- `[checksums][protocol-design][testing]` A checksum byte appended with no explicit presence flag makes a CORRUPTED checksum indistinguishable from a genuinely checksum-less frame that's one byte longer for any other reason — a receiver checking frame shape alone can't tell "trust this byte as a wrong checksum" from "this sender never sends one"; reserve an explicit flag/version field rather than inferring checksum-mode from length. → projects/local__sandbox-checksums.md#E1
- `[checksums][security][testing]` Forging a payload with a different meaning but an identical sum8 checksum took one free padding byte and one subtraction — checksums are linear/invertible by design (that's what makes them cheap), which is exactly what makes them trivial to forge against; a checksum proves absence of ACCIDENTAL corruption, never absence of deliberate tampering, regardless of which non-cryptographic algorithm is chosen — that needs a keyed MAC or signature. → projects/local__sandbox-checksums.md#E2
- `[checksums][streaming][testing]` Checksum placement sets streaming verification's peak buffer cost, not just format convenience: a trailing checksum on a 10,000-byte payload forces buffering the WHOLE payload (verification can't start until the last byte arrives), while a leading checksum plus a declared length lets a receiver verify incrementally with peak buffer bounded by chunk size (500 bytes here, a 20x reduction) — prefer front-loaded checksums for anything read as a stream. → projects/local__sandbox-checksums.md#E3
- `[checksums][determinism][testing]` Sum8/XOR8 checksums miss real structured corruption a CRC catches outright: 0.5-0.6% of simulated burst (adjacent-byte) corruptions slip past sum/XOR while CRC32 catches all 2000, and both sum8/XOR8 are provably blind to byte transposition (swap two bytes, checksum unchanged) since addition/XOR are commutative and discard byte position entirely — use a position-sensitive check wherever corruption can be structured, not just independent noise. → projects/local__sandbox-checksums.md#E4
- `[security][gamedesign][state]` collective#E5's server-side default-deny gate is defeated in NET EFFECT by august-10#E6's exact anti-pattern (extras applied outside the gate) even while correctly denying the primary action — net effect (40) still lands above idle baseline (34); wrap every side effect in the same unconditional gate, not just the headline action. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E1
- `[testing][determinism][tooling]` A decorrelated-order agreement sweep run under set -e silently truncates at its first deliberately-divergent witness order, understating the measured agreement rate (0.80 true vs 0.67 measured, 3 of 5 runs) with no error raised — seven#E3's known-red-kills-set-e failure composes directly with august-10#E4's own recommended methodology. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E2
- `[testing][performance][process]` brain#E5's sqrt(n) batch-budget fix needs the same across-scale sweep brain-builder#E1 demands of a balance constant — checked only at one n, a flat-budget bug and the sqrt(n) fix both look like reasonable numbers; swept across n=2/20/200 the flat bug is 10x worse relatively than the fix, invisible without the sweep. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E3
- `[testing][architecture][gamedev]` brain#E4's camera-scale core passes its own unit tests completely while content sits off-frame on day one — dark-goblin#E3's core-to-shell seam, reproduced concretely: a pure schedule function's correctness says nothing about whether the real content extent it drives stays inside the viewport; add an extent-level check, core tests alone won't catch this. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E4
- `[tooling][process]` Two unrelated root causes (brain#E3's unhandled-end-condition parser drop; draft#E5's ambiguous project-local vs shared-cache incoming/ path) produce the SAME '0 found, reported as success' signature — scanning the wrong-but-identically-named directory returns a correct, empty, and misleading result; always ground-truth a zero-count against the confirmed target. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E5
- `[state][testing]` brain#E3 (dropped block on an unhandled end-condition) and the-game-the-recursion#E1 (NaN-poisoned field with no constructor initializer) are the same failure shape verified together: an addition not wired into every path the old system already used fails with zero exceptions, only a silently wrong downstream number — grep every existing exit/init point before trusting a new field or branch is complete. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E6
- `[browser][rendering][testing]` brain-builder#E3's undersized-canvas bug and seven#E2's context-eviction bug produce OPPOSITE silent symptoms on the same page — after 4 mounts with no cleanup, the earliest correctly-styled canvas goes black (evicted) while a later mis-styled one stays alive but renders at 300x150; debugging by symptom alone can't distinguish them, check size and context liveness separately. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E7
- `[state][testing]` collective#E6's own fix (normalize by total weight, not raw unbounded sum) reintroduces dog#E3's NaN-poisoning if one input's weight is corrupted — the normalized average goes NaN, and a downstream clamp silently converts it to a plausible in-range number (0.0), reproducing the exact 'looks pinned' symptom collective#E6 was filed to fix, via dog#E3's failure mode instead. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E8
- `[security][testing][ci]` collective#E3's injection-safe env: pattern still leaks a probing oracle if the defense's own response visibly differs on detection — mirage#E5's 'a refusal is an observation' applies directly: 2 distinct outputs across 3 probes vs 1 for a byte-identical acknowledgement of the same detection logic, same protection, different leak. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E9
- `[deploy][process][tooling]` A raw-URL fetch honors whatever ref is in the URL (collective#E1) while GitHub Pages only ever serves the default branch (opticon#E3) — fetching against a non-default ref returns 200 with content that was never actually deployed, and the mismatch is invisible from status code alone; pin the fetch ref to what's actually served, verify the round-trip. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E10
- `[testing][gamedev][determinism]` dark-goblin#E4's clamped-delta headless timer (3x slower) delays new content past a test's check window; mirage#E2's bare length>0 assertion passes anyway on stale prior text, going green while the content under test never arrived — assert the specific expected value, not presence, especially under known timing distortion. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E11
- `[state][testing]` draft#E1 (derived key after a spread overwrites it) and mirage#E1 (opts spread after reserved fields overwrites them) are the SAME JS mechanism, last-key-wins, verified together — draft#E1's bug has the identical reorder fix mirage#E1 already recorded, moving the intended winner last. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E12
- `[process][data-integrity]` draft#E6's own fix (reconstruct real data before locking in a creative decision) is undermined if the reconstruction doesn't filter seven#E6's unhandled sentinel — a naive reconstruction over 5 nominal days (2 sentinels) totals 7.5, filtered reconstruction over the 3 real days totals 9.5, same input, no error either way. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E13
- `[gamedesign][state]` draft#E4's storyIdx-gated boss availability composed with waiting-city#E1's full-session-revert respawn silently reverts a story unlock earned earlier the SAME session on a boss-fight loss — storyIdx 3 (just unlocked) reverts to the session-start snapshot value of 2, not just the fight's own progress. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E14
- `[testing][gamedesign][ai-agents]` mirage#E7's zero-sensitivity ground-truth oracle finding is specific to information-gap mechanics, not a blanket flaw — the identical oracle function scored flat (1.0/1.0/1.0) across deception on an info-gap mechanic and properly monotonic (1.0/0.7/0.4) on a non-info-gap one, confirming recursion#E3's classification-not-contradiction pattern in a fresh domain. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E15
- `[state][gamedev][architecture]` mirage#E3's clear-queue-at-tick-start bug is fragile to write order from ANY producer, including a boundary-violating 'read-only' renderer (test#E5) — the same write silently survives or vanishes-until-next-tick depending only on whether it landed before or after the tick's own clear, with no error either way. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E16
- `[ai][testing][gamedev]` opticon#E6's best-ever stall-gate never fires when fed a score from an argmax-ratchet instrument (dark-goblin#E1) — the ratchet's self-reported metric climbs monotonically by construction even as true behavior collapses (0.10 down to 0.01), so the gate passes on exactly the input it can't trust; validate against real task outcome, not only the gate's own input stream. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E17
- `[testing][state][gamedev]` A name-literal checker over a grouped-focus system reports PASS after the leaking group is renamed, though the leak is unchanged — seven#E4's vacuous-checker failure reproduces on opticon#E4's grouped-focus target; derive the check target at run time, never hardcode it. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E18
- `[determinism][state][testing]` prologue#E1's raw-state-words PRNG save is exposed to seven#E6's sentinel-handoff risk if the state value space overlaps a reserved 'never seeded' sentinel — a naive loader passes it through as a valid seed with no error, silently starting a meaningless deterministic stream; branch on the sentinel before reconstructing, as seven#E6 already prescribes. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E19
- `[determinism][state][testing]` test#E2's saved-{seed,count} determinism discipline breaks under seven#E1's callback-hidden variable roll count — resuming from a save that only counted the 'obvious' top-level draw (1) instead of the true total (4, hidden crit-check draws included) produces a genuinely different next random value, silently. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E20
- `[testing][deploy][content]` dark-goblin#E7's per-item well-formed check and august-10#E1's deploy-step-success signal are two independent insufficient proxies for correctness, verified together — per-item validation passes 6/6 while an aggregate defect exists, AND a 'success' deploy silently serves a stale fallback, at the same time, neither check catching the other's blind spot. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E21
- `[testing][gamedesign][balance]` An isolated conjunction test (only the stone term varied, wood/labor pinned generously true) passes while the real summed demand across the whole tech tree (august-10#E7) already exceeds supply (201 vs 48) — zanegpt#E4's mutation-testing discipline, applied to the suspect term, is exactly what would have caught it. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E22
- `[state][architecture]` seven#E5 (two games colliding on unnamespaced storage) and waiting-city#E3 (two NPC roles colliding on unnamespaced view-state) are the identical bug shape and identical fix, verified together across unrelated domains — namespace the key by writer identity, regardless of whether the shared store is deploy storage or UI state. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E23
- `[testing][state]` wrong-sky#E4's own noted blind spot (re-querying between picks misses an exclusive-offer violation) is seven#E4's vacuous-checker shape, verified fresh — a per-call check passes both times an offer is accepted (including a should-be-impossible 2nd accept) because it never sees the cumulative count; scope the check to the invariant's real scope. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E24
- `[input][gamedev][state]` test#E4's correct dodge-command withholding does nothing to stop lockstep#E4's separate failure — a second system (block) reading the SAME raw input edge unconditionally still fires from the identical press; each consumer of a shared edge needs its own context gate, independently of how correctly another consumer handles it. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E25
- `[gamedev][rendering][state]` brain-builder#E7's grouped-beat combat replay fix is a specific case of answering-deep#E1's general compose-not-overwrite rule — a naive per-event renderer setting the same display slot each time loses 2 of 3 simultaneous hits, identical to answering-deep#E1's modal-overwrite failure, now on a trace replay. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E26
- `[testing][balance][ai]` brain-builder#E5's smoothness assertion passes on an argmax-ratchet bot's measured difficulty curve (flattened to 1.00/1.00/0.95/0.95) while the true curve (0.79 down to 0.16) genuinely fails it — dark-goblin#E1's warning applies directly: a balance-testing bot's own narrow strategy can hide the exact step the assertion exists to catch. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E27
- `[gamedesign][state]` A crit-flash feedback channel (brain-builder#E6) derived directly from draft#E2's combat-balance flag can't express a move wanting the presentation without the loud feedback — draft#E2's own recorded override-field fix is exactly what's needed; without it the move is forced LOUD_FLASH regardless of design intent. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E28
- `[input][testing][harness]` prologue#E2's sub-frame-press-invisible-to-sampling finding is a fourth confirmed instance of dark-goblin#E5's snapshot-vs-moving-system diagnosis — swept across 4 frame durations, the sampling harness misses a fixed 5ms press every time while event-time capture never does, confirming the failure is structural, not a one-off flake. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E29
- `[performance][rendering][gamedesign]` draft#E3's bespoke-procedural escape hatch, implemented as a per-tile filter call inside the draw loop, reintroduces wrong-sky#E3's cost-scales-with-draw-count bug (10/100/1000 filter calls) even while correctly fixing visual quality — composite override-tiles once and filter once instead, holding constant regardless of tile count. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E30
- `[camera][viewport][layout]` A content-fit clamp on camera zoom must be applied with no smoothing and on both axes: smoothing it at 0.12 per frame re-clipped 172 to 273 item-frames the hard clamp held at 0, and a height-only fit was clean in landscape but clipped 958 item-frames in portrait → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-motion-camera.md#E1
- `[determinism][floating-point][testing]` Rounding transcendental results to float32 makes two libms that differ by 1 ulp agree everywhere except float32 rounding midpoints (4000/12000 constructed, 0/200000 ordinary), and a sim where sin() only nudges a large state hides the libm difference completely (0/200 raw) until state is fed directly (200/200) → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-system-determinism.md#E1
- `[floating-point][animation][state]` In IEEE doubles, value *= k never reaches exactly 0 for any k above 0.5, sticking near 0.5/(1-k) times the smallest subnormal, so a fade that completes on alpha == 0 never completes (still 2.5e-323 after 20000 frames at k=0.9); derive from elapsed time with pow, which does underflow to 0 → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-system-determinism.md#E2
- `[animation][browser][timing]` A setTimeout loop tuned for 60fps repeats about half of all frames on a 120 or 144Hz display (575/1200, 815/1440) while rAF repeats none, but take the effect's start from the first rAF timestamp, since a performance.now() start gives a negative first-frame elapsed and Math.pow(-0.008, 0.5) is NaN → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-motion-animation-loop.md#E1
- `[determinism][rng][state]` Keying RNG draws symbolically only fixes stream shifts if the key holds no position: by spawn slot, removing one entity still flipped 1033/2850 other decisions; by (id, tick, draw index), inserting one check flipped 2 later rolls in that entity; by (id, tick, purpose), nothing moved → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-system-determinism.md#E3
- `[testing][process]` dbz-combat#E5's trustworthy-looking-but-wrong coverage report has a cheap early tell from august-10#E2's own finding — an exact-match parser's '' miss bucket saturates to 57% from ordinary case/whitespace label variants, a fast first signal before running the full independent-oracle recomputation dbz-combat#E5 otherwise requires. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E31
- `[deploy][tooling][content]` A shipped fix for answering-deep#E4's silent-placeholder fallback (a missing sprite-map entry) has NO effect for users stuck on mirage#E4's exact failure — entry-point stamped, nested sprite-map import not, so their browser keeps serving the pre-fix file from cache; 'fix had no effect' is the composition, not a new bug. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E32
- `[determinism][testing][gamedev]` opticon#E1's terminal-state reachability simulation is still exposed to answering-deep#E3's shared-RNG coupling — one unrelated entity consuming a single extra draw before the sim runs flips its terminal outcome for the identical seed (-1 vs -3); simulating to terminal state and isolating the RNG stream are independent fixes, both needed. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E33
- `[security][testing][state]` recursion#E2's bypassable payload-sourced gate passes a seven#E4-style existence checker (the field is referenced in source) while a caller supplying threshold=0 defeats it at runtime — a static presence check can't see a dynamically-exploitable gate; call the gated function with adversarial values instead. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E34
- `[state][testing][process]` brain#E2's silent delta-sum drift and skilltree-formula#E5's NaN-propagation compose in one accumulator: a clean stream still drifts from ground truth by a plausible amount (5), and the SAME accumulator poisons its entire total to NaN forever the moment one delta is malformed — both silent, both need an independently-recomputed ground truth to catch. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E35
- `[gamedesign][determinism][testing]` august-10#E3's hysteresis margin, correct for a continuous oscillating field, completely eats a discrete transient detection pulse (answering-deep#E5's own named case) under the identical margin — zero detections across the sequence; classify discrete-vs-continuous first, a stabilizing margin for one property class can suppress the other entirely. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E36
- `[ui][testing][gamedev]` brain#E7's control-bar clipping only shows as a TRANSIENT state when content comes from answering-deep#E1's sequential overwriting events — a mid-sequence message overflows the viewport while the final message doesn't, so checking only final state misses the clip entirely; sample every intermediate state, not just the last. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E37
- `[security][testing][state]` dbz-combat#E3's unguarded null sentinel in an aggregated Set is also an information leak under mirage#E5's framing — its mere presence lets any observer detect an unclearable scenario existed, something the sentinel-guarded set never reveals; guard-before-aggregate is a privacy fix, not just a correctness one. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E38
- `[gamedev][state][determinism]` dbz-combat#E1's cancel-flag-set-after-input-sample bug and opticon#E5's passable-tile-not-updated-everywhere bug are the identical off-by-one-frame shape, verified together — both flags read stale on the frame they're written and correct only from the next frame on, regardless of subsystem; order writers before readers within the frame. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E39
- `[testing][balance][tooling]` dbz-combat#E2's monotonic power-axis sweep, run under seven#E3's set -e pattern, aborts at a deliberately-included worst-case fixture (level 3 of 5) and never reaches the axis's TOP — where a dominant strategy would actually show; a truncated monotonic sweep isn't a partial sample, it's missing exactly the informative end. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E40
- `[tooling][process]` brain#E6's own recorded finding (brain train edge ranks purely by score, no accessibility check, wrong most of the time) is zanegpt#E5's warning in concrete form — a confident top pick from one unchecked criterion is exactly the kind of self-report zanegpt#E5 says needs independent verification before trusting it. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E41
- `[process][agent][testing]` collective#E2's proxy-category gate ('looks like a fact lookup' standing in for 'is decidable') mismatches real decidability in BOTH directions when checked concretely — a human-taste task is wrongly admitted, an oddly-phrased-but-decidable task is wrongly blocked; verify both failure directions, not just the one that motivated the gate. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E42
- `[tooling][process][ci]` dark-goblin#E2's pkill -f self-kill (silently skips every && step after it, including a sync call) produces the exact same '0 pushed, 0 held' signature as draft#E5's wrong-incoming-directory case — a third independently-confirmed cause for an already-known-ambiguous zero; the signature alone never tells you which happened. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E43
- `[browser][rendering][testing]` dark-goblin#E6's own recommended SVG-rasterization fix (via canvas) creates a new context per call with no disposal — called repeatedly it becomes exactly seven#E2's leak, silently evicting an unrelated, earlier-mounted, legitimate game canvas once the context cap is hit. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E44
- `[testing][gamedesign][ai-agents]` test#E1's rung-3 smoke-playthrough check passes using dark-goblin#E1's exact argmax-ratchet bot policy, while a realistic, only-slightly-suboptimal playstyle gets stuck on the identical content — a validation ladder's strongest rung is only as strong as its bot's strategy diversity, not just its presence. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E45
- `[gamedev][state][testing]` dog#E4's safe() try/catch stops a frame from blanking but doesn't undo a partial overwrite the failing subsystem already made to a shared slot (answering-deep#E1) — the frame renders the WRONG content instead of blanking OR showing the earlier subsystem's correct output, a state worse to debug than either alternative. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E46
- `[determinism][viz][testing]` garden-viz#E9's integer-accumulator reveal scheduler survives dog#E2's exact measured headless rAF rate (0.4x wall-clock): the naive overshoot-discarding scheduler drops from 60 to 24 sprouts at that specific multiplier, the fixed one holds 62/62 — confirms both lessons compose with a real number, not just qualitatively. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E47
- `[testing][state]` lockstep#E3's missing-writer bug (a stat only updates if something writes it at the real event) is mirage#E2's weak-assertion shape verified fresh — the stale pre-event value passes any 'looks plausible' check, because a reader was never wrong, it was just never fed a fresh write; no reader-side assertion catches a missing writer. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E48
- `[tooling][documentation][testing]` zanegpt#E3's doc-path bug is mirage#E6's own goal-vs-visibility split applied fresh — install logic is 100% correct (the goal axis) while the doc-displayed path never matches the real resolved one (the presentation axis), the same structure that made 'companions scatter' a rendering bug, not a behavior bug. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E49
- `[git][process][tooling]` opticon#E7's stale-pre-squash-commit conflict and lockstep#E10's harness-level push block present as the identical 'push refused' symptom, but lockstep#E10's own remedy (push locally) does nothing for opticon#E7's real cause — a local push carries the same stale commits into the same real conflict; check for actual diff overlap before picking a fix. → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E50
- `[testing][determinism][architecture][state]` An oracle for a gated precondition must recompute the protected set from raw input per spec: a one-tick-late i-frame window showed 148 violations in 54/60 seeds under ground truth and 0 under an oracle reading the gate's own flag, while replay parity and a bug-era golden passed 60/60 → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-system-testing.md#E1
