- `[concurrency][correctness][debugging][scheduling]` An unprotected load-inc-store yields 5 results from one input across 48620 interleavings, yet under rare preemption (p=0.001/step) only 4.4% of runs fail (~68 runs for 95% odds); a probe inside load..store doubled failures (39.9%→84.6%), outside left them flat (40.5%) → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-concurrency.md#E1
- `[concurrency][lock-free][correctness][data]` An ABA version tag fails exactly when the head changed a multiple of 2^bits times during a stall: 4 bits stopped all 69 corrupt CASes at 40 changes but none of 53 at 16, 8 bits failed all 26 at 256; size it for the longest stall → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-concurrency.md#E2
- `[debugging][git][regression][algorithm]` Bisection's ceil(log2 n) tests (10 for 676 commits) assume a deterministic monotone test: a 5% flaky test names the wrong commit 20.3% of the time (best-of-3: 3.1%), and a bug that was masked then returned gets its reintroduction named ~half the time (50.3%) → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-debugging-strategy.md#E1
- `[determinism][gamedev][timing][fairness]` A reset-to-STEP float cooldown fires every ceil(STEP/dt) frames (60000/(ceil(108/dt)*dt) = 450/515/540 at 30/60/144fps vs ideal 556); carrying overshoot fixes it only in a while loop (an if caps at one fire per frame: 480 at 8fps), and the gap is flat at ~16.7% relative → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-system-determinism.md#E4
- `[testing][combat][measurement][balance]` A symmetric control needs hundreds of trials: the original 25/40 skew has p = 0.154 and a 40-trial control catches a true 62.5% bias only 31.6% of the time (400: 99.9%); shuffle ties (50.1%), since alternating team order each round still skewed 46.1% → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-system-testing.md#E2
- `[api][data][correctness][performance]` Cursor pagination beats offset (1500 vs 45750 rows scanned, 0 vs 177 duplicates under inserts) only on a unique key: a timestamp-only cursor on burst-written rows skipped 295/1500 with < and repeated 295 with <=; use a (ts, id) tuple → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-api-design.md#E1
- `[narrative][design][content][state]` A salience selector's not-recently-shown stage after an exact best-fit stage is inert (287 repeats in 300 picks); put it between specificity and fit (169 repeats, 0 less-specific) not first (0 repeats, 142 less-specific); a blended score matches staging only while fit weight ≤ the specificity multiplier → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-system-narrative.md#E1
- `[determinism][floating-point][state][measurement]` Past 2^53 a compounding stat stays accurate (rel. error 2.7e-17) but +1 is lost (50.5% just above, 100% from 2^54) and integer-valued sums become order-dependent (0% below, 53.1% just above), so fingerprints break where summation order varies → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-system-determinism.md#E5
- `[cutscene][timeline][state][animation]` A lookup-only seek fires nothing (vs 383 for fast-forward over 60 scrubs) but leaves marker-set state wrong in 54/60 seeks, so fold state markers without firing; chapter jumps land on marker times, so choose bisect_left (cue plays 7/7) or bisect_right (0/7) deliberately → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-system-cutscene.md#E1
- `[architecture][security][state][testing]` A gate value pinned in state is only safe if no client command can write it: a payload threshold over-advanced 4801/5000 honest streams (omitted param = 0), and a state-pinned but client-settable one still passed 2435/5000 fuzzed streams vs 0 when fixed at construction → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-system-architecture.md#E1
- `[combat][balance][gamedesign][measurement]` A hard %-max-HP cap holds fights at 5 hits from 1x to 100000x but while both sides reach it (to 1.2x here) the stronger side wins only 50% on first move; a saturating cap 20%·r/(1+r) keeps 5.5-10.3 hits and the stronger side winning 99% from 1.1x → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-rpg-combat.md#E1
- `[security][web][xss][encoding]` XSS encoding must fit the context and keep text intact: HTML escaping is safe in body/quoted attrs but leaks 6/13 payloads unquoted and 3 javascript: hrefs, and in a script string corrupts 3/4 benign strings (JS hex is the safe, intact choice); CSP script-src passed 2 markup-only injections → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-security-web.md#E1
- `[determinism][floating-point][state][architecture]` Accumulation orders of a compounding value differ in bits from event 2; a gate comparing a recomputed float to the pinned one flips from the start (135-169/200 seeds from event 5), only an integer-floor gate waits for the crossover (0 through event 18, 72 at 20, still below 2^53) → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-system-determinism.md#E6
- `[rendering][geometry][correctness][precision]` Piercing faces have no correct draw order (682 and 150 of 1790 pixels wrong; a split is exact), and a depth buffer is exact only while its precision covers the intersection: 16-bit perspective depth erred on 18 and 64 of 832 overlap pixels at near 0.01 and 0.001, 24-bit on none → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-geometry-visibility.md#E1
- `[determinism][animation][floating-point][timing]` Frame-from-elapsed-time fixes render-rate sprite playback only with exact time: a float acc += 1/hz hit 0.9999999999999999 at 1 s/30 Hz (same wrong frame as the per-tick bug) and differed on 294/1800 renders over 60 s; use integer time and accumulate phase on rate changes (else a 151-frame jump) → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-system-determinism.md#E7
- `[verification][testing][javascript][measurement]` Ten checks on one substrate are one experiment: a Python model of a JS bucket hash passed 10/10 range checks while real Node failed 10/10 (~49% negative indexes from `x | 0` and JS's sign-keeping %); `>>> 0` fixed it — run numeric-semantics claims on the real runtime → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-verification-method.md#E1
- `[performance][measurement][compiler][benchmark]` Sinking a benchmark's output isn't enough: with a constant input gcc and clang kept the loop but folded it to 0 multiplies; only an opaque input plus a sink kept the 2 real ones, and a consumed sum of i*x still became a closed form (clang) — read the generated code → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-performance-measurement.md#E1
- `[camera][animation][correctness][gamedev]` Snap-in/ease-out camera pull-in fixes edge flicker (1.595 vs 0.221 units/frame hovering) and lerp clipping (309 and 138 frames vs 0), but only if the camera updates after the player moves: reading the pre-move position clipped once on each of 6 entries into cover → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-motion-camera.md#E2
- `[architecture][save][state][content]` Snapshot saves miss every content patch (318/400) and a version wipe costs all 400, but a live reference only helps with stable step ids: stored as an index, 105 saves pointed at the wrong step after insertions and removals; as an id, only 23 (current step removed) needed a rule → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-system-architecture.md#E2
- `[networking][prediction][correctness][state]` Buffered-input reconciliation fixes current-input replay (500/500 runs wrong vs 0) only while acks arrive in order and the buffer covers the unacked window: late acks broke it in 500/500 until stale acks were ignored, and an 8-entry buffer at 12 ticks latency broke every variant → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-networking.md#E1
- `[gamedev][feedback][measurement][probability]` A tell's visible rate is nominal x gate fractions only for independent gates (0.0057 vs 0.0056/s); correlated gates with the same marginals gave 0.0103/s (1.8x), so measure the joint fraction; median window = typical player (4.0%), mean = share of windows (6.1%) → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-gamedev-feedback.md#E1
- `[determinism][collision][measurement][testing]` Lattice collision error scales with lattice/half-extent (~0.4% of events at 0.01, 4.8% at 0.1, 20.7% at 0.4) and net counts hide it (+1.2% net vs 16.0% of events wrong at 0.1/0.3); compare per event, keep lattice ≤ ~0.04 of the smallest half-extent → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-system-determinism.md#E8
- `[testing][harness][ui][timing]` Settle on a signal the reveal emits after it ends: ending punctuation settled early on 638-830/1000 multi-sentence reveals, two identical polls on 1000/1000 whenever the poll interval was below a 400 ms pause, a follow-on control was right 1000/1000 → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-testing-harness.md#E1
- `[dsp][audio][stability][filter]` Under fast cutoff switching DF1 isn't reliably safer than DF2T (DF2T worse at Q 0.707: 28.43 vs 5.62; DF1 worse at Q 10/40: 90.98/160.41 vs 6.32/2.02; both diverge switching every 7 samples) while a TPT SVF stayed 1.24-3.16 — use the SVF → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-dsp-filter-design.md#E1
- `[vfx][animation][particles][rendering]` Per-particle phase 2πi/N kills global sway sync (1.000→0.000) but if index follows spawn position neighbours stay fully correlated (+1.000), a travelling wave; scramble the index (+0.001), and golden-ratio steps give a zig-zag (-0.737) → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-vfx-weather.md#E1
- `[cutscene][timeline][animation][state]` A pure state(t) track seeks in O(log N) (8.3 probes vs 154 beats replayed at 300 beats; a stomp seek was wrong 200/200) only with absolute beat starts: 13 of 30 beats relative to the previous end made direct eval wrong on 89/200 seeks until starts were baked at load → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-system-cutscene.md#E2
- `[cutscene][determinism][testing][state]` Watch-vs-skip cutscene parity held 500/500 only with a pointer-based skip: re-selecting markers by at >= skip time double-fired in 14 scenes, wall-clock payloads broke 431, one camera-track dispatch broke 226 → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-system-cutscene.md#E3
- `[level-design][graph][testing][gamedesign]` A zero-ability BFS audit of one-way drops missed 0 of 327 softlocked levels but raised 257 false alarms (44% of flags) where every route to the drop already needed the escape ability; confirm flags with a (room, abilities) state search → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-level-design.md#E1
- `[api][webhooks][correctness][security]` A webhook id log stops duplicates only if committed atomically with the effect (after: 165 dups, before: 158 lost, atomic: 0/0) and claimed via insert-if-absent (check-then-record double-applied 18/20); it misses out-of-order status (63/200) and signed replays (200/200) without version and timestamp checks → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-api-design.md#E2
- `[dsp][audio][algorithm][emulation]` The SN76489-style 16-bit LFSR (taps 0,3) has four cycles, 57337/8191/7/1: the reset seed gets 57337 but 12.5% of non-zero seeds fall on the 8191- or 7-step cycle; seed with the hardware reset value, and use a primitive tap set (0x002D: one 65535 cycle) → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-dsp-lfsr.md#E1
- `[concurrency][compiler][correctness][c]` A plain-flag spin-wait reloads at -O0 but at -O2 gcc makes it a jump-to-self that never re-reads (hangs) and clang deletes it and returns at once (never waits); an atomic acquire load re-read every iteration under both → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-concurrency.md#E3
- `[procgen][algorithm][gamedev][measurement]` 4-5 CA cave connectivity is set by density: one cave in 52/60 seeds at 0.40, 18/60 at 0.45, 1/60 at 0.50, with keep-largest discarding a median 0%/1.8%/39.2% of floor (worst 75.1%); region counts settle by iteration 3-8 while cells change to 15-18 → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-procgen.md#E1
- `[api][graphql][performance][async]` DataLoader collapses N+1 resolver calls to one per level (2501 vs 4 for 500 authors x 3 reviews) only when siblings start together: awaiting loader calls in a loop gave exactly the naive 2501, and a 100-key cap gave 26 → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-api-design.md#E3
- `[text][unicode][javascript][correctness]` Code-unit string ops break most non-ASCII text: slicing at every interior point of 10 mixed strings cut a grapheme 34/48 times (13 lone surrogates) and [...s].reverse() broke 5/10; count with Intl.Segmenter graphemes (family emoji: 11 units, 7 code points, 1 grapheme, 25 bytes) → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-text-unicode.md#E1
- `[architecture][state][caching][correctness]` An invalidation hook fixes a pinned derived value only if it covers every input-changing command and recomputes in dependency order: one hook of three, or all hooks with a dependent recomputed first, went stale in 500/500 runs; ordered hooks or a versioned recompute-on-read: 0 → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-system-architecture.md#E3
- `[database][performance][indexing][measurement]` In SQLite each index taxed every INSERT (20→71 VM ops from 0 to 10 indexes) and DELETE (10→41), but an UPDATE paid only for indexes on changed columns (26 flat for an unindexed column; 37 with 1 or 10 indexes when it had one); the indexed select fell 3307→9 → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-database-design.md#E1
- `[dsp][emulation][fixed-point][audio]` A float cleanup of a fixed-point oscillator (20-bit phase, 10-bit table, 12-bit out) differed on 69-95% of samples (≤6.8 LSB) and erased the chip's spurs: -59.3 dBc with phase truncation, -88.1 dBc without, vs -270 dBc float; emulate exact widths, test bit-exact → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-dsp-chip-emulation.md#E1
- `[dsp][audio][fm][measurement]` High-index FM aliases with peak fixed at 1.0 (aliased energy -129 dB at index 0.5, 73% of the signal at 10), and THD+N is blind too: 34.5% for aliased and clean alike at 0.5, lower for aliased at 10 (86.4% vs 91.0%); measure energy outside the expected sidebands → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-dsp-fm.md#E1
- `[performance][algorithm][sorting][measurement]` CPython 3.11 sorts lists under 64 by binary insertion (comparison count matched 100/100 below 64, ~10/100 above), and binary insertion never loses on comparisons (8826 vs 8948 for merge at n=1024); quadratic moves cap it (955 vs 377 at 63, 261437 vs 10240 at 1024) → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-performance-algorithms.md#E1
- `[testing][harness][input][debugging]` Per-layer input counters localise a fault in one round trip only if the last one sits at the expected effect: DOM/input/hit counters found 5/7 faults and read 20/20/20 for a handler that throws after the hit, while adding an 'action done' counter found 7/7 → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-testing-harness.md#E2
- `[dsp][audio][realtime][concurrency]` Audio glitches come from the tail, not the mean: every design averaged ~900 us of a 2667 us deadline, yet in-callback allocation missed 27 deadlines in 10 min and a mutex shared with the UI thread 1643 (worst 10.4 ms); an SPSC ring or try-lock missed none → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-dsp-realtime.md#E1
- `[testing][save][pathfinding][state]` Move a check removed as wrong-in-place to the boundary where it holds: a two-step save left the agent in a wall in 16/5000 sessions (0 atomic), the trusting replanner flagged none, a load-time position-vs-grid check flagged 16/16 without touching 237 legit live door moments → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-system-testing.md#E3
- `[architecture][state][save][javascript]` A command/reduce seam replays exactly only with a read-only view (200/200 matched; a view writing back broke 200/200), and save = JSON.stringify(state) holds only for JSON-plain state: -0, NaN, Infinity, undefined, Map, Set, Date and array holes changed or vanished; BigInt threw → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-system-architecture.md#E4
- `[architecture][security][state][pathfinding]` Trust-by-provenance only works if provenance is checked at the write: replanning from a payload position failed 1572/2000 fuzzed sessions, storing it into state unchecked then trusting state failed 1573/2000, validating at write failed 0 → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-system-architecture.md#E5
- `[ui][state][architecture][testing]` Across all 6 handler registration orders, guards leave stray effects (12, or 5 if whole-handler) and flicker (6; batching zeroes flicker only) while resolve-before-dispatch gives 0/0/0 — arbitrate before dispatch, test every order. → projects/local__sandbox-modal-priority-guard-order-permutations.md#E1
- `[ui][state][architecture][testing]` No-guard and first-writer guard are mirror images over all 6 handler orders (both wrong 4/6; reverse fixture flips) while a priority guard is 0/6; batching cuts wrong frames but stray effects stay 12 — sweep every order, assert effects. → projects/local__sandbox-first-writer-guard-mirror-batched.md#E1
- `[ui][state][architecture][testing]` A two-role entity's correct final view can be an accident of event emission order (swap it: 2/8 states wrong; handler registration order matters 0/4) and naive dispatch still flashes/side-effects wrongly 2/8 — resolve the role before dispatch (0/8 on all three measures) → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-the-waiting-city.md#E18
- `[ui][state][architecture][testing]` A priority-consulting guard with strict-greater compare is still handler-order dependent when top priorities tie (39/162 wrong, 12/27 assignments order-dependent) — compare (priority, role id) as a total order: 0/162, and sweep tied priorities in the test → projects/pappydapimp69__the-game-the-waiting-city.md#E19
- `[ui][gamedev][state][input][testing]` dog#E20's shared resolver keeps hint and press in agreement only within one tick — sandbox: with hint-to-press lag 1/2/4 on a period-5 state cycle, re-resolving at press disagrees with the shown hint 120/240/480 of 600 (0 at lag 0); sweep lag in tests, freeze or token-validate the hint → projects/local__sandbox-combined-action-resolver-dog-stale-hint-press-lag.md#E1
- `[gamedev][state][testing][determinism]` dog#E11's max-dwell exit silently fails when its limit is a formula output that goes NaN or infinite — sandbox: exit-when dwell >= limit stays trapped 2000/2000 ticks under NaN (the inverted comparison exits), and both raw forms are trapped under infinity; clamp the limit finite (1 exit each) and test with NaN/inf → projects/local__sandbox-combined-dog-maxdwell-nan-formula-limit.md#E1
- `[ui][gamedev][state][input][testing]` Freezing or token-validating dog#E20's hint (stale-hint-press-lag#E1's fixes) converts the mismatch rather than removing it — sandbox-confirmed 0/120/240/480 of 600 mismatches become the same count of dropped presses (token) or stale executions (frozen) at lag 0/1/2/4; assert dropped-press rate in a lag sweep and shrink the lag itself. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-action-resolver-dog-hint-fix-converts-mismatch.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][simulation][state][testing]` The dwell repair for august-10#E9's level-keyed degeneracy (dog#E40) is a step at agent travel time, not a dial — sandbox-confirmed 0 interiors at dwell 0 and 1, 25 at dwell 2, then only fewer events (25/17/13/9/5 opened at dwell 3/4/6/10/20); set minimum event lifetime to travel cost and sweep it. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-august-10-dog-dwell-sized-by-travel-time.md#E1
- `[save][state][browser][gamedev]` seven#E5's project-namespaced storage keys do not fix dog#E64's cross-slot flag leak — sandbox-confirmed a bare key leaks 8 cross-project and 4 cross-slot of 12 pairs, the project prefix removes only the 8, and project+slot removes all; test a leak matrix over every scoping axis, or keep the flag in the per-slot payload. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-dog-seven-project-namespace-does-not-fix-slot-leak.md#E1
- `[ui][state][architecture][input][testing]` Execution-time re-resolve (action-resolver#E1) and hint fidelity (stale-hint-press-lag#E1) cannot both hold above lag 0 — sandbox-confirmed execute-time stays rule-valid 600/600 but matches the hint only 520/440/280, while a frozen hint executes a lapsed role on 40/80/160 presses at lag 1/2/4; sweep lag and count both violations, or re-validate and reject at press. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-action-resolver-execution-time-resolve-vs-stale-hint.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][simulation][state][testing]` dog#E40's minimum-dwell repair for august-10#E9 is safe as an unconditional guard — sandbox-confirmed phases 1 and 2 go from 0 to 60 reachable interiors at dwell 3 (travel time) while phases 3 and 5 are identical with and without it (40/40, 24/24); verify a repair on both sides of its threshold. → projects/local__sandbox-combined-august-10-dog-dwell-is-a-noop-when-phase-exceeds-travel.md#E1
- `[ui][gamedev][state][input][testing]` Hint-vs-press mismatch scales as lag/period, not a fixed 120 per lag step — sandbox period 5 gives 0/120/240/480 of 600 but period 7 gives 0/85/170/342 at lag 0/1/2/4, and freeze/token/re-resolve policies only relabel the same presses; sweep period as well as lag → projects/local__sandbox-combined-action-resolver-hint-lag-period-scaling.md#E1

- `[dialogue][speech][testing]` Detect refusals by polarity first, delivery markers second (markers alone 8 of 14, with polarity 13 of 14); a logistics answer that refuses needs the request it answers, word cues caught it only by accident → projects/local__sandbox-refusal-linter-misses-logistics-answers.md#E1
- `[songwriting][lyrics][testing]` A show-don't-tell ratio linter passes verses that show then explain in the last line (3 of 3); add a last-line stating check (8 of 8) but skip colour words modifying a noun (blue truck) → projects/local__sandbox-show-dont-tell-linter-needs-the-last-line-check.md#E1
- `[songwriting][lyrics][testing]` A lyric stress checker must let one-syllable function words take either beat: strict dictionary stress falsely flagged 4 of 7 well-set lines, while a syllable-count check passed all 5 mis-set ones → projects/local__sandbox-stress-checker-needs-flexible-monosyllables.md#E1
- `[dialogue][speech][testing]` Speech-pattern features pass a strip test only when characters really differ (10 of 10); a one-voice pair gives all ties and short lines default to the plainer speaker, so mark featureless lines unattributable → projects/local__sandbox-strip-test-speech-features-fail-on-short-lines.md#E1
- `[songwriting][lyrics][testing]` Report every line a title hits, not the first: first-hit-only placement was right on 2 of 8 choruses and substring on 4 of 8, content-word matching with both hyphen views 7 of 8 (missed irregular held) → projects/local__sandbox-title-position-report-all-hits.md#E1
- `[ui][input][state][gamedev][testing]` Age-filter cost (N/P)*min(l,W,P-W,P-l) held on 18415 swept cases (0 mismatches, zero at whole-cycle lag) but only for one contiguous legal window; a two-window shape gave 24 vs 18 predicted → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E67
- `[ui][input][state][gamedev][testing]` A hard age cutoff gives a pure cost step at T on periodic and Fibonacci-word state (0 mismatches over 6560 cases, validity-only peak 200 and 370 of 600), but a soft age weight is a ramp not a step, so the step belongs to the hard cutoff → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E68
- `[ui][input][state][gamedev][testing]` Validity-only has zero invalid presses exactly at whole-cycle lag (0 violations in 28152 swept cases) but above an age threshold it also wastes less than the age gate at other lags (P15W5 lag 8: 40 vs 120), so weigh invalid execution against rejection before choosing → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E69
- `[ui][input][state][gamedev][testing]` Hint-state mismatch detects staleness at 2*min(l,W,P-W,P-l)/P on periodic state (exact over 26100 swept cases): zero at whole-cycle lag and only 2/P at one tick off, while age flags every lag past threshold → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E70
- `[ui][input][state][gamedev][testing]` On a 3-role cyclic resolver the age gate wastes (N/P)*min(3l,P,3(P-l)) agreeing hints past threshold (all N at whole-cycle lag, 0 errors in 2010 cases) while validity with fallback stays 100% valid; k=3 only → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E71
- `[tooling][cli][windows][encoding]` brain zanegpt crashed on cp932 Windows: text-mode subprocess decoded claude CLI UTF-8 with the locale codec, stdout became None, caller's .lower() failed; pass encoding="utf-8" to subprocess reads → projects/local__suno.md#E1
- `[tooling][browser][scraping]` Claude-in-Chrome javascript_tool blocks whole results containing key=value text (P=1.2K L=354) as query-string data; switch to slash/colon separators and strip ?&= instead of re-cleaning content → projects/local__suno.md#E2
- `[songwriting][suno-prompting][distribution]` Suno Trending rank ignores plays (19K beats 97K; 0/22 text-thread lyrics, genres scattered) and splits 2-5% broadcast vs 12-87% community like rate, so target likes/plays and community reach, not song traits → projects/local__suno.md#E3
- `[tooling][browser][suno-prompting]` Suno's lyrics box is a Lexical editor left read-only under automation; unlock via the fiber's editor.setEditable(true), paste the sheet as a ClipboardEvent (insertParagraph drops line breaks), verify with the app's char counter → projects/local__suno.md#E4
- `[songwriting][suno-prompting][distribution][testing]` CORRECTS suno#E3: Suno Trending rank does not ignore plays (rho -0.43 weekly) but likes/comments predict it better (tau 0.46 vs 0.32) and reach vs like rate is inverse (-0.93); rank-correlate every signal before calling one ignored → projects/local__suno.md#E5
- `[ui][gamedev][state][input][testing]` Exact lag compensation gives 0 mismatch where freeze/token only relabel it (naive == token drops), and a 1-tick estimate error costs 2N/P (200/120/100 at P6/10/12) at any lag but 600 on a per-frame-changing state, so budget error as edges per window → projects/local__sandbox-lag-compensation-error-vs-fix-conversion.md#E1
- `[process][tooling][state][stats][testing]` A writer-fed stat undercounts (370/400) when an event path skips the writer and a history delta-sum overshoots (395 vs 195) on renames, both silently; only an independent recount sees both, and clamping to it fixes overshoot only → projects/local__sandbox-event-writer-vs-delta-sum-ground-truth-check.md#E1
- `[songwriting][suno-prompting][distribution][testing]` Suno Trending ignores creator followers like TikTok's FYP (rho -0.05 now, -0.22 weekly; follower ranker tau 0.04 vs engagement 0.35) and Weekly is velocity-weighted (likes/hour -0.79 > totals -0.72); Now defeats single-read signals → projects/local__suno.md#E6
- `[songwriting][suno-prompting][distribution][testing]` Suno's reach-vs-like-rate split (-0.93 across Trending) vanishes inside one creator's catalog (-0.05 over 40 songs with 20+ plays; all-songs -0.47 is small-denominator noise); floor plays and re-test within a creator before applying board patterns → projects/local__suno.md#E7
- `[songwriting][lyrics][structure][testing]` On 28 real tagged Suno lyrics, repeated-first-line chorus detection found nothing in 14 (fixtures had 8/8) though it agreed with tags 13/14 when it fired; locate by tag regex first, repeated text as fallback → projects/local__suno.md#E8
- `[songwriting][lyrics][testing]` Title-in-chorus first/last looks confirmed on Suno Trending (89%) but random placement already gives 78% for short title-saturated choruses (excess 11 pts, bar 20); compute a per-song chance baseline before citing a placement rate → projects/local__suno.md#E9
- `[songwriting][structure][testing]` Missing-reply + payoff budget: in an order-2 listener the final chorus's new line (9.4 bits) out-surprises the first dropped reply (6.5), later omissions fade (4.1, 3.4, outro 0.5); keep the rest of that chorus verbatim so the silence is the only change → projects/local__suno.md#E10
- `[songwriting][structure][narrative][testing]` Causal ledger on a real story song: 11/18 setups reach the end and the bridge is the lone relay hub (delete it: 61% to 25%, six tokens stranded); rerun the ledger with each beat removed and give hub tokens a second route → projects/local__suno.md#E11
- `[songwriting][lyrics][tooling][testing]` Spelling-rule syllable counter cuts held-out lyric-word error 44.7% to 13.8% (dev 8.7%); residue is compounds (something, maybe) and mid-word hiatus (ideas, fluid), which need a lookup, not more regex → projects/local__suno.md#E12
- `[songwriting][lyrics][singability][tooling][testing]` Open-vowel check from letters is 44-46% wrong (fine, time, love, truth); digraph rules 10% held-out; dropping a reply can leave a closed vowel (eat) as the held line end, so re-check held vowels after cutting words → projects/local__suno.md#E13
- `[songwriting][rhyme][testing]` "ABBA stays open" is invisible to open-rhyme count (ABAB = ABBA = 1.0) and visible only as oldest-wait age (2 vs 1 vs AABB 0); over all 203 six-line schemes three summary metrics collide for 74%, so report the per-line trace → projects/local__suno.md#E14
- `[songwriting][lyrics][conversation][suno-prompting][testing]` Casual ellipsis saves 2.25 syllables/line and cuts over-budget lines 9/12 to 4/12 in a 170 BPM song; the rest are content-dense (a padded no is long by design), so cut ideas or add a bar there → projects/local__suno.md#E15
- `[songwriting][rhyme][tooling][testing]` Rhyme-resolution verdicts flip on 18% of same-vowel pairs between last-consonant and whole-coda comparison (ask/back: perfect vs assonance); a declared-target checker must state its coda rule and flag cluster pairs → projects/local__suno.md#E16
- `[songwriting][structure][lyrics][testing]` Word novelty misses a late-section budget overrun (final chorus 27% new words, below chorus 2's 38%; no line over the >3 flag) that a line-level listener catches; measure final-chorus novelty in unheard lines, not words → projects/local__suno.md#E17
- `[songwriting][structure][lyrics][suno-prompting][testing]` Verse/chorus contrast is invisible in Suno Trending lyric text: chorus vs verse 1 shows 2+ contrast axes in 49% of songs, same as verse 2 vs verse 1 (49%); prompt contrast through style/section cues, not lyric shape → projects/local__suno.md#E18
- `[songwriting][structure][lyrics][testing]` Suno Trending choruses mutate: 26% verbatim; with 3+ instances the last differs 93%, the second already 48%; don't assume fixed chorus text, and make the final chorus change one thing, not several → projects/local__suno.md#E19
- `[ui][input][state][gamedev][testing]` Agreement-vs-state aliasing follows the true repeat length: with period jitter 15/16 agreement flagged 40 stale at L=15 and 0 at L=31 while the age tag flagged all 620; find the real cycle or stamp age → projects/local__sandbox-agreement-aliasing-period-jitter-reverify.md#E1
- `[ui][input][state][gamedev][testing]` Mismatch staleness on a two-role priority resolver aliases at lcm(15,20)=60 (0/600 flagged at L=60,120; 240-320 at L=15..45) while the age tag flags all 600; per-role periods do not locate the blind lag → projects/local__sandbox-resolver-mismatch-aliasing-lcm-lags.md#E1
- `[testing][state][determinism]` A sweep tally can be wrong twice at once: a coerced no-winner sentinel inflated distinct coverage 4 to 5 and fail-fast stopped after 5 of 12 cases; keep winners, no-winner and errors as separate counters and run to completion → projects/local__sandbox-sentinel-and-failfast-coverage-sweep.md#E1
- `[songwriting][suno-prompting][distribution][testing]` Suno Trending Now is a cached snapshot: 0/28 positions moved in an hour while songs gained 70 likes, and hour like-deltas don't track rank (+0.09); check whether a board's order moves before fitting what ranks it → projects/local__suno.md#E20
- `[gamedev][state][determinism][testing]` A dropped frame-perfect cancel has two silent causes: gate set after the read (0 vs 1 accepted) and a NaN phase timer (0 of 12 frames accepted, no error); order the gate first and isFinite-guard timers → projects/local__tick-gate-order-nan-timer-poisoning.md#E1

- `[localization][ai-video][testing]` Size Spanish ad dialogue by syllables, not characters: 12 lines were +6% chars but +55% syllables and 1.21x spoken time (line range 0.85-1.61x), so count each Spanish line against the clip before generating → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E2

- `[tiktok-shop][testing][stats]` A creative A/B test needs ~7,750 impressions per creative for CTR 1.0% vs 1.5% (1,000 impressions gives 0.17 power) and ~383k impressions per creative for CVR 2% vs 3%, so hooks and CTR can be judged early but conversion cannot → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E4
- `[tiktok-shop][unit-economics][testing]` Break-even ROAS = price / contribution: on a 30 USD SKU +10 pts commission (3.26) equals a 15% refund rate (3.29), and the refund admin fee is 20% of the referral fee capped at 5 USD (binds only above ~417 USD) → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E5
- `[tiktok-shop][unit-economics][testing]` A free sample pays back after ~1.26 affiliate orders (15% refunds), so a 30% posting rate needs 4.2 orders per poster and 10% needs 12.6; 25% commission lifts break-even 39% → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E6
- `[tiktok-shop][compliance][testing]` Corrected claims-lint sources: 'even with disclaimers' covers exaggerated body-transformation promises, wellness cure/prevent claims are banned outright, before/after photos have a narrow exception, and free product is a material connection under 16 CFR 255.5 → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E7
- `[tiktok-shop][compliance][testing]` Size claims-lint expectations: a regex lint caught 9 of 25 (36%) of TikTok's own unseen prohibited examples (0 false blocks), so use it as a pre-filter and review scripts against the live policy page → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E8
- `[tiktok-shop][compliance][localization]` A spoken Spanish disclosure costs 6 syllables short or 18 plain; an 18-syllable Spanish hook plus the plain one needs 4.93 s vs a 2.8 s window, so put the disclosure on screen instead of spending hook syllables → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E9
- `[tiktok-shop][unit-economics][testing]` Break-even ROAS is 2.32 for paid-only orders and 3.29 for affiliate orders (29.99 SKU, 15% commission and refunds); a 25% affiliate mix blends to 2.50, so one 2.5 target loses money on affiliate orders → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E10
- `[tiktok-shop][process][testing]` Sample and commission windows: free-sample posts up to 33 days after request, refundable refunds up to 49 days after purchase, and an Open commission edit needs a 30-day lead (Target 5) before it applies → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E11
- `[tiktok-shop][testing][stats]` For a 10% lift, completion needs ~3,763 views per arm and 6-second view ~2,389 but click-through ~163k impressions, so decide the Spanish-register and AI-vs-native tensions on completion or 6s view → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E12
- `[tiktok-shop][testing][stats]` A 4-style test with 6 pairwise comparisons has a 26.5% false-positive rate unadjusted and needs ~11,957 impressions per style (1.54x) after correction, ~143k for a 4x3 matrix; a fifth style adds only 1.10x → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E13
- `[tiktok-shop][ai-video][compliance]` TikTok Shop allows AI video if the real product is shown accurately and AI content is labelled (toggle or on-screen note); AI product shots that change or fabricate the item are prohibited, while AI dubbing and translation are permitted → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E14
- `[tiktok-shop][unit-economics][testing]` A refundable sample earns cash when fewer than ~59% of purchasing creators hit the sales threshold and beats a free sample by (1-hit rate) x net price at equal success, but low take-up cuts order volume (22 vs 90 per 100 offers at 25% take-up) → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E15
- `[tiktok-shop][unit-economics][testing]` Break-even ROAS rises convexly with the Shop Ads commission (assumed to replace the affiliate rate): 2.32 at 0%, 2.89 at 10%, 3.83 at 20%, ~160 at 50% → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E16
- `[localization][ai-video][testing]` Dubbing English to Spanish by speed-up alone fixes 6/22 lines at 1.10x, 8/22 at 1.15x and 14/22 at 1.25x (worst 1.61x), so rewrite or split about a third of lines → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E17
- `[tiktok-shop][unit-economics][testing]` GMV Max commission saving (-3 pts) cuts paid break-even ROAS from 2.32 to 2.19 and saves $0.90/order, but join only if the required ROI target sits above break-even (2.0 would lose $1.27/order) → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E18
- `[tiktok-shop][process][testing]` SPS-style order weighting (0.7 per 15 days): a 10-day 25% return stretch peaks at 8.0% and clears under 5% in 42 days (flat window 82), a 30-day stretch in 69; 3.5 SPS gates affiliate access → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E19
- `[localization][ai-video][testing]` Spanish on-screen captions are only ~8% more words (+9% words/sec) and none of 22 exceeded TikTok's 10 words/sec, so timing pressure is in spoken syllables, not captions → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E20
- `[tiktok-shop][unit-economics][testing]` A discount must be paid back in volume: 1.28x orders at 10% off, 1.79x at 20%, 2.95x at 30%, 8.5x at 40%, and contribution hits zero at 45.4% off (29.99 SKU, fixed cost and shipping) → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E21
- `[tiktok-shop][unit-economics][testing]` Price floor for ROAS 3 by cost share (shipping 4.50, 15% refunds): 16 at 20%, 24 at 30%, 48 at 40%, none at 50%; a 10 USD item at 30% cost needs ROAS 14, so screen products by cost share and price → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E22
- `[tiktok-shop][testing][stats]` Peeking inflates false winners: stopping at the first p<0.05 gives 8.2% with 2 looks, 14.1% with 5, 16.5% with 7 daily looks, 19.2% with 10; Pocock critical z is 2.41 for 5 looks → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E23
- `[tiktok-shop][testing][stats]` Daily monitoring of a CTR test (6 looks, Pocock z 2.46) needs 10,748 impressions vs 7,750 (1.39x), moving the finish from day 6 to day 8 at 1,500/day → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E24
- `[tiktok-shop][localization][compliance]` A Spanish hook plus a 6-syllable spoken disclosure fits 2.8 s for only 2/10 hooks (5/10 at 1.15x speed-up, all at 1.25x); English fits 8/10, so show the disclosure on screen → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E25
- `[tiktok-shop][testing][stats]` Style matrix with daily looks: 6 comparisons + 6 looks give z 3.08 and 15,186 impressions per style (1.96x), below the 2.14x product of the two corrections; 4x3 matrix ~182k impressions, 11 days at 1,500/day → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E26
- `[tiktok-shop][unit-economics][testing]` Samples during a 20% promotion: free-sample payback 1.26 -> 2.26 orders (7.5 per poster at 30% posting), refundable cash break-even hit rate 0.59 -> 0.49; at 40% off payback needs 10.7 orders → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E27
- `[tiktok-shop][unit-economics][testing]` A 3-point GMV Max fee saving equals 3.11 points of Shop Ads commission; with the saving, paid break-even ROAS is 2.41/2.68/3.03/3.48 at 5/10/15/20% ads commission → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E28
- `[tiktok-shop][process][testing]` A new shop at 5 organic orders/day opens sampling on day 11, posts by day 30, can judge its first free-sample batch on day 44 (day 53 at 2/day), refundable refunds by day 60 → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E29
- `[tiktok-shop][unit-economics][testing]` Paid ads on the 29.99 SKU break even at ROAS 2.32 (list), 2.56 (10% off), 2.94 (20%), 3.23 (25%), 3.64 (30%); the deepest discount keeping ROAS 3 is 21.1% (price 23.66) → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E30
- `[testing][state][input]` A size-only cover keeps only the flat at N=P (none at 2P, 3P, 4P) for P=5..12 and differs from the exact cover at N=P (100 vs 10 at P=5), so use the full plateau as the control → projects/local__2026-10-08__a-size-only-cover-keeps-only-the-flat-at-n-equals-p-and-loses-2p-3p-so-the-multiple-of-p-plateau-needs-the-exact-subset-test.md#E1
- `[ui][input][state][gamedev][testing]` Jitter amplitude sweep J=0..8: validity plus cutoff at max valid lag lost 0 each time, one below lost 2-14; best gate alone erred 2/4/6/10 → projects/local__validity-cutoff-jitter-amplitude-sweep-sixth-angle.md#E1
- `[determinism][testing][gamedev][state]` Seed-and-count save/load resumes exactly but does not isolate: an extra RNG consumer changed 9 of 20 shared-stream outcomes, 0 with per-mechanic streams → projects/local__seeded-rng-save-load-vs-shared-stream-coupling.md#E1
- `[api][data][correctness]` Offset paging lost 160 of 1000 rows when the consumer deleted rows mid-scan while a keyset cursor lost 0, and deletes alone did not break a non-unique key cursor (inserts on ties do) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E92
- `[concurrency][correctness][debugging]` A lock around only the check or only the act of check-then-act still oversold the last unit (20/70 and 60/70 interleavings vs 4/6 unlocked); only a lock spanning both gave 0/70 → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E93
- `[api][graphql][performance][correctness]` A DataLoader cache shared across requests cut queries to 1 but served 200 of 250 stale values after writes; a per-request loader gave 5 queries and 0 stale (250 with no loader) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E94
- `[tooling][process][testing]` Checking a zero-pushed summary against the confirmed source count caught 3 of 3 failing worlds (naive trust 0 of 3) but a scanned-vs-source directory comparison separated only the wrong-directory one; check the source first, then a cause-specific signal → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E95
- `[tiktok-shop][ai-video][compliance][testing]` No single publish check caught all 9 violating videos (3 to 5 slipped through) and mix-real-and-AI mode still left 5 of 8 violating, so run label, accuracy and claims checks together even in mix mode → projects/pappydapimp69__tiktok-shop.md#E31
- `[calibration][measurement]` Certainty from an easy cue overstated accuracy by 20 points (1.00 vs 0.793) and a fitted recalibration map broke when the input mix shifted (ECE 0.010 to 0.197); fix the cue, or re-fit per distribution → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E96
- `[testing][process]` A quarantine skip hides 4 defects until release and a presence-only check hides 5 more after it; run structural pre-checks on held files and use a substance check at the release gate → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E51
- `[viz][data][testing]` A structural-checks suite stays green while a shared fallback id silently merges two sources and inflates counters; assert displayed-equals-source totals and id disjointness, and prefix catch-alls per source → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E97
- `[viz][narration][timing]` A lexical novelty gate catches captions that restate on-screen counts but not paraphrases, and exact accumulated-dt compares drop the final reveal cue; use a frame tolerance → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E98
- `[modding][architecture][correctness][data]` Staged define/patch/final loading cut 8 load-order outcomes to 1 for additive mods but left 2 when two mods SET the same field; priority ties need a unique-id key (priority-only 2 outcomes, with id 1) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E12
- `[gamedev][testing][state][process]` A pause test on the guarded base update let 4/4 missing-guard wrapper mutants survive and a partial field list 3/4; only a whole-state diff killed 4/4 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E13
- `[git][workflow][process][state]` Conflicts per merge grew near-linearly with branch age on hot files (0.10 at 6h, 1.3 at 1d, 27.4 at 20d; slope 1.08) and superlinearly only on sparse overlap; rebase matched merge except edit-then-revert (1 vs 0); a squash-landed prefix gave 4 phantom conflicts on merge vs 0 on patch-id rebase → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E14
- `[architecture][input]` owning the hit-test comparisons only pays off if you also own the coordinate mapping, keep presses in a list and disable the framework path → projects/local__sandbox-own-four-comparisons-boundary.md#E1
- `[testing][state]` a test hook that sets wizard or FSM state without the real transition passes route assertions yet skips invalidation, derived state and overlay; route hooks through the transition and diff against a fresh rebuild → projects/local__sandbox-wizard-test-hook-vs-real-transition.md#E1
- `[simulation][architecture]` a shared state vector keeps picture and ending in agreement only if both read it through one predicate and no eased presentation value feeds the picture → projects/local__sandbox-one-state-vector-pheromone-view-outcome.md#E1
- `[ux][animation][accessibility]` A directional 200-300ms step transition still loses its direction cue if rapid input restarts it from the old rest slot (0.296-unit teleport vs 0.000 when retargeted from the current position), so make step transitions interruptible by retargeting and announce once per state change. → projects/local__sandbox-step-transition.md#E1
- `[distribution][analytics][browser]` Opt-in localStorage telemetry: size the export for the paste limit (a raw per-run log passes 2000 chars at 222 runs; quota only at ~554k), store aggregates (42 chars), and add a hit counter for totals since exporter self-selection bias reached 10.1% once only 3 of 40 players exported. → projects/local__sandbox-opt-in-telemetry.md#E1
- `[performance][verification][testing]` Behind a cull, a proxy edit is decoupled from true cost only when it lands outside the filter's pass-through (200->102 objects, draw calls 5->5); the same-size in-view edit moved calls 5->2 and widening the window moved them with the proxy fixed, so read the true-cost gauge per edit → projects/local__sandbox-proxy-gauge.md#E1
- `[teaching][proxy][testing]` Modelled spacing: in-session recall falls with gap (0.98 to 0.00) while delayed retention is an inverted U peaking at gap 4 (0.25; massed 0.00, gap 64 0.005), and with no difficulty-gain bonus every gap ties (0.0718), so the effect rests on that mechanism → projects/local__sandbox-desirable-difficulty.md#E1
- `[songwriting][rhyme][testing]` Two-pass lettering only helps when assonance competes with perfect rhyme; it ties on clean stanzas and cannot fix a spelling judge or an unscoped search → projects/local__sandbox-two-pass-lettering-needs-scope-and-phonetic-judge.md#E1
- `[music][audio][design][state]` Bar-queued horizontal bounds lag to one bar but cuts once per authored branch; vertical never cuts but lags by its fade length, so a short fade closes most of the gap → projects/local__sandbox-adaptive-music-horizontal-vs-vertical-lag-and-cut-tradeoff.md#E1
- `[ui][testing]` 2-lag shadow average stays flat only at N=P and N=2P with bisect and intmask covers (P6 7/2, P9 100/9); sorted-search covers need ascending lists → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E15
- `[testing][method][input]` A cover test weaker than full containment shifts the 2-lag shadow average (P9 100/9 becomes 288 with any-for-all, 168 with length-only; P11 215/11 becomes 550 and 330), while bisect, groupby, reduce and sorted-union covers at offset 1811 all keep the dict values → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E16
- `[ui][input][state][gamedev][testing]` Dict, heappop, heapq-merge-union and carried bisect-cursor covers all give the same 2-lag averages at offset 4211 (P8 8 then 18, P9 100/9 then 220/9), flat at N=P and 2P; the any-for-all mutant gives 196 and 288 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E17
- `[gamedev][economy][design][scaling]` Crafting raw demand grows like (inputs/yield)^depth, not k^depth: yield 3 with 3 inputs stayed flat at 1.0, a 0.8 soft tier penalty lifted depth-6 raw from 729 to 2781, rarity share 1/4 only scaled trips (31 to 122) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E18
- `[data-toy][gamification]` Decoupled daily game loops match real-time feedback only if logged actions truly change the outcome; a zero-effect proxy engages fully and saves nothing → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E19
- `[web][indexing]` Client-rendered content indexes empty until render and stays empty past the budget or on a script error; vitals only tiebreak so they cannot rescue relevance 0 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E20
- `[determinism][rng][state][testing]` Splitting RNG into streams isolates only when stream identity is a stable name or key: shared stream flipped 20/20 outcomes, ordinal-seeded streams 19/20, name-seeded and symbolic keys 0, raw-state save resumes exactly in O(1) vs 5000-draw replay → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E21
- `[pathfinding][geometry][testing]` Conservative voxelization keeps a gap only if its width is at least 2*cs-1 (width = cs only at aligned offsets: 8/24 at cs3, 6/24 at cs4); unflanked diagonals cut corners on 7 of 7 paths; flow-field crossover about 1.6 agents at any cell size → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E22
- `[prompt-engineering][structure]` XML tags beat plain headers on lookalike headers but collide when data contains the closing tag; escape untrusted data or use a nonce tag name → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E23
- `[gamedesign][simulation][method]` A per-action decay meter is playstyle-neutral only for the action set you count: count every action and exploration is taxed 4x again, count only goal actions and wanderers pay nothing, so name the counted set and keep a 10-20% timer weight (sandbox, 5 scenarios) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E99
- `[ui][input][state][gamedev][testing]` Exact 2-lag shadow averages recorded at offsets 709 and 3001 reproduce at offset 503 across dict, two-pointer and bitmask covers (P7, P10, P11); rerun recorded values at a fresh offset with a must-differ control → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E24
- `[testing][algorithm][state]` Age-cutoff 2-lag averages are offset-independent (P10: 15 and 65/2 at offsets 3001 and 709), but a heap-pop cover collapses to 0 on descending lists while two-pointer and dict hold, so stream covers need a reversed-order control → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E25
- `[nlp][fairness][measurement][testing]` Judging an embedding's corpus bias from nearest-neighbour analogy answers fails in a toy PPMI model: 3CosAdd returned the input's own topic neighbour 199/200 and scored lower on the biased corpus (0.22) than the unbiased control (0.30), a topic-constrained parallel-offset readout scored 0.99 vs 0.46, and a correct queen survived deleting the gender axis (40/40; forced-choice 1.00 -> 0.61), so read bias with a matched control, a topic-cancelling readout and a forced-choice test → projects/local__sandbox-analogy-bias-readout.md#E1
- `[gamedev][input]` local-multiplayer join cloning and reconnect identity registry fail independently (lockout vs slot theft) and do not compound; test second-type join and stranger-before-owner separately → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E100
- `[documentation][testing][tutorial]` Doctest catches stale doc examples only where they are wired in and attempted (4 of 5 stale claims caught; a plain-fence claim stayed invisible, unrun docs caught 0), and a file with no prompt examples passes with failed=0, so assert attempted is greater than 0 per doc file → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-doctest-wiring.md#E1
- `[3d][performance][compression]` Draco shrinks only the download and adds decode CPU, so it loses end-to-end on fast links; cut VRAM with LOD and KTX2 instead, and enable Draco by measured link speed vs decode throughput → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E26
- `[accessibility][html]` audit the alt attribute's presence in markup, not spoken output (lenient readers hide omission); alt="" on a meaningful image silently deletes it → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E101
- `[testing][state]` first-cover dict and stream covers (two-pointer, heap) agree on ascending landing lists; 2-lag averages flat at N=P and 2P for P9 and P11 at offset 1201 → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E102
- `[state][testing]` stream-style covers (iterator two-pointer, heappop walk) equal dict membership only on ascending landing lists; reversed or rotated input breaks them while dict stays order-free → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E103
- `[testing][state]` Dict first-cover and two-pointer/heappop covers also agree at P11 (215/11) and P12 (25) with small offsets 61/83; lower the offset, not the modulus, to keep cross checks cheap -> projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E104
- `[testing][state]` Dict first-cover and iterator two-pointer/heappop covers agree across offsets (P7 38/7, P10 15 at N=P); two-pointer cover needs ascending input or it silently diverges -> projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E105
- `[testing][input][state]` stream covers match dict membership only when both lists share an order (heap-pop needs ascending); reversing one side alone breaks two-pointer too, so use set membership for unordered input → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E106
- `[typography][web-fonts][layout][testing]` Only font-display optional is shift-free for every load time (6 of 6 in a timeline model), while swap plus one size-adjust factor leaves per-string residuals up to 3.4px in both directions, so measure real strings in fixed-width labels → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E27
- `[gamedesign][input][state][testing]` four-clause game audit: clauses leak (drop a fails b and c; drop c fails b), so probe legibility before ordering or a clause-c defect gets blamed on scoring; a signal is legible only if it leaves the true conjunction as sole survivor → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-gamedesign-four-clauses.md#E1
- `[ml][benchmark][stats][evaluation]` GPT-3 few-shot's SuperGLUE win over fine-tuned BERT-Large (71.8 vs 69.0, recomputed 71.79 vs 69.03) is a mean over a 4-4 task split: dropping COPA and ReCoRD gives -3.06, WiC is -20.2, and GPT-3 leads in 65.9% of 6435 task weightings, so read per-task rows and the worst task before generalizing → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-ml-benchmarks.md#E1
- `[ui][accessibility][input]` build the focus grid from rows visible through their whole ancestor chain and assert by identity; shared-origin save keys and any-key start also reshape or hijack the title menu → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E107
- `[search][testing][evaluation][regression]` In a 10-topic model, a silent tags-boost bug passed a 3-query spot-check and needed a 4-query judgment list (MRR 1.0 to 0.85, nDCG 0.998 to 0.903), while a body-boost reorder inside the top 3 left P@3 and MRR flat and only nDCG@5 caught it (0.998 to 0.843); gate on graded nDCG over a list covering each decoy-prone query class. → projects/local__2026-10-09__judgment-list-catches-silent-boost-bug-spot-check-and-precision-miss-it.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][simulation][narrative][testing]` Playtest bots must be limited to interface-visible state: a tag-reading bot completed a scarce chain 300/300 where legible bots (attachment, bedtime, mood-keyed self-interest) completed 47%; attachment is not free realism (47% at 0.5 to 36% at 0.8 when the selector repeats beats) and a verb-guard check passes vacuously unless the key-summary line is stripped (0 vs 4 misses) → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-play-the-legible-game.md#E1
- `[simulation][npc][architecture][testing]` In a 24-NPC decay toy the sticky floor kept 24/24 strong bonds (and 12/12 hostile ones), asymmetric rate only delayed loss to 62 versus 15 days, and debounce length versus contact cadence decided whether decay ran at all — sticky flags failed 20/20 on mid-strength bonds → projects/local__sandbox-npc-memory-guardrails.md#E1
- `[simulation][narrative]` Toy oral-tradition model: lineage beats geography only until neighbours borrow 3 of 5 types, a relative pitch-range cue transfers across baselines where level fails (1.00 vs 0.50), and character identification breaks when cue spacing drops below resolution (K=8) -> projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E28
- `[testing][isolation][state]` order-dependence hides in the declared order; search orders and shrink to a failing pair, rebuild state per test since cleanup only covers remembered keys, and accumulation bugs need run length not order → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E108
- `[layout][viewport][mobile][testing][text]` word budget per screen = min over viewports at the real font size (105 words at 16px but 68 at 20px on 375x560; a 900px-tall-only test missed 86 failing counts), so write to about half and top-align → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-presentation-layout.md#E1
- `[speech][narrative]` Pitch range alone detects a quoted voice at ~0.71 balanced accuracy and decays toward 0.55 as retelling flattens quotes; new-voice registration is capped near 0.11 by listener rejection, so mark new voices explicitly -> projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E109
- `[dialogue][speech]` Quoted-voice cue: widen pitch range and break before the quote, not pitch level; at the paper's variance range is only a moderate cue (AUC 0.76), and a new voice near a known one needs explicit marking → projects/local__quote-voice-range-vs-level-and-unfamiliar-voice-rejection.md#E1
- `[rigging][skinning]` rotation-blend skinning keeps ring radius at any twist/bend but only with the pivot on the limb axis; off-axis pivot drifts the centerline 2*d*sin(theta/4) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E110
- `[search][evaluation][method]` A search regression hides from a spot check unless it touches the sampled queries and from any metric that discards the changed dimension (P@3 and MRR missed a best-doc swap nDCG saw), so gate on a full judgment list with nDCG plus MRR; chunks smaller than the repeat unit lost the frequency signal (toy tf-idf sandbox, 4 scenarios) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E111
- `[game-design][asymmetric-hidden-info][fairness]` Seeker fairness is set by marker frequency: a noise marker every hidden move equals omniscience (0.544 hidden wins), none gives the hidden side 0.810; grace plus safe spawn removes turn-1 captures → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E29
- `[rng][input][state][determinism]` hidden session seed beats timing manipulation only until a few rolls are observed (seed recoverable in 7 for strong generators); record the seed for replay and limit roll-driven parts to rarity → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E112
- `[simulation][method][scheduling]` Thin a reward schedule to variable-ratio only after an acquisition criterion is met (switch-after-learning got CRF-speed acquisition with VR persistence; switching at 2 rewards never acquired) -> projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E30
- `[viz][canvas][animation][mobile][testing]` Two-futures growth time-lapse: fit each panel to min(W,H) (height-only clips 68% on portrait side-by-side) and composite the glow per panel, still cap nodes since composite fails at 1000 → projects/local__2026-10-09__two-futures-timelapse-fit-min-axis-and-composite-filter-cost.md#E1
- `[ui][gamedev][accessibility][testing]` Non-diegetic wins urgent reads only while always-on clutter keeps the read inside the deadline (4/4 up to 10 elements, 0/4 at 14 in a model), so pair it with contextual fade and give a meta cue a non-hue carrier (hue-only gave a colour-blind viewer 0/4) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E31
- `[reinforcement][agents]` Extinction tolerance is set by the longest dry spell training exposed, not by schedule variance (narrow variable 5 vs wide 16); size patience windows above the max gap -> projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E113
- `[procgen][worldbuilding][testing]` In a toy 6-civ history a state constraint took a template generator from 4126 timeline violations to 0 yet 701 of 751 claimed causes stayed non-genuine (thick rationalizer: 597) — a timeline checker proves ordering, not causality, so validate causes separately → projects/local__sandbox-worldgen-coherence-fork.md#E1
- `[ui][input][state][gamedev][testing]` The 2-lag period-average shadow total is flat at every multiple of P (P5-P8, to 4P for P5/P6), not just N=P and 2P, so "flat only at P and 2P" holds only for N<=2P+1; state the lag range and test one multiple past it. → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E32
- `[ui][input][state][gamedev][testing]` issuperset, ChainMap and OrderedDict first-cover agree exactly on the 2-lag period-average shadow total (P5-P7, base 2207) and are flat at every multiple of P to 3P, so "flat only at P and 2P" is bounded by the lag range tested. → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E33
- `[search][evaluation][testing][chunking]` In a shared-vocabulary tf-idf model the best chunk size equals the answer span (MRR 1.000 at 4 sentences vs 0.611 whole-doc, 0.000 at 1-2), and overlap fixes a misaligned size (0.333 to 1.000) but hurts an aligned one (1.000 to 0.667), so sweep size and overlap together on a judgment list. → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E34
- `[search][testing][evaluation][regression]` An analyzer collision that creates score ties is invisible to a judgment list when the tie-break favors the relevant doc (MRR 1.000 to 1.000) and shows as 1.000 to 0.500 when it does not, so make the tie-break adversarial and include sole-discriminator queries before trusting a green list. → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E35
- `[physics][integration][floating-point]` Semi-implicit Euler fixes explicit Euler's energy blowup (6.9e56x vs 1.055x at 60fps) only for w*dt<2 — at 1.99 it is bounded but swings 200x, at 2.01 it explodes, and fps still changes the phase — so cap/sub-step dt and fix the timestep as well as reordering. → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E36
- `[algorithm][testing][state]` First-cover streaming and four cover predicates (dict, bitmask, sorted-intersection, frozenset) agree at fresh base 4007 for P8/P11 (8,18 and 215/11,460/11), flat only at P and 2P, with base-0 and any-rule controls differing → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E114
- `[algorithm][testing][state]` First-cover plus symdiff, reduce and tuple-index covers agree with dict at base 1409 for P5/P6 (2,26/5 and 7/2,17/2), flat only at P and 2P; an equality mutant (avg 0) and base-0 control differ, so agreement is evidence, not coincidence → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E115
- `[search][evaluation][testing]` Tuning boosts on a 3-query one-class judgment list reached nDCG@5 1.000 but held out 0.809 (MRR 0.750 vs 0.919) while an 18-query mixed list generalized (0.859 vs 0.871): keep an untouched holdout and cover every query class, and note that overfit did not appear when truth was one linear function → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E116
- `[deploy][web][testing]` Preview the shipped bundle at the host's exact URL shape: plain-relative refs load 5/5 at /myrepo/ but 0/3 at /myrepo with no redirect and absolute paths 0/2 under any prefix; fetch() paths follow the page (deeper page 1/3), so also check the served URL with a retry window, not the build status → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E117
- `[process][research][algorithm]` Distinct-root counting fixes the citation echo chain (5 sources/1 root loses to 2/2) but inflates when a derived_from link is dropped (1→2, 3 if all), double-counts aliases until merged, needs cycle handling, and scores independent-but-wrong sources 2: roots measure independence, not truth → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E118
- `[physics][algorithm][determinism]` Semi-implicit Euler stays bounded only at a constant dt with dt*omega<2 (1.05x at 0.1, 200x at 1.99, inf at 2.01) and a variable step like 0.1/1.9 peaks 200x vs 2x at constant 1.0; use a fixed-step accumulator → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E119
- `[speech][communication][simulation][identifiability]` A 7/38/55 channel split is measurable only from stimuli where words, tone and face disagree (conflict fit error 0.003; all-channels-equal data returns 0.33 each), so quote it only for liking/sincerity judgments under conflict and treat fact transfer as a function of words delivered -> projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E120
- `[audio][browser][tooling]` OfflineAudioContext.currentTime is 0 until render, so effects keyed to ac.currentTime collapse to t=0: give Web Audio one injectable now() and a step-function score so offline export renders the same mix → projects/local__brain-film-explainer.md#E1
- `[viz][narration][timing]` Stretching a caption window to meet reading time slows every animation in it (factors hit 2.4): print per-scene warp, keep it near 1.5 and buy the rest with fewer words or authored holds → projects/local__brain-film-explainer.md#E2
- `[tooling][process][ci]` A steward count that does not move after sync may be a workflow run that died before any step (3s, no log): check the run for that SHA, prove the proposal by running the steward locally on it, then rerun the workflow → projects/local__brain-film-explainer.md#E3
- `[knowledge-management][process][viz]` A tension ledger's heading count is not its open count (70 entries, 46 open by entry_status): label raw counts as tracked and take open from the tool's own status classifier → projects/local__brain-film-explainer.md#E4
- `[garden][soil][testing][measurement]` In a Stokes model the soil jar test's 3-minute silt mark is wrong (silt read 7.7 of 40, clay 44.6 of 20; read silt at 2 hours: error 7.6), while testing pH and pooling before leaf colour cut wasted fertilizer from 4 of 14 cases to 0 → projects/local__2026-10-09__home-soil-jar-test-stokes-timing-and-leaf-triage-order.md#E1
- `[astronomy][lunar-phase][verification][timekeeping]` A mean-period lunar phase misses the true new moon by up to 11.9 h (corrected model: 1.2 min over 17 reference moons) and 7th-decimal tuning moves it 0.002 h, so compute the corrected UTC instant and localise per zone (a fixed offset flipped 0 dates in 2024-26 but 8 over 2000-50) → projects/local__sandbox-lunar-phase.md#E1
- `[testing][state][process]` Age-cutoff family roll-up: ~640 variants repeat ~9 findings (2-lag average flat at every multiple of P, cover tests need absolute slots in one order, zero residues need P at least max lag, contiguous-lower residues are one interval around 0, cutoff is largest valid lag) so read this instead of the variants; limits listed → projects/local__age-cutoff-roll-up.md#E1
- `[communication][plain-language][testing][teaching]` A plain-language scorer built on averages ranked 2 of 2 labeled pairs right and survived a shuffle control, but scored a 55-word run-on of easy words as plainer (-1.0); add a longest-sentence term and keep the facts when shortening, since one rater and two pairs show direction only → projects/local__plain-language-scorer.md#E1
- `[ui][input][state][gamedev][testing]` Bisect and union-length covers at offset 1217 reproduce the recorded 2-lag averages of the base-3001 and base-307 entries (P5 2 and 26/5, P6 7/2 and 17/2, P11 215/11 and 460/11), flat at N=P and N=2P; base-0 and any-for-all controls differ → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E37
- `[compression][testing][performance][measurement]` Lossless compression always has losers: a 1-byte stored-flag fallback bounds growth to +1 (RLE grew 4446/8190 inputs, zlib 4096 random bytes -> 4107), and tiny messages expand per-message but shrink 10x batched → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E38
- `[astronomy][lunar-phase][verification][timekeeping][sandbox]` New-moon accuracy depends on the term set: mean period errs 11.9 h, one term 4.4 h, four terms 21 min, extended Meeus 1.2 min (16 moons); a fixed -8 h offset flips the local date for 8 of 620 moons → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E39
- `[narrative][simulation][state][testing][headless]` A state-classifier ending is verifiable by replaying all 729 ordered paths (4 endings, 0 same-state conflicts vs 62 for handler flags), but clamping makes order matter (49/81 sampled choice sets change ending), so enumerate ordered paths → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E40
- `[accessibility][visualdesign][gamedev][testing]` An eased backdrop hides contrast failures from an endpoint-only audit (dog#E104 pairs 16.8:1 and 10.7:1, yet the in-between path bottomed at 3.55:1, 4.11:1 with a per-frame token pick) so audit the dense colour path, not sampled frames (dog#E10 follows change dwell, not path), or put text on a fixed scrim → projects/local__sandbox-cvd-transition-audit.md#E2
- `[chronobiology][caffeine][sleep][simulation]` A caffeine cutoff given as a clock time hides half-life and dose: 100 mg at 14:00 leaves 9 / 30 / 55 mg at 23:00 across half-lives 2.5 / 5 / 10 h, 400 mg at 12 h before bed outweighs 100 mg at 6 h, and a 14:00 cutoff passes a 25 mg target only up to a 2.7 h half-life, so state it as bedtime - half_life*log2(dose/target) and keep the linear model to dietary doses (saturation moved the 400 mg fraction by 20% at an illustrative Km) → projects/local__sandbox-caffeine-cutoff.md#E1
- `[legal][licensing][copyright][sandbox][verification]` No license file means the author keeps all rights (Berne Art 5(2)/9(1), GitHub and GPL-2 s.4 quotes verified verbatim and embedded in the sandbox; 5 of 5 no-license controls grant nothing), but a keyword detector mis-sorted 3 of 14 real licenses on grants and found 6 of 10 expected copyleft files (corpus part, needs the system license directory), so use it only to flag "no grant found", never to say what a license allows; not legal advice → projects/local__open-source-licensing-check.md#E1
- `[design][state][npc]` Per-viewer derivation reveals a phantom only when a lucid viewer's panel is rendered and the other viewer is fooled on that slot; shared delusion, unrendered panel and panel-less NPC give 0 reveals (sandbox 9/9), so budget an offer verb whose receiver runs on world truth → projects/local__sandbox-per-viewer-derivation.md#E1
- `[compression][testing][measurement][limits]` Over the full input space no lossless coder shrinks everything and the best average saving on uniform data is under 2 bits ((L-2)+(L+2)/2^L, enumerated); pair-RLE expanded 92.9% of 4^8 strings, zlib on 4096 high-entropy bytes gave 4107, a 1-byte stored flag capped growth at +1 on all 65792 tested inputs, and a skew-tuned Huffman code averaged 2.755 of 8 bits yet expanded 219 of 256 inputs → projects/local__sandbox-counting-argument-and-coder-expansion.md#E1
- `[algorithm][pathfinding][testing][process]` A grid-cell lookup is not an independent check on a smoothed path: built by centre sampling it certified 120 of 400 clipping paths (G=0 vs continuous C), and with the cell-square grid fix it stays sound only when sampled at most one cell apart (C=0 at step 2.0, 19 clips at 3.0, 380 at 6.0) — keep one continuous padded segment-distance check plus a cell-corner probe obstacle (dog/E34, smoothing-validation/E1, dog-pathfinding/E1) → projects/local__sandbox-grid-validator-sampling.md#E1
- `[insurance][risk-pooling][correlation][simulation]` Pooling cut independent ruin to 1.7e-5 at n=10,000 but a 2% shared catastrophe pinned ruin at 0.020 for any size (capital 15x vs 1.17x), and splitting into 5-50 independent zones made ruin worse (0.096-0.32), beating one zone only past ~100 zones → projects/local__insurance-seed.md#E1
- `[sorting][correctness][algorithm][determinism]` Invalid comparators are unspecified, not just unsorted: a boolean 0/1 comparator echoed 720 of 720 input orders, NaN gave 22 outputs from 120 orders, a cycle gave 12 outputs on 3.11/3.12 but 24 on 3.13, with no error raised; they only bite when the violating tuple is present, and round(v/eps) fixes grouping but still gave 4 orders until the key was (bucket, value) -> projects/local__sandbox-kernel-sorting-comparator.md#E? → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-sorting-comparator.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][simulation][testing][state]` smallmouth#E1's hearsay band guarantees nothing if a trait bonus is added after the clamp (august-10#E6 shape) - sandbox: 5 of 6 confrontations hearsay-only with channel max still 0.25, fires-at-least-once audit green; wrap the whole non-firsthand sum in the band and assert each crossing has a firsthand cause -> projects/pappydapimp69__smallmouth.md#E? → projects/pappydapimp69__smallmouth.md#E3
- `[negotiation][sandbox]` A bargaining model confirms the alternative sets floor, ceiling and deal existence and caps what stubbornness buys (premium 7.1 to 41.3 as the other side's ceiling rises) but refutes "not willpower": toughness still explains most variance (r 0.96 within BATNA), so bound the BATNA kernel instead of dismissing nerve → projects/local__sandbox-negotiation-batna.md#E1
- `[photography][precision][simulation]` Raw's edit headroom is bits recorded where you push, not the format name: in a synthetic model 14-bit raw lifted shadows +4 EV smoothly (100% code coverage) while an 8-bit gamma JPEG banded (31%), yet 12-bit raw was only 1.2x the JPEG in the deepest shadows and 8-bit linear raw was far worse; nothing beyond sensor clip survives in either → projects/local__sandbox-raw-vs-jpeg-tonal-headroom.md#E1
- `[security][algorithm][verification]` Score signing stayed forgeable unless a keyed MAC, a canonical whole-payload encoding and a constant-time compare all held: early-exit compare leaked a full tag in 7949 queries, and unkeyed, score-only and delimiter-free signing each accepted a forgery despite constant-time compare (step count as a timing model) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E41
- `[measurement][attribution][verification][marketing]` Last-click credit has no fixed error sign: in a two-cohort model display got 60*f against a true 54 lift (0 at f=0, over-credited above f=0.9) and a brand channel on organic intent kept 80 credit for 0 lift at every f, so compare with removal lift before cutting or funding a channel → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E42
- `[security][algorithm][verification]` PKCE S256 reproduces the RFC 7636 Appendix B vector only with unpadded URL-safe base64 (standard base64 gives a 44-character challenge ending =), an interceptor redeemed under plain and failed under S256, and the 43-128 verifier rule held at 42/43/128/130 characters (hand-written server model) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E43
- `[gamedev][state][determinism][testing]` Save a seeded RNG as its four state words (0 restore rolls versus 100000 for seed+count, both exact) but give each subsystem its own stream: one added cosmetic roll on a shared stream changed 97% of later gameplay rolls, 0 on split streams; mask restored words to 32 bits (sfc32 in Python only) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E44
- `[empathy][design][research][simulation][method]` Sandbox model of the empathy-map kernel: a sparse-quadrant "needs more research" alarm only works when items need corroboration - an imagined map (precision 0.29), a colleague-only interview map (0.25) and a thin map padded by a vivid simulation exercise (0.31, alarms 4 to 0, false items 0 to 9 at N=3) all look full, so audit item provenance rather than density; harm vanishes at high-fidelity exercise or when designer equals user (a logic model, not human data) → projects/local__sandbox-empathy-map-density-hides-evidence-gap.md#E1
- `[checksums][streaming][security][testing]` A leading whole-stream checksum bounds peak buffer (500 vs 10000) but releases the entire payload before any verdict (10000 bytes on a flipped first byte), and per-chunk CRCs fix that yet silently accept dropped, swapped and truncated chunks unless the header carries a sequence number AND a declared total (seq alone still passed the truncated tail); unkeyed checks all accept a recomputed rewrite (5 of 5) so only a keyed per-chunk MAC closes it, and verified chunks must be decoded incrementally (independent UTF-8 decode corrupted 37 of 40 chunk sizes, incremental 0) → projects/local__sandbox-checksums.md#E5
- `[memorization][teaching][proxy][testing]` Leitner box toy (20 cards, 120 days): once strength grows 2x per recall (gain 1.0) nothing lapses, reset and drop-one-box tie at 140 reviews vs 2,400 daily; below that the better miss rule FLIPS (drop-one 0.88x reviews at gain 0.2, 1.44x at gain 0.7, 4 of 20 cards forgotten when slow), so measure the miss rate before picking one → projects/local__sandbox-leitner-demotion.md#E1
- `[taxes][verification][method]` In a progressive bracket system crossing a threshold taxes only the slice above it (one dollar over 48,475 still nets 0.78), so a raise cannot cut take-home from the brackets alone; the loss shape is a cliff in a benefit or eligibility rule (sandbox: layered 0 decreases, naive-rate myth 6, hypothetical cliff 1; not tax advice) → projects/local__sandbox-taxes.md#E1
- `[painting][perception][sandbox][measurement]` Op-art toy retinal-shift model: zero signal without eye shift, energy exactly 9.00x for 9x contrast, ~1/scale when painted larger (x1/x4 = 4.26) but normalised flow stays scale-invariant and the cited human data show no grating-wavelength effect, so specify contrast and drop the unqualified coarse-dies rule; incoherent-shimmer signature not reproduced (sandbox 9/13) → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-painting-op-perception.md#E1
- `[film][rendering][canvas][structure]` A renderer-swap guard rail of "the first drawer sets the transform" fails on the swap back (120/240 misplaced) and per-frame set still fails when a helper resets after it (80/400); set right before the world draws and make helpers restore, and treat once-per-takeover as safe only for exclusive handoffs with clean helpers (mock canvas) → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-film-renderer-swap.md#E1
- `[ui][architecture][narrative][sandbox]` Porting a piece as a segment: test elements by "does it assert the piece is its own work", not "is it useful" (the useful test kept 100% of the chrome in the model); re-express the narrative half in the host idiom only if the host can show the same value pair in one frame (a one-value host dropped the argument from 10 thesis frames to 0), keep guest idiom when rupture is wanted, and split fused elements by attribute → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E121
- `[testing][state][gamedev]` The age-cutoff 2-lag shadow count is pinned by containment: dict first-cover and xor-reduce covers agree exactly at base 4093 for P5-P7 (flats at P and 2P) while an equality-of-landings rule gives 0 shadows → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E45
- `[cooking][food-safety][method]` Danger-zone time is one cumulative sum that includes the cooling phase (stretches of 57/30/45 min total 132 while a per-stretch reset sees 57; deep pot 674 min vs shallow pan 118; a bath just under 140 F stays in the band) — count minutes in the band, not stretches → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E46
- `[ui-state][defaults][testing]` A pre-selected control duplicating a code default drifts silently after a retune and shows only on an empty profile with a loose camera clamp (stored prefs and a binding clamp mask it) — derive the marker from the constant or test copy-vs-copy on a cleared profile → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E47
- `[input][gamepad][gamedev]` A per-axis stick deadzone skews the angle at every radius and adds extra direction edges only above DZ times sqrt 2 (dead diagonal gaps below it), and a hardcoded cursor modulo is a separate bug — sweep two radii and derive the modulus from the list length → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E48
- `[algorithm][simulation][verification]` Regret tracks travel diameter, not state or action counts (slip factor 8 costs 8x; S=6 at diameter 40 loses 16.0 vs 11.0 for S=24 at diameter 23), and a horizon shorter than the diameter hides the cost — compare worlds by diameter and measure past it → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E49
- `[research][local-events][data]` Recurring annual event dates: resolve conflicts by latest-published primary statement, not citation count or distinct roots — stale values trace to the authority's own prior-year page (majority 3v2 wrong, roots tie 1v1) → projects/pappydapimp69__dhw26.md#E1
