- `[tooling][scraping][local-events]` Empty official events page usually embeds a no-code app (Softr/Airtable): capture its datasource XHR headless, replay in-page with its own paging field (pagingOption.count, not limit — 130/623 otherwise), assert fetched == total, and pull per-record details for venue/links → projects/pappydapimp69__dhw26.md#E2
- `[data][scraping][local-events]` Upcoming-only feed: archive across fetches and explain each missing record by its own end time (ended → aged out, keep; not ended → removed, drop); plain replace loses past events, keep-all resurrects cancelled ones; refuse short fetches → projects/pappydapimp69__dhw26.md#E3
- `[tooling][scraping][local-events]` No-code app (Softr/Airtable): a page block's filter is not the data's filter — the list block gave 623 live records, the detail page's datasource gave all 832 incl. 195 ended; check other blocks for the unfiltered table before archiving around a missing slice → projects/pappydapimp69__dhw26.md#E4
- `[algorithm][determinism][floating-point][testing]` Kahan summation fixes dropped small addends (0.1 x 1e6 error 1.3e-06 to 5.6e-12; ones above 2^53 recovered) but still gave 2 distinct results over 4 orders and returned 0 instead of 2 for 1, 1e100, 1, -1e100, so use Neumaier or fsum for mixed magnitudes → projects/local__sandbox-numerics-kahan-order.md#E1
- `[film][geometry][testing][algorithm]` The 180-degree line-of-action side test matched a real projection in all 179796 pairs tested but needs a half-open rule on an exact cross product: inclusive double-assigns on-line cameras and floats misplace 22 of 59 exactly-on-line decimal positions → projects/local__sandbox-cinematography-line-of-action.md#E1
- `[regex][security][testing][algorithm]` Counted backtracking steps: nested ^(a+)+$ doubled per character (327680 at n=16) and ^(a|aa)+$ grew 1.62x, while ^a+$ and ^(ab+)+$ stayed linear, and a matching input cost only 25 steps, so test crafted non-matches (a trailing newline plus a bang defeats the end anchor) → projects/local__sandbox-regex-redos-steps.md#E1
- `[rendering][algorithm][testing]` sRGB-space lerp darkening grows with colour distance (0.286 at white/black, 0.0001 at 0.02 apart) and the model-matrix normal fails only under non-uniform scale; verify normals by tangent perpendicularity, since a symmetric-light Lambert comparison showed no difference → projects/local__sandbox-shaders-pipeline.md#E1
- `[audio][animation][sync]` Make the audio clock the film's master clock (T = T0 + currentTime - A0), start steps on the bar grid and look up a cut-landing step just past the boundary; a rAF-delta clock and boundary float error each desync score and cuts by up to a bar → projects/local__brain-film-explainer.md#E5
- `[audio][music][design]` A continuous film score needs one bar grid and tune, every scene in a mode of the same parent scale, and layer levels glided over about a bar; resetting the phrase or jumping to unrelated keys at each cut makes every scene sound like a restart → projects/local__brain-film-explainer.md#E6
- `[audio][browser][tooling]` A Web Audio voice cap released in onended never releases in an OfflineAudioContext render, so the export silently drops most notes (3067/4863 windows near-silent); bypass it offline and RMS-scan exports for silence → projects/local__brain-film-explainer.md#E7
- `[algorithm][determinism][content][testing]` Hash-sorted round-robin deals 42 ids over 6 buckets with spread 0 in 200/200 namespaces (modulo median 7, worst 15) but moves 53% of ids when one is added vs 0% for modulo; both move 86% on K 6 to 7, so freeze the cast or keep modulo for growing sets → projects/local__brain-train.md#E1
- `[persuasion][conversation][verification][sandbox][model]` Toy model of tone-escalation drift: unchecked claims all drift (0.00 at turn 6 to 1.00 at 24), a same-turn whole-document fact check holds exposure at 0 at full tone while local-only or softened-tone does not, but late correction cost fewer edits (0.92 and 0.76 ratio), so justify same-turn checks by exposure, not edit cost; model only → projects/local__proj.md#E1
- `[process][prompt-engineering][observability]` Against a renderer that silently ignores input, a hand deny-list leaked 10/16 dead uses and a hand allowlist blocked 4 working ones; an allowlist derived from a probe of observed effect leaked 0/0 and survived drift, but only for stateless tags (a single-use canary admitted a once-per-block tag, 3/6 leaks) → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E52
- `[process][harness][authorization]` Ask Brain before asking the owner, and train it on a miss; keep asking only for guard-blocked or irreversible steps, and then prepare the patch and report in one line → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E122
- `[tooling][process][testing]` Anchoring the steward's provenance negation check to the first verification marker fixes 3 of 12 false holds and still holds every genuine negation (patch filed as a sandbox, unapplied) → projects/pappydapimp69__memory.md#E11
- `[multi-agent][architecture][testing][security]` Hold the channel, do not grant authority: in a toy model 120 forged privileged messages executed 120 under a believed sender field, 90 under a written rule applied 1-in-4, 0 under port-stamped identity, but 120 again when a relay was granted authority or identity came from a sender-written label, and 120 on a stolen port → projects/local__sandbox-channel-held-authority.md#E1
- `[monetization][model][sandbox]` A revenue model reproduced premium as default (12/20 cells at fit 0.5, all reach 1e5 and below), hybrid winning earlier as retention rises (reach 1e8 at 0.5 to 1e6 at 0.95) and free-to-play needing fit near 0.8 at scale (0 cells at fit 0.5 or with x1.5 spend), but halving premium store conversion handed free-to-play 8 cells and pay-to-win only lost past a churn slope of 0.18 to 0.72 → projects/local__sandbox-monetization-model-choice.md#E1
- `[serialization][json][javascript][verification]` JSON big-id rounding is a property of the double-typed consumer, not the JSON text (Python keeps 2^53+1, Node rounds it): only ids not exactly representable corrupt (500/1000 at 2^53, 750/1000 at 2^54, 1000 ids collapse to 251 values), a parse-stringify-parse test passes 1000/1000 while 500 are wrong, and the exact guard is BigInt(Number(lit)) vs BigInt(lit), not printed text or digit count; quote ids and re-test on the real runtime → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-serialization.md#E1
- `[astronomy][lunar-phase][verification][testing][sandbox]` A lunar regression table of 7+ spread-out new moons cannot be passed by a mean-period model under 5 h but 1-3 rows can (2.3 h), a spacing check passed it, and the table max 11.85 h understates the 620-moon max 14.1 h (17.6 h if anchored on the table's first row) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E50
- `[tooling][process][testing][search]` A keyword gate's fire rate climbs with diff size (live-style 1/7/36/93% at 50/200/800/3200 words of unrelated code; idf-mass 0/0/1/5%) so rarity weighting shifts the curve rather than removing it (topical prose still 89% at 3200), misses edits using only a heading's common words (recall 0.38), and gains from 200-word co-location (0.89 to 0.46) → projects/local__sandbox-keyword-gate-rarity.md#E1
- `[governance][refusal-design][security][testing]` A refusal that returns the rule + remedy beat class-only (0.57x guard calls, 20/20 named-rule receipts) but only for public operands: printing matched values cut secret-threshold recovery from 8 probes to 1 and listing all matched rules leaked a hidden flag, while naming the rule without values leaked no more than class-only → projects/local__sandbox-refusal-rule-not-bucket.md#E1
- `[accessibility][visualdesign][testing]` Silhouette-first beats recolouring on a luminance-matched palette (greyscale 28 of 28 recolour pairs confusable vs 0 for body-only shapes) but bodies are the only strong channel: small props separate nothing (4 of 4 confusable shape pairs were prop-only), the silhouette vanishes at the eased-backdrop frame where fill meets backdrop luminance (28 of 28, fixed by a per-frame black/white outline at 4.75:1), and below a pixel floor luminance-spread colour wins; audit props, shared slots and smallest size separately → projects/local__sandbox-silhouette-first-cue-audit.md#E1
- `[security][verification][testing][sandbox][architecture]` Classifying payloads before an instruction slot leaked 17-89% of 108 adversarial payloads (split payloads beat every per-item detector); a nonce fence or JSON encoding leaked 0 but made legitimate rules inert (0/20) and leaks 108/108 to a text-obeying reader; only a typed closed-vocabulary rule record blocked all and still delivered 50-70% of rules, and a free-text slot let 12/12 through → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E123
- `[tooling][process][testing][search]` A hybrid keyword gate (windows + rarity + allowlist/floor) only relocates the recall-vs-precision tradeoff on common-word edits: on topical prose at 3200 words best is false fires 0.20 at full block recall or recall 0.42 at false fires 0.07, and narrow windows that look feasible on code break on sentence-spread and dispersed edits → projects/local__sandbox-hybrid-keyword-gate.md#E1
- `[governance][refusal-design][security][testing]` Refusal operand disclosure is graded and priced in bits, not allowed or forbidden: an 8-wide bucket of the secret limit kept 0.86 of exact-operand repair saving for 0.48 of its leak, but exact values, near-miss-gated values and first-K-per-identity values gave the secret in 1 to 2 probes, a rate cap only taxed honest actors (1.75 to 3.92 calls), derived or shared-pool "public" fields voided per-field rules, and no design gave 0.5 of the benefit at zero leak → projects/local__sandbox-refusal-operand-disclosure.md#E1
- `[accessibility][visualdesign][testing]` The silhouette size floor is real near 2 px per entity and survives every viewer-style metric (peak pooling after a Gaussian low-pass, surround-invariant) but area-mean hides it and mislabels visible props as confusable; anchor the threshold between a 2 percent luminance change and a visible feature, since the 4 px answer depends on threshold and blur → projects/local__sandbox-silhouette-size-floor-metric-audit.md#E1
- `[testing][harness][gamedev][3d]` T11 measured on a real headless WebGL render: a no-errors check catches 1/23 injected defects, golden-diff by MAE can't separate defects from benign (17/23 with 5/10 false alarms at MAE>1; exact bytes 10/10 false alarms), but lighting-invariant checks (chroma ratios, luminance vs N.L from a reference normal pass, decal-footprint + camera-jitter stability) catch 18/23 with 0/10 false alarms in a calibration scene — the real ceiling is luminance-only material changes, marginal z-fighting and anything outside a known scene → projects/pappydapimp69__tension.md#E2
- `[film][rendering][canvas]` A reusable canvas helper drawing into a w x h slot must clip itself to that slot; a caller's outer clip (a phone-screen UI) hides sloppy fillRect overflow until the helper is reused above other UI in the same region → projects/local__film.md#E1
- `[film][rendering][process][testing]` Before a minutes-long render(t) export, tile one still per story beat into an ffmpeg xstack contact sheet; it caught ~8 placement bugs in seconds, and hiding a character behind furniture needs a clip at the occluder edge, not a downward offset → projects/local__film.md#E2
- `[narrative][comedy][structure]` Chain a short's beats with only "because/therefore": 3 of 9 links in a 60s comedy were "and then", each fixed by one ~2s on-screen motive (a character line or an in-world caption naming the prior outcome), not a new scene → projects/local__film.md#E3
- `[gamedev][debugging][display]` A diagnostic ratio of two same-unit quantities (outer/inner window width) is always about 1 and misleads; use a device-pixel against css-pixel pair → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E19
- `[gamedev][history][deception]` A verb whose event is deliberately identical across outcomes cannot feed history: record the fact from the world state the outcome changed, never from the event → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E20
- `[gamedev][onboarding][softlock]` Tutorial props are ordinary items: refuse use, drop and give while a later step needs them, or one keypress strands the lesson → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E21
- `[audio][dsp][tooling]` A numpy synth that layers tone()+noise() with a bare + crashes on few-sample length mismatches (17640 vs 11025, 116916 vs 110762, 3 scripts); layer through a pad-to-longest helper and cap envelope attack/release at n//2 → projects/local__film.md#E4
- `[ui][input][state][gamedev][testing]` First-cover dictionary and arithmetic membership covers give identical 2-lag totals in all 23 cells at base 4217 P8 P9, flats at N=P and 2P; compare cells not just flats → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E51
- `[geospatial][method]` Antimeridian-safe haversine and half-open longitude fix distances but not midpoints: shortest-arc longitude midpoint drifts 42 km at lat 60 and 11 km near the pole, so interpolate with unit vectors → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E52
- `[hashing][sampling]` Birthday collisions arrive near sqrt(space) draws, earlier if modulo bias skews buckets; size for sqrt and reduce by rejection → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E53
- `[voice][scripting][architecture]` One row per utterance only makes re-rendering cheap if output names use a stable row id: index-named files re-rendered 21 of 41 after one insert vs 1 for stable ids; use one canonical label per speaker → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E54
- `[determinism][gamedev][testing]` Seeded streams give exact save/replay, but removing one entity reshuffles 89 percent of later rolls on a shared stream; id-keyed substreams keep 100 percent, index-keyed only 18 percent → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E55
- `[audio][film][tooling]` Splicing a finished peak-normalized mix into a new score then peak-normalizing the whole drops the spliced act ~3x (RMS 0.015 vs 0.05) because one new transient sets the level; RMS-match the splice and soft-limit instead → projects/local__film.md#E5
- `[music][audio][design][state]` Bar-cut vs stem fade for binary combat state: the cut wins only when wait-to-next-bar is under half the vertical fade (1 of 8 phases at 0.5 s fade, 4 of 8 at 2 s, all at 4 s), and off-grid at one bar it tracks worse than constant music → projects/local__sandbox-bar-quantized-horizontal-vs-stem-fade-flip-phase-rate-sweep.md#E1
- `[humor][psychometrics][simulation][typology]` Four-style humor classes by max subscale: with correlated benign/injurious and self/other axes, retest class agreement fell 0.69 to 0.53 (68% had a top-two margin under 0.5 SD) while the sum score stayed 0.96 and a disattenuated-r screen (0.75) still passed, so report profile plus margin and label only above a band → projects/local__humor-styles.md#E1
- `[humor][measurement][bias][simulation]` Shaded humor-style self-report: forced choice cuts top-style misclassification 0.373 to 0.168 but loses between-person rank (Spearman 0.525 vs 0.728 under a strong general factor) and forces scale correlations to -1/3; a 3-rater blend cuts it to 0.308 but breaks when raters share the bias at self-level susceptibility, and judge any fix on several metrics at once → projects/local__sandbox-humor-self-report-bias.md#E1
- `[humor][simulation][social]` "Affiliative safe, aggressive backfires" holds only on a mixed audience: a shared-target in-group flips aggressive positive, a high-power sender sees fake-fine laughter, and repetition turns affiliative negative out-group (joke 6) and aggressive negative by n=8-20, so check target, power gap and repeat count before style → projects/local__humor.md#E1
- `[humor][localization][simulation]` Rank jokes for localization by required items (content blanks, norm items misread) times locale-boundness, not style label: observation 0.77 vs wordplay 0.06 at 50% overlap, but only teasing and self-deprecation can injure (0.08 and 0.05), so transcreate content and cap norm-bearing intensity → projects/local__sandbox-humor-localization.md#E1
- `[cinematography][lighting][simulation][testing]` Key:fill ratio sets screen contrast only at matched exposure and with its unit, sites, angle and ambient stated: in a Lambert face model '8:1' read as 8 stops is 256:1 (68 L*, crushed) vs 44 L* as lux, a bounce at K/8 cuts 3.17 stops to 2.32, key azimuth alone spreads a nominal 8:1 cheek ratio 1.4 to 7.5, and fixed ambient makes brightness change the ratio 4.3x → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-cinematography-lighting-ratio.md#E1
- `[film][editing][sandbox][method]` A master shot guarantees some edit exists (0 of 1728 edits without it under defects) but keeps pacing reachable only on the singles' side of the line with matching continuity: a wrong-side master leaves 1 zero-cut edit, and only neutral cutaways over most beats win the cuts back → projects/local__sandbox-cinematography-coverage-line-of-action.md#E1
- `[tooling][graph][verification]` Negative edges earn their place (a positive-only graph re-offers 38 refuted pairs as untested seams, top-ranked one included) but merged into one edge set they inflate the largest cluster 573 to 608, reset the top-20 weakest-link seams completely, and collapse 38 mixed edges to one flag: store refuted and confirmed counts apart → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E124
- `[film][camera][geometry][testing]` At fixed framing pinhole compression is R = d/(d+gap), distance alone: focal length at one spot changes R by 0 (a rescaled wide shot matched tele to 1e-16), a zoom leaves R fixed while a dolly cut it 2.1x, though lens still changes background blur (about 4x at 200 mm vs 24 mm, f/2.8) → projects/local__sandbox-pinhole-depth-compression.md#E1
- `[humor][sandbox][negotiation][pitch]` Self-directed joke habit modelled as a dose: warmth saturates while credibility cost accumulates, so in a high-stakes new-relationship setting the break-even was 0.175-0.35 jokes/turn (optimum 0.10-0.23) and in casual settings none appeared; listener base-rate expectation and flaw severity moved it most, prior pitched numbers failed 5 of 7 → projects/local__sandbox-self-directed-humor-dose-response.md#E1
- `[cinematography][optics][sandbox]` Shallow focus directs attention only when the second subject sits outside the depth of field: aperture sets deep-vs-shallow (x11) then sensor (x5), focal length barely at fixed framing (x1.1 near, x6.5 far background); a 0.5 m two-shot at 2.9 m is still sharp at f/2.8 on 35 mm, and a 0.6-12 m deep-focus stage on full frame is out of reach (diffraction floor, 2.6 circles, 8.5 stops) → projects/local__sandbox-depth-of-field.md#E1
- `[color][visualdesign][accessibility][testing]` A teal-orange grade moved skin-vs-backdrop separation only +0.2 to +1.7 chromatic units against a ~43 delta E luminance-dominated baseline in an OKLab model, helped just darker or blue-teal backdrops, gave exactly 0 at matched luminance, lost on orange/red backdrops and drove the darkest skin from hue 61 to 145 deg, so get separation from backdrop and lighting, mask the grade off skin, and audit per tone and under dichromat simulation → projects/local__sandbox-teal-orange-grade-separation.md#E1
- `[testing][process][tooling]` On a second codebase (Brain CLI, 232 functions) deliberate test coverage follows code age (rho +0.43, oldest third 18% vs newest 56%) while importance reaches coverage only incidentally as callee reach (+0.27 overall, +0.09 for functions a test names directly) — so audit old, high-reach, never-named functions first; sandbox prediction half-refuted (importance not near zero) → projects/local__sandbox-brain-selftest-coverage.md#E1
- `[governance][verification][testing][harness]` The validate and steward intake gates disagree in both directions (60 crafted proposals: steward-stricter 29, validate-stricter 10, both reject 3, both accept 15), so neither is authoritative and effective acceptance is their intersection; a finding cleared elsewhere must re-enter as an unverified proposal, and validate failing after canon is written stalls the commit → projects/local__gate-differential.md#E1
- `[memorization][teaching][proxy][testing]` Leitner miss rule at equal review budget under a soft power forgetting curve: drop-one-box beat reset in 100 of 113 cells (edge at most 0.04 retention, shrinking toward zero as gain rises), reset wins only for slow-strengthening learners (gain 0.3 or less, first-gap recall 0.7 to 0.9) and the pocket changes sign with budget (-0.005 at 13, +0.028 at 20 reviews), so the earlier toy's flip direction did not survive budget matching → projects/local__sandbox-leitner-miss-rule-budget.md#E1
- `[humor][sandbox][pitch][presentation][model]` Toy pitch model: an opening joke leaves change-first beating product-first in uptake for an attentive audience (0 of 384 break cases) but can reverse it for a cold audience or strong attention refill (226 of 1152) and, in a warmth-plus-uptake sum, whenever payoff weight is low; put the joke mid-pitch and name the currency → projects/local__sandbox-pitch-opening-joke-order-rule.md#E1
- `[humor][localization][simulation][testing]` Published timing figures leave the 11-English-syllable hook cap safe on the ratio law (bounds allow 12-13) but a 0.6 s pause or a 6.63 syll/s voice cuts it to 9 and a human rate spread drops fit(11) to 0.87, so charge pauses and the real voice rate against the window; the one study read found no systematic pre-punchline pause → projects/local__sandbox-humor-timing-figures.md#E1
- `[humor][narrative][comedy][escalation][simulation][testing]` Toy escalation model: habituation alone makes a jump straight to the ceiling beat any linear or geometric ramp (flat 1.410 vs 1.264 vs 1.183), so "escalate" needs a stated step-coherence limit; doubling the ceiling at fixed beats lowered the total (-6.6%), a pre-expected catch cut reversal payoff 86.4%, and "reversal last" held in only 5 of 12 cells; model only, constants assumed → projects/local__sandbox-comedy-escalation.md#E1
- `[humor][simulation][measurement][performance]` Missing laughter reads as a failed joke only against an established response model: a window at 0.25x the true latency tail gave posterior 0.446 and any window at or above it kept 0.90+ in a quiet room, but n=3, text-channel and late-laugh cases still missed 0.40-0.94 of landed jokes with the window known or tuned, so check window coverage, room size and the baseline before reading silence → projects/local__sandbox-missing-laughter-needs-the-response-window.md#E1
- `[film][rendering][canvas]` A character mirrored with scale(-1,1) for facing mirrors any fillText inside its transform too (a PIZZA label on a held prop rendered backwards); counter-scale glyphs locally or pass facing into prop callbacks → projects/local__film.md#E6
- `[security][testing][model]` Least privilege only shrinks reach as far as the edges you count: in a model, a scoped token reached 1 of 8 resources versus 8 shared, but a CI-to-deploy edge made it 2, a force-push left a leaked secret usable while rotation voided it, and a slow hash cut cracked users from 500/500 to 1/500 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E56
- `[networking][netcode][model]` In a tick model, unordered no-retransmit cut worst position staleness from 9 to 4 ticks, a rewind cap of 5 stopped a 20-tick liar but also denied an honest 8-tick player, and a sender that skipped the buffered-amount check closed its socket at tick 18 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E57
- `[text][unicode][testing]` Normalizing is necessary but not sufficient: NFC unified accent forms (raw False, NFC True) but only NFKC unified the fi ligature, no form unified a Cyrillic lookalike, and a two-mark letter still splits on a code-point cut; choose the form by purpose and cut on graphemes → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E58
- `[debugging][timing][model]` Print debugging beats a breakpoint for a timing race only while the probe costs less than the race window: in a model a 1-tick print kept the stale read, a pause hid it, and any probe of 5+ ticks (window 5 of 40) hid it too, so confirm the failure survives the probe → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E59
- `[film][animation][audio][tooling]` Insert a beat into a timeline-hard-coded film by remapping T past the seam at the dispatcher (not editing ~40 literals); shift only absolute-time tables, remap inside every helper that branches on absolute T, and splice the audio with the same map → projects/local__film.md#E7
- `[narrative][structure][comedy]` A smash-cut "DAY 1" reset that continues into Act Two reads as a time loop the visuals contradict unless the count has an owner: restyle post-reset cards as the antagonist's UI, plant the trigger rule legibly (freeze plus push-in), and add the missing "chooses to stay" beat → projects/local__film.md#E8
- `[speech-recognition][disfluency][model][simulation][sandbox]` A fragment-hallucination recogniser model gives only 1.05x clean WER at the 5.97 per 100 base disfluency rate and 1.20x at 30 per 100 with 20 percent insertions; adding derailment peaks at 2.98x but insertions fall to 8 percent, so tripling with insertion dominance needs other mechanisms; model only → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E60
- `[networking][webrtc][lag-compensation][simulation][sandbox]` Reliable ordered channel under 1-in-7 loss stalls state for about one RTO (max staleness 160 vs 64 ms, ratio 1.75/2.5/4.5/8.25 at RTO 60/120/240/480) and the stale claim lets an uncapped rewind hit a covered target while a 50 ms cap blocks it; model only → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E61
- `[humor][measurement][bias][simulation][sandbox]` Subtracting a population shade offset from humor self-report is exact for uniform shading (misclassification 0.485 to 0.000) but only 0.453 to 0.315 when susceptibility varies, and the style correlation collapses 0.479 to 0.002 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E62
- `[lyrical-composition][rhyme-tooling][algorithm][testing][sandbox]` Two-pass rhyme lettering gave 0 false rhyme claims against 19 for single-pass over six stanzas, but only when read by case; a case-blind scheme check misjudged 3 of 6 correct outputs and an all-assonance stanza comes out all lowercase → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E63
- `[accessibility][keyboard][testing][sandbox]` A static linter scored 0 errors on a trapped dialog and a positive-tabindex reorder but 5 on a mechanically broken page, so walk Tab and Escape on every custom widget and compute contrast per pair (#6c757d on #f8f9fa is 4.45, an AA fail) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E64
- `[procgen][lsystem][density-budgeting][sandbox]` The bracketed plant rule grows 5x segments but 9x footprint per iteration so a fixed viewport gains only 3.5% lit cells from iteration 5 to 6 (48 segments per lit cell); budget by overdraw, cap at 3-4, and expect 9% unspent waterfill budget from the 5x level step → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E65
- `[npc][social][simulation][sandbox]` A social-interrupt bonus outside a mandatory-shift gate abandoned shifts only once it exceeded the gated base (96 shift-hours at 1.1, zero at 1.0), while sparse pair scores cost 18% of all-pairs and one shared engine kept player and NPC symmetric → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E66
- `[networking][lag-compensation][websocket][sandbox]` A rewind cap blocks shot-behind-cover from queue- or stall-inflated latency only when it is shorter than the target's time in cover (50ms blocked, 100ms leaked) and it also denies honest high-ping hits, so stop transport delay inflating claimed latency at the source → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E67
- `[storytelling][economy]` Check Chekhov's promise with typed uses plus a chain to the ending (promise-only misses echo-only and dead-end details) and mark deliberate motifs, which the typed rule otherwise flags → projects/local__2026-10-10__chekhov-ledger-checker-unfired-spotlight-unintroduced-resolver-dead-end-and-motif-false-positive.md#E1
- `[gamedev][feedback]` A sub-display-increment tell is not invisible but intermittently visible at about err/increment, so rate it as nominal x rest-gate x err/increment and count accumulated drift → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E68
- `[architecture][state]` Carry-origin fixes deferred-effect attribution only until the sweep dedupes coincident arms (credit lost); use an origin set, and test credit not final state → projects/local__2026-10-10__deferred-effect-attribution-carry-origin-vs-apply-now-coalescing-breaks-carried-origin.md#E1
- `[astronomy][lunar-phase]` Mean-phase new-moon error is bounded near 15 h (observed 14.2 h, 10% of moons above 12 h), not "about 12 h" from a sample; check against an independently computed elongation root → projects/local__2026-10-10__mean-synodic-new-moon-error-vs-independent-elongation-root-meeus-ch49-vs-ch47.md#E1
- `[marketing][advertising][model]` Rotate creatives rather than add budget only when same-creative fatigue is steep (decay 0.85: rotation 3.75x vs doubled budget 1.00x; at 0.99 doubled budget wins 1.47x), then add norm copy for about 1.26x → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E69
- `[3d][performance][hysteresis]` LOD hysteresis is a one-sided band: it kills boundary flicker only when camera jitter is smaller than the band (2.5-unit band beat amplitude 2, not 6) and delays detail on approach → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E70
- `[security][oauth][hashing]` PKCE S256 only holds if the verifier has its own entropy: a 5-digit one fell in 48,214 guesses, plain is redeemed from the observed request, and a leaky compare is harmless behind S256 but not on plain → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E71
- `[statistics][replication][power]` The .48 chance a z=2.23 n=20 result replicates at n=10 is a ceiling; shrink for winner's curse or half-effect and it drops to .20-.29 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E72
- `[ui][input][state][gamedev][testing]` Independent arrival-side landing predicate plus time-sweep cover reproduces age-cutoff 2-lag totals 26/51/88/144 (P5-P8, base 211); the closed-target clause carries all shadows → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E125
- `[painting][color][perception]` Chromostereopsis in an eye-optics model: red-vs-blue disparity flips sign with pupil decentration (6.55 arcmin/mm modelled, about 3x literature), symmetric viewers average to 0 and 3 arcmin is only 3.5 mm depth at 0.5 m; do not place objects by hue temperature → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E126
- `[timekeeping][dst]` Local times can be nonexistent or ambiguous and zoneinfo will not raise: validate with fold 0/1 round-trip through UTC, and never assume the gap is an hour (Lord Howe is 30 min) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E127
- `[procgen][method]` Karesansui with several rocks: independent ring families cross most at about 0.75 of max ring reach (R=10: peak d=7.5), none beyond 2R; one min-distance field = rings clipped at the bisector, judge by form not count → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E128
- `[evaluation][model]` Regret comparator: linear Q = expected value with 0 cycles, nonlinear Q makes strict preference cycles (cubic: 1082 over 125 acts, survives skewed probabilities), so test transitivity over triples; regret matching still converges on cyclic RPS → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E129
- `[testing][verification][method]` Dict and bytearray covers still agree beyond both recorded ranges (P13 and P14, base 3011, flats only at P and 2P, totals 854 and 1127 at 2P); cost is steep (8 minutes), so check only claim-carrying cells for larger P → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E73
- `[testing][verification][method]` Dict and bytearray covers agree on each other's ranges at a fresh base (P5-P12, flats only at N=P and 2P, P11/P12 totals reproduced); run each family on the other's range and name the shared predicate as residual common cause → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E74
- `[narrative][design][ai]` A habit-counting antagonist beats a script 1.00 to 0.25 but guards a stale spot for 31 rounds after a deliberate habit switch unless counts decay (1 round at 0.5); store its memory as state and voice callouts from it, since history-referencing lines dangle in 11 of 16 skip orders → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E75
- `[design][rpg][combat]` A seed-plus-mash clash is deterministic from ratio (base+M)/(base+1) up (2.5 at M=10, 25/6 at M=20); size the window to the top tier ratio and report overturn per ratio band since a ratio cap changes the high band while the aggregate barely moves → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E76
- `[storytelling][structure][method]` Three-act 25/50/25 on whole scenes needs a stated rounding rule: floor and ceil make Act One differ from Act Three on 87 of 117 N (half-even on 0), and minimum N for 5 plants ranges 17 to 20 by rule, so mirror the cut points and size N from the promise count → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E77
- `[verification][trust][method]` Deriving trust from review lines removes the author's field but an origin-string gate is still self-asserted: sybil origin alice2 reached confirmed in a 6-scenario sandbox, so bind origin to identity and require a declared different substrate for rebuilds → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E78
- `[accessibility][color][testing][gamedev]` An exhaustive search over 24 colours found one 4-colour palette with worst pair distance 314 under all three CVD types (naive set: 30/78/303), so hand-swapped palettes fail but a searched universal one exists; per-type presets add only 10-95 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E79
- `[ui][input][state][gamedev][debugging]` Cap the world at chrome_top-1 computed from the chrome rect, not a literal: a fixed cap held at 0 misroutes until the chrome grew (then 20), while overlap gave 40 stray clicks and 41 scene steps → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E80
- `[git][tooling][documentation][testing]` Real git treats the first PARAGRAPH as the commit title: a three-line opening with no blank line became a 108-char subject in log and format-patch while blame summary kept only line one, so enforce the blank line, not the 50 chars → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E81
- `[audio][state][testing][pipeline]` Stateless TTS style drift is cured only by pinning identical settings per chunk (11 of 12 drifted otherwise, even with stitching context), but pinned chunks still need a gold diff because stacked contradictory tags average to neutral → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E82
- `[localization][dubbing]` the "about a third need rewriting" share is not constant: it moved 42%-92% with speech-rate ratio, so compute it per script → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E83
- `[text][unicode]` grapheme-boundary caps are necessary not sufficient: cap after length-changing transforms (ß->SS) and walk graphemes for byte limits; raw byte slices can be clean by alignment luck → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E84
- `[database][transactions]` on real Postgres 16, check-then-write skew passes at read committed AND repeatable read; only SELECT FOR UPDATE or serializable+retry stopped it, and one repeatable-read txn fixes OFFSET paging skips → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E85
- `[ai-video][prompting]` style cards can be mixed slot by slot (81/81 lint-clean) but appending two cards' values in a slot conflicted 3/3 and a filled form still allowed a two-verb action → replace per slot and lint the composed prompt → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E86
- `[geometry][visibility][testing]` Bbox overlap on thin rotated bars had 0 misses but 40.3% false hits (1392/3452), while vertex-in-frame and centre-fit tests missed 1318 and 1460 of 2060 true overlaps, so use bbox only as a reject filter and confirm exactly; half-open ranges give a shared tile edge exactly one owner → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E87
- `[performance][database][testing]` N+1 issued exactly 1+N selects (501 at N=500, about 1002 ms at a 2 ms round trip) vs 2 for IN-batch and 1 for join, but zero round trip hides it, an inner join dropped childless authors, and a 100-key cap gives 6 queries, so assert a query-count budget → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E88
- `[humor][measurement][negotiation]` Price a silence as a likelihood ratio: 40 independent listeners gave P(failed) 0.99947, 2 gave 0.593, 40 fully correlated gave 0.547, a short window with 40 still gave 0.914, and one pause read 0.50 vs 0.029 by the counterpart's baseline reply speed (toy model, assumed rates) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E89
- `[documentation][testing][process]` Why-comments survive value refactors (0/6 stale vs 6/6 for restating comments) but go stale undetected when the premise changes with values identical (6/6 stale, tooling catches 0), so pin each rationale to a checkable premise → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E90
- `[search][chunking][bm25][testing]` On BM25 with adjacent-sentence answers whole-doc and size-1 chunks sit at MRR 0.52; size 2 overlap 1 hits 1.00 but wider windows fall (0.94, 0.81), so chunk to the answer span with overlap span-1 and sweep → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E91
- `[game-design][turn-based][model][testing]` The N+1 caution break-even holds only for flat hazard (floor N+1 at 3, 6, 12 percent) and broke to N-1 or M=1 with back-loaded hazard, while a 5-question budget stays fully spent until hazard passes about 14 percent → projects/local__caution-turn-break-even-n-plus-one-and-question-budget-hazard.md#E1
- `[geometry][visibility][rendering][testing]` Bounding boxes of a piercing pair overlapped at all 18 yaws while the centroid-sort error ran 154 of 614 overlap pixels face-on down to 0 only edge-on, so use the box as a screen and count wrong pixels at your own camera angles → projects/local__painters-centroid-sort-vs-zbuffer-yaw-sweep-bbox-overlap-not-sufficient.md#E1
- `[gamedev][determinism][testing][state]` Four saved sfc32 words restore in O(1) versus a 12000-draw replay, and the golden fingerprint must be canonical and long since a float-fed roll diverged across runtimes on just 1 of 20 seeds while integer-fed never did → projects/local__seeded-rng-state-save-canonical-fingerprint-transcendental-roll-divergence.md#E1
- `[time-management][estimation][bias]` Summed per-task worst cases are safe only under independent delays (1.000) and fall to about 0.88 with one shared delay, and a late-session decline in favorable rulings can come from duration-based scheduling alone; use past project totals and check durations before blaming fatigue → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E92
- `[modding][security][sandbox]` A restricted language subset is not a sandbox, and a verb-name allowlist leaks too: schema-check every argument and freeze the capability table before mods can patch each other → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E93
- `[testing][verification][rng]` Stub-roll mechanism tests and rate tests catch disjoint bugs and the rate depends on a stated behaviour model (10.5x swing); A/B builds need keyed draws, not a shared seed → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E94
- `[testing][bias][debugging]` Positive-only hypothesis tests are safe only when the true rule is not wider than the hypothesis, and a negative test both rules reject teaches nothing; use near-miss disconfirmations alternated with confirmations → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E95
- `[presentation][design][verification][testing]` Data-ink ratio is blind to provenance and readability: a fabricated chart and an over-stripped unreadable one both score 1.00, so show the source log and keep calibration marks → projects/local__data-ink-ratio-is-blind-to-provenance-real-output-artifact-check.md#E1
- `[narration][testing][viz]` Gate state-driven copy on the full predicate it asserts, not a usual companion: a mat-gate lied on 40/80 states, a partial predicate on 12, the complete one on 0 → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E130
- `[regret][model][negotiation][sandbox]` Near-miss regret beats clear-miss only below a gap ratio of exp(K·Δdistance) (flip at 13x in the model), and regret-matching averages converge in rock-paper-scissors (0.005) but not Shapley's game (0.19) → projects/local__regret-ease-of-imagining-vs-regret-matching-convergence-bargaining-floor.md#E1
- `[lyrical-composition][rhyme-tooling][algorithm][testing]` Two-pass rhyme lettering needs a bounded assonance pass: with no resolved rhyme four same-vowel lines collapse to one letter, and a mislabel flips closure distance [2,2] to [1,1,1] → projects/local__two-pass-rhyme-letters-assonance-mush-boundary-and-closure-distance.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][disappearance][rescue]` Rescue search for a missing member: derive find-chance from a hidden position plus an unnamed call bearing, not dice (dice skill gap is 0); the call must fade with nights missing or drift is erased, and cap its noise or deep searches become coin-flips → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E22
- `[gamedesign][disappearance][missing]` An unnamed night call from a missing member announces the disappearance if only the missing ever call (posterior 1.0); add false calls that do not depend on anyone missing and make cross-night bearing consistency the learnable tell → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E23
- `[gamedesign][doppelganger][rescue]` Replacement mechanics: do not gate rescue of the missing member on exposing the fake first (real back 0.27) nor let rescue expose it free (investigation bypassed); return them as a same-name duplicate so rescue becomes a paid 1-of-2 choice → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E24
- `[gamedesign][disappearance][missing]` A missing member's call-direction tell must be sized to what the player can hear (8 directions: ratio 5.00 falls to 2.67), and its rule width, not resolution, sets strength; silence signals abandonment only with a strong call (0.909 vs 0.745 after 3 nights at 0.3) → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E25
- `[gamedesign][doppelganger][deduction]` A rescued member returning beside their doppelganger: ask both the same question; with two, comparing answers only locates the bent fact and memory decides (0.633, not the assumed 0.80); the question-budget rule only bites from three suspects → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E26
- `[gamedesign][disappearance][determinism]` A missing member's fading rescue call needs a floor (unfloored 0.9x0.6^n is silent by night 6, a hidden time window on top of the spatial one), and its night step must draw drift, call and bearing every night for every slot (naive 0/3/0 draws fork the seed) → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E27
- `[gamedesign][disappearance][rescue]` With false calls and a fading real call, answering a missing member's night call pays early and stops paying by itself (night 3 at risk 0.15, night 2 at 0.30), so the night risk number, tuned with the fade, sets when players give up → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E28
- `[gamedesign][doppelganger][rescue]` Doppelganger rescue ordering: rescue-narrows beats expose-first on fake-held nights (2.040 vs 2.540), knock-on losses and pylons, but the missed window dominates holding time, so keep a pylon fallback after it closes → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E29
- `[gamedesign][abandonment][rescue]` Measure a missing member's spatial rescue window from where the lead sleeps, not their feet: feet-rule abandonment hit 225/256 plans and 0.844 of them while scouting; the camp rule abandoned only on deliberate camp moves → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E30
- `[gamedesign][disappearance][balance]` A spatial find-chance for a missing member is as tunable as a weighted roll: bearing noise rising with depth under a cap plus search hours hit a 0.90-0.35 target table within 0.017, skill gap at least 0.27; tune for the least noise → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E31
- `[gamedesign][disappearance][balance]` Tune a missing member's find-chance on the direction the player hears, not the internal bearing: at 8 heard directions search hours 1-5 found exactly the same and the target table missed by 0.207; size search effort in whole heard wedges → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E32
- `[gamedesign][doppelganger][balance]` A pylon fallback after a missed doppelganger rescue window is cheap (0.248 pylons per disappearance) but cuts held nights only 0.153, because misses are rare; most holding time is the first nights, so make the same-night search the strong default → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E33
- `[gamedesign][abandonment][missing]` With two abandonment routes for a missing member (call fades out vs camp moves on), measure which usually ends the rescue: time came first in 0.534 of plans, and allowing sleeping out flipped it to 0.860 space-first; neither was ever accidental → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E34
- `[gamedesign][disappearance][rescue]` Waiting for a second, consistent night call before searching for a missing member makes nights out 3.49x more productive but halves rescues (0.952 to 0.505) once calls fade; price wasted nights with real night risk → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E35
- `[research][process]` atomic per-claim agents make quotes auditable and contain blocked sources, but confidence still needs distinct roots with aliases normalized and cycles guarded, and independent roots can both be wrong → projects/local__sandbox-atomic-claim-agents-and-root-confidence.md#E1
- `[communication][defaults]` the concise-reply default recorded in two projects is one root, and answer-only dropped 3 of 3 required safety items in the model, so pair it with expansion triggers → projects/local__sandbox-concise-default-one-root-and-expand-triggers.md#E1
- `[empathy][motivation]` efficacy feedback lifts empathy choice only for people whose cost is under the benefit (overloaded 0.35 to 0.60 cap) and a calm forecast of the lift halves in the hot state (0.51 to 0.26) → projects/local__sandbox-empathy-avoidance-efficacy-lever.md#E1
- `[presentation][credibility]` pasted real output scores the same evidence ratio as a fabricated one and goes stale silently when behavior changes, so store the producing call and version, re-run it, and freeze expected outputs → projects/local__sandbox-pasted-output-goes-stale-evidence-ratio-blind.md#E1
- `[gamedev][testing][procgen]` Placing a test agent on the nearest open cell is not legal placement on a dense procedural map: sealed pockets took the pass rate from 1.000 to 0.630 at rock density 0.40; place inside the goal's connected region instead → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E36
- `[gamedesign][disappearance][rescue]` Score a missing member's night call on the player's history, not nights since disappearance: the last night answering pays ranged 0 to 6 across histories, so fade plus false calls set no single stopping point → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E37
- `[gamedesign][disappearance][balance]` Coarse hearing does not make find chance a step function: a search grown outward from the heard centre gains every sector and hits a 0.85 to 0.30 table within 0.007; a plateau means effort is mapped to ground badly → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E38
- `[gamedesign][doppelganger][rescue]` Rescue-narrows holds a doppelganger fewer nights than expose-first only if exposure tests stop at the window; with elimination allowed after it, expose-first held 1.949 vs 2.992 nights (pylons still favour rescue-narrows) → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E39
- `[gamedesign][doppelganger][deduction]` Two-suspect identity check: a single answer matching memory is evidence too; read optimally, asking one equals asking both (0.631 each), so a second question only adds accuracy when memory can be wrongly sure → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E40
- `[narrative][structure][testing]` But/therefore between adjacent beats is only a smell test: it catches chronicles but false-flags cross-cut plots, misses dead-end subplots and lucky rescues, and therefore-only rejects every conflict beat; check each beat uses or opposes an earlier one and reaches the ending → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E41
- `[gamedesign][disappearance][testing]` Never test a coarse-perception search with the target pinned to one sector: at a bin centre it fakes a step (0.294 flat over H 1-5, seven E32's numbers); averaged over positions find chance rises every sector → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E42
- `[gamedesign][disappearance][testing]` Two-observation Bayes: apply the prior once (p L1^2, not (p L1)^2); a doubled prior cancels at p 0.5 but moved seven E23's consistent-pair posterior from 0.942 to 0.741, flipping its proof threshold → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E43
- `[narration][testing]` Gate adaptive copy on the predicate the sentence asserts: a cause gate is exact only when one cause drives the state and its threshold matches the claim (0 false lines), else 7 of 20 and 19 of 40 lines lied once temperature or predators drive it → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E96
- `[negotiation][regret]` Apology advice stable across studies is own-it plus offer-repair and never lead with a forgiveness request; the third component flips by study, and repair is worth nothing where no deal zone or repair path exists → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E97
- `[gamedev][narrative-systems]` A checkable-speaker filter for deceptive lines costs no extra draws (6 per lie at any bias) and lifts checkable lies 0.40 to 0.90, but also lifts guess-by-speaker precision 0.50 to 0.70, so pair it with truthful lines from the same kind of speaker → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E98
- `[input][ui]` Per-axis deadzone and per-axis clamp errors do not compound (0 of 11800 samples worse than the worse stage, either order), so fixing only the weaker stage changes nothing; make both radial or uniform and test a non-square box → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E99
- `[determinism][gamedesign]` Per-entity RNG streams isolate entities but do not make scrub/seek safe when roll count is state-dependent; address draws by (seed, entity, tick, slot) and test seek on an entity that takes the extra-roll branch → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E100
- `[simulation][narration]` Viewer-built parameters must be shown to move the outcome alone (volume tied at 27.6 when ignored, -85.5 vs 65.3 wired), measured on an unclipped value because a failure floor hid the swing (0.00 vs 41.99), with numeric narration gated on live state → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E101
- `[belief-revision][memory][ai][testing]` Hold a contradicting observation beside the stored rule and flip only when RECENT evidence outvotes it by a margin: overwrite thrashed 24 times under 30% noise, first-wins kept a bad first note, all-time counts never followed a real change → projects/local__neekolaus-film.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][rescue][regret]` In a spatial rescue search the near-miss share of failures falls with depth (0.397 to 0.157) but near misses per search peak at mid depth (0.119), since shallow searches rarely fail; aim near-miss regret beats there → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E44
- `[browser][testing][animation]` A render pass is not enough to snapshot an animated generated page: with a live clock 0.80 of baseline/check pairs differed by >= 1 px at 120 px/s on an unchanged page; pin the page clock for capture → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E45
- `[gamedesign][hallucination][perception]` A phantom target that backs away on screen cannot be both unnoticeable and unbeatable: below a 0.10 speed threshold it delays a disciplined player only x1.111, and tripling the walk needs 6.7x that threshold; recede off-screen only or visibly on purpose → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E46
- `[search-theory][rescue][probability]` After a failed search, lower each searched area by a factor of 1 - POD and search where probability x detection is highest; treating searched areas as cleared at POD 0.5 found 0.500 vs 0.737 Bayesian, worse even than repeating (0.694) → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E47
- `[determinism][rng][save]` Save the RNG's full state words, never a seed and roll count: with hidden callback draws the counted total was 3000 of 6000 and the restore silently diverged, while state words restored exactly at zero replay cost; hash saved state with sorted keys → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E48
- `[film][audio][tooling]` Inserting a film beat by remapping time at the dispatcher keeps discrete cues in sync (0/40 off vs 3/40 for hand edits), but continuous clips spanning the insert end exactly the insert length early; pause or split them through the same remap → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E49
- `[search-theory][rescue][probability]` Spread a missing member's search hours by water-filling: the searched area should grow with hours and call noise (3 to 11 sectors); all hours on the likeliest spot found 0.261 vs 0.782, and a fixed 4-sector block lost 0.141 at high noise → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E50
- `[gamedesign][abandonment][missing]` Make an unprompted abandonment felt with a voice as camp comes down that matches the dawn call's bearing: posterior 0.92-0.97 with false calls kept, vs 0.29-0.55 dawn-only and 0.46-0.69 for a lone camp-down call; no false calls turns it into an announcement (1.0) → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E51
- `[game-design][progression]` Bank reset-run progression only on newly crossed personal-best thresholds, and activate it next run to preserve the first-run wall while preventing safe repetition farming → projects/pappydapimp69__od76.md#E16
- `[painting][color][perception]` Pick a paint's neutraliser by test mix at the target depth: in a spectral toy the worst partner drifted hue 7.4 degrees at 25% chroma but 41.2 at 10%, narrow pigments left 10 of 17 partners unable to reach 10%, and a cyan-biased blue gave the cleaner green → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E131
- `[testing][tooling][process]` A fully covered green unit suite missed 6/6 label drifts between a hand-kept sidecar and its form; real-artifact end-to-end caught 6/6 and a derived map removed them, but id renames still silently broke consumers 6/6 → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E132
- `[3d-modeling][materials][rendering]` Sample the glTF metallic-roughness (ORM) texture as linear data: sRGB mis-flagging erred 0.284 at mid values (0.5 reads 0.216) yet 0/1 masks hide it, and a G/B swap was wrong for 5 of 6 materials → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E133
- `[songwriting][rhyme][metrics]` Score rhyme-scheme openness by max closure span on uppercase-resolved pairs from a two-pass lettering: span ordered AABB<ABAB<ABBA (1,2,3) while mean distance tied ABAB/ABBA and stop counts inverted them, and one-pass lettering collapsed stray assonance into AABB → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E134
- `[search-theory][testing][allocation]` Water-fill test hours over suspect modules by marginal value: searched modules grew 2 to 12 as the budget rose 1h to 48h, greedy beat concentrate and uniform in 7/7 cases, and concentrating only tied it for tiny budgets or a sharp prior → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E135
- `[browser][rendering][performance]` A low frame cost can mean missing layers: with more weather layers than the canvas-context cap, per-layer canvases cost 0.69 ms with 3 of 5 live vs 1.15 ms for one shared canvas with 5 of 5, so assert live-layer count with cost → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E136
- `[gamedev][camera][gamedesign]` Swapping first person for top-down to dodge camera bugs deleted the out-of-sight mechanic fakes hide in; a grid raycaster with yaw-to-world input restored it with 0.00° drift at 4 headings × 3 device scales → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E52
- `[gamedev][camera][testing]` Correction to seven/E52: its 0.00° drift is keyboard-only and its 360° sweep hand-places companions — mouse-look, gamepad and trailing-follower (mirage/E6) cases are untested; list each claim beside the input the test actually drove → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E53
- `[gamedev][camera][testing][input]` Seven first person now verified on mouse (44.00° per physical sweep at DPR 1/1.25/2), pad sticks (0.00° off) and trailing followers (≤4 cells, all seen); a follower test only bites if the lead truly walks away along the real route → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E54
- `[algorithm][testing]` A spot check scored 1.00 and accuracy fell only to 0.917 while per-class recall exposed each classifier defect; one defect masked another → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E137
- `[rendering][evaluation]` A shadow bias tuned on a narrow non-grazing angle list gave 38/90 held-out acne; a grazing-inclusive list gave 1/90 but a 23x larger peter-pan gap → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E138
- `[cooking][stability]` Slow oil addition cures the coating-rate break but not a yolk-capacity break; in a toy model 4x rate lost 74% and slow over-capacity lost 33% → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E139
- `[cooking][timing]` Salting in the middle window pools surface water that delays browning; in a toy model pat-dry before searing removed ~90% of it → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E140
- `[determinism][testing]` One seeded stream restores in 0 rolls via state words but desyncs on swapped event order and shifts on one extra roll; derive per-system streams → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E141
- `[cooking][balance][model]` In a toy flavor model acid beat extra salt only once salt was adequate (contrast 0.44 to 0.57), underseasoned dishes needed salt first, and the best acid dose rose with richness → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E102
- `[disfluency][voice][speech-recognition][model]` Rate-matched causal and uniform disfluency placement give identical blind WER but rarity lift 3.33 against 0.99, so check placement not rate → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E103
- `[garden][pest-control][model]` In a toy model marigold nematode suppression rose smoothly with coverage (0.59 of control at 10 percent, 0.006 at 95) instead of a cover-crop-only switch; grade companion claims by mechanism and required dose → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E104
- `[garden][soil][water][model]` In a dry-spell model 3-4 in of mulch with drip saved about 47 percent of water with flat returns after 4 in, while stem wetness depended only on clearance (57 of 60 days touching, 0 at 3 in) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E105
- `[cooking][timing][model]` Conduction model: sear carryover 7.3 C, sous vide 0, but pulling 3 C under target trimmed only 1.2 C of it, so size carryover from the cook not a flat subtraction → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E106
- `[algorithm][determinism][testing]` Hash-sorted round-robin dealing is steerable by chosen ids (6 of 6 buckets); a secret deployment salt cut success to chance at no stability cost, a per-session salt moved 85 percent of ids → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E107
- `[gamedesign][hallucination][perception]` A hallucination is believed in proportion to what the player has learned to expect: a phantom where the world taught them things appear was believed 0.800 vs 0.020 at random (x40); teach the pattern honestly first, then break it there → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E55
- `[gamedesign][hallucination][sanity]` Schedule fourth-wall hallucination effects from a no-repeat shuffle bag (x1.32 the scare of random picks) and switch to derived variants past about two showings each; random picks waste a quarter of triggers by twice the pool → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E56
- `[accessibility][hallucination][vfx]` Cap hallucination flicker per sliding one-second window, never by average rate: irregular flicker averaging 1.5/s still hit 7 flashes in a second, while a window cap held every window to 3 and kept 0.75 of flashes → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E57
- `[gamedesign][hallucination][audio]` Hallucinated voices are content up to about 2 in one place or 4 spread around the head (words landing 0.216 vs 1.000 at 4 voices) and texture beyond about 6; duck the rest when one line must land → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E58
- `[gamedesign][sanity][balance]` Never let a sanity meter reduce competence: failures lower sanity and lower sanity raises failures, so a 0.03-per-level penalty raised collapse x1.82 and 0.10 x3.97; let low sanity change perception, not skill → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E59
- `[humor][psychometrics][simulation]` Style label to joke-safety rule: perfect label still erred 0.207 mixed and 0.428 in-group, noisy 0.268, margin gating 0.209 on 47% labeled, so combine the label with audience context (toy model, assumed constants) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E108
- `[lyrical-composition][rhyme][testing]` Read stanza shape from two-pass rhyme letters: closure AABB 1, ABAB 2, ABBA 3 and an assonance-start stanza kept 2 open lines where one-pass showed 0, with repeated end words counted as unresolved (toy lexicon) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E109
- `[cooking][timing][model]` Mid-window salting pool in a toy sear model: browning started 26 s later (97 to 123 s) and the 60 C band was 0.2 mm deeper, far below my predicted 4x and 0.5 mm, because heating a dry surface dominates; a wet unsalted surface matched exactly → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E110
- `[determinism][testing][state]` Seeded sfc32 saved as unsigned state words restored in 0 rolls vs 5000 for count replay, but unsorted-key hashing gave 6 hashes for one state and unmasked signed words diverged → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E111
- `[garden][water][design][model]` Check drip emitter spacing against soil class and mulch: sand needed 12 in, 24 in gapped even in loam, 60 psi lifted flow x1.55 and 3 in mulch cut run time 42 percent (assumed radii, illustrative) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E112
- `[garden][soil][water][model]` Pick mulch top-up from decay rate: 4 in wood chips fell under 2 in at month 24 and leaves at 7, a 1.5 in yearly top-up held wood but not leaves, and saving fell 23 percent from 4 to 2 in (assumed constants) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E113
- `[architecture][process][testing][state]` Park alternatives with every reason and re-check all before skipping: blind title skip missed 8 of 40 reopened options, one scan log re-walked 0 vs 20 for fragmented folders, partial re-check left stacked-reason re-walks → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E114
- `[garden][water][soil][model]` Rain garden /6 to /8 sizing took all of a 25 mm storm but 39 percent of 75 mm, and the swale 8-12x gain appeared only above 50 mm storms, so quote design storm; mulch stretched stored water x1.72 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E115
- `[belief-revision][ai][state][testing]` Release a held selector on a recent-window refusal margin, not on the first veto (60 percent less work under noise) or never (0 work when blocked); add a re-probe or a burst strands the agent → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E116
- `[browser][rendering][testing]` Size labels from the widest real translation after canvas width is set: in a model an English-sized box overflowed 3 of 8 German labels, flat +30% still 1, and font-before-resize gave 3 false fit passes → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E53
- `[garden][water][design][model]` Drip specs of 20-25 psi and 0.5-1 gph emitters hold only to a run length: end emitter kept 95% at 200 ft but 73% at 400 ft, halving emitter flow did not help at 800 ft, and a 2 ft barrel head gave 0.21x flow → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E117
- `[garden][soil][water][model]` A 3-5 in mulch stem gap failed in a model once mulch crept 0.05 in/day (60 and 20 wet days of 120) and an emitter 4 in out wetted the stem, so re-rake on schedule and keep emitters outside the wet radius → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E118
- `[browser][rendering][testing]` Overflow checks on overwritten text need every intermediate state and measured widths: final-only missed 11 of 14 overflows and an average-width estimate with 20% slack still missed 5 while adding false alarms → projects/local__sandbox-edges.md#E54
- `[garden][water][design][model]` Model a first-flush diverter as a fixed gallons-per-event subtraction, not a 10% haircut: on a 1000 sq ft dirty roof capture was 0 below 0.09 in and lost 18% at 1 in, and the /6 rain garden took 52% of a 75 mm storm → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E119
- `[determinism][testing][gamedev]` Seeded RNG state words restore exactly at O(1) (0 versus 400 replayed rolls) but equal RNG state did not give equal worlds once draw order, float sum order or a missing world save changed → projects/local__sandbox-rng-determinism-not-sufficient-for-replay.md#E2
- `[garden][water][design][model]` The drip kernel's 30 to 40 percent saving vs a soaker is unreachable from its own efficiency ranges (max 26 percent; sprinkler 22 to 47), and mulch cut gallons saved 42 to 47 percent while the percent stayed 35, so quote gallons per bed (assumed constants) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E120
- `[narrative][simulation][state][testing]` Hand-built state tests hit 4 of 4 endings while replay gave one ending 0.14 percent of paths and a one-step threshold change made it 0, so assert a minimum share per ending from ordered-path replay (toy constants) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E121
- `[garden][water][design][model]` A 55 gal barrel caught 11 percent of a 60-day roof yield and even a storm-sized 1100 gal barrel overflowed 650 gal, so size from storm clusters vs drawdown, route overflow to a roof/6 garden, and expect x0.21 drip flow at 2 ft head (assumed constants) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E122
- `[viz][timing][testing][process]` A reading-time warp (up to x2.22) left a cut-present check green while 6 of 8 hand-synced cuts drifted a bar or more, so derive cuts and the bar grid from warped scene lengths; capping warp at 1.5 left 4 captions unreadable (assumed constants) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E123
- `[garden][water][design][model]` A swale chain gained under 1x on sand and at most 5.2x on clay (not 8 to 12x), and once per-swale catchment exceeds its capacity more swales never clear a roof overflow, so quote gain by soil and storm and route roof water to its own sink (assumed constants) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E124
- `[garden][soil]` a 3-min jar silt read mislabels loam as clay and oversizes a rain garden 33%; read silt at ~2 h before choosing the divisor → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E125
- `[garden][soil]` mulch depth and stem clearance decay with time in a model: top up every (depth-2)/decay years and re-measure clearance from the flare at each top-up → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E126
- `[determinism][state]` RNG state words restore O(1) but only for a bare generator; wrapper caches (spare gaussian) must be saved with them → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E127
- `[seo][verification]` Google's 2009 blog and title-link page quotes check out verbatim, but no page states the title as a ranking signal, so date and hedge the title-vs-description claim → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E128
- `[ui][input][state][gamedev][testing]` Index try/except cover matches the first-cover dictionary cover in all 29 cells at base 409 for P11 and P12 (flats at N=P and 2P, totals 460 and 636 at 2P); extends the index form past P7 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E129
- `[garden][pest-control][research]` Rank companion-planting claims by independent trial roots, not page count (basil 12 pages/0 trials vs marigold 3 pages/2); uncited copies inflate citation-graph roots (7 vs 2) until merged by text → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E130
- `[garden][water][design]` Drip lateral evenness (Hazen-Williams, half-inch tube) is nearly insensitive to inlet pressure (230 vs 236 emitters at 90% evenness, 25 vs 60 psi) while the 5 gpm budget falls from 300 to 193; size a zone by the smaller of flow budget and friction cap → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E131
- `[garden][water]` rain-barrel 10% haircut holds only for few large storms (diverter is per event: 24% loss at 30 small storms); mulch cuts barrel needed ~36%; one 3in storm overflows barrel and roof/6 rain garden alike → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E132
- `[testing][state][model]` Dictionary and bit-mask and-not cover rules gave identical 2-lag shadow averages and the same flats at N=P and 2P on P7 and P10 at base 211 (synthetic model, Python only, P5-P12) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E133
- `[garden][soil][model]` In a heat-conduction toy mulch temperature damping does not flatten at 4 in like water saving: daily 5 cm swing 6.38 C bare, 0.50 at 4 in, 0.13 at 8 in; annual cut 32 percent at 4 in; a decayed 2 in layer doubles the swing (assumed properties) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E134
- `[garden][water][design][model]` A rain barrel on a 2 ft stand gives 0.87 psi against drip's 20 psi (46 ft needed), flow about 21 percent and 33 h drain, and a 55 gal barrel is 0.088 in of roof rain so it barely helps the rain garden (assumed constants) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E135
- `[garden][water][model]` A 30 m contour swale keeps 84 percent of its storage at 0.5 percent floor grade, 67 at 1, 38 at 2 and 25 at 3, so level means under 1 percent grade, and a 100 mm storm overflows the 2 percent trench but not the level one (assumed runoff and geometry) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E136
- `[garden][water][design][model]` Zoning drip by need class removed a 105 percent worst-case watering mismatch from one shared valve, while the 4 gpm supply ceiling only bound at 481 small emitters; mulch cut every zone's run time 42 percent (assumed plant needs) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E137
- `[garden][soil][water][model]` Stem clearance needed scaled with mulch depth in a wicking toy model (2.1 in at 2 in, 4.2 at 4, 8.3 at 8), so the fixed 2 to 4 in band holds only to about 3 in unless emitters sit 4 in out (assumed constants) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E138
- `[gamedev][state][testing][determinism]` Seeded sfc32 state words restore in 0 draws vs 500 for seed+count, but one cosmetic roll on a shared stream desynced two lockstep clients: give effects their own stream and hash with sorted keys → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E139
- `[garden][water][design][model]` A 30 ft by 18 in swale kept 90 percent of its volume at 1 percent grade error, 50 percent at 5 and 25 percent at 10, and a 55 gal barrel could not make up the loss; check level end to end (pure geometry, assumed size) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E140
- `[garden][water][design][model]` Drip lateral model: 1 gph at 12 in held 99 percent at the end over 100 ft but 68 percent over 400 ft (5.1 gpm, above a 4 gpm supply), 60 psi did not help and a 10 ft rise cost 10 percent, so cap run length (assumed constants) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E141
- `[garden][water][soil][model]` Rain garden /6 to /8 sizing held for one 1 in burst on clay (0 percent overflow, barrel lost 91) but sand /8 lost 25 percent and tight storm clusters 58 to 66 percent, easing to 13 at 48 h gaps, so check ponding volume and drain-down gap (assumed constants) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E142
- `[gamedev][state][testing][determinism]` RNG state words restored in 0 draws vs 50000 for seed-plus-count (which diverged after one uncounted draw); removing one of four entities flipped 8 of 8 shared-stream rolls, and name-keyed substreams held but index-keyed did not (toy fixture) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E143
- `[garden][water][soil][model]` Swale kernel's 2 m spacing is only a minimum: max overflow-free spacing was 11.1, 5.6 and 3.7 m at 50, 100 and 150 mm (10 m at 100 mm overflowed 44 percent) and a 25 percent slope kept 67 percent of capacity (assumed constants) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E144
- `[garden][water]` 1 gph bridges 18in drip spacing in ~1h in sand/loam/clay (0.5 gph/1h never does); sand loses 20-40% of bulb below 12in root zone at 2-6h; mulch stretches intervals, not bridging; assumed soil constants → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E145
- `[garden][pest-control]` cover-crop season every 3rd year beat tomato-only and 10% interplant (0.58 vs 0.19) at 6x pest growth, flips to every-2nd with weak kill and to interplant at 2x growth; toy → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E146
- `[garden][soil]` stem clearance costs only 3-6% of mulch on tree beds (12in) but 44% on a 6in-radius single-plant bed; same volume piled at the trunk is 3x deep; dripline emitters never collide with clearance → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E147
- `[garden][water][design][model]` drip lateral: 25 psi at 12 in spacing keeps uniformity above 0.94 to 300 ft and 0.89 at 400 ft; 60 psi does not fix a long run (x1.55 flow, 300 to 193 emitters per zone), only compensating emitters on a high feed hold 400 ft → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E148
- `[garden][pest-control][design][model]` fennel allelopathy 3 to 10 ft radius: in a grid model the affected share changed 20x across the range (1 to 20 percent of a 20 ft plot), a 10 ft separation cleared every plot, and smooth decay cut expected loss to 0.22 of the hard-radius count → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E149
- `[garden][water][soil][model]` rain-garden roof/6 to roof/8 divisor is a one-inch-storm rule: both overflow 50-65% at 3 in, a clay pond needs 60 h to drain vs 3 h on sand, and a jar-test misread of loam as sandy clay inflates drain time x3.3 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E150
- `[gamedesign][hallucination][audio]` Voices heard inside the head cannot be spread, so they collapse the binaural voice budget: with half the voices inner, 4 voices landed 0.288 of words and the budget fell from 5 to 2; count inner voices as one-place voices → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E60
- `[gamedesign][hallucination][perception]` A hallucination that alters a real object survives touch checks that kill an inserted phantom at once (2.00 vs 1.00 checks; touch-only never exposes it); prefer altered objects and felt presences where the player can touch → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E61
- `[gamedesign][hallucination][balance]` Measure a hallucination system's modality mix from its own code before claiming fidelity: SEVEN's flat pick gives the player voices 0.20 of the time vs about 0.55 in real psychosis; its chattiest companion reaches only 0.314 → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E62
- `[garden][pest-control][model]` Fennel's 3-10 ft allelopathy range flips a tomato row from 0 to 8 of 8 hit at one placement, so place by the 10 ft bound; marigold stayed 0.73 at 10 percent cover, and rank claims by trial roots not pages (assumed constants) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E151
- `[garden][water][design][model]` Rain-garden roof/6 sizing on clay still ponds 60 h after a 1 in storm (200 h compacted), so add a depth/infiltration under 48 h check or cap ponding near 3 in (assumed constants) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E152
- `[gamedev][state][testing][determinism]` Saving sfc32 state words restored in 0 replayed rolls versus 200000 for seed plus count, and a 1000-roll fingerprint with sorted keys caught an ambient clock source and key-order hashing (Python rebuild) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E153
- `[songwriting][rhyme][testing][model]` A one-pass rhyme labeler read town/night/mine/line as XAAA where two passes gave XXAA, assonance-only stayed lowercase, and stopping points ran AABB 2, ABBA 1, ABAB 0 (22-word hand lexicon) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E154
- `[gamedesign][hallucination][pacing]` A conditioned phantom spends the expectation that makes it work: after 3 honest pairings, all-phantom schedules land only 1-2 of 12 cues however long the teaching, while alternating real and phantom lands 4-5; budget one honest pairing per phantom → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E63
- `[accessibility][hallucination][captions]` Caption hallucinated sounds exactly like real ones: captioning only real sounds is worse than no captions (0.30 vs 0.80 of a caption-heavy audience unspoiled), marking them is as bad, identical captions reach everyone (1.00) → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E64
- `[gamedesign][hallucination][audio]` On phone speakers a hallucinated-voice scene has the one-place budget of 2 voices whatever the mixing (headphones 5, or 4 with a third inner); write lines that must land for 2 and treat extra voices as texture → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E65
- `[gamedesign][hallucination][sanity]` When hallucinations shift from sound to sight as sanity falls, avoid a 50/50 mix at any stage (the least readable point); a banded curve makes a visual mean deep (0.933 vs 0.833 linear) and spends a third of linear's early visuals; flat signals nothing → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E66
- `[gamedesign][sanity]` easier recovery at low sanity does not undo a sanity-competence penalty (collapse stayed x10): keep competence flat → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E67
- `[gamedesign][hallucination]` an invisible on-screen recede still ends runs when the walk races a deadline: size the recede against slack, not just the perception threshold → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E68
- `[gamedesign][audio]` hallucinated-voice budgets are a readout of masking width and talk share (spread 11 to 2 voices over 30-120 deg): measure the mix, never quote a count → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E69
- `[process][tooling][verification]` Take a lesson's id from its own header (or its index line's arrow, matched by text), never from the order index lines appear: a batch of eight came back shuffled and seven E60, E61, E63 and E65 composed on wrong edges (corrected here) → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E70
- `[api][correctness]` a unique (key, id) cursor still silently misses rows whose sort key moves ahead of it (162/1800 by updated_at): page a snapshot or sort on an immutable key → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E142
- `[api][performance]` batching a per-parent-limited GraphQL field into one IN query fixes query count but reads every child (x9.2 rows on skewed data): use a window/LATERAL query, key on (id, args) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E143
- `[gamedesign][hallucination][audio]` Count hallucination modality by time, not by who has it or episode count: voices were 0.992 of hallucinating time in the central case and at least 0.766 in every swept case; play sound as a continuous bed and visions as brief events → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E71
- `[3d][compression]` price Draco against the gzipped buffer, not raw (4.7x not 17x on noisy terrain), and quantize attributes for VRAM: int16 halved GPU bytes, Draco never does → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E144
- `[gamedesign][hallucination][audio]` Let players read hallucination depth from the sound bed's thickness, not the last episode's modality: the bed alone gave 0.952 of 2 bits and bed plus visions 1.069, against 0.396 from visions alone; make the bed's steps distinct → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E72
- `[gamedesign][hallucination][audio]` In mono a hallucinated line that must land 80% cannot share the scene with even one equal voice; headphones carry about 4; raise must-land lines about 6 dB above other voices (mono then carries 6) rather than counting on spatial spread → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E73
- `[gamedesign][hallucination]` depth reads from the modality mix only where neighbouring bands differ: banded 0/0.1/0.5/0.9 loses on shore vs wading, and moving 0.5 to 0.3/0.7 made it worse → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E74
- `[gamedesign][hallucination]` conditioned phantoms need ~2 honest pairings each over a run: at 1:1 belief settles at 1/(2-a) (0.588, under a 0.6 threshold) and landed 0 of 15 → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E75
