- `[api][correctness]` WHERE updated_at <= t0 on live rows is not a snapshot: it dropped every row edited while paging (70 of 1800); use versioned reads or an immutable (created_at, id) key → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E145
- `[gamedesign][sanity][balance]` Easier recovery at low sanity cancels a competence penalty only when it rises about 3-5x as steeply (0.03 penalty needed 0.10, 0.06 needed 0.28), and a 0.10 penalty cannot be cancelled at all; keep competence flat, or the penalty mild and damping steep → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E76
- `[gamedesign][disappearance][rescue]` Letting a missing member's call fade to silence ends hopeless searches (late search nights 1.647 to 0.163) but costs about 0.13 of rescues even when easy to find, as call-following players stop going out; pair the fade with a lasting non-call cue → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E77
- `[gamedesign][audio]` size a must-land voice duck for a shallow psychometric slope: about 8.5 dB, since 6 dB carries only 2-3 mono voices at 5 %/dB → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E78
- `[gamedesign][hallucination]` a conditioned-phantom budget is a learner artefact: RW says teaching buys nothing, a Bayesian count says 20 pairings buy 9-12 of 12; tune from play data → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E79
- `[gamedesign][hallucination]` voices dominate hallucination time only pooled (>=0.888); up to 28% of voice-hearers are vision-led by time, so allow a vision-led profile → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E80
- `[accessibility][hallucination][comfort]` Keep hallucination sway out of the motion-sickness band (about 0.2-0.4 Hz, a 2.5-5 s period) and off the full frame: 2 Hz jitter cut the weighted dose 96% and a 30% vignette 70%; always offer a reduce-motion switch → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E81
- `[garden][water]` first flush is a fixed depth per storm, not a 10% haircut: a 2 mm diverter took 55% of a small-storm season and 7.5-15% of a big-storm one; size it per storm → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E155
- `[garden][water][sizing]` One 55-gallon barrel catches about a fifth of a 1,000 sq ft roof's yearly runoff even when emptied between storms, and runs a 10-emitter drip bed about 5.5 hours; pair it with a rain garden of roof area / 8 to / 6 for overflow → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E82
- `[gamedesign][accessibility]` a sway synced to breathing that quickens as sanity falls (5 to 2 s) spends 2/3 of the visual stages in the 2.5-5 s nausea band: use slow swells, edge-only or lurches → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E83
- `[state][architecture][data]` When merging two independently keyed collections, namespace every key by its source, not just the fallback bucket: renaming only 'other' still misfiled 2 of 4 members once a real id appeared in both; test with a fixture sharing a real id → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E84
- `[accessibility][comfort]` hallucination sway: avoid everything below ~0.5 Hz, slow drift included (as bad as in-band sway at a 0.06-0.1 Hz peak); 2 Hz jitter cuts 85-98% and an edge vignette 59-70% → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E85
- `[database][performance]` re-running ANALYZE cannot fix a skew the statistics can't hold: SQLite's per-key average still underestimated a 95% value 48x after refresh; check for histograms, measure the plan first → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E146
- `[gamedesign][disappearance][deduction]` A cue that fires only when a hidden swap happened is the answer, not a cue; give it side-stream false positives (a third of quiet nights) before it ships → projects/pappydapimp69__sevin.md#E1
- `[ui][input][gamepad]` DOM menus that rebuild on choice drop focus to the top; restore it from a per-menu memory (label, then nth, then index) via a MutationObserver, and keep spent options focusable but inert instead of disabled → projects/pappydapimp69__sevin.md#E2
- `[workflow][agent][pacing]` Build an owner's explicit number as a named constant and put any doubt in the report as a tuning note; questioning it costs a round-trip and reads as not listening → projects/pappydapimp69__sevin.md#E3
- `[workflow][agent][questions]` An owner who builds forward ignores questions about edges not yet reached and almost always takes the agent's stated lean; ask only when the answer changes this turn's build and no default is defensible, otherwise build the lean and report it in one line → projects/pappydapimp69__sevin.md#E4
- `[concurrency][lock-free][correctness]` An ABA tag wraps on its own increments mod 2^k, not head changes: per all schedules a per-change tag let through exactly the untagged count at L%2^k==0 (930 at L=8) and 0 else, but a push-only 1-bit tag failed at every odd L from 3 → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-concurrency.md#E4
- `[concurrency][correctness][debugging][scheduling]` A print that takes its own lock inside a load..store window keeps every wrong result reachable ({2,3,4}) and under rare preemption (q=0.01) raised lost updates 7.8%→11.4%: blocking forces a switch mid-window; only a lock spanning load..store gave 0 → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-concurrency.md#E5
- `[concurrency][correctness][memory-model]` Release/acquire visibility is transitive through a relay thread: a 3-thread chain with rel/acq on both hops allowed 0 stale reads of 7, but any ONE of the 4 annotations made relaxed allowed one (1 of 8 each), as did a volatile-style relaxed flag in plain MP (1 of 4); audit every hop → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-concurrency.md#E6
- `[gamedesign][accessibility]` hallucination sway sickens by speed not period (flat 0.05-0.8 Hz at equal velocity, Chen 2016): 1 deg 2 Hz jitter doses 2.7x a 3 deg breathing sway; cap rms deg/s and area → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E86
- `[database][performance][indexing][measurement]` Merging indexes (a),(b) into composite (a,b) cut SQLite's per-insert index tax only 36% (11 to 7 VM ops), not half, and b=? became a 6311-op full scan unless few distinct a plus ANALYZE enabled skip-scan (48.7); keep (b) if b-only reads matter → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-database-design.md#E2
- `[gamedesign][hallucination]` real conditioned hallucinations persist through extinction (Ellson 1942) but reach a minority (29% placebo arm): make conditioned phantoms optional extras, not required beats → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E87
- `[gamedesign][disappearance][deduction]` A day-keyed false whistle (a third of quiet nights) hides a take for one night only (0.346) but two same-direction whistles are 0.957 proof; phantoms must match a take over time, as drifting multi-night episodes → projects/pappydapimp69__sevin.md#E5
- `[gamedesign][audio]` a must-land voice's duck must cover the SUMMED level of the other voices (+3 dB per doubling, Brungart 2001): 6 dB carries only 2-3 voices in mono; 6 voices need 8-11 dB → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E88
- `[concurrency][correctness]` release/acquire publishes data but does not stop store buffering: 'set my flag, read yours' saw both 0 in 3909/200000 x86 rounds with acq/rel, 0 with seq_cst → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E147
- `[debugging][git][regression][algorithm]` Untestable commits don't add bisect tests (9.97→9.80 at s=0.10), they turn the answer into a range fixed by the skips beside the bug: scattered skips give a range 2s-s^2 of the time (0.195, any pick order), a 10-commit broken stretch rarer (0.102) but wider (2.04) → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-debugging-strategy.md#E2
- `[determinism][save][deduction]` Anything that indexes a draw is save state too: with a fixed draw count and exact rng restore, a rebuilt witness list moved a fake's bent fact on 0.797 of reloads (0.395 for one lost fact), letting ask-reload-ask catch the fake 0.899; save it verbatim, pick by min-hash → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E89
- `[concurrency][correctness]` GC removes ABA only for fresh objects: a pooled or indexed Treiber stack gave 6/62 corrupt CAS successes and re-pushing a popped object 22/363; tag those or never recycle identity → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E148
- `[concurrency][testing]` search schedules by preemption count to find races: a lost update showed at schedule 3 vs ~68 stress runs, but the worst 2-thread result needed 4 preemptions (schedule 1171) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E149
- `[queueing][staffing]` staff ceil(R + beta*sqrt R) + 1 servers for a wait target (beta 1.06 at 20%, 2.37 at 1%): the bare square-root rule was 1 short of exact Erlang C in 25/44 cases, never over → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-queueing-theory.md#E1
- `[queueing][performance]` queue wait follows rho/(1-rho) x variability: 0.8->0.95 load cost ~5x, one shared queue for 4 servers waited 0.22 of separate queues; Kingman is accurate near saturation, ~25% low at 50% load with bursty traffic → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E150
- `[queueing][performance]` size in-flight limits by Little's law (throughput x latency): on 4 servers, 4 -> 16 in flight kept throughput at 4.01/s and quadrupled latency → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E151
- `[concurrency][testing]` search schedules by preemption count: a check-then-act overdraft and an A-B/B-A deadlock both showed at bound 1 (schedules 2 and 3), bound 0 found neither → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E152
- `[api][correctness]` sync a changing collection by paging an immutable key + replaying a changes feed as a versioned upsert: exact (0/0/0) where an updated_at cursor missed 73 and an appended feed made 120 dups → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E153
- `[database][performance]` index a skewed column's rare values with a partial index (WHERE col != hot): rare lookups stayed 1/600 of a scan, index shrank 58 -> 5 pages, hot value scanned automatically → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E154
- `[ai][gamedev][simulation]` Removing an agent's anchor (camp struck) fired a sticky day-long "stay with the player" fallback written for dusk; give each anchored task a fallback to what still exists (the pile, a site, the deadfall) and key the sticky fallback to its event → projects/pappydapimp69__sevin.md#E6
- `[reliability][redundancy][measurement]` A redundant pair with common-cause fraction beta fails at ~beta*lam*t on short missions, not (lam*t)^2: beta=0.1 at lam*t=0.01 is 10.9x the squared estimate while MTTF moves only -1.75% (28/19 vs 3/2), so judge redundancy by mission Q and beta, not MTTF → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-reliability-engineering.md#E1
- `[social-choice][condorcet]` Guilbaud's 8.77% cycle rate is an odd-n upper limit (5.56% at n=3); even electorates have 29-67% no-Condorcet-winner from ties, so enumerate exactly → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-social-choice.md#E1
- `[inventory][newsvendor][sandbox]` Newsvendor: the critical-fractile quantile matched brute force 4/4 on Poisson demand and a rounded normal approx lost at most 0.15% (mean 2), but stocking the mean lost 3% at mean 50 and 21% at mean 2 with CR 0.95; order at F^-1((p-c)/p) → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-inventory.md#E1
- `[epidemiology][sir]` SIR epidemics overshoot the herd threshold 1-1/R0 (that is the prevalence peak); final size solves z=1-exp(-R0 z), R0=2 -> 0.797 not 0.5, max overshoot ~0.30 near R0 2.15 → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-epidemiology.md#E1
- `[gamedev][state][determinism]` Splitting a fixed-draw night with player time in the middle: draw it all at the first boundary and keep the unused rolls in save state for dawn, or the draw count depends on the path and resumed runs fork → projects/pappydapimp69__sevin.md#E7
- `[gamedesign][rendering][hallucination]` A receding unreachable objective placed relative to the viewer falls behind occluders; anchor it to open ground, scale it up as it recedes, and drive the recession from the player's attempt → projects/pappydapimp69__sevin.md#E8
- `[testing][process][verification]` Stored sandboxes mostly reproduce (792 of 794), but drift on the interpreter (sum() of floats is compensated since Python 3.12) and on unshipped input files; demo float order with a plain loop, ship every input, print the Python version, keep runs under ~30 s → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E90
- `[wayfinding][gamedesign][navigation]` Without a sky cue, heading noise alone walks people in circles in both directions (16 of 20 paths looped at moderate noise), and a fixed-strength cue stops loops only while noise is moderate (18 of 20 looped at high noise); use the visible sky as the navigation dial → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E91
- `[acoustics][reverberation][simulation][sandbox]` A diffuse ray trace reproduced the Sabine +25% / Eyring -10% / Kuttruff +3% bracket at a=0.5, but in a flat hall with absorbing floor and ceiling Eyring ran 40% low while Sabine matched, and Kuttruff overshot in uniform dead rooms (+13% at 0.7) → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-room-acoustics.md#E1
- `[queueing][concurrency][sandbox]` An in-flight cap equal to server count kept 4.00/s behind one shared queue even with variable service, but per-server queues dropped to 3.13/s (22% lost) at that cap and recovered by 1.5x; pool queues before tightening the cap → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-queueing-theory.md#E2
- `[inventory][newsvendor][sandbox]` Newsvendor fractile (p-c)/p is exact only with zero salvage: salvage 4 moved the optimum 19 to 22 (2.8% loss) and a fixed order cost of 15 made ordering nothing optimal; use (p-c)/(p-s) → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-inventory.md#E2
- `[humor][reliability][measurement][sandbox]` Blending k raters cuts error only in the independent share: misclassification fell 58% from 1 to 8 raters at beta=0 but 13% at beta=0.6, so measure the shared bias fraction before adding raters → projects/local__sandbox-humor-self-report-bias.md#E2
- `[epidemiology][sir][sandbox]` SIR final size z=0.797 at R0=2 matched RK4 to 1e-3 only for homogeneous mixing; with susceptibility 1.8 vs 0.2 at the same R0 it fell to 0.208 and the peak moved from 0.50 to 0.11 cumulative infection → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-epidemiology.md#E2
- `[github][pages][deployment][process]` A push is not a deploy: one failed Pages run meant the owner rejected a build they never got, and it was re-designed instead of re-deployed; read the run for the commit before calling it live or changing the design → projects/pappydapimp69__sevin.md#E9
- `[gamedev][state][rendering]` A new entity state the player should notice (asleep) shipped with no render path and was reported as "nobody sleeps"; add the visual in the same change, or the state does not exist to the player → projects/pappydapimp69__sevin.md#E10
- `[acoustics][reverberation][simulation][sandbox]` Fitzroy and Arau-Puchades do not settle Sabine-vs-Eyring in a diffuse room: uniform absorption makes them Eyring exactly (corridor -53%) and with lumped absorption a reflective axis pair carries their decay (Fitzroy 1.3x-7.6x too long, Arau-Puchades within 15% in 1/9); bracket with Sabine and Eyring unless walls reflect specularly → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-room-acoustics.md#E2
- `[database][performance]` a partial index skipping a 95% hot value halved real WAL writes over 5000 single-row commits (22.5 vs 44.5 MB, 6% over no index) but saved only 16% in one batch txn → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E155
- `[api][correctness]` versioned upserts need tombstones once deletes exist: without them a late redelivery resurrected the deleted row in 48 of 60 delivery orders, same as plain overwrite; with tombstones 0 → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E156
- `[queueing][statistics]` bursty-traffic queue waits depend on distribution shape, not just mean and cv: at cv 2, load 0.5, gamma/H2/lognormal waited 5.03/4.06/3.67 (1.37x); simulate from measured traces → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E157
- `[garden][water]` rain-tank capture is capped by use between storms, not tank size: 500 gal caught 79% if emptied every storm but 24% at 10 gal/day, 7% at 2; size storage to demand x days between storms → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E92
- `[gamedesign][disappearance][deduction]` A false cue that does not answer the player's cheapest probe (whistle back, hear silence) just moves the tell one press; make the false source answer too, so only acting on it (walking there) resolves it → projects/pappydapimp69__sevin.md#E11
- `[gamedesign][ux][onboarding]` A core mechanic reachable only from a conditional menu item went unfound by a daily play-tester; put it inside a flow they already pass through (the nightly fire scene) and teach it in training, with the menu as a second door → projects/pappydapimp69__sevin.md#E12
- `[gamedesign][sanity]` the ratio of cancelling damping to a sanity penalty is a fixture constant (3-5x in one model, 27-76x in another); quote the shape, not the number → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E93
- `[testing][floating-point]` Python 3.12+ sum() of floats is order-free on random lists (0/80) but not exact: an exactly cancelling huge pair still varied 8/40; use math.fsum when order must not matter → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E94
- `[gamedesign][rescue]` fading a missing member's call costs ~0.03 of easy rescues and ~0.13 of hard ones; a lasting non-call cue won back 59% of the hard loss, hopeless searching still 40% under a floor → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E95
- `[accessibility][color]` a hue-only hallucination tint loses most contrast for colour-blind players (dE 61 to 7.5 protan); carry it in luminance too and check all three CVD types → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E96
- `[accessibility][motion]` moving sway out of 0.2-0.4 Hz: at the same pixel amplitude a 0.05 Hz drift cuts the modeled dose more than 2 Hz jitter (99 vs 90%), 1 Hz only 61%; say what is held fixed → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E97
- `[testing][state][model]` Dict and bytearray-OR cover rules agree on P8 and P10 at base 3011, and the 2-lag flat recurs at N=3P (P5, P6) and 4P (P5), so it is not limited to P and 2P → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E156
- `[social-choice][condorcet]` Exact margin-vector DP matched brute force at n<=7; a 20000-election seeded sample at n=31 (se 0.20%) cannot tell 8.50% from the 8.774% limit, so compute exactly → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E157
- `[gamedesign][balance][power-scaling]` A lore power number stays honest if combat spread exceeds (5+M)/(5+1) (far-band upset 0.061, lie 0); full decoupling gives 0.47 upsets but 50% displayed-order lies → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E158
- `[songwriting][rhyme][tooling]` Closure distance cannot tell perfect AABB from assonance-only AABB (both 2 pairs, mean 1.0); two-pass letter case does (AABB vs aabb), so report resolved pairs too → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E159
- `[ux][onboarding][state]` One-spine tour beats a per-mode fork on edit drift (3, 9, 21 stale screens vs 0), not screen count, and a hidden auto-play step needs a trace check (final state matched) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E160
- `[workflow][agent][questions]` Before asking the owner, ask Brain and ZaneGPT and build on their answer as a reversible default; then close the questions already left open in earlier reports the same way (a rescue soft-lock was settled by an existing Brain lesson, no owner needed) → projects/pappydapimp69__sevin.md#E13
- `[gamedesign][ux][scene]` A chained evening scene read as "never starts" through three silent exits (skipped when nobody was home, a song that looked like chat, a hand-off waiting on an unprompted press); fetch participants, make each act unmistakable, auto-advance → projects/pappydapimp69__sevin.md#E14
- `[ui][gamedesign][input]` Co-located actions under one resolver: a priority bonus for the branch starved "Light the fire" when the fire was out; gate each action on the state where it applies, then rank → projects/pappydapimp69__sevin.md#E15
- `[ui][gamedesign][input]` A target class that is everywhere (forest trees) hijacks a single nearest-in-cone resolver from sparse ground items; give it a narrow cone and handicap, and read camera pitch as intent (eyes down, the ground) → projects/pappydapimp69__sevin.md#E16
- `[reliability][redundancy][measurement][sandbox]` Beta-factor redundancy: extra identical units gain under 1% below lam*t = beta/(1-beta)^2, and the 2-of-3 break-even with one unit is ln 2 only at beta 0 (0.770 at beta 0.1, my predicted downward shift was wrong), so quote it per beta → projects/local__sandbox-kernel-reliability-engineering-2.md#E1
- `[gamedev][state][determinism][testing][sandbox]` Save PRNG state words, not seed plus count: reload was exact 8/8 in 4 words vs 49,999 rolls and the only exact form once one draw bypassed the counter (count replay broke 8/8); per-subsystem streams stop a spawn roll shifting combat (8/8 to 0/8) but a roll added inside the same stream still shifts it 8/8 → projects/local__sandbox-seeded-rng-state-words.md#E1
- `[data-toy][gamification][feedback][simulation][measurement]` Self-vs-past-self feedback is not gentle by itself: with a real 2% saving and 15% monthly noise it still says "worse" in 45-47% of household-months (vs 50% at no saving), while peer comparison pins "above neighbours" on 25% of households for 11+ months, so add a noise tolerance band to self comparison and an approval signal to peer comparison → projects/local__sandbox-self-vs-peer-comparison-feedback.md#E1
- `[distribution][seo][pipeline][rendering][sandbox]` In a toy crawler the JS-only indexing penalty is just the render-queue lag at one page but grows with page count (200 pages at 5 renders a day: day 41 vs day 1), and a build-time prerender goes stale on 86% of days if the build is weekly and content daily, so put text in the shell first and match build cadence to change rate → projects/local__sandbox-seo-shell-vs-render-queue.md#E1
- `[regret][experimentation][statistics][testing]` Greedy adaptive allocation biases every arm mean low (-0.07 to -0.10 in a 12-pull exact enumeration) while the apparent winner's in-test mean is still overstated; fixed design is unbiased and a fresh holdout of the chosen arm restores it → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E161
- `[testing][state][model]` The 2P-to-3P step: a third flat at N=3P shows on P7, P8 and P9 at base 601 in both the dict and set-inclusion counts, so the P5/P6 3P flat is a periodic feature, not a small-period artefact (checked to N=3P+2 only) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E162
- `[testing][state][model]` Two-pointer subset and dict cover rules agree on P9-P11 at base 1213 with flats only at N=P and 2P (to N=2P+2), so the P8 limit was a run limit, not a rule limit → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E163
- `[zen][procgen][content][testing]` koan commentary that critiques all but one response solves the case by elimination (and a verse naming a candidate does too): cap critiques at n-2 and validate the finished text → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E164
- `[ml][evaluation][testing][generalization]` A k-NN memorizer hits 100% train on random labels (held-out 0.49) and on a noisy rule (held-out 0.84): train accuracy is only a capacity check, so run the same model on random labels and judge by a held-out set covering every class → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E165
- `[songwriting][rhyme][sandbox]` one-pass vowel-match rhyme lettering mislabeled 1164 of 1296 enumerated stanzas and merged AABB with ABBA; two-pass uppercase-first fixed it (true by construction) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E166
- `[pathfinding][sandbox][tradeoff]` on 8-connected grids weighted-A* gain appeared only on scattered blocks (435 to 56 expansions at w=1.25); open map had none because octile is exact → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E167
- `[pathfinding][sandbox][tradeoff]` weighted A* gain sat entirely at w<=1.1 on open/scattered grids and was absent on a wall-gap map; sweep per map class → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E168
- `[braille][unicode][testing]` Braille codepoint = U+2800 + bitmask of raised dots held for all 256 Unicode names; letter decades follow +dot 3 and +dots 3,6 except w in my unchecked table → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E169
- `[barcodes][checksums][testing]` EAN-13 check digit catches every single-digit error but misses 12% of adjacent swaps, all where the digits differ by 5; an unweighted sum misses all swaps → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E170
- `[documentation][testing][model]` In a toy first-K-words scanner, answer-first README lines captured 4.2/5 of the five questions vs 0.8/5 buried, and one decorative top line cost an answer at the tightest budget → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E171
- `[legal][testing][model]` Commissioned software is never work-for-hire in a 48-case grid; only a written assignment moves ownership, so an unassigned MIT repo grants rights the client lacks (8 of 16) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E172
- `[music][testing][model]` Greedy nearest-voicing matches the offline optimum and keeps common tones on 6 cadence runs but yields more parallel 5ths/8ves than fixed voicing in 2 of 6 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E173
- `[speech][coaching]` rank filler cuts by expected delay seconds, not type: cut um first until the speaker's uh share passes about two thirds, then ban-uh wins → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E158
- `[economy][gamedesign]` sink-coverage 95 to 105 percent only informs for flat fees; percentage sinks always read 100 percent and starvation hides above it, so track wealth stock and unmet spend → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E159
- `[teaching][memory]` self-testing beats restudy at delay only while spaced items stay retrievable; past that gap unaided testing loses to rereading → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E160
- `[reinforcement][schedule]` variable-interval flattens the post-reward lull only if intervals are wide or memoryless; plus or minus 20 percent jitter keeps scallop index 3.2 vs 3.7 fixed → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E161
- `[seeding][bracket][sandbox][model]` Fold seeding (seed k faces seed n+1-k) keeps seeds 1-4 from meeting before the semifinals at n=8..64 while adjacent pairing meets them in round 1, but it guarantees the draw, not the outcome: one seed-16 upset made the final 2 vs 4. → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E162
- `[optimization][knapsack][planning][sandbox]` Value-per-cost greedy was suboptimal on 1.47M of 10.3M small 0/1 knapsacks (worst 8.3 percent of optimum, 2/C on an adversarial pair); best-of(greedy, top item) stayed above 52.4 percent, but only the fractional version is exactly optimal. → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E163
- `[error-correction][testing][limits][sandbox]` Hamming(7,4) is a perfect code (16x8=2^7) so all 336 double errors miscorrect; an overall parity bit detects 448/448 doubles but 896/896 triples still miscorrect, so state the guarantee per error weight. → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E164
- `[traffic-flow][queueing][model][sandbox]` Ring-road jam persistence flips exactly at rho_c=1/(vmax+1) (N=33 free, N=34 jammed at vmax 5), brake length 1 to 50 is irrelevant below it, and above it the no-brake ring is already congested, so tune density or headway not the brake. → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E165
- `[animation][timing][determinism][sandbox]` A held contact pose beat 24 uniform frames on impact dwell (240 vs 25 ms at 6 frames) but lost on path error (50 vs 15 px); one smear frame cut the max jump 45 to 25 px, and a float clock mis-framed 302 of 3600 renders. → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E166
- `[reinforcement][schedule][model][sandbox]` Size interval spread from the hazard: modelled scallop index 27.6 at 20 percent spread, 14.9 at 60, 1.7 at 95, 1.00 memoryless (floors 0.02 to 0.5 agree) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E167
- `[prompt-engineering][caching][model]` In a toy exact-prefix cache, a 2-token date line ahead of a stable prefix cut hits from 5/6 to 0; order stable-to-volatile and breakpoint after the stable block → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E174
- `[testing][debugging][model]` A repro test seen red-then-green caught 3/3 reintroduced mutants vs 1/3 for an easy-input test written after the fix, and a flaky repro gave false green and red; fix the flake first → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E175
- `[tournament][model][testing]` Fold seeding (round-one pairs sum to size+1) with byes to top seeds gave a 1v2 final for all n=5..32 vs none for neighbour pairing; checked for fixed strengths and one upset → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E176
- `[testing][state][model]` Dict first-cover and bytes-OR age-cutoff tests agree cell by cell at base 3001 for P9-P11 (zero disagreements, flats at P and 2P, P11 215/11 and 460/11), extending the P5-P8 cellwise check → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E168
- `[seeding][bracket][sandbox][model]` Fold seeding at every field size 5..32 sends byes to seeds 1..m-n with no bye-v-bye and games n-1, but only the layout is guaranteed: a seed-8 upset at n=11 made the final 2 v 4 → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E169
- `[humor][sandbox][model][measurement]` A margin band lifts four-style label retest agreement (0.53 to 0.79 at 0.5 SD, coverage 0.49) but shading also lifts agreement while cutting accuracy, so agreement alone cannot certify a label → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E170
- `[testing][state][model]` Dict, set-inclusion and bytes-OR age-cutoff tests agree cell by cell to lag 3P at base 8191 (P5-P7), the flat steps recur at exactly P, 2P and 3P not as a plateau, and the set test needs its emptiness guard → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E171
- `[negotiation][sandbox][model][conversation]` Naming a frustrated counterpart's emotion cut a stuck deal from 28 to 7 rounds at full accuracy but was slower than silence at zero accuracy, did nothing for a calm seller or an empty deal zone, and shifted the price toward the labeller → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E172
- `[knitting][sandbox][model][algorithm]` Spreading k increases over n stitches: the midpoint rule keeps all runs within 1 (888 cases) where the naive interval piles the remainder at the end, and symmetric charts fail exactly when k is odd and n even → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E173
- `[worldbuilding][sandbox][model][design]` Redundant story cues only help when found independently: three cues per event read in 92.9 percent of visits when spread, 7.1 percent when stacked in one room; sightlines or index cues that imply earlier events repair the clustered case → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E174
- `[flooring][sandbox][model][algorithm]` Tile row layout: wall-start or a single centring leaves a sliver under a quarter tile in about 25 percent of room widths, but taking the better of tile-centred and joint-centred left none (smallest cut 152 mm of 300) when the grid is free to shift → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E175
- `[testing][state][architecture]` Assertion discrimination (fixed-green and buggy-red share of cases): generic tutorial trigger 0.015, position-only 0.000, pinned and FSM checks 1.000; combine with all-pass since any-green stays 64/65 falsely green → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E177
- `[woodworking][cut-list][kerf]` Charge k-1 kerfs per board and sort longest-first: kerf-blind plans are invalid in 4.0 percent of lists but change the board count in under 1 percent, and longest-first sits within one board of optimum → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E178
- `[3d-modeling][texture][gltf]` Download size says nothing about VRAM (91 B against 65536 B flat), BC1 pins VRAM at 1/8 but drops to 11.6 dB on noise, base64 glTF is 1.333x the GLB, and a bake cage under the bumps hits only 57 percent of rays → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E179
- `[decision][group][model]` Independent simultaneous voting beats a follower cascade by about 4 points at q=0.7 and a weak leader costs 4.5 points when speaking first, but a far better leader should speak first (0.845 against 0.807) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E180
- `[regret][negotiation][model]` Positive ease weights on regret keep rock-paper-scissors regret matching at the equilibrium (0.006 to 0.007 gap) but masking one action's regret collapses it to pure play (0.667) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E181
- `[architecture][data][modding]` Content-declared enumerations stop silent undrawn entities (2 of 8 to 0) but need source-namespaced keys against shared-name collisions and a union rather than replacement to stay load-order independent (3 end states to 1) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E182
- `[flooring][tile-layout][remainder]` Best-of-two centred tile rows leave no cut under a quarter tile at three tile sizes; spreading the remainder over joints with the knitting midpoint rule is even but makes joints over twice grout in 91 percent of widths → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E183
- `[surveying][traverse][closure]` A good traverse closure is blind to scale error (0.000 m misclosure with 0.294 m mean error) and Bowditch smears a single blunder onto correct stations (0 to 1.031 m); find the bad leg and check a baseline before adjusting → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E184
- `[narration][measurement][scene][sandbox]` A pinned-at-zero readout proves absence only down to its resolution: number-only at 0 decimals read a real 0.0002/yr decay as still over 43 years, a 200 px bar rescued it, 1e-6/yr escaped even 3 decimals; size display to rate times longest clock and branch copy on the true predicate → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E185
- `[electrical][wire-sizing][voltage-drop][sandbox]` Ampacity picks the minimum wire gauge but run length picks the real one: 14 AWG at 15 A, 120 V breaks 3 percent drop from 40 ft (7.68 percent at 100 ft, needing 8 AWG), and even a 3 A load fails at 300 ft; check drop for every long run → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E186
- `[woodworking][cut-list][kerf][sandbox]` Kerf k-1 charge plus longest-first stays within one board of optimum from 3 to 40 mm kerf, but a wrong kerf charge costs a board in 2, 7, 16 of 400 lists at 3, 10, 40 mm and kerf-blind plans go invalid in 36 percent at 40 mm → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E187
- `[surveying][traverse][geometry][error]` A misclosure bearing finds a distance blunder on every shape tested (hit 1.00) but returns two suspect legs on figures with parallel sides (hexagon, octagon) and misses angle blunders (hit 0.00 to 0.17), so pick the blunder type before trusting the shortlist → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E188
- `[balance][gamedesign][simulation][method]` A saturated meter-only encounter scale spoils outcomes (player wins 1 of 30) but NOT fight length (3 to 6 ticks, no collapse), so the steady-state predicate degeneracy needs an absolute threshold against a relative level; an anchored multiplier capped at exactly 1.0 makes symmetric fights mutual kills → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E189
- `[dead-reckoning][navigation][geometry]` The 1-in-60 rule reads 4.5 percent low at small heading errors, exact at 30 degrees, high beyond (1.15 at 60), but correcting a leg flown by planned time costs far more (12.98 nm miss at 40 degrees even with the exact bearing), so re-fix position after any correction → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E190
- `[tiktok][content][metrics][method]` Format variety only pays with enough probes per format: at 2-8 percent hit rates 1 probe per format lost to repeating one format (3.93 vs 4.14 expected hits at 90 posts), 6 probes won at 300 posts (15.88 vs 13.80), and equal formats gain nothing — budget probes before judging a format → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E191
- `[algorithm][gamedev][correctness][testing]` The squared-distance-versus-linear-radius placement slip yields invalid layouts in 120 of 120 orderings (correct predicate 0 of 120) while point counts move with order (2-3 correct, 4-5 slip), so judge placement predicates by layout validity across orderings, not one run's count → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E192
- `[algorithm][determinism]` a reading-order chained sort as toposort tie-break was valid 720/720 on friendly names and invalid 720/720 on reversed names, and two valid canonical rules disagree (abcdef vs abcdfe) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E193
- `[navigation][dead-reckoning]` one-in-sixty is reliable only near 30 degrees or less and a zero route misclosure never proves heading or speed calibration (0.000 misclosure with 12.34 worst error) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E194
- `[monetization][disclosure]` disclose pity rules and real-currency cost at a confidence level (62.3 expected pulls, $174.92 for 99 percent) because the headline rate alone implied $367, and without pity needs 766 pulls → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E195
- `[brewing][measurement]` correct every hydrometer reading for sample temperature (an uncorrected 100 F original gravity cost 0.81 ABV points) and name the formula, which differs by 1.08 points at 1.100 to 1.020 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E196
- `[seo][backlinks]` plain PageRank still favoured a 12-link farm over 2 authority links (0.2585 vs 0.1623); only trust-seeded scoring reversed it and one hub link into the farm undid that, so also count distinct owner roots → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E197
- `[algorithm][testing]` a spatial hash sized to the query radius finds every epsilon pair but pairwise-epsilon dedupe is still arrival-order dependent (3 versus 2 survivors) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E198
- `[beekeeping][projection]` project a doubling mite count with the doubling time (day 48 versus 60 by a straight line) and compare per-bee rates, not totals; model values only → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E199
- `[electrical][wire-sizing]` at the same wattage 240 V quarters the percent voltage drop (7.68 to 1.92) and keeps 14 AWG where 120 V needs 8 AWG; a 0.63-per-two-gauge rule lands within 1.3 percent of the table → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E200
- `[lyrical-composition][rhyme-tooling][testing]` Bounding the assonance pass stops the aaaa collapse but turns four same-vowel lines into aabb with closure [1,1], identical to perfect AABB; resolved pairs (2 vs 0) and distinct tails (2 vs 4) separate them, so print those too; 17-word toy lexicon → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E201
- `[plumbing][measurement][rounding]` Toy drain fall budget: 3.5 in usable fall allows 14 ft at 1/4 in per ft and a 10+2 ft mixed run fits exactly, but whole-inch rounding rejected the 14 ft run (12 ft accepted); sum falls unrounded; model values, arithmetic only → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E202
- `[gamedev][quest][state]` Reused-interactable quest chain over 1092 early touch sequences: existence gating and current-quest effect gating both 0 soft-locks (effect gating also gave hints in 1050), a per-object touched flag locked all 1092 even for a perfect player; gate on the current quest, not a flag; toy chain → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E203
- `[brewing][measurement][projection]` Toy fermentation model: two equal 0.001-quantised gravity readings call the finish 3 to 4 days early and temperature correction moves it only 1 day; a log-decay projection from corrected readings was closer (12.5 vs 11.3 against true 14), so project, don't wait for equal readings; model values → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E204
- `[quilting][measurement][correction]` Toy quilt row: a seam 1/16 too wide shortens 12 blocks by 1.375 in and a test strip fixes it, but on a wandering seam the one-strip fix made it worse (0.125 to 0.333 in), so confirm with a second strip; model arithmetic only → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E205
- `[algorithm][graph][determinism]` Rank-pull layout fed by toposort index inherits tie-break instability (declared order: 2 layouts, max move 0.91 over 720 orders; lexicographic: 1; tie-free chain: 1), so canonicalise ties before the rank feeds a force; toy forces → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E206
- `[gamedev][determinism][testing]` Re-check of seeded-rng plus replay fingerprint: state-word saves load in 0 draws (seed+count 250000) and sorted-key hashing gives 1 hash over 24 orders, but cosmetic rolls on the shared stream changed the sim result (3 fingerprints vs 1), so give cosmetics their own stream; toy sim → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E207
- `[beekeeping][projection][sampling]` Toy varroa cadence model: weekly washes leave 1 exposure day, 14 and 28 day washes 8; doubling projection removes exposure but acted 20 days early at 28 day washes, so log early-action days beside exposure; model values only → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E208
- `[ui][input][state][gamedev][testing]` A set-containment cover at base 4219 reproduces the age-cutoff 2-lag flats at N=P and N=2P exactly (P6 7/2, 17/2; P7 38/7, 88/7) and a strict-window mutant loses them, so the P6-P12 values hold on a third independent path → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E209
- `[statistics][confounding][simulation][testing][sandbox]` Healthy-user bias flipped a harmful 1.29 treatment to crude RR 0.82 once uptake spread across health passed 0.46; exact stratification recovered 1.283 but a proxy off by 30 points only 1.009, so adjust only for well-measured confounders, else randomize → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E210
- `[procgen][lsystem][testing][sandbox]` The "bushier rule = more segments but smaller bbox" inversion did not reproduce: 0/14 comparisons over 4 bushy rules and 5 angles (bushy always bigger, e.g. 10.5x segments, 14x area at iter5), while plant width 22.0 to 40.8 over 20-45deg stayed monotonic → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E211
- `[personal-finance][numerics][simulation][testing][sandbox]` In a 30-year 4 percent model, 2.9 points of lost savings rate cost about 3.1 times a 0.75-point fee at every income, flipping only at a 26 percent contribution rate; the SEC and Texas examples reproduced at 28,204 and within 0.5 percent → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E212
- `[tides][simulation][numerics][testing][sandbox]` A two-constituent tide's fortnight is the M2/S2 beat 14.765 d (measured 14.75 by Fourier scan), but day-resolution peak counting read 13.0 d once a diurnal K1 term was added, so measure cycles by dominant-period scan → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E213
- `[npc][ai][gamedev][state][testing][sandbox]` Time-to-collision tiers as a plain priority list matched a utility argmax on 400/400 cells, but the max(1, closing/3.6) clamp fired the soft tier on non-closing hazards at 5-6 m (14 cells) and a 3-tick refused brake turned a stop into a collision, so gate on positive closing speed and escalate on a refused actuator → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E214
- `[cartography][geometry][numerics][testing][sandbox]` Web Mercator area inflation measured by Jacobian is sec^2(lat) (2.00x at 45, 4.00x at 60, 67.33x at 83, cutoff 85.0511); a 20-60 N band averages 1.833 but its midpoint factor says 1.704, so integrate over bands → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E215
- `[origami][geometry][sandbox][testing]` Single-vertex flat-foldability matched Kawasaki's alternating-sum rule in 44528 of 44528 grid patterns and no odd degree folds flat, but a tolerance as wide as the 15 degree step wrongly accepts 6180, so use exact angles → projects/local__2026-10-13__origami-single-vertex-kawasaki-equals-reflection-composition-identity.md#E1
- `[presentation][credibility][pitch][sandbox]` Pitch evidence regenerated by running the current tool on its own pitch stayed fresh 6/6 versions while a pasted output matched 1/6 and claimed success 6/6 against truth 5/6, so gate the build on the self-run succeeding → projects/local__2026-10-13__pitch-self-demo-regenerates-pasted-output-goes-stale-and-hides-regression.md#E1
- `[gamedev][state][testing][determinism][sandbox]` One-word PRNG state restore costs 1 step vs count replay of 200000 with identical draws, but a cached spare gaussian makes state-only restore diverge at odd draw counts (1 and 3), so save buffered values and test odd and even counts → projects/local__2026-10-13__seeded-rng-state-restore-vs-count-replay-and-hidden-state-fingerprint-order.md#E1
- `[lab-dilution][sandbox][numerics][model]` For a 1000x dilution with pipette error max(0.5 percent, a/volume) two balanced steps beat one once a is above about 0.024 uL (1.95 vs 2.5 percent at a=0.02) but three and six steps lose (2.85, 5.05) and one step wins at a=0, so compute the split → projects/local__2026-10-13__serial-dilution-step-count-worst-case-error-crossover-with-pipette-floor.md#E1
- `[tides][sandbox][sampling]` Fortnight = M2/S2 beat 14.765 d (scan 14.75 with or without K1), flat when S2 is removed, but a lone M2 sampled daily fakes a 14.75 d cycle by aliasing, so test by removing a constituent → projects/local__sandbox-tides-fortnight-beat.md#E1
- `[algorithm][gamedesign][procgen][testing][sandbox]` Eager-propagation no-backtracking WFC contradicted in 49 of 2451 non-trivial three-tile sets at 5x5, even 18 of 1940 with a self-compatible tile, and 877 of 2402 completed grids had disconnected walkable cells, so keep restart and flood-fill → projects/local__2026-10-13__wfc-no-backtracking-contradicts-on-non-self-compatible-tilesets-and-completes-disconnected.md#E1
- `[change-ringing][permutation][sandbox][algorithm]` Plain Bob Minimus plain course covers all 24 rows but Doubles' covers 40 of 120 and a single bob never reached the extent (4 of 2509 bob sets did), so count distinct rows and the return to rounds → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E216
- `[debugging][concurrency][testing][sandbox]` ddmin on a failure that shows 4.4% of runs returned the exact culprit pair 0% of 200 offsets with one run per test (always a superset, never smaller) and 100% with 20 or 68 runs, so repeat each test about 1/p times → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E217
- `[origami][geometry][numerics][testing]` Float equality on a decimal-degree grid wrongly rejected 17.7% (alternating sum) and 34.4% (reflection identity) of 358,801 foldable vertices while integer tenths and a 1e-9 tolerance rejected none, so test angles as integers of the grid unit → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E218
- `[temperament][music][numerics][sandbox]` Twelve pure fifths overshoot seven octaves by 23.46 cents; the wolf is 678.49 cents for Pythagorean but 737.64 for quarter-comma meantone (pure thirds), so tempering thirds pure makes the wolf worse, not better → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E219
- `[presentation][credibility][pitch][sandbox][testing]` A pitch's live self-run stayed fresh but caught the regression in only 4/6 versions when its inputs skipped the broken path (pasted also 4/6, wide-input run 6/6), so add adversarial inputs to the self-run and gate on them → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E220
- `[tides][simulation][numerics][sandbox]` Adding N2 (0.2 of M2) left spring spacing at 14.78 d but made successive spring heights differ by up to 24.1% (0% without N2), and dropping S2 gave a spurious 13.04 d peak spacing, so take timing from M2/S2 and heights from the N2 modulation → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E221
- `[algorithm][procgen][testing][sandbox]` Eager WFC with up to 8 restarts rescued only 37 of 151 first-try failures over 560 three-tile sets, and 104 of the 114 left were provably unsatisfiable, so pre-check satisfiability and cap restarts → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E222
- `[security][governance][testing][sandbox]` A zero-allow history was ambiguous in 1 of 3 toy histories (calibrated gate on all-risky traffic looked like a hold-all gate) and a replayed benign canary resolved 3 of 3, while a score-dependent hold response let a prober recover the threshold in 7 probes → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E223
- `[lab-dilution][measurement][numerics][sandbox]` Toy dilution plans through a rounded readout: at step 0.05 the 6-step plan (5.17 percent worst) read exact more often (91 percent) than 1 step (2.51 percent, 75 percent), so a coarse readout can invert the plan ranking; a 20 percent error still showed; model values only → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E224
- `[lab-dilution][sandbox][numerics][model]` Dilution step-count crossover depends on how step errors combine: 0.024 uL for summed worst-case bounds but 0.011 uL for independent (root-sum-square) errors, and at a=0.02 the 2x31.6 split beats 3x10; model values only → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E225
- `[performance][memory][sandbox][model]` Toy generational GC: young size trades throughput (0.105 to 0.882) against pause (55 to 218 us) and promptness, best size is the largest in RAM, the next size up lost 99.9 percent to paging, all-surviving data caps near 0.33; model constants assumed → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E226
- `[cartography][geometry][numerics][sandbox]` Mercator band factor: 1.833 for 20-60 N is the true-area-weighted mean (plain-angle mean is 1.960, midpoint 1.704); midpoint error is 0.0% at 2 degrees wide but 31% at 0-80, so label the weighting → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E227
- `[nonogram][puzzle-design][sandbox][combinatorics]` Nonogram clues: 79.9 percent of all 4x4 pictures are unique (72.1 and 69.7 sampled at 5x5, 6x6) and line propagation fails on about 2 percent of those, so gate a generator on one solution AND a no-guess line solve; fixed-stride samples above 4x4 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E228
- `[determinism][gamedev][state][sandbox]` sfc32 model: restoring 4 saved state words matched 10000 rolls vs 50000 replay calls for a count save; one extra render roll on the shared stream desynced 3999 of 3999 later ticks, a private generator did not; hash state with sorted keys; integer-only single-process model → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E229
- `[juggling][siteswap][sandbox][testing]` Siteswap validity is distinct (beat+throw) mod n, not an integer average: matched a landing simulation in all 111110 sequences, while the average alone wrongly passed 17804 of 22892 (543 averages 4 but collides); one throw per beat only → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E230
- `[fitness][statistics][confounding][sandbox]` Toy saturating-response model: twice-weekly beats once only through per-session saturation (equated gap 0.155, 0 if linear, 3v2 only 0.063) and a volume confound inflated the binary frequency coefficient to 0.295 vs 0.163 equated; assumed constants, not fitted to the 2016 meta-analysis → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E231
- `[testing][state][gamedev][sandbox]` A shift-algebra two-lag cover test at offset 301 reproduces P11 215/11 and 460/11 and P12 25 and 53 and keeps the one-period average flat at exactly N=P and N=2P for P13 to P15 (base-0 control 150/11 differs); contiguous lag pairs, P up to 15 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E232
- `[empathy][burnout][sandbox][model]` Toy two-channel caregiver model with assumed constants: shifting from distress to compassion prevented burnout in 103 of 168 cases against 33 for halving engagement and cut the in-group gap from 0.56 to 0.14, but it fails from load 56 because replenishment is capped, so cap load too → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E233
- `[lab-dilution][sandbox][numerics][model]` A model reproducing the recorded 2.5/1.97/2.85/5.05 percent at a=0.02 puts the 1-to-2 step crossover at 0.0147 uL summed and 0.009 root-sum-square, not 0.024 or 0.011, so compute the crossover; readout rounding flipped the winner at a=0.011; reconstructed model → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E234
- `[roof-framing][geometry][sandbox][formula]` A hip rafter is the 3D diagonal sqrt(2 run^2 + rise^2): common length times root two runs long by 5.4 percent at 6/12, 15.5 percent at 12/12 and 21.3 percent at 16/12 and is exact only when flat; equal-pitch hip roofs only → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E235
- `[nonogram][puzzle-design][sandbox][sampling]` All 65536 4x4 nonograms: 79.9 percent unique and 2.15 percent of those fail line solving, but fixed-stride samples misread the rate (stride 64 by minus 10.6 points, 4096 by plus 20.1, stride 7 by 0.1), so use coprime strides or enumerate; 4x4 only → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E236
- `[statistics][bayes][sandbox][testing]` For a 1 percent condition (90 percent sensitivity, 9 percent false positives) one positive is 9.2 percent, two independent 50.3 percent, but with half the false positives shared it is 14.3 percent and fully shared it falls to 8.3 percent; the 20 percent line is crossed at a shared fraction of 0.31 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E237
- `[juggling][siteswap][sandbox][permutation]` Landing-permutation test matched a landing simulation on all 16807 five-beat sequences (697 valid; average alone wrongly passed 2660) and all valid patterns returned to their start state, though the orbit never discriminates; single throw per beat only → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E238
- `[stair-building][layout][sandbox][rounding]` Over 881 total rises (30-140 in) a fixed 7.5 in riser plus remainder broke the 3/8 in variation limit on 93.5 percent, a repeated rounded riser on 45 (worst 0.5 in), while equal division marked from the top at 1/16 never exceeded 0.0625 in; code limits not checked locally → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E239
- `[change-ringing][parity]` Plain Bob Minor: plain course is 60 of 720 rows and bobs-only touches cap at 360 because lead heads stay even; a single flips parity and reaches the full extent → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E240
- `[sundial][geometry]` Horizontal-dial hour lines tan(a)=sin(lat)tan(15h) hold at every declination only for an axis-parallel style (a vertical stick strays up to 47 deg) and collapse at latitude 0; equation of time adds up to ~16 min → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E241
- `[teaching][method]` Style-matched teaching beats uniform interleaving only if the matching effect exceeds 0.34 over (2q-1) of label accuracy, and finding one learner's better method takes ~80 probes per method at a 0.1 effect, so default to the uniform change → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E242
- `[nonogram][puzzle-design]` Exhaustive 4x4 and 4x5: both gates pass ~77%, non-uniqueness concentrates in sparse pictures and line-stalls peak near 7 filled cells, and a rejected picture is always fixable by flipping at most two cells → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E243
- `[reinforcement][gating]` Premack gate in a bliss-point model binds only when earn ratio is below the free-choice time ratio (nothing at 9, x2.5 at 3) and a gate longer than 1/threshold steps is never started, so split it → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E244
- `[state][gamedev][testing]` Sorted-concat adjacent-dedupe shadow test at base 4219 matches the first-cover dict exactly for P8-P12 (flats at P and 2P); budget about 7 minutes for the full sweep → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E245
- `[conversation][dialogue][model]` In an assumed-weight model a quote's attitude rides the frame (6/6 right, 0/6 once the quotative is removed), but the frame only beats opposing words when its weight exceeds their combined weight → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E246
- `[empathy][bias][model]` Harm endorsement tracks the in/out-group empathy gap and is invariant to general empathy level in a ratio decision, but a fixed action cost makes the level matter too → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E247
- `[determinism][state][gamedev]` Saving sfc32's four state words restores in 1 step against 50000 replay, but lockstep parity also needs equal tick counts (a clamp truncates the stream) and a fixed draw order → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E248
- `[presentation][architecture][model]` In an assumed-cost reader model, context-first layering serves the novice but deferring depth with no reachable path fails the expert at any budget, and a click cost of 6 or more breaks it → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E249
- `[empathy][method]` a double dissociation can come from one latent with crossing task curves, so confirm two systems with a non-monotone state-trace or a one-measure manipulation → projects/local__sandbox-double-dissociation-state-trace.md#E1
- `[narrative][research]` a survivorship legend ends at the first survivor, and eyewitness roots can be plentiful while explainer roots are zero, so count them separately → projects/local__sandbox-legend-survivorship-first-survivor.md#E1
- `[marketing][simulation]` in a quality-weighted second-price model a lower bid with better quality wins and pays less, quality doubling halves price, and a rank floor makes bids irrelevant below it → projects/local__sandbox-quality-weighted-auction.md#E1
- `[3d-modeling][texture]` block compression saves 8x for RGBA but 6x for RGB, mips add a third, WebGL 1 power-of-two padding costs 1.4x, and tiny mips compress worse than raw → projects/local__sandbox-vram-block-compression-npot-mips.md#E1
- `[origami][geometry]` Maekawa and Kawasaki are necessary but not sufficient for a flat-foldable vertex: 8 Maekawa-valid assignments gave 8, 4, 0 and 4 foldable, so add a layer-order check → projects/local__sandbox-origami-single-vertex-flat-foldability.md#E1
- `[ui][input][state][testing]` First-cover dict and reduce-and-cover shadow counters agreed at every N for P6-P8 at base 6101 (flats at P and 2P; P8 gave 8 and 18) while a size-only cover differed, so cross-check two verified paths on shared inputs → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E250
- `[negotiation][anchoring]` Detected-extreme-anchor backfire needs a repeat deal or trust at stake; in a capped one-shot extremes still pay, so anchor to the plausibility edge only when it repeats → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E176
- `[pitch][persuasion][model]` Collapsing a fiction onto the room's real situation beat an appended CTA only when the story shared at least 4 of 6 real features (0.332 vs 0.323, narrow); at 3 of 6 it lost at 0.155, so check the mapping first → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E177
- `[negotiation][model][sandbox]` Stating pay is negotiable closes an asking gap only when it comes from unequal belief about whether asking is allowed (gap 0.154 to 0.0); a cost-of-asking gap stayed at 0.128 and widened with a bigger shift, and zero employer headroom meant no asks → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E178
- `[origami][geometry][numerics][sandbox]` Single-vertex flat-fold check on integer 30-degree sectors: exact Kawasaki always leaves a valid crease assignment, but only 42-60% of Maekawa-valid assignments pass a crimp layer check, and float equality rejects 40 of 100 foldable vertices → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E251
- `[genealogy][model][sandbox]` Ancestor slots double each generation but distinct ancestors cap at population size or the mating-reachable share: N=64 capped at 64 from generation 6, close-kin mating held at 32, a large population lost none by generation 10 → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E179
- `[structures][truss][model]` Maxwell's count classified four simple trusses correctly but a braced square with a one-bar dangling joint scored 0 and was still a mechanism (rank 9 of 10), so confirm determinacy with the equilibrium-matrix rank → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E180
- `[testing][state][gamedev]` Running one cover implementation on the other entry's moduli: gap-delta walk at offset 9203 reproduced first-cover dict values 215/11, 460/11, 25 and 53 for P11 and P12 with flats only at P and 2P, while an any-overlap cover gave 550 and 726 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E252
- `[gamedesign][state][determinism]` A mid-dispatch unsubscribe in a live-iterated event bus skipped one listener and on a shared RNG stream shifted 4 of 5 scores versus 1 of 5 with addressed draws; snapshot the listeners and address the draws → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E253
- `[negotiation][logrolling][sandbox]` Packaging three issues beat midpoint-per-issue 130 vs 90 with opposed priorities, 110 partial, 0 gain identical, and a walk-away line between the totals turned no deal into 65/65, so roll issues only where priorities differ → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E254
- `[determinism][testing][sandbox]` State-word saves restored exactly in 4 copies versus a 20000-roll replay, canonical fingerprints caught drift, and a fourth entity on a shared stream changed 3 of 3 outcomes at constant roll count versus 0 with per-entity streams → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E255
- `[voice][tts][ssml]` In a toy TTS model a words-per-minute estimate without break seconds missed 2 of 6 over-budget lines and a duration check caught dropped breaks 6 of 6 but a dropped emphasis tag 0 of 3 at 5 percent, so add pauses to the filter and check each tag per voice → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E256
- `[accessibility][captions][sandbox][design]` Curb-cut reach is a default-and-friction effect: in a threshold model captions reached 1000 users on by default, 356 with a toggle and only the 50-person minority when buried (friction 0.6), cutting the caption-tell audience 8.9x; population shares are assumed → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E257
- `[algorithm][gamedev][pathfinding][sandbox]` Flow-field vs A* crossover is not size-independent: expansions ratio ran open 2.1→5.8 agents (n=15→41) but serpentine stayed 1.2-1.5, so recompute per map class instead of quoting 5-7 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E258
- `[determinism][gamedev][state][sandbox]` Seeded-RNG kernel verified: state words restore in 4 words vs 10000 replayed rolls, but a shared seed does not give lockstep (28/30 rolls diverge on different consumption order) or fairness (frame-count seed selects 6/6 outcomes), so split gameplay and cosmetic streams → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E259
- `[songwriting][rhyme][sandbox][testing]` Two-pass rhyme letters differ from one-pass on 4704 of 20736 stanzas (cat bat map tap: AAAA vs AABB) because assonance fuses perfect pairs, but both are order independent; make family rhymes narrow and test on a stanza built to separate them → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E260
- `[testing][detection][sandbox]` Stacking two detector cues gains AUC only with independent errors (0.759 to 0.843 at rho 0, 0.766 at rho 0.9) and equal-weighting a weak cue onto a strong one cost 0.032, so weight by effect size and measure error correlation first → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E261
- `[localization][captions][measurement]` a flat word-growth budget for captions is fine at generous durations but at 0.35x duration +8% missed 3 of 3 overruns and +30% gave 5 false alarms; measure the translated string (own small sample) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E262
- `[decision-theory][regret][escalation]` under minimax regret a sunk cost on every act cannot change the choice but an unchosen decoy (stall payoff 11-23) flipped continue to withdraw; recompute rankings without each unchosen option (toy payoffs) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E263
- `[narrative-design][tournament][power-scaling]` format variety lowers the tier of arc winners only when power is a vector (lowest winner tier 2 vs 3) yet the tier ceiling still decides 25 of 28 duels at niche 5 (toy roster) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E264
- `[speech-errors][monitoring]` a slip-filtering monitor gives lexical bias only with time and attention; zero time budget erases it in the model (fitted, mechanism sufficiency only) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E265
- `[dsp][emulation][audio]` a spectrum check sees only the 7-step cycle of the taps 0,3 noise LFSR (0.011% of seeds); the 8191-step cycle (12.5%) is white and needs lag-8191 autocorrelation (1.00) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E266
- `[scheduling][timezone][estimation]` a wall-clock window padded with summed worst cases fits 0.904 of normal nights under shared delay, 0.829 on spring-forward; the extra fall-back hour does not repair it (illustrative distributions) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E267
- `[art][texture][sampling]` Pad+extrude stops atlas bleed only at mip 0: nearest/bilinear with no pad bled 1.0/0.5, a transparent pad left a 0.5 alpha hole, and the minimum clean extruded pad doubled per mip level (1,2,4,8 texels at levels 0-3) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E268
- `[marketing][advertising][model]` In a quality-weighted auction a fatiguing creative drops out of serving in 2 weeks at decay 0.85 (3 weeks at double the bid and a pricier click, 0.733 against 0.605) while rotating six served all 12 weeks with 4.9 times the clicks, and at weak fatigue rotation still led by 13.5% → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E269
- `[elevator][dispatch][starvation]` Nearest-first dispatch halves travel on a batch (29 against 55) but starved a far call for 418 ticks under a near-call stream where a one-direction sweep served it in 33 at the same travel, and nearest-first even lost on mean wait (3.4 against 2.8) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E270
- `[speech][model][metrics]` Eight fillers each, an all-uh, all-um and mixed speaker score identically on count but carry 8.00 / 32.96 / 20.48 s of post-filler delay, so rank by delay seconds, and note that cutting fillers while leaving the silence changes nothing but loses the 47 ms uh benefit → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E271
- `[humor][pitch][model]` A mid-pitch joke beat an opening one in only 38 of 55 cells of an independent model, not all: opening won whenever initial attention was 0.75 or lower, earlier slots beat the exact middle (52/43/38/33/29 for slots 1 to 5), and removing the change-frame benefit changed nothing → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E272
- `[data-toy][statistics][model]` A p<.05 gate on a lag slider that searches 8 lags passed 36.7% of noise pairs at n=14, alpha/8 cut it to 4.0% but missed an r=0.4 effect 87% of the time, and daily series with 0.8 autocorrelation pushed even alpha/8 back to 30.5% → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E273
- `[load-balancing][dispatch][simulation]` Always-pick-the-minimum balancing went from 1.01 to 1.82 peak imbalance when its load report was 100 jobs old while two hashed choices went 1.03 to 1.12, but fresh least-loaded beat two choices on heavy-tailed sizes (1.08 against 1.25) and round-robin by count hit 1.62 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E274
- `[networking][prediction][state]` One stamped-version guard fixes both late acks (5/28 wrong to 0) and click-versus-timer double advances (19 to 0 of 36), duplicates were harmless, and a counter reset froze the guard (0/3 fresh acks applied), so pair the stamp with an epoch → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E275
- `[narrative][algorithm][testing][simulation][sandbox]` Arc classifier tie default misread 112/729 choice paths (flat openings and the all-flat path), and arc class changed with order in 15/28 sets with or without a clamp → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E276
- `[ui][gamedesign][combat][sandbox][testing]` A flashy decoupled power number only forecasts duels while monotone in real stats (0/66 inversions); saga bonuses cost 16/66, silent scouter suppression 24/66 until an unreadable state, and a damage cap pins fights at 1/cap rounds → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E277
- `[pathfinding][algorithm][gamedev][sandbox][testing]` Octile vs flat-diagonal A* changed the route on 39% of 28%-wall maps (0% on open ones), a greedy mover was stuck on 54% while the path consumer was never, and a one-cell patch replanned 277/277 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E278
- `[rendering][determinism][gamedev][sandbox][testing]` Seed shadow-test jitter from a private stream: shared stream made acne vary 27-31 and let sample count shift gameplay rolls, and slope bias lost to constant on 12/12 seeds at low jitter → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E279
- `[search][ranking][algorithm][sandbox][testing]` A noisy-channel corrector trained on its own raw suggestion clicks stays wrong when the true popularity ratio is under the 3.33 exposure ratio (8/8 wrong at 1.5 and 3, 0/8 at 4); IPS fixed all → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E280
- `[algorithm][state][simulation]` A biased sort-shuffle cost exactly the same 3.0 steps as Fisher-Yates when averaged over uniform targets, yet 2.32 to 4.06 steps at fixed targets, so average regret cannot see shuffle bias → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E281
- `[gamedev][coop][state]` Only unpossess-before-parent adopted a dropped co-op pawn (1 of 4 teardown orders), and the adopted bot still left the info-holding role hollow and silenced a pacing trigger (4 needed vs 3) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E282
- `[statistics][survey][verification]` A fear ranked above death on an independent mean rating had 60 percent of respondents choose death as worse head-to-head in a split population, so a list ranking implies no pairwise result → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E283
- `[security][auth][mfa]` Only origin-bound FIDO blocked a relay attacker (0 of 3 attackers) while stacked sms+push stayed breached, and stolen session cookies bypassed even FIDO, so tier the factor and harden the session separately → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E284
- `[api][api-design][rate-limit]` A header-aware client had 0 rejections vs 12 for backoff and 100 for blind retry, but five header-aware clients on one shared window still collided 48 times at the reset, so add jitter and never retry a 403 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E285
- `[coop][state][gamedev]` A merge-style share import matched the reference only when the stale local cooldown happened to equal the sender's, so replace the whole state, ship draw-gating timers, and snapshot on export → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E286
- `[reinforcement][progress-design]` Put progress milestones in the early low-vigor trough, not evenly spaced; sandbox gain over uniform was modest and vanishes with tiny or fast-decaying bonuses → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E287
- `[storytelling][narrative]` A regex irrealis count scored precision 0.55 and recall 0.86 for Labov evaluation clauses; negatives in orientation are the false positives, so count only in the pre-climax window and keep subjective feeling clauses as a separate tally → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E288
- `[gamedev][testing][level-design]` A completability bot passing proves one takeoff point; a jump that passes or fails on upstream drift is a margin bug in the level, not the bot → projects/pappydapimp69__sweety.md#E1
- `[gamedev][level-design][coop]` Every co-op gate must be reopenable from the far side by whoever got through, plus a both-players back-to-checkpoint that needs no death → projects/pappydapimp69__sweety.md#E2
- `[input][state][gamedev][ui]` A consumer-drained input latch must count edges, not flag them, where the consumer acts per press (menus, steppers), or a slow frame eats taps → projects/pappydapimp69__sweety.md#E3
- `[gamedev][state][physics]` A door kept open "while occupied" lets the mover through if occupancy is read mid-collision; snapshot occupancy at step start → projects/pappydapimp69__sweety.md#E4
- `[gamedev][physics][testing]` Fixed-step Euler jumps peak below v*v/2g (3.17 vs 3.33 tiles); design level rises from the measured peak with sideways margin, and pin it in a test → projects/pappydapimp69__sweety.md#E5
- `[testing][gamedev][physics]` After route bots and reviews, a seeded mash fuzzer with physical invariants (no NaN, no body in a wall), started from mid-route states too, still finds collision bugs → projects/pappydapimp69__sweety.md#E6
- `[gamedev][physics][coop][state]` Size a constraint correction (co-op leash) to this step's own motion, never to the whole violation: after a respawn the full pull teleports through walls → projects/pappydapimp69__sweety.md#E7
- `[gamedev][physics][level-design]` Stop a lift slightly above the ledge a rider steps onto, never flush: eased motion ends a hair low and the ledge becomes a wall → projects/pappydapimp69__sweety.md#E8
- `[networking][determinism][testing][gamedev]` Test deterministic lockstep with an open-loop input tape replayed against an offline end fingerprint, not closed-loop bots, which react to the very delay under test → projects/pappydapimp69__sweety.md#E9
- `[gamedev][physics][state]` Moving platforms: carry riders by the platform's full delta before their own physics and test one-way landing against its previous y, or descents flicker and rises tunnel → projects/pappydapimp69__sweety.md#E10
- `[gamedev][level-design][state][coop]` A timed hazard (rising water) must take its own checkpoint when it starts: in co-op the partner's last flag can sit under it → projects/pappydapimp69__sweety.md#E11
- `[gamedev][state][physics]` Record a respawn safe point only after the step's hazard checks and only if the player survived, or the killing spot becomes the respawn → projects/pappydapimp69__sweety.md#E12
- `[networking][ui][state][gamedev]` A stall indicator checked right after a drain-until-not-ready loop is false by construction; reset it on work done this frame (n > 0), not on readiness → projects/pappydapimp69__sweety.md#E13
- `[gamedev][level-design][state]` Reused switch colours leak into far machines with accumulating state (a mover's clock); scope movers to nearby switches → projects/pappydapimp69__sweety.md#E14
- `[couch-multiplayer][level-design]` A power that only changes the partner's footing makes a gate co-op only if the gate is above the solo jump and within the duo's power budget; check both edges → projects/pappydapimp69__sweety.md#E15
- `[simulation][testing][method]` A sandbox on guessed parameters only supports claims that hold across the guess range: a 6-setting sweep kept "10 posts cannot judge a hit-or-miss format" (47-93% misjudged) but killed the "30-60 posts" figure (7-63% at 60) and a loop result implied by its own hazard assumption → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E7
- `[algorithm][scheduling]` Earliest-finish-first beat brute force on all 401,929 small interval sets while earliest-start lost on 101,256 and shortest-first on 1,712; fewest-conflicts survived to 8 intervals → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E289
- `[performance][measurement][compression]` Batching ratio measured on recycled messages overstates batch-128 ratio 1.9x-19x and shows a jump at the first batch above the pool size; the varied-message knee was 64 here, not 8 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E290
- `[dsp][film][audio][stability]` A DF2T cutoff switch in a film mix lowers the peak-normalized mix by 4.1-9.2 dB (Q10 7-sample switching diverges) while the TPT SVF stays within 1 dB; pick the SVF before normalizing → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E291
- `[reinforcement][progress-design]` front-loading milestones only helps if each leaves lasting momentum; with no carry-over bonus spacing is irrelevant → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E292
- `[algorithm][hardware][testing]` A binary counter read mid-change is off by up to 2^(k-1)-1 counts (127 at 8 bits) while Gray is exactly old or new; a truncated mod-10 Gray changes 3 bits at the wrap, so use the middle slice (codes 3-12) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E293
- `[agent][selector][state]` No release rule for a held selector won every refusal pattern in a 40-tick toy: hold livelocked (0 work), first-refusal bounced 10 times under noise, and release after 2 consecutive refusals had the lowest total shortfall (13) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E294
- `[search][reinforcement-learning][algorithm]` A prior-guided search reached a best move only after 1,301 / 113,101 playouts at prior 1e-2 / 1e-3 and never in 2M at 1e-4, and retraining on its own counts cannot rescue a never-visited move; give low-prior moves a prior-free first look → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E295
- `[rpg][combat][testing]` A percent-HP damage cap kept the 3v1 boss losing under cap-division and extra actions, and the burst skill's bands 0.25-0.50 vanished while the skill count stayed flat only when burst tied for best in a low band; check per-band wins → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E296
- `[ui][input][state][gamedev][testing]` A delimited-string cover matched dict at base 4801 for P8 and P9 in any list order, and the undelimited string cover differed from dict at base 4801 and base 0 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E297
- `[painting][method][sandbox]` Editing only the best chance patches cost 0.73 changes per motif against 8 planned from blank (0.6% of cells touched), but a 16-patch field cost 2.91 and each edit shifts 8 neighbours → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E298
- `[database][migration][sandbox]` In sqlite a one-step column rename broke the old app on 2 of 2 statements, staged expand then contract kept 0 errors, and rebuilding a cascade parent table deleted 3 of 3 children → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E299
- `[economy][itemization][sandbox]` Adding a rarity tier above the top devalued owned items 0.167 (0.667 cost-weighted) but below or middle inserts left the top at 0, so add tiers below the top → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E300
- `[narrative][process][research]` Both big story fixes across six shorts were textbook storyboarding (therefore/but beats, continuity of state across a reset) that the user supplied after a render; run a researched fundamentals pass before scripting and treat a critique citing a known rule as a skipped research step → projects/local__film.md#E9
- `[time-management][planning][sandbox]` Calendar blocks beat a loose list only via an assumed zero start delay (6 vs 5 tasks at bias 1.0), tied it at bias 1.5, and hard-stop slots flagged overrun at minute 60 but finished 0 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E301
- `[film][animation][canvas]` A clamping keyframe tween parks a prop at its first key between entrances (a cursor sat visible in the TikTok UI band for up to 10 s, 4 of 24 stills); key entering props from fully off-canvas and cull off-canvas draws → projects/local__film.md#E10
- `[film][video][research]` TikTok publishes no pixel safe zone and third-party figures disagree by up to 410 px at the bottom (250-660 px on 1920); lay out with the strictest margin per edge (120 sides, 240 top, 660 bottom) and confirm against the platform overlay → projects/local__film.md#E11
- `[brain][testing]` derived score fed by optional fields: check intake writes them and cite coverage N-of-M (tension confidence was 20/122) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E181
- `[narrative][comedy][structure]` In a format parody (fake mobile ad), never let a character or caption name the format ("That's the ad", disclaimers); copy the surface exactly and land the satire in-world, so a real success undone by the format's own habit beats any explaining line → projects/local__film.md#E12
- `[audio][film][process]` Removing one audio layer (voice blips) turned a designed "music off" beat into 10 s of dead air (RMS 0.003); rescan for silent windows after any layer change, and author silence beats as under ~1 s followed by a low ambient bed → projects/local__film.md#E13
- `[audio][film][video]` Sound synced to an animation's phase (stomps at k*pi/3) left a short's hook silent for its first second (RMS 0.001 vs 0.20); put an explicit hit on frame 0 and scan the opening with 1 s RMS windows → projects/local__film.md#E14
- `[process][research][legal]` An "invented" parody game name built from its genre's stock words often already exists (Crowd Clash matched a real Count Master title); search the name in the app stores before rendering and prefer names outside the genre vocabulary → projects/local__film.md#E15
- `[film][animation][canvas]` An overshoot (back) ease returns ~-2e-15 at exactly t=0, and canvas arc() throws on any negative radius (killed a render at frame 504, first frame of a pop-in); clamp max(0, r) inside the circle helper, not at call sites → projects/local__film.md#E16
