- `[narrative][comedy][gamedesign]` In a progression gag (merge-to-evolve parody), partner tiles from the destination tier placed on the opening board spoil the climb (office workers visible at t=0); keep later-tier items off the opening frame and drop them in as in-world rewards when earned → projects/local__film.md#E17
- `[comedy][narrative][process][testing]` Across six shorts, lead agency, a physical sight-gag payoff and a benign violation each separated the user's liked from disliked (concordance 0.79, rubric 0.86) while the "avoid cliches" check alone scored at chance (0.50); gate no-dialogue comedy on those three, polish originality second → projects/local__film.md#E18
- `[cooking][balance]` In a toy emulsion model, acid water raises only the oil-fraction ceiling, not the pour-rate or lecithin limits, so slow the oil first → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E302
- `[testing][state]` String and bitstring cover tests match a set only with delimiters or fixed width; no-delimiter failed 59 of 864 pairs and a narrow bitstring 18, silently → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E303
- `[cycling][gear-ratios]` Nominal gear count (rings times sprockets) overstates usable distinct ratios: 22 became 16 usable and 15 distinct under stated assumptions → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E304
- `[locksmithing][counting]` Pin-tumbler key space is not depths to the pins once an adjacent-cut limit applies: 100,000 nominal became 27,142 at a limit of 4 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E305
- `[prompt-engineering][ui]` A live tag-count meter beats a written range but is not sufficient: a 7-tag in-band style still lost bracket elements; pair it with an inventory check → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E306
- `[git][tooling]` Atomic monorepo changes remove broken and skewed states but need affected-only CI and owner-wide review; a root change gets no CI saving → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E307
- `[ui][input][testing]` Hint-lag mismatch sweeps are phase-flat only over whole cycles; a 600-tick window varied 171-172 by start and lag=P aliases to 0 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E308
- `[fitness][timing]` In a toy DOMS model twice-weekly sessions per muscle start with 0.5-0.75 of peak soreness, once-weekly starts clear, and spacing matters more than a 2-10% load step → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E309
- `[performance][mobile]` full-screen hallucination effects scale with DPR squared: ~31 ms on a mid phone at DPR 3 vs ~1 ms desktop (bandwidth model); render post at DPR 1, blur at half res → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E98
- `[lyrical-composition][rhyme-tooling][testing]` A rhyme-letter assigner that keys on a None no-match class merges every orphan line into one letter: free verse ABCD reads AAAA and ABBC reads ABBA (closure 1 -> 3); give orphans unique letters and count them → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E8
- `[gamedev][determinism][testing]` Raw sfc32 state words reload in O(1) where seed+count replays 5000 calls, and float order flips a trigger while an integer zone holds; test float drift in Python 3.12+ with reduce(operator.add), since sum() is compensated → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E9
- `[tuning-systems][closure][error]` A closed circle of fifths hides a 1.2-cent octave stretch (0.000 misclosure), and spreading one 10-cent bad fifth evenly makes 12/12 thirds wrong (was 4/12); find the outlying fifth before tempering → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E10
- `[card-magic][permutation][exhaustive]` Eight perfect out-faros restore only a 52-card deck: the count is the order of 2 mod n-1, so 54 cards need 52 and 50 need 21; in-faros for 1 bits and out-faros for 0 bits send the top card to any position → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E11
- `[testing][concurrency]` a Gray counter read across two steps is no longer old-or-new but stays within 1 count (binary off by 2^(k-1)-1); one-bit wraps exist only for even moduli via a centred slice → projects/local__gray-counter-torn-read-two-steps.md#E1
- `[brain][testing]` steward validated the merged batch and aborted on any error: 8 bad Linked refs froze 46 kernels ~30h; hold the bad file, alert on failure → projects/pappydapimp69__ideas.md#E4
- `[determinism][state][concurrency]` Copying sfc32 state words while the sim steps gave 112 of 112 torn snapshots that are no real state and diverge from both streams; Gray can't fix a 4-word state, so double-buffer it and flip a 1-bit (or Gray-coded) index → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E12
- `[lyrical-composition][rhyme-tooling][state]` Scope a rhyme-letter table by section type: global made 6 false cross-section links, per-section reset lost 8 chorus-repeat links, per-type made 0 of each; still label letters by scope (34 look-alikes) → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E13
- `[brain][testing]` two promotion gates in series: give precedence per defect type to the gate that checks it (structure→Brain, evidence/dup→owner), never batch-abort → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E182
- `[gamedesign][input]` one use button: state gates + per-class tree tolerance reach every target, but two actions valid in the same state always lose one to any ranking; give one a second input → projects/pappydapimp69__sevin.md#E17
- `[accessibility][gamedesign]` content warnings: one game-wide list + per-type filters leaks 0 bits of a run, per-day warnings leak all of it; name the twist only under an expected umbrella word → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E99
- `[permutation][parity][change-ringing]` All-even moves can't reach an odd ordering (plain+bob ring 360 of 720; 8, 16, 24 card faros never go odd) but can reach far less than half (8-card faros: 24 of 20,160), so use parity to rule out, not promise → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E14
- `[gamedev][harness][process]` A test step(n) hook must own the game clock (pause the rAF accumulator) or real-time ticks interleave and deterministic tests flake → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E1
- `[wayfinding][navigation]` a sun compass is biased not noisy: 15 deg/h correction turns a walker 54 deg over two June midday hours at 40N (177 near the zenith); shadow-tip is exact only at the equinox → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E100
- `[concurrency][state]` a double-buffered state save tears once the writer completes 2 updates during one copy (1540/3003); n slots tolerate n-1; a seqlock never tears but retries nearly always under a busy writer → projects/local__double-buffered-save-writer-laps-reader.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][research]` playing Hellblade lowered social distance via identification (N 198) but not the 'dangerous' stereotype; both studies detect only d >= 0.4, so keep the person as experiencer and claim no stigma benefit → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E101
- `[determinism][testing]` differing sin() libms forked a feedback sim in 75 steps but never a branch-only sim in 100k; ban transcendentals where they feed state, and back the grep guard (missed Math['sin']) with a cross-runtime replay → projects/local__transcendental-drift-forks-only-when-it-feeds-state.md#E1
- `[physics][simulation]` a particle speed cap per tick guards only a fixed step: frame-time stepping tunnelled a 4 px wall at 30/10 fps and on one 250 ms hitch; an accumulator never did → projects/local__particle-speed-cap-per-tick-fixed-step.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][audio]` voice hearers mostly report resistible commands (51% vs <=16% irresistible) and mixed hostile+helpful voices; a voice layer of hostile commands that seize control inverts the real proportions → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E102
- `[gamedev][testing][design]` Measure whether best and worst autopilots actually separate before asserting bad play loses; in a cascade-heavy match-3 worst beat best on 2 of 3 levels, so test rules plus the win path → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E2
- `[brain][testing]` hub-specificity weighting did not reduce the paths echo; per-day fade and the weakest-link tie-break did the damage → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E183
- `[audio][gamedesign]` hallucinated voices barely shrink a whistle's range: +3 dB per doubling cost 4-6% in woods, 7-11% in the open (air absorption caps it); drowning a whistle needs louder voices or a changed signal → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E103
- `[testing][physics]` a win-only level test passes broken physics inside its margin: at 20 fps 12/40 particles tunnelled and the level still won; assert trajectory invariants at the lowest frame rate → projects/local__win-only-level-test-passes-tunnelling-run.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][survival]` a windbreak's value scales with cold more than with how fully it stops wind: 15 mph->calm is worth 5 F at 50 F but 14 F at -10 F; cutting a gale to 15 mph gives ~75% of full calm → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E104
- `[gamedesign][survival]` at camp scale (~10 logs a night) green wood's penalty vanishes into whole numbers: +1 feed, 0 extra trips at 50% moisture, 2x at 65%; put the cost in weight or burn time, not counts → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E105
- `[firewood][units][simulation][testing]` Convert a dry-basis moisture reading to wet basis before the firewood heat formula: the mistake costs under 8% on seasoned wood but makes green wood negative (-2.4 vs 8.3 MJ/kg), so test on green wood → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E15
- `[gamedev][physics][simulation][determinism]` The pin-puzzle speed cap and a fixed-step accumulator are both required: frame-time steps tunnel 33% of start phases at 30 fps and 17% with one hitch, and the accumulator alone tunnels 50% at 900 px/s → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E16
- `[sundial][geometry][simulation]` A sundial style tilted off the latitude angle reads wrong by up to 2.3 x tilt / sin(latitude) minutes, worst 3-4 h from noon and exact at noon, so check a new dial mid-morning (3.6 min per degree at latitude 40) → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E17
- `[state][determinism]` two save bugs can mask each other: torn rng/sim save + dropped spare gaussian diverged 400/800, fixing only the spare raised it to 800; snapshot draw-gating state atomically → projects/local__two-save-bugs-mask-each-other.md#E1
- `[audio][wayfinding]` SEVEN's answer whistle tells direction but not distance (no distance term) and front/back only by level (74/360 bearings ambiguous at 4 dB); add falloff and a rear low-pass → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E106
- `[algorithm][packing]` first-fit decreasing is near-optimal only for spread sizes: items at 20-50% of capacity left ~17% of 12-item lists a bin over optimum; Johnson's mix 11 vs 9; check ceil(sum/C) → projects/local__ffd-misses-optimum-near-a-third.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][balance]` personal-best banking is a record-only reward and stalls (median drought 76/80 runs); a +20 capped per-run term left 57 because it was under one stage's cost; size it to the next stage → projects/local__personal-best-banking-stalls.md#E1
- `[rendering][perception]` SEVEN's linear fog keeps mid-range far clearer than real fog (50 vs 14% contrast at half range) and pops objects in 12x faster at the edge; use exp(-3.912 d/V) for weather → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E107
- `[songwriting][rhyme]` feed the rhyme-shape table only two-pass resolved letters on sections found by repetition: one pass turned open XXAA stanzas with stray assonance into 'stable' AABB (3/3) → projects/pappydapimp69__suno-builder.md#E9
- `[testing][state]` a state-agreement check is legality not freshness: on a jittered wave it passed every legal stale press (360 at lag 16/47, 400 at the 31 super-cycle), not just aliased lags; gate staleness on age → projects/local__agreement-gating-passes-every-legal-stale-press.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][survival]` a dying incandescent torch starts ~2x rated, passes rated at 70% of runtime and ends dim (38%), never dark; a regulated LED holds full to ~90% then cuts out; neither is a linear bar → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E108
- `[testing][state]` on a Fibonacci world the lossless cutoff is still the largest valid lag, but mismatch falls with age at Fibonacci lags (0.069 at 13 to 0.010 at 89) and stays low near their sums; gate on age → projects/local__fibonacci-world-older-presses-look-fresher.md#E1
- `[creative-arts][calligraphy]` broad-nib thickest stroke runs at pen angle + 90; 30 degrees weights verticals, 45 degrees flattens V/H contrast → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E18
- `[creative-arts][ceramics]` size wet clay as target/(1-shrink), not target*(1+shrink); the shortcut fires short by shrink squared → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E19
- `[creative-arts][theater]` a stage stagger needs ~3 body radii of sideways offset; a small nudge blocks nearly as many seats as a depth line → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E20
- `[audio][gamedesign]` cricket chirp rate is an honest night clock: Dolbear T = 50 + (N-40)/4 F slows a bed from 128 to 60/min as the night cools, then silence; drive it from air temperature → projects/pappydapimp69__seven.md#E109
- `[songwriting][rhyme][tooling]` suno-builder upper-cases the two-pass scheme before the shape note: aabb reads very stable, AA+bb renders AAaa then AAAA; look up shape on resolved letters only → projects/pappydapimp69__suno-builder.md#E10
- `[songwriting][suno-prompting][tooling]` suno-builder's over-budget hyphenate button never lowers the counted syllables (0/36) and raises silent-e words; letters survive, the warning cannot clear → projects/pappydapimp69__suno-builder.md#E11
- `[narrative][tooling]` time each reveal from the line that opens its gap: an absolute cue list spoiled reveals in 14/20 one-line rewrites and left dead air when lines were cut → projects/local__film.md#E19
- `[gamedev][input][state]` the cutoff that is lossless with a validity check is the worst for the same gate without one; per-lag invalid = (N/P)·min(L,W,P-L,P-W) → projects/local__lossless-cutoff-is-worst-without-validity.md#E1
- `[game-design][gamedev]` night-sight cost from staring at a fire saturates in ~10 s (12x look time = 1.1x minutes); use a fast-fill slow-drain bleach meter, not minutes per second → projects/local__night-sight-cost-saturates-after-ten-seconds.md#E1
- `[card-magic][permutation]` one faro slip only disturbs cards interleaved after it: the 8-faro restore keeps ~51% home and Elmsley still lands the top card 81% (93% shallow, 73% deep) → projects/local__one-faro-slip-only-disturbs-cards-below-it.md#E1
- `[braille][unicode]` braille digits reuse the a-j cells: 15.2% of short alphanumeric strings misread without a letter sign, exactly digit-then-a..j; emit dots 56 there → projects/local__braille-digits-reuse-a-to-j-so-letters-after-numbers-need-a-sign.md#E1
- `[braille][unicode]` the from-memory braille table is confirmed by liblouis: 26/26 letters fit the French-order decade rule, digits = a-j, numsign 3456, terminator 56 → projects/local__braille-table-confirmed-against-liblouis.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][economy][sandbox]` Idle cost growth g walls progress (g 1.07: first 60 s wait at buy 109); a prestige multiplier m buys only about log(m)/log(g) more purchases (x10 gave 38), flat costs never wall → projects/local__film.md#E20
- `[rendering][canvas][art]` Pixel-art mixels come from a fractional upscale (x2.5 gave art pixels 2 and 3 wide), not from a sub-pixel offset at integer scale (stayed 3); Bayer 4x4 gives 17 tones from two colours → projects/local__film.md#E21
- `[film][rendering][narrative]` A Bayer 4x4 reveal has only 17 states, so a cubic suspense ramp over 48 frames held 15 frames at level 0; schedule the 16 level changes, not frames, and anchor the matrix to the world (0/47 nesting breaks vs 43/47 screen-anchored) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E184
- `[songwriting][rhyme][testing]` Stanzas written for seven rhyme shapes got the intended letters from the real suno-builder in 1/8 (first draft) and 4/8 (spelling-matched) cases, and an XXAA fixture came back AABB; score the closure rule on returned letters and check them against intent first → projects/pappydapimp69__suno-builder.md#E12
- `[gamedev][determinism][testing]` A replay fingerprint of game state alone missed a final stray RNG draw in 50/50 seeds and an early one in 8/50; folding the 4 sfc32 state words into the hash caught all, so fingerprint the generator state too → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E185
- `[knot-theory][topology][algorithm]` Fox colouring counts from PD codes separate the trefoil (9 mod 3) from the unknot and figure-eight (3), but figure-eight and cinquefoil tie at 3 mod 3 and 25 mod 5 because both have determinant 5; use a finer invariant for equal determinants → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E186
- `[rendering][testing][process]` A hand-typed Bayer 4x4 kept 17 distinct tones under all 120 two-cell swaps, so a level count cannot catch a typo; assert equality with the recursive construction (caught 120/120) instead → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E187
- `[film][rendering][pixel-art]` At 4.5x a one-column-per-frame reveal wipe steps 4 or 5 px (200/200 columns) but two columns per frame is a steady 9 px; step wipes in multiples of the scale's period, or use integer scale 4 with 320 px bars → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E188
- `[color-science][rendering][numerics]` Averaging sRGB bytes is not averaging light: black and white give byte 128 at 21.6% luminance (true mean is byte 188), and an 8-bit linear buffer merges 73 of 256 codes, so blend in linear at 16-bit or float and re-encode once → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E189
- `[songwriting][rhyme][testing]` Hyphenating every word in 42 builder stanzas left two-pass letters and the shape note unchanged (42/42) and raised counted syllables in 14% of lines, lowering none, so budget the cut not the hyphen → projects/pappydapimp69__suno-builder.md#E13
- `[gamedev][input][state]` release a held press on every tool switch; a press that survives the switch applies the new tool at the old position (27 strands cut short in one tick) → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E3
- `[audio][browser][gamedev]` a same-origin iframe opened by a user tap can start Web Audio at load (Chromium, gesture-required policy: running with no in-frame tap); try at load, keep tap-unlock as fallback → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E4
- `[gamedev][input][ux]` after a tap-driven stage change ignore presses for longer than reaction time (~0.5 s); no pause leaks 1 tap per change and a 0.15 s debounce leaks just as many → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E5
- `[algorithm][procgen][state]` a done() that is vacuously true for empty containers, reused as a deal rejection filter, rejected 0 of 2000 boards and hung the page; filter on non-empty and cap the loop → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E6
- `[gamedesign][wayfinding][audio]` out of earshot in woods a sky cue lifts finds 18 to 40 of 40 while seeking a clearing first adds only 4; drift kills the blind leg, so test strategy claims over many seeds → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E7
- `[gamedesign][dark-adaptation][survival]` saturating bleach makes glances dearest per warmth (0.57 vs 0.16 min/pt) but 5 s looks repeated before the debt clears kept sight under half all night (85 s blind) → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E8
- `[card-magic][permutation][exhaustive]` Elmsley's binary in/out rule is also the fewest faros to any position (exhaustive, n=8 to 54); any card returns to the top in at most 6 faros at 52 → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E9
- `[elevator][dispatch][starvation]` nearest-first starvation shows even at light load but only in the tail: longest wait 2.6x the sweep's while means differ 0.5 s; score dispatch by the longest wait → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E10
- `[rendering][canvas][performance]` Film-renderer drawing code reused in a live game redrew static paper and card-back patterns every frame (about 70 ms); caching them to offscreen canvases made the same run 2.7x faster → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E11
- `[gamedev][physics][simulation]` Particle water leaked through glass walls because the neighbour-spacing pass ran after wall collision; resolve walls last, choose the side from the previous position, and substep so travel stays under the contact radius → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E12
- `[gamedev][testing][puzzle]` A solver-certified TriPeaks deal stalled in the real game because the game drew stock from the end and the solver from the front; replay the solver's path through the live game to catch state-order drift → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E13
- `[drywall][estimating][packing][sandbox]` Drywall count is remainder packing: opening-deducted area under-ordered in 76 of 91 rooms (12x14: 8 vs 9 sheets) and flat 10% waste under-ordered 16 and over-ordered 54, so order from a cut list → projects/local__drywall-sheet-count-layout-vs-area-estimate.md#E1
- `[roofing][geometry][estimating][sandbox]` Plan area times sqrt(1+(p/12)^2) matches a 3D face sum exactly for gable and hip, so roofing misestimates come from the plan input (1 ft eaves = 10.7% short) and mixed pitch zones, not the formula → projects/local__roofing-pitch-multiplier-vs-surface-area-sum.md#E1
- `[sandbox][structure][comedy]` 15 s beats 30 s only above a ~1.2%/s viewer drop-off (flat in-app decay flips it to long cuts), and fixable-fail vs clean-win endings flip by network → projects/local__sandbox-ad-hook-length-ending-network-crossover.md#E1
- `[sandbox][performance][caching][tradeoff]` Size the cache to the loop (5 slots: 95 hits, 4: 0) and judge by cost under a memory ceiling, not hit rate; policy is irrelevant only on pure loops (FIFO faults 9 then 10 going 3 to 4 slots on the Belady trace) → projects/local__sandbox-cache-size-sweep-loop-cliff-and-ram-ceiling.md#E1
- `[sandbox][narrative][structure][game-design]` Withholding lag has a cliff: best lag is affordable open-question count minus 1 (7/3/2/1 across growth 1.3-3.0), one past scores 0, a recap or budget reset moves the wall → projects/local__sandbox-gap-lag-wall-from-exponential-hold-cost.md#E1
- `[sandbox][narrative][structure][motion]` A gap-closing caption at the broadcast title-safe bottom shows in 1 of 4 vertical-video viewports; only y 0.25-0.75 survives all four, leaving thin bands beside centred action (44 of 941 positions) → projects/local__sandbox-reveal-caption-safe-band-in-vertical-video.md#E1
- `[sandbox][songwriting][rhyme][suno]` To cure a rushed Suno line shorten from the front so the rhyme word survives (4/4 shapes kept, 11.1 to 5.0 words); truncating line ends kept 0/4 and hyphenation kept shape but not length → projects/local__sandbox-shorten-rushed-lines-keep-the-rhyme-word.md#E1
- `[sandbox][determinism][gamedev][state]` Float-free authoritative state plus saved sfc32 words still diverged across frame rates (shared cosmetic stream) while reload stayed exact; give render consumers their own stream and sweep frame rates → projects/local__sandbox-split-state-with-rng-state-words.md#E1
- `[pixel-art][art]` an outline only separates where it differs in value from the background: black 0% on night, light lit-side tint 50% on grass, none 26% on sky; squint per background → projects/local__pixel-outline-must-follow-background-value.md#E1
- `[shaders][cel-shading]` close-up cel edges need a continuous field thresholded after interpolation, not vertex storage: bilinear SDF texture 1.8 px vs vertex 2.0 px at 32x; painted 0/1 masks drift 16-21 px → projects/local__cel-shading.md#E1
- `[shaders][cel-shading]` cel inner lines as axis-aligned texture beams with rotated UVs stay straight at 24x (0.5 px) vs 2.8 px anti-aliased and 10.6 px hard-painted diagonals; UV scale sets width → projects/local__cel-shading.md#E2
- `[shaders][cel-shading]` cel characters need a body-frame key light: a scene point light altered 44.8% of the authored pattern on a walk, a matched sun 41.0% on a side switch → projects/local__cel-shading.md#E3
- `[shaders][cel-shading]` cel step moves the terminator by normal error over the N.L gradient: 1.5% noise flipped 7.8x more area and made 6 islands on a flat close-up; clean normals there first → projects/local__cel-shading.md#E4
- `[shaders][cel-shading]` cool shadows cost luminance: Gooch's blue end gave only 1.29-1.45 contrast against a black outline; check shade tones vs the line colour and keep the band off the cool extreme → projects/local__cel-shading.md#E5
- `[shaders][cel-shading]` inverted hull: hard normals crack corners (1.41w), averaged normals thin them (0.26w at 30 deg), 1/cos spikes them (3.9w); smoothed hull normals + clip-space width + clamped fix → projects/local__cel-shading.md#E6
- `[determinism][testing][gamedev]` give cosmetic randomness its own seeded stream: line picks on the gameplay stream changed 30/30 seeded fights; on their own stream 0/30 → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E14
- `[elevator][dispatch][state]` a dispatcher that never targets its own floor must board people there first, or a lobby rush where the lift stands delivers 0 of 40 → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E15
- `[rendering][canvas][art]` never dither small text: a 1-bit Bayer pass turned 7-12 px labels into unreadable dots after a 3x upscale; draw text in a crisp full-res layer in the same two inks → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E16
- `[shaders][cel-shading]` vertex-interpolated N.L and per-pixel normals give the identical cel terminator at threshold 0; a moved threshold drifts with vertex N.L (1.8x at 0.3, 5.1x at 0.6, 16 segs) → projects/local__cel-shading.md#E7
- `[shaders][cel-shading]` depth-only post outlines cannot span 2-50 m: an absolute threshold marks receding floors from ~15 m, depth-normalised loses 0.3 m steps from ~21-30 m; combine with normal edges → projects/local__cel-shading.md#E8
- `[animation][cel-shading]` held-pose (limited) animation needs refresh = n x 60 Hz: on twos 144 Hz alternates 4/5 refreshes (1.25x), 165 Hz 1.20x, 50 Hz 1/2 (2x); 60/120 Hz stay even → projects/local__cel-shading.md#E9
- `[shaders][cel-shading]` toon specular: under a step pow(N.H, s) is exactly a threshold; smoothstep(0.005,0.01) keeps the edge ~7% of radius at any glossiness, so expose size and edge width separately → projects/local__cel-shading.md#E10
- `[creative-arts][music][chiptune]` drop the fifth first because the root's 3rd harmonic already sounds it (2.8x the major third, sine or square); a minor third is the one chord tone nothing supplies → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E21
- `[creative-arts][songwriting][suno]` rejoin hyphenated words before lettering rhymes: rejoined kept 4/4 schemes, the raw token or first fragment kept 0/4 (13 rhyme words lost) → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E22
- `[creative-arts][painting][rendering]` shadow blur is 0 at contact and 0.93 cm per metre under the sun, but under a near light it grows faster than linear (2 m object, lamp at 3 m: 3x the straight-line estimate) → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E23
- `[creative-arts][songwriting][rhyme]` shortening rushed lines from the front keeps two-pass letters exactly, case included (4/4), end-trimming keeps 0/4, and one-pass still misreads XXAA → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E24
- `[audio][dsp][sandbox]` Karplus-Strong pitch is fs/(N+.5) or fs/(N-.5) depending on which neighbour the loop averages; measure the sign, and integer N detunes past 10 cents above ~509 Hz (1760 Hz: -31c) so add a fractional delay → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E17
- `[film][editing][sandbox]` Score a trailer cut list vs measured pacing (ref 1.09 s ASL, thirds .96/.79/.54) before eyeballing: Stonewake ran 2.0 s ASL, flat 1.4/1.4 into the climax, a 3.6 s hold at 61%, hook at 3.6 s → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E18
- `[rendering][canvas][sandbox]` 2D parallax: drift = Zfocus/Z, push-in scale = Z/(Z-d) per layer; a canvas zoom has zero parallax (all 1.43 vs fg 2.50/titan 1.005) and a giant 60 units back moves at 0.017, not ~.2 → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E19
- `[gamedev][mobile][sandbox]` Portrait pixel games: tap targets need >= 44*canvas_w/390 art px (22 at 180 wide, integer x6 = 2 pt/px); 12 px tiles are 24 pt and 16 px are 32 pt, so enlarge hit areas or snap taps → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E20
- `[audio][dsp][sandbox]` Comb reverb: solve feedback per comb as g=10^(-3D/(fs*T)); one shared g spreads tails x1.45 across Freeverb lengths and the summed decay (1.31 s) matches neither mean nor longest → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E21
- `[audio][dsp][sandbox]` Virtual bass needs an asymmetric (DC-biased) saturator: symmetric x-x^3/3 makes odd harmonics only (H2/H4 -238 dB), so the octave that implies a phone-missing sub never appears; +0.3 bias gives H2 -15 dB → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E22
- `[rendering][canvas][sandbox]` Tilt-shift: blur K*|1/zf-1/z| is exactly linear in screen y on a ground plane (resid 4e-16) but a y-band blurs an upright titan's head 2.8 px vs true 1.08; blur upright sprites as a whole at their foot depth → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E23
- `[creative-arts][cel-shading][color]` shade cel shadows cooler but hold their luminance: a 20-deg blue shift at equal HSV value cut outline contrast 18% (5/6 colours); a luminance floor restores it → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E25
- `[creative-arts][cel-shading][canvas]` a canvas offset-copy shadow is a stylisation (IoU 0.79-0.84 frontal to side light); a half-ellipse with minor axis cos(light angle) is the exact cel terminator → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E26
- `[game-ads][narrative]` cut fixable-fail ads by beats, not clock: truncation lost the fail ending in 4/4 cut-downs; anchoring fail + end card kept it down to 10 s → projects/local__fail-ad-cutdowns-keep-the-gap.md#E1
- `[game-ads][captions]` a sound-off hook's proposition must read in 3 s: 8-9 words at BBC 160-180 wpm, ~10 at Netflix 20 cps, 5 under this film engine; the kernel's 5-10 words/s is 3x too fast → projects/local__hook-caption-budget.md#E1
- `[game-ads][narrative]` '15 s beats 30 s' needs the 15 s cut to keep its fixable-fail ending: truncation moves the crossover from 1.2% to 4.6%/s drop-off, so the master wins at 1-4%/s → projects/local__ad-length-crossover-depends-on-the-cut.md#E1
- `[load-balancing][queueing]` with draining queues stale least-loaded herds (89 vs 27 peak) but two choices on a 100-job-old report lost to a blind hash (27 vs 22.6); drop stale reports → projects/local__stale-load-two-choices-vs-hash.md#E1
- `[comedy][humor]` escalate only as far as the framing's distance allows: with a fixed grounded distance the go-big schedule scored 0.00 and a mild one won; matched distance restores going big → projects/local__escalation-needs-matching-distance.md#E1
- `[game-ads][comedy]` fact-check a kernel's labels as well as its numbers: the fake-ad kernel matched 6/6 numbers but renamed 1/5 study patterns ('borrowed fame' for 'popular externalization') → projects/local__fake-ad-kernel-source-check.md#E1
- `[humor][comedy][structure][simulation]` A callback survives a short clip only if its antecedent is inside it: in 60 s clips a 30 s gap landed 51% and 60 s+ gaps about 0%, so for clip-bound sets keep callback gaps under the clip length, space a runner under it, or add a 5 s premise reminder (0.087 to 0.918) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E1
- `[audio][realtime][determinism]` A drain cap must not defer a generator-state restore: behind a 2048-message burst at cap 64 the LFSR restore landed 32 callbacks late and left 49.8% of noise bits wrong for good with peak still 1.000; apply restores ahead of the capped drain → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E2
- `[localization][typography][rendering]` A 1.3x character budget is not a 1.3x width budget: on 25 German UI labels it gave 3 false passes and 1 false reject (7 false passes as an integer max-length), and '!'-padded pseudo-loc grew long strings only 17-20% px; give translators a measured pixel limit → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E3
- `[ideation][sampling][testing]` An idea slot machine that picks columns in order among still-allowed options over-serves ideas through heavily banned options (max/min 16x, 59% of draws on 21% of ideas); a regular ban list stays uniform, so sample whole valid combos and test on your real ban list → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E4
- `[audio][speech][dsp][voice]` Key a de-esser on HF share, not HF level: a level threshold set on one take caught 76% of esses at -12 dB, 0% at -18 dB and fired on every breathy frame up close, the share key held 100% at all distances but fires on 50% of whispered frames → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E5
- `[comedy][structure][simulation]` A fake-ad parody stays readable while 2+ trope-unique features stay on screen (2 left 45/54, 1 left 15/51, 0 left 0/6); one swap of any slot never broke it (35/35) and kit items carry no identity → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E6
- `[songwriting][rhyme][suno-prompting]` A front-cut that keeps only the last word broke 4 of 5 mosaic-rhyme stanzas (AABB read xxAA, ABAB xAxA, ABBA AxxA) while keeping the last two words held 5 of 5; keep from the rhyme's stressed vowel to the end, not just the rhyme word → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E7
- `[storytelling][comedy][structure][ai-video]` Genre-plus-one-swap needs the swap to spare the recognition cues: subject swaps kept vlog, doc and talk show readable by 1 s (5.9-6.5 s left of 8), but a pharma ad's product is a cue (1.5 to 4.0 s, 2.5 s left); give ad parodies 15 s → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E8
- `[pixel-art][art][cel-shading]` A 32 px deep-shadow step, not the hue shift, drowns a selective black outline: 44.8% of shadow-side pairs under contrast 2.0 on 4/5 hues for same-hue and shifted ramps alike, 0% at 16 px; floor the deepest tone at 2.0 vs the outline after shifting → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E9
- `[cel-shading][visibility][rendering]` Without a depth buffer an inverted-hull outline dies before its body: big backdrop triangles under a centroid sort erased 31-68% of the outline ring at 3/25 positions while the body lost 1 px; two disjoint spheres sorted exactly; sort per object, hull then body → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E10
- `[check-digits][error-detection]` weighted mod-11 misses every twin error where adjacent weights sum to 11 (ISBN-10 weights 6,5: 90/90 missed); Luhn misses 22/55, 33/66, 44/77 everywhere → projects/local__isbn10-blind-to-twins-at-weights-6-5.md#E1
- `[vfx][canvas][rendering][color]` Lighter plus an alpha-0.2 black trail clear settles a held neon stroke at 5x its colour: orange (255,128,26) clipped to (255,255,121), hue 27 to 60 deg by frame 2, and 8-bit fades leave a (2,2,2) ghost; keep trails with lighten (max), add only this frame with lighter → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E11
- `[songwriting][rhyme][suno-prompting]` After splitting rushed lines for Suno, score rhyme openness on source lines: raw closure distance doubled and turned 2/2 ABAB stanzas open (max 4), halving fixed full splits but not partial ones (ABBA read stable), source-line end words gave 0/8 wrong → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E12
- `[3d-modeling][materials][rendering]` Mipping a metal|dielectric footprint with linear baseColor still erred 18.5% (gold leaked into diffuse, red 0.251 vs 0.002) because base and metalness multiply after the average; average derived diffuse albedo and F0 instead (exact), and handle roughness apart (95% off at the peak) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E13
- `[ideation][creativity][evaluation]` Do not rank originality by a count of rare ideas: it tracked fluency r +0.82 and originality only +0.36 (top-2 rarest +0.44/+0.19) while mean rarity per idea gave +0.14/+0.57; average rarity, report fluency separately, score against 200+ → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E14
- `[film][animation][audio][determinism]` In a render(t) film a repeated line is a verbatim callback only if its start is on the frame grid (10 ms off changed 34/38 frames); snap table times to frames, keep render(t) stateless, mux at the exact rate (23.976 drifts 45 ms by 45 s) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E15
- `[rendering][dither][narrative][viewport]` Meeting a 90% safe zone by scaling a pixel frame to 5.4 broke a world-anchored Bayer reveal's equal beats (1.21x, tone shimmer 0.012 on scroll; phone fit 4.00x) though swim stayed 0; floor to an integer scale inside the safe box (1.00x, 0 shimmer) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E16
- `[storytelling][prose][testing][measurement]` Sentence-length spread is not rhythm variety: CV ranked a mechanical short/long alternation above a varied paragraph (0.74 vs 0.63), and autocorrelation flagged 4/4 paragraphs; test binned periodicity over 16+ sentences (random 8-sentence paragraphs false-flag 22-36%) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E17
- `[songwriting][rhyme][suno-prompting]` Splitting a rushed line at its midpoint halves words/line (11.2 to 5.6) and keeps resolved letters, but the exposed mid-line word took assonance letters in 3/6 stanzas, turned XXAA into xaAA, and an internal rhyme became a new uppercase stop; re-letter after splitting → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E18
- `[cinematography][lighting][rendering]` An sRGB-lerped soft shadow edge is harmless in high-key (0 at 1:1, 3% at 2:1) but at 8:1 pulls the edge 0.133 W toward the light and widens the dark part of the edge by 0.14 W, with lit and shadow patches exact; check the edge, not the patches → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E19
- `[art][pixel-art][rendering][testing]` Run a style's rules as framebuffer checks: each Game Boy pipeline-order bug tripped its own rule (bilinear 51 shades, screen-res dither 1080 bad blocks, x4.5 widths 4/5), but screen-anchored Bayer changed 50.9% on a 1 px pan and 0% on a 4 px pan, so pan by 1 px → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E20
- `[algorithm][scheduling]` earliest-finish greedy is only safe for unweighted jobs: with weights it was optimal on 38.5% (best greedy, heaviest-first, 85.7%); use the weighted-interval DP → projects/local__earliest-finish-fails-weighted-jobs.md#E1
- `[numeral-systems][parsing]` re-encode-and-compare rejects all attested Roman variants (9/9); a lenient parse reads 6/9 right but silently misreads stacked subtraction (IIX as 10); flag stacked forms → projects/local__roman-variants-canonical-vs-lenient.md#E1
- `[structures][physics]` 'every joint has two non-parallel members' does not confirm a truss: two braced squares on parallel links pass it and Maxwell's count yet sway (rank 15/16); use matrix rank → projects/local__truss-local-check-not-sufficient.md#E1
- `[latin-squares][puzzles]` 4x4 Sudoku 4-clue uniqueness depends on the grid class: 96 grids have 12 unique sets, 192 have 128 (0.7% vs 7.0%); the two-distinct-digit rule holds on all 288 → projects/local__sudoku4-uniqueness-depends-on-grid-class.md#E1
- `[stable-matching][game-theory]` under deferred acceptance a receiver can gain exactly when several stable matchings exist (40.7% of 4x4 instances), mainly by truncation (reordering only 4.7%); proposers never gain → projects/local__receiver-manipulation-needs-truncation.md#E1
- `[chess-clocks][timekeeping]` simple and Bronstein delay end every move with the same time, but with R left a move may last R+d under simple delay and only R under Bronstein; it decided 1% of time-trouble games → projects/local__bronstein-flags-earlier-than-simple-delay.md#E1
- `[rotor-machines][cryptography]` Enigma's no-self-map property survives the plugboard and leaks crib position: offsets ruled out = 1-(25/26)^L (33% at 10 letters, 55% at 20, 73% at 33) → projects/local__enigma-no-self-map-survives-plugboard.md#E1
- `[rendering][canvas][tooling]` Canvas films rendered over file:// and read with toDataURL must embed images as data URIs: any file:// image taints the canvas and only the export throws (SecurityError) → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E24
- `[film][editing][design]` Ads mixing cinematic and real gameplay: group each look into a block and switch only at block edges via a match cut; Stonewake v2 switched look 9 times in 26 s and read as jumping around → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E25
- `[creative-arts][game-ads][comedy]` four shipped spoof ads carried 8-9 of 11 kit elements but none had the 'not actual gameplay' disclaimer, and all used one swap axis of five; put it in the kit, rotate axes → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E26
- `[creative-arts][music][voice-leading]` parallel fifths stay fused every step running (8/8, 22% shared partials); contrary motion fuses as much on average but never past one step, so judge independence by fused runs → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E27
- `[film][editing][design]` Ads end on the held call to action, not a film-style story button after the title; in a looping tile fade the end into the opening (Stonewake v3 ended on gameplay and read as unfinished) → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E27
- `[songwriting][rhyme][tension][testing]` The open cluster-coda question (ask/back) flips the rhyme-shape verdict in 16/26 four-line cases: open-coded it makes a first-closure pair read OPEN but ABBA's outer pair read STABLE, so run shape both ways and name the pair → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E21
- `[songwriting][rhyme][suno-prompting][testing]` Un-stretch sung elongations (lo-o-ove, seeee) before a phonetic rhyme judge by trying letter runs at 1 and 2 and flag two-reading words (goood); hyphen-strip only lost 3/3 elongated stanzas, collapse-to-one 6/9 wrong → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E22
- `[3d-modeling][texture][rendering]` face-normal extrusion cannot bake a hard corner: it doubled 12.5-37.6 deg or missed 5.27 deg per corner at any reach, a same-order cage left 0.45 deg worst gap, and rotating the cage's vertex list by one baked 100% of texels wrong → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E23
- `[narrative][research]` a strange detail survives millennia of retelling only with independent errors: majority-of-5 at 3% loss lasts 67158 yr vs 569 for one chain, but 0.5%/gen shared rationalisation caps any group near 3455 yr → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E24
- `[humor][comedy][timing][simulation]` Hold for a laugh by its level, not a clock: a fixed 1.0 s pause left a big laugh's next line 0.43 audible, resuming 10 dB under the voice kept 0.97+ and beat silence by up to 1.4 s; a second swell cut it to 0.62 without a still-falling check → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E25
- `[ideation][creativity][simulation]` For rarer group ideas, cross-strike any idea two members listed instead of only dropping first ideas: late ideas gained 0.64 bits, striking 1.78; both fall to 0.20 or less when associations are idiosyncratic or the pool is flat (model) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E26
- `[photography][composition][perception][simulation]` In a hand-written saliency gaze model a leading line sped finding a strong subject only when far dimmer than it (TTFF +19% at line 0.2, -3% at 0.4, -35% at 0.6) and helped a faint subject more (+14 vs -3), reversing the kernel's prominent-subject qualifier: rank the line's contrast below the subject → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E27
- `[writing][mystery][narrative][testing]` Find a mystery's real deduction point by counting suspects left per chapter, alibis included: trait clues plus alibis solved a plot at ch 7 though its pointing clue came at ch 10, a shadow suspect held it to 10 (+51% curiosity), and gate fair play before curiosity → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E28
- `[comedy][structure][simulation]` Copy the ad exactly only if the turn lands just after recognition: a perfect-copy parody beat a loose one 2.69x at a 3 s turn, gained nothing at 1 s, and under heavy ad-skipping lost from 5.6 s → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E29
- `[rendering][dither][viewport][mobile]` Floor a pixel-art scale in device px, not CSS px: a CSS-integer fit in the safe area became 3.75 at 125% (dither beats 1.78x apart) and 2.625 on a phone, while device-integer stayed exact on 10 devices even at a fractional inset origin → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E30
- `[animation][state][determinism][testing]` If animation lives outside undo history, a replay seek must rebuild it from the raw command log incl. undone commands: undo-stack rebuild matched playback 72.2% of frames, log 100%, carry 15.1%; float ticks broke even the log (91%) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E31
- `[speech][voice][acoustics]` slow down for reverb, speak up for noise, and only far from the talker: RT60 1.5 s, 4->2 syl/s lifts envelope depth 0.355->0.596 at D/R -10 dB but +0.049 at +6 dB and 0 against 0 dB noise; +10 dB level does nothing to reverb → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E32
- `[game-ads][comedy]` pick a spoof ad's joke on two dials (the ad's lie x how the game answers it) and change both each ad: the disc used 10/35 cells, rotation gave 8 new with 0 repeats vs 8/8 for copy-the-hit; 1 answer type leaves no legal ad → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E28
- `[pixel-art][art][cel-shading]` the squint test sees only luminance: iso-luminant hue shift splits identical-silhouette poses by 0, a hue-shifted value step by 0.059-0.167, beating a 2 px silhouette change (0.005-0.050) on 5/5 colours; give inner poses a value step → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E33
- `[voice][automation][browser][testing]` Tapping a TTS page's stream survives a markup redesign (scraper 0 vs tap 100%), but a renamed message type gave 0% with a normal stream end and a 600 ms pause cut a take to 50%: reject unknown types and set the silence cutoff above the longest pause → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E34
- `[comedy][humor][structure][simulation]` Surreal distance is not a free safe cue: when it mutes the harm, go-big under matched distance lost to mild from kappa 0.8, and a competing-norm cue (victim respawns) restored it 2.35 vs 1.13 → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E35
- `[testing][state][attribution]` A test hook routed through the real transition but sweeping deferred effects once passed a full-view rebuild oracle while player credit fell 3 to 2; keep production sweep cadence and check credit against an arm-time intent ledger → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E36
- `[cinematography][lighting][rendering]` A sub-pixel rim light keeps its on-set ratio through a downscale only in linear light: a 1-px 16:1 rim averaged in sRGB kept 49% of its separation (2.83 vs 4.75); exposure changed nothing until clip or the sRGB toe → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E37
- `[game-ads][dither][narrative][viewport]` A 9:16 fail-ad cut-down must keep the goal in the crop (a centre crop lost the gap in 0/10 cuts, beat-anchored too), and the follow-pan that fixes it makes Bayer swim: anchor per parallax layer (world-anchored 0.5x layer 0.419 swim, per-layer 0) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E38
- `[dsp][audio][realtime][testing]` Gate audio on per-callback time vs one period, not the device xrun count: one trace with 26 overruns read 26, 8 and 0 xruns at 1, 2 and 3 periods of slack, and a 1-in-100 sampler saw max 990 us → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E39
- `[game-ads][comedy][sandbox]` a fixed fake-ad kit keeps a batch 0.625 identical even with new scripts (name-swap 0.94); rotate 3+ kit assets per video to get under 0.5 → projects/local__fake-ad-kit-template-similarity.md#E1
- `[comedy][game-ads][sandbox]` engine-first keeps a 10-idea batch at 2 per engine (vs 3+ in 76% topic-first), but no batch over 8 avoids a repeat, so cap at ceil(k/8) not zero → projects/local__engine-first-idea-batches.md#E1
- `[game-ads][comedy][sandbox]` do not add kit rotation for the finite trope grid: it adds only about 0.02 pair similarity (30 videos, 3 rotated 0.460 vs fresh 0.442), kit count sets the templated look → projects/local__kit-rotation-with-finite-grid.md#E1
- `[performance][caching][memory][sandbox]` GC cost keeps the loop-sized cache binary: size 5 wins until pause per slot passes savings/(collections x slots) (985 vs 39.4 by GC frequency), partial caches never win → projects/local__gc-cost-vs-loop-sized-cache.md#E1
- `[comedy][humor][sandbox]` escalation room comes from any safe cue, not only distance: a 0.7 competing-norm cue turned flat-at-ceiling from 0.02 to 51.12 at grounded distance, but a safe cue with no violation scored 0.00 → projects/local__safe-cue-escalation.md#E1
- `[game-ads][comedy][sandbox]` the fake-ad slot machine's 30 cells run out fast: 10 random videos repeat a cell 82% of the time and any 7 repeat a trope, so rotate without replacement and add a third axis → projects/local__slot-machine-cell-budget.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][economy][sandbox]` deckbuilder: cutting a below-mean card beat adding any card under value 8 (deck mean 6.0; +0.39/turn, key-card odds 1/2 to 5/9); an added 6 lost value; thinning only stops when the hand covers the deck → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E29
- `[game-ads][comedy]` sight-gag audit of 2 spoof ads: 0/24 in-ad beats fit a Carroll type and 6/7 payoffs ride a caption, but 14/15 ad-to-game cuts are switch images; in a caption-format parody the gag lives at the boundary → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E30
- `[gamedesign][probability][sandbox]` pick-1-of-3 level-ups: a target is seen in 20 level-ups 0.88 at pool 30 but 0.64 at 60 (two-piece synergy 0.77 to 0.41); keep pool <= 3L/ln10 for 90%; rarity 1/4 drops it to 0.41 → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E31
- `[gamedev][balance][testing]` Tune RPG difficulty with human-timing player profiles and min HP during the fight: a perfect autopilot and post-win HP (level-up refills) hid that FIGHT-only play won everything; counters should expose bosses, not multiply damage → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E32
- `[gamedev][balance][sandbox]` A boss acting twice is a constant factor, not party scaling: leftover HP N=2->4 still rises +0.70 (single-target +0.75); AoE halves the slope (+0.40) but does not flatten it → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E33
- `[gamedev][balance][sandbox]` Timed-hit grades follow a Gaussian tap-error model (matched a real-game playtest within |z|<2); a 1.6/1.25/1.0 ladder makes skill worth x1.13 damage (x1.24 with combo), 2.0/1.2/0.6 gives x1.31 → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E34
- `[experiment][balance][testing]` before a human A/B on performance, sweep an autopilot's skill and confirm the measure is off its ceiling: Pop It popped 100% at every reaction 0.3-0.8 s until speeds rose (then 98/82/61%) → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E35
- `[experiment][memorization][testing]` an equal-budget arm comparison is only equal where the cap binds: a 16-review cap vs peak load 9 bound 0 days; simulate load and log overflow per session (6-cap bound days 3-5) → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E36
- `[experiment][gamedesign][determinism]` a juice A/B is clean only if same seed + same timed taps score identically on and off (4190/93 both, hit-stop 279 vs 0 ticks); hit-stop still adds human wall time, so log it → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E37
- `[legal][copyright][model]` AI-output copyright is a per-element, per-country claim (UK protects computer-generated works; whole-work rules misstated 159/161 registrable mixes) and training fair use is separate, now split 2-1 in district courts on whole-work copying; not legal advice → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E1
- `[sailing][physics]` Trim to apparent wind, not true wind: a 12 kn beam reach at 6 kn reads 63 degrees, a puff swings it aft (69) and the acceleration that follows brings it forward (65) at every angle from 30 to 175, and only on a dead run does a puff leave the angle alone → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E2
- `[avalanche-safety][search][physics][sandbox]` The lowest transceiver reading is a zone, not a point: a flat 2 m burial shows its minimum over +/-0.91 m, and a 45-degree tilt moves the field peak 0.32 m and the bracket midpoint 0.39 m off (about 0.2 x depth); bracket, then probe-spiral a radius of a quarter of the depth plus one spacing → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E3
- `[bookbinding][paper][folding]` grain must run along the last fold, which flips axis each fold: folio/octavo/32mo need short grain, quarto/16mo long, so long-grain A4 folded once is cross-grain; fails on 2:1+ sheets → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E4
- `[ornithology][migration][navigation]` A clock-fixed sun-compass route tracks a great circle by departure hour, not latitude: arctic daylight misses ran 23-1289 km across hours and 8-341 km even under 80N midnight sun; sweep departure hour before claiming the shortcut → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E5
- `[drywall][estimating][packing][kerf]` a kerf that changes under 1% of random cut lists changed 26 of 91 whole-foot drywall rooms (+40 sheets) since round sizes fill sheets exactly; charge k-1 kerfs when saw-cutting → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E6
- `[game-ads][narrative][video][sandbox]` A 15 s cut-down that ends on the fixable-fail payoff can still lose its gap: 167 of 488 such cuts dropped the setup question and sit at the truncated crossover (0.0455/s, not 0.0122), losing to the master at 1-4%/s; check the gap-opening beat survives and trim escalation first → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E7
- `[determinism][gamedev][state][testing]` A cosmetic sin() branch that gates a draw forks a shared seeded stream across runtimes: on 30-degree steps two sin()s split 0.5 on 173/3600 ticks and forked 8/8 seeds (generic angles 0/28800); give effects their own stream, branch on integer angles → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E8
- `[comedy][narrative][process][testing]` A gag rule card lifted benign-violation precision 0.300 to 1.000 with 32 setups, but only never-wins and humiliated-not-harmed filter; who-can-hurt-whom added 0 once R5 held and cut funny beats 160 to 96, no want gave 0/96 funny, and gravity-only with 3 tools in 2 places starved to 6 setups → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E9
- `[comedy][humor][narrative]` A safe cue that lands a beat late costs only the severity jumps: flat-at-ceiling lost just its unframed opener (5.11 to 4.11, 0 with the frame set first) while escalating schedules lost 32-38 percent, so frame first and escalate in small steps → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E10
- `[gamedev][web][ads]` Check a playable's size on the built base64 HTML, not raw assets: only 0.747 of a 5 MB cap (3.73 MB) is left for assets, and a 3.9 MB bundle became 5.22 MB, failing 5e6 but passing 5 MiB, so budget to decimal MB → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E11
- `[determinism][testing][state]` Leave presentation floats out of a replay fingerprint rather than rounding them (whole-unit rounding still mismatched 36/40 seeds across 30-144 fps) and run it at two frame rates: run-twice at 60 fps missed a frame-count leak caught 40/40 → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E12
- `[roofing][origami][geometry]` Pitch multiplier keeps roof area exact but not angles: 30x40 hip apex sectors fall 6.03 deg short of 360 at 4/12 and 38.94 at 12/12, a valley junction overshoots by the same, only gable ridges unfold flat; lay out flat patterns from 3D angles → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E13
- `[tiktok][youtube][search][captions]` a searchable-phrase card loses to fixed budgets: a footer above it takes YouTube's 3 shown hashtags, tag 61 hides all, and a separate 4-word phrase left 1-3 of 6 hooks readable in 3 s; write it into the hook → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E14
- `[game-ads][comedy][sandbox]` a one-trope fake-ad series cannot swap its way out of a templated look (1 swap 0.767, 2 swaps 0.691, +3 rotated kit 0.534); rotate 4+ kit assets (0.482) or vary the trope → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E15
- `[songwriting][rhyme][testing]` Stray assonance also opens stanzas: 1728/104976 toy stanzas flip stable to open, all XAAX read as wistful ABBA via assonant outer lines, and case-blind two-pass is worse (2016); read shape from uppercase only → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E16
- `[songwriting][suno-prompting][testing][tooling]` Subject-aux ellipsis frees only 1-2 counted syllables, so on 19 over-budget suno-builder lines it cleared 2 (hyphenation 0, raised 3); offer it only when a line is 1-2 over, guard against a following determiner or pronoun (the naive linter mangled 4/4 copular lines), and cut a clause otherwise → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E17
- `[performance][caching][memory]` Under generational GC a looping cache one slot short costs more than hits: 59 slots on a 60-loop promoted 5980 entries and forced 33 majors (19,188 work against 120), while a 4-slot thrash cost 0 because entries died young, so entry lifetime sets the GC bill → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E18
- `[volcanology][measurement][classification]` Compute VEI from bulk tephra volume, never DRE: DRE (2-5x smaller) dropped St. Helens, Pinatubo and Tambora a class and shifts 40% of eruptions at f=2.5; give a range when bulk straddles a decade (Pinatubo VEI 5-6) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E19
- `[negotiation][model][sandbox]` A written recap only blocks walk-backs whose gain sits between the verbal and written cost: reneging cut 82% for small outside offers, 10% for large ones, and a recap missing 1 of 3 terms drew 73% of retreats onto it → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E20
- `[comedy][game-ads][testing][sandbox]` An engine repeat above 8 ideas is only safe with a new beat skeleton: same skeleton scored 1.00 similarity, a one-beat variant 0.60 (at the flag line), a new skeleton 0.07, and two skeletons per engine still top out at 16 ideas → projects/local__engine-repeat-above-eight-needs-new-skeleton-not-new-topic.md#E1
- `[comedy][narrative][testing][sandbox]` Game-of-the-scene X check fails if it means any new noun: naive column mislabelled 2 derived beats and passed flat, jump and stock-premise sheets (4/6 exact vs 6/6 with a derived-from-game link plus F, J and stock checks) → projects/local__game-of-scene-hxe-check-column.md#E1
- `[game-ads][legal][testing][sandbox]` Invent-every-game-name needs more than exact match: it caught 0 of 8 lookalikes, edit distance 7, character checks 0 of 12 compound lookalikes, and plain word overlap flagged 12 of 12 clean generic-word names, so screen with edit plus distinctive words and review hits (not legal clearance) → projects/local__invented-name-check-exact-match-misses-lookalikes.md#E1
- `[game-ads][testing][model][sandbox]` Ranking ad variants on IPM times D1 only changes the pick when retention opposes conversion (+18% retained installs in a toy model, nothing with flat D1) and the winner flipped with sample size (1 of 3 at 300 and 100 impressions, 0 at 1000), so confirm at two budgets → projects/local__rank-ipm-times-d1-needs-fidelity-retention-link.md#E1
- `[game-ads][comedy][ideation][sandbox]` Fake-ad slot machine over the 20-trope catalog: only 72 of 100 cells are non-native swaps and a distinct-axis batch caps at 5; a paraphrased axis label silently added 3 redundant cells, so reject axis strings not verbatim from the source → projects/local__trope-catalog-native-axis-redundant-swap.md#E1
- `[comedy][game-ads][testing][sandbox]` Distinct comic-engine labels do not make an ad batch non-templated: 6 distinct labels over identical content scored similarity 1.00 and a shared endpoint alone only 0.17, so compare content fields per pair, not labels or one aggregate → projects/local__vary-engine-not-label-template-similarity.md#E1
- `[heraldry][color][accessibility][sandbox]` The rule of tincture is a lightness-class convention, not a contrast threshold: all 10 metal-on-colour pairs reached 3:1 yet forbidden sable on vert hit 5.11 and or on vert fell to 2.64 under protan, so measure rendered pairs under colour-vision simulation → projects/local__heraldic-rule-of-tincture-is-a-lightness-class-not-a-contrast-threshold.md#E1
- `[bicycle][gearing][arithmetic][sandbox]` count a double drivetrain's gears as distinct usable ratios: 24 nominal became 17 after merging 3% steps and avoiding cross-chaining → projects/local__bike-drivetrain-usable-gears.md#E1
- `[comedy][game-ads][testing][sandbox]` a fake-ad batch is capped at min(16 engine-skeleton slots, 6 x per-trope cap): 6, 12, 16 at trope caps 1, 2, 3, and engine-axis limits did not move it → projects/local__batch-ceiling-trope-cap.md#E1
- `[comedy][humor][narrative][sandbox]` a held pause before the peak beat adds nothing when the framing distance does not fit it: gain 0.00 under fixed grounded distance vs +0.26 matched, so set distance first → projects/local__held-pause-needs-matching-distance.md#E1
- `[game-ads][legal][comedy][sandbox]` scrub real game names out of the trope catalog and invent names from syllables: 15/20 entries named a real game and generic-word names matched real titles 4/60 vs 0/60 → projects/local__invent-every-name-catalog-leak.md#E1
- `[breadmaking][arithmetic][recipes][sandbox]` count the starter's flour and water in dough hydration: 70.0% on added ingredients was 72.7% true (100 g at 100%) and 67.6% with a stiff 50% starter → projects/local__starter-hydration-true-percentage.md#E1
- `[garden][pest-control][taxonomy][sandbox]` grade a companion-planting claim by plant, target and setting: marigold got 3 grades over 3 claims and a per-plant grade misgraded 2 of 6 → projects/local__companion-planting-claim-keyed-grading.md#E1
- `[game-ads][comedy][verification][sandbox]` filter banned tropes and check sourcing before drawing from the 20-trope catalog: 7/20 trace to a cited source, 2/20 are banned, so 8 uniform picks hit a banned one 65% of the time → projects/local__trope-catalog-traceability-and-banned-picks.md#E1
- `[garden][soil][compost][sandbox]` weedy material needs a hot pile that is turned at all: 3/3 seed batches die turned, 2/3 unturned, 0/3 in a cold pile or mulch layer (turn interval 5-14 days made no difference) → projects/local__weedy-material-needs-a-turned-hot-pile.md#E1
- `[comedy][game-ads][testing][sandbox]` A same-trope repeat with a different axis scored mean 0.43 (1 of 10 pairs at the 0.6 flag), not the 0.60 one-beat-variant figure, so score pairs of cells instead of cell versus base; the 7th rotated idea peaked at 0.455 → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E21
- `[tiling][algorithm][testing][sandbox]` Compare the two checkerboard colour counts before searching for a domino tiling: equal counts tiled in 388 of 388 pairs on 4x4 and 6x6, unequal in 0 of 362, and an odd board minus one square tiled only on the majority colour → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E22
- `[comedy][game-ads][testing][sandbox]` The engine-repeat cap check needs a closed list of verbatim engine names: with free-text labels a 16-idea batch that truly repeats an engine 3 times passed the counter 48% to 90% of runs at 20% to 60% paraphrase, and the closed list costs relabelling → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E23
- `[comedy][game-ads][testing][sandbox]` The 6R fake-ad batch ceiling needs engines that reach 2+ tropes: tied to one trope each, cap 3 gives 14 not 16 (slots per trope 4,4,2,2,2,2), so count slots per trope before raising the cap → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E24
- `[game-ads][comedy][legal][sandbox]` Passing the label and not-templated gates does not clear names: syllable-pool 'invented' names hit a lookalike in 1 of 8 (edit 3 from a real title), so screen every generated name and redraw hits, which leaves batch similarity unchanged (0.143) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E25
- `[magic-squares][algorithm][testing][sandbox]` Choose the magic-square construction by n mod 4 and validate the output: Siamese worked for 8 of 8 odd sizes but 0 of 6 even, and Strachey for n = 2 mod 4 failed 3 of 3 sizes without its swap step → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E26
- `[comedy][humor][timing][sandbox]` Do not credit one peak with beating stacked beats until the stack gets the same pause: single peak won 3 of 3 vs an unpaused stack but a stack paused before every beat scored 1.364 vs 0.295 total at d 0.3 and only tied on peak → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E27
- `[garden][pest-control][taxonomy][sandbox]` audit a coarse grading key by its off-majority share: all 4 plants disagreed with their own majority on 33-50% of claims (5 of 12 misread), and a repeated key with a conflicting grade is rejected, not overwritten → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E310
- `[comedy][game-ads][testing][sandbox]` engine rotation, trope cap and kernel-label audit act on different things in a fake-ad batch: rotation cut same-engine runs 2 to 1, cap set size 6/12/16, audit caught the 1 of 5 renamed labels, no interference → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E40
- `[garden][soil][compost][sandbox]` a hot-pile kill clock needs completed ticks, not just integer ones: over 32 step and spell cases float time was wrong 6, rounded ticks 4 (killed at 2.9 days), completed-tick counting 0 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E311
- `[game-ads][legal][testing][sandbox]` three separate gates for a fake-ad batch: name-swapped template scripts scored 1.00 similarity vs 0.35 for own jokes, exact name match caught 0 of 6 simple lookalikes vs 6 for edit similarity, and the audit caught 1 of 5 renamed labels → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-creative-training.md#E41
- `[game-ads][comedy][statistics][sandbox]` A 16-variant ad batch picked the true best 35% of the time at 300 impressions each vs 54% for 6 variants, and needed 3000 each to match → projects/local__batch-size-vs-impressions-pick-accuracy.md#E1
- `[bowling][scoring][testing][sandbox]` Score bowling by a ten-frame counter, not the roll list: a roll-loop scorer gave 330 for a perfect game and failed 5 of 7 known totals → projects/local__bowling-tenth-frame-scoring.md#E1
- `[comedy][game-ads][testing][sandbox]` In an engine-first batch strip real-title words from each engine's theme vocabulary before naming: word-overlap flagged 90% of themed names vs 0% of syllable or stripped ones → projects/local__engine-vocabulary-name-overlap.md#E1
- `[comedy][game-ads][testing][sandbox]` Greedy engine-first fill reaches the min(16, 6R) batch ceiling at caps 1-2, but at cap 3 with 2-trope engines makes 15 of 16 (1-trope engines 14), so match there → projects/local__greedy-fill-vs-flow-ceiling.md#E1
- `[comedy][game-ads][testing][sandbox]` Screen invented ad names against each other in the batch: 30 three-syllable names from 12 syllables had an exact duplicate in 24% of batches but a one-syllable-apart pair in 100% → projects/local__batch-internal-name-lookalikes.md#E1
- `[comedy][game-ads][testing][sandbox]` Stacked redraw gates multiplied in a 16-video ad batch: label 1.42 x name 1.02 gave 1.45 draws per video (observed 1.45) at 30% paraphrase, so price each gate alone → projects/local__stacked-redraw-gate-cost.md#E1
- `[tennis][scoring][testing][sandbox]` Check a tie-break serve rotation by its sequence (0,1,1,0,0,1), not its counts: two wrong rotations tied the correct one on serves at 12 and 13 points → projects/local__tennis-tiebreak-server-rotation.md#E1
- `[comedy][game-ads][testing][sandbox]` Key the per-trope cap on a closed verbatim trope list: at 30% paraphrased labels a free-text counter let a trope exceed a cap of 2 in 96% of 12-video batches, vs 0% with the closed list → projects/local__trope-cap-verbatim-labels.md#E1
- `[garden][decoy]` A CO2 mosquito decoy cut bites to 0.67x only while fueled; fuel loss released the stored mosquitoes (0.87x), so pair it with removal → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E312
- `[game-ads][disclosure]` Disclosure, core-experience footage and a core-loop playable are separate ad gates; a disclaimer or perfect disclosure label cured nothing in the 8-case model → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E313
- `[performance][gc]` Score caches on pause, footprint and time together: LRU sizes 1-4 are dominated by size 0 on a loop, pinned sizes stay on the front → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E314
- `[cache][gc]` Pinning does not give a partial cache under GC cost on a uniform loop (winners only 5 or 0); partial sizes won only on a skewed loop → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E315
- `[performance][gc]` Promptness is the only goal that keeps short GC intervals on the front; without it the longest interval dominated all five shorter ones in the model → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E316
- `[game-ads][comedy]` Passing the invented-name gate says nothing about templating: 0 lookalikes coexisted with 7 of 12 repeats, and the name gate itself fired at 30 videos (14 pairs) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E317
- `[sorting][text]` call-number fractions must sort as right-padded digit strings: an integer fraction misordered 4 of 5 sets and float tied distinct numbers past 16 digits, with every comparator still a valid weak order → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E318
- `[comedy][narrative][testing]` a game-of-scene flat/X check cuts a joke-to-confession return as flat unless it has a figurative-to-literal callback rule that requires an earlier target and shared noun → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E319
- `[testing][concurrency]` preemption-bounded search finds lost updates and torn reads at k=1 but a three-step ordering needed k=4, so a clean k=2 run does not clear multi-step interleavings → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E320
- `[comedy][humor]` a whole-sketch self-inflicted rule card raised severe-beat scores at distance 3 to 10 but cut mild-beat scores at distance 2 to 7 because the added distance flattens mild harm; apply it per beat severity → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E321
- `[comedy][humor]` a self-inflicted-only rule card raised the score of the best escalation at framing distance 0 to 10 of 12 and lost only at 11 to 12, where stronger outside-force gags fit; the win did not need a capacity boost above the strength bonus → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E322
- `[statistics][testing]` a second positive lifts a low-prevalence posterior only if its errors are independent (gynecologist case 0.092 to 0.503); a fully correlated repeat adds exactly nothing, and at a 60.7% false-positive rate it takes 8 independent positives → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E323
- `[packing][algorithm]` largest-first signature planning keeps blanks minimal but needs a tail merge to reach the fewest signatures on sizes 4, 8, 16, 32 (0 misses of 240); on sizes 16, 12, 4 it still misses 101 of 240 so use the exact DP → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E324
- `[comedy][narrative][testing]` in a Bayes model of a sketch, neutral delay adds no surprise to a specific-turn payoff (4.91 bits at 0, 2 and 4 beats) while fair-play misdirection reaches 11.70 bits at the cost of the pre-reveal gap → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E325
- `[narrative][art][testing]` Revealing a 16x16 image by Bayer threshold order left a guess error of 0.022 at the midpoint versus 0.066 for raster at equal cells shown, and no advantage on a uniform image → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E190
- `[crossword][grid][testing]` On a 5x5 crossword grid, paired block placement kept symmetry in 8192/8192 patterns but only 15 passed connectivity and the 3-letter minimum, with the length check doing nearly all the rejecting → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E191
- `[writing][comedy][testing]` Deleting the stated-moral sentence fixed every toy draft's ending but erased the only specific turn in 2101 of 5971 (35.2%) that had one and flipped the derived-beat check in 406 → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E192
- `[comedy][humor][testing]` In a toy ladder, a distance dial rising with harm scored 13.84 against 8.6 for the best fixed dial; card-after cost only a third of the score at budget 20 (9.25) though it completed 15.5 percent of ladders → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E193
- `[ladder-placement][geometry][testing]` Set an extension ladder's 4:1 base offset from support height (length minus 3 ft stick-out, over 1.03, over 4), not a quarter of length: the shortcut over-offsets 0.85 to 0.97 ft on 16 to 32 ft ladders → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E194
- `[narrative][comedy][testing]` In a toy nine-beat short the information gap lasted 8 beats when chained and derived, 3 with three and-then links, 4 with one non-derived beat, while revealed content stayed identical → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E195
- `[comedy][humor][testing]` In a toy gag model, writing the rule card first completed a distance-matched ladder in 0.95 of seeds at budget 20 versus 0.155 card-after, but 10x budget erased the gap and a card that forbids a rung never completes → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E196
- `[comedy][humor][testing]` In a toy gag model, escalation scored 1.00 only with a rule card and distance matched to severity; card-blind got 0.27, fixed distance 0.25 and a violation-free schedule 0.00 even with both → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E197
- `[ai-video][character][consistency][sandbox]` A character-bible sheet audit between panels found 12/12 single-panel flaws but 0/4 uniform ones, and a verbatim descriptor left an unlocked jaw at 2 values; lock every attribute and compare sheet to descriptor too → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E198
- `[storytelling][economy][scoring][sandbox]` Give the final scene its own rule in a Chekhov look-ahead checker: a strictly-later scan mislabelled 2 of 6 stories (intro and use both in the last scene), a scene counter 0, as in bowling's tenth frame → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E199
- `[narrative][comedy][dither][sandbox]` Step a Bayer reveal only on beats derived from the game: naive per-beat stepping spent 4 reveal steps on new-noun/stock beats across 2 of 3 sheets, gated stepping 0, and an all-derived sheet matched → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E200
- `[ux][demo][state][sandbox]` Clear a demo's in-flight lock in the shared slide-change path, not just the jump handler: jump-only reset left 1587 of 19530 event sequences on a dead slide once Next was ungated, all-paths reset 0 → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E201
- `[comedy][humor][gag-design][sandbox]` A gag rule card is a violation ceiling, not an escalation engine: it scored 0/5 benign beats at distance 0 and 5/5 vs 2/5 at distance 2, but strict escalation steps fell from 4 to 2 (violations stall at 3) → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E202
- `[weaving][textile][drafting][sandbox]` Pick a satin move number coprime to the shaft count and not 1 or n-1; a drawdown check over n up to 24 found none for 2, 3, 4, 6 shafts and phi(n)-2 otherwise, matching the rule on 276 pairs → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E203
- `[sourdough][baking][ratio][sandbox]` Count the starter's own flour and water in dough hydration: 500 g flour, 350 g water, 100 g 100% starter is 72.73% not 70.00% (a 50% starter gives 67.65%), error zero only when starter and dough hydration match → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E204
- `[tennis][scoring][testing][sandbox]` Score a recorded tie-break by stopping at the first 7-point 2-lead state: tallying every point failed 3 of 8 logs (all with trailing points), and serve order must be checked as a sequence because counts tie at 12 points → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E205
- `[film][dither][narrative][sandbox]` Split a Bayer reveal's 16 level changes over k derived beats by largest remainder: floor(16/k) stalls short of full for 11 of 16 counts, remainder split finishes for all with steps within 1, and more than 16 beats leaves idle ones → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E326
- `[comedy][humor][sandbox][process]` One safe distance for the worst gag spoils the mild ones: with mild harm needing closeness and severe needing distance, a far constant left 6 harm beats and a near one 9, while per-beat distance by severity band left 0 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E327
- `[narrative][dither][testing][sandbox]` Drive a Bayer reveal from game-derived beats, not new-noun beats: naive rule saturated the 15-level ramp on 3/3 sheets vs 1/3 derived, but derived under-advances a heighten that adds a noun (12 vs 15), so flag those explicitly → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E328
- `[comedy][humor][sandbox][process]` A rule card that licenses go-big escalation must pin framing distance per beat and callback: matched distance gave go-big 19 funny vs 10 mild with 0 harm beats, fixed distance 2 gave 2 vs 10 with 4 harm, and a reset callback brought back 2 harm beats → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E329
- `[film][video][dither][sandbox]` A Bayer 4x4 reveal split into 6 beats stepped 3,2,3,3,2,3 levels while 4 or 8 beats were even, and a screen-anchored pattern differed in 384 of 384 px at crop offset 285 but 0 at multiples of 4; use 4 or 8 beats and world coordinates → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E330
- `[film][video][comedy][sandbox]` A six-row check-column caption stack on 1080x1920 video cleared the platform overlay 0/6 at 90 or 93 percent safe margins and 6/6 at 120/240/660 strict, but only 3/6 survived the 16:9 crop; intersect overlay and crop bands → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E331
- `[rendering][dither][viewport][mobile]` Flooring a pixel-art scale in device px kept all 6 test viewports integer where the CSS floor broke 2 (2.625 and 1.5), but cost the 360x640 ratio-1.5 phone fill, 98.8% down to 65.8%; check fill per device → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E332
- `[comedy][humor][sandbox][testing]` A two-sided distance band scored 130/130 funny when framing distance followed each beat's severity, but one fixed setting scored 64 to 66 on a rising schedule and maximum distance scored 0 on a mild one; set the dial per beat → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E333
- `[comedy][humor][sandbox][testing]` A gag rule card alone left a go-big schedule at 37 funny beats against 111 for mild under fixed grounded framing; card plus matched distance gave 111 at precision 1.000, and a card missing the harm rule fell to 0.483 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E334
- `[comedy][humor][sandbox][measurement]` The same 30 gag sets gave 26 abstained style labels under grounded framing and 7 under surreal or matched framing (affiliative 4 versus 23), so report the framing distance with any humor-style label → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E335
- `[ai-video][disclosure][compliance][sandbox]` In a toy label-policy model skipping the label below realism 4 missed 0/22 flagged videos but skipping below 8 missed 6/22 (strict detector) and 2/22 (lenient), and labelling never fixed the 11/22 wrong-product videos, so keep the exemption conservative and accuracy a separate gate → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E336
- `[film][dither][viewport][sandbox]` A 4x4 Bayer swatch keeps all 17 levels only at integer scale (17/17 at 1x to 3x, 10/17 at 1.5x, 5/17 at 2.5x worst-phase), so floor to an integer scale before letting dither levels carry meaning → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E337
