- `[tooling][comedy][testing][sandbox]` An index-keyed check-column sidecar was wrong on 5 of 9 beats after one insert and 4 of 8 after a reorder, a name-keyed one still missed every added beat, and a verdict derived from the beat stayed 0 wrong, so derive it at render time → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E338
- `[comedy][humor][algorithm][sandbox]` Measuring a comedy framing's distance in straight lines landed every beat (25/25) yet used only 0.54 of the budget with 56 order inversions on a walled map (0.78 and 6 on an open one), so use walk steps and score headroom and order → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E339
- `[locksmithing][security][sandbox][model]` In a toy pin-tumbler lock 79,666 usable keyings fell to 50 probes by binding-order picking (65 with security pins) and a 5-digit early-exit compare fell in 25 of 100,000, so remove per-position feedback instead of enlarging the keyspace → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E340
- `[ai-video][compliance][model]` In a toy model a front-view reference image cut noncompliant AI shots only from 39/40 to 30/40 and gave no help on a back view (40/40), and the label never fixed drift, so gate fidelity per angle and the label separately → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E341
- `[film][comedy][sandbox][viewport]` In a toy check column a correct derived verdict (8/8 vs 3/8 naive) still gave 0/8 usable beats when captions sat at broadcast-safe positions hidden in 4/4 viewports, so place captions in the mid band and grade verdict and visibility separately → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E342
- `[scuba-diving][planning][sandbox][model]` A toy gas plan from the mean breather (19 min) left 4 of 10 divers without reserve and 0 of 5 pairs able to share a tank, while planning from the worst breather at stress rate (5 min) passed all, so plan to the heaviest breather → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E343
- `[game-design][input][state][testing]` in quasiperiodic state the age cutoff is lossless only at the largest lag with any validity-passing press (89 here); cutting at the best near-recovery lag 55 lost 594 presses → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E344
- `[concrete][modeling][simulation]` time a pour by hourly degree-hours above the datum; the mean-temperature shortcut matched an 18/2 C swing but gave 864 h versus 288 h with nights below the datum (arithmetic only, modeled) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E345
- `[game-design][input][state][testing]` the lossless age cutoff is per gate: on a half-duty period-12 wave the symmetric gate was lossless at 41, the one-sided at 42, and the wrong cut lost 300 presses → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E346
- `[film][rendering][dithering][testing]` a filmic toe did not starve a Bayer dither of shadow levels (6 kept, 5 with no curve) but cut the overall count to 13 of 17, and a near-black scene fell to 6; check levels per region on the real scene → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E347
- `[baking][modeling][simulation]` scale proofing time by 2 per 10 C but only to about 35 C; a model with yeast heat-kill made the rule 3.3x too fast at 42 C (assumed constants, modeled) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E348
- `[film][rendering][dithering][testing]` index the Bayer matrix by world x: pans of 1 to 5 px stayed rigid (0 error) versus up to 56 percent swimming screen-anchored, and a stale swap offset then appears as a clean shift → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E349
- `[comedy][humor][sandbox][gamedesign]` A distance dial snapped to 2, 3, 4 and 6 bands kept 0.615, 0.651, 0.794 and 0.897 of the continuous fit on a mixed mild-and-severe ladder; size bands by the narrowest gate → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E350
- `[comedy][humor][sandbox][pathfinding]` An 8-way BFS comedy distance dial over a 4-way world scored 0.342 on an open map against 1.000 for 4-way BFS, partly from tie-breaking; match the dial's movement rule and break ties explicitly → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E351
- `[plumbing][drainage][sandbox]` A 40 ft drain at 1/4 inch per foot needs 10 inches of fall (14.5 inches depth with pipe and cover) and rounding 7 ft sticks down lost 1.5 inches, so budget depth from run length first → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E352
- `[comedy][humor][sandbox][testing]` A full gag rule card held precision 1.000 only with an exact distance dial; dial error of 1, 2 and 3 steps gave 0.769, 0.685 and 0.638 and a +2 bias gave 0.346, so the card caps gag type not severity: margin the dial → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E353
- `[game-ads][comedy][testing][sandbox]` IPM x D1 ranking of recuts gained 0.43 over a random pick when the pool varied comic engine but only 0.05 when it varied topic and 0 with no spread, so rank engine-varied variants → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E354
- `[meteorology][safety][testing][sandbox]` Flash-to-bang seconds over 3 is within 3% up to 20 C but reads 5% near at 35 C, the miles rule 6-9% near in warm air, and a 0.5 s count error is 17% at 1 km and 57% at 300 m → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E355
- `[storytelling][teaching][suspense][testing][sandbox]` Curiosity peaks at felt confidence near 0.46, so a fluent teller (felt +0.22) should seed about 0.24 not 0.46 (15% less curiosity at 0.46), while at a 0.10 seed fluency helps → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E356
- `[tire-sizing][vehicles][estimating][testing][sandbox]` Tire diameter is rim x 25.4 + 2 x width x aspect/100: a 18 inch swap with aspect 40 stays within 3% of 225/45R17 but aspect 35 does not (-3.1%), and a 265/50R17 reads 9.9% slow → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E357
- `[game-ads][narrative][testing][sandbox]` A 15 s cut that leaves the information gap open won IPM-only ranking every time (0% correct) but lost on IPM times D1 (58% correct for the beat cut), so rank gap-leaving cuts on the product, not IPM → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E358
- `[game-ads][narrative][model][sandbox]` If one ending drives both conversion and D1, ranking by IPM times D1 flips no pick over IPM alone (0 flips across 8 decay rates) and only widens the truncated cut's crossover from 4.55% to 7.89%/s → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E359
- `[transit][simulation][model][sandbox]` With dwell proportional to the gap, buses always platoon (headway cv 0.55-0.86 to 2.45 over 40 stops); a minimum-headway hold equal to the nominal headway fails, one just above it (11 vs 10) gives cv 0 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E360
- `[game-ads][comedy][model][sandbox]` Reskinning the best comic engine beats a diverse 8-engine ad batch only while per-repeat fatigue is under about 3.4% (1.498 vs 1.424); beyond that, or if the approved engine is weak, vary the engine → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E361
- `[comedy][game-ads][verification][sandbox]` Enforce a fake-ad batch's per-trope cap on canonical ids: one renamed trope label let a cap of 1 allow 2 videos of the same trope (batch 7 vs 6), and the different-engine rule only holds up to 8 ideas (reuse 2 at 12 to 16) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E362
- `[railway][simulation][model][sandbox]` A fixed signalling block adds about B/v seconds to the moving-block headway v/(2b)+L/v, so moving block gains most at low speed and long blocks (1500 m: 9.7x at 10 m/s, 2.1x at 40 m/s) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E363
- `[determinism][gamedev][state][sandbox]` Save a seeded PRNG's state words as unsigned values and re-mask on load: restore is O(1) vs a 5000-roll seed+count replay, but unmasked signed words diverged in 145 of 175 swept states while a single-state test can pass → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E364
- `[game-ads][film][model][sandbox]` A trailer pacing curve (1.0 to 0.4 s holds) is readable for only 0.057 of a 15 s phone ad at a 1.0 s read time, so floor the hold at the read time and compare 15 s and 30 s cuts only at equal readability → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E365
- `[songwriting][rhyme][testing][sandbox]` A two-pass rhyme labeller is only shown by a stanza with a stray assonant line: on my table it differed from one-pass on 4 of 5 stanzas (AABBA vs AABBx) and a near-rhyme beside a perfect trio read AAAA vs AAxA → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E366
- `[comedy][testing][model][sandbox]` A floor-plus-ceiling gag card beat a ceiling-only card for any distance error under the band width (1.00 vs 0.67 at 0, 0.75 vs 0.42 at +3) and fell to 0.00 at +4 where ceiling-only kept 0.42, so the predicted crossover at 2 did not exist → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E367
- `[game-ads][film][testing][model][sandbox]` Beat-snapped recut points left the IPM-times-D1 length pick within 1 s at all 8 drop-off rates (act 2 moved 0.25 s, even with a cliff D1), but bar snapping shifted it at 6 of 8, so sweep lengths finer than the snap grid → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E368
- `[comedy][testing][model][sandbox]` A harm-ceiling card alone decays from 1.00 funny share at D=3 to 0.47 at D=9 once mild harm needs closeness; add a floor stated from the true distance (a floor 3 too low gives nothing), noting the 1.00 band result is by construction → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E369
- `[game-ads][narrative][testing][model][sandbox]` A bait-payoff gap cut wins IPM (90-100% of runs) but the honest gap cut wins IPM times D1 only 75-82% and only when bait costs retention; an unresolved gap lost nothing because the toy has no frustration term → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E370
- `[comedy][testing][model][sandbox]` A harm-ceiling rule card doubled funny score at D=6 (share 0.514 to 0.872, capped at 7/8 by the non-violating opener) but only when the stated ceiling equals the true distance; stated 3 too high it gave 0.514 and at D=1 nothing helps → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E371
- `[paper-formats][standards][model][sandbox]` ISO A-series sizes come from halving the longer side and flooring to the mm (reproduces A0 to A8 exactly); nearest rounding is wrong at A1 to A8, so derive each size from the previous rounded one → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E372
- `[game-ads][testing][model][sandbox]` Composing length, IPM-times-D1 and engine variety did not stack as predicted: a truncated cut flips the pick only below 1-penalty^(1/15) (2.35%/s at 0.7, not 4.6), and a 5% fatigue reskin still beat the diverse batch (9.99 vs 9.72) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E373
- `[hillwalking][estimation][model][sandbox]` Naismith time puts 100 m of climb at 0.83 flat km (12 km with 900 m up equals 19.5 km flat, 234 min) and Langmuir's descent term swings the same route by 40 min between 8 and 20 degree slopes, so compare in flat-equivalent km → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E374
- `[ui][input][state][testing]` Lossless age cutoff is max valid lag plus max late jitter: at max lag alone it lost J presses for J=1..8, max+J lost 0 at every J, one below lost 1, and early-only jitter kept max lag lossless → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E28
- `[ai-video][compliance][testing]` One shot-list lint can enforce real product footage, a verbatim character bible and the AI label together, but the label rule must take the platform: an unlabelled cartoon passed on YouTube and failed on TikTok in 6 modelled cases → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E29
- `[game-ads][film][testing]` Recut a winning ad by beat, not clock: in a 25 s toy fixture the beat recut kept the reveal gap at 20, 15 and 10 s, scaling only at 20 s and tail trim at none, and nothing survives below the 10 s of role minimums → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E30
- `[baking][thermal][model]` The baker's three-factor dough water rule is accurate (0.9 C) only when flour, preferment and water thermal masses are near equal and missed by up to 12.5 C otherwise, asking for -6 C water in a hot kitchen; a heat balance hits the target exactly → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E31
- `[algorithm][scheduling][testing]` Earliest-finish interval scheduling is optimal only with exactly equal weights: weights 1 or 2 already dropped it to 64.3 percent of 3000 six-job sets, 1..9 to 38.4, and length-proportional weights to 24.6, so use the weighted DP → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E375
- `[comedy][humor][testing]` A gag-card harm ceiling makes go-big escalation safe only when it equals the framing distance: the over-stated ceiling leaked at 6 of 8 distances like no card, the under-stated one was safe but lost score at 7 → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E32
- `[distribution][process][testing]` Five venue rules composed into one launch calendar: the Saturday self-promo thread, not the jam end, set Show HN (day 6 and day 13 for jam ends 3 and 8), and a same-day duplicate-copy plan tripped 7 rules → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E33
- `[building-physics][condensation][model]` Window condensation starts at the RH whose dew point equals the coldest inner surface: 37, 63 and 88 percent for single, double and triple glazing at 20/0 C, and a cold-bridge corner cut double glazing to 36 → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E34
- `[determinism][timing][gamedev][testing]` Carry cooldowns still drift in float time (601 exact vs 600 at 30/60 fps for a 100 ms step) and a representable 125 ms step drifts too, since dt is the inexact part: count in integer ticks and state the boundary convention → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E376
- `[determinism][timing][gamedev][balance]` A max-delta cap also starves carry cooldowns: a 50 ms cap with 400 ms stalls left the sim 1400 ms behind and lost 14 of 101 fires (lag/step), a 500 ms cap lost none; feed timers uncapped time or catch up → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E377
- `[determinism][animation][timing][floating-point]` Integer microseconds are not exact time at 60 fps: 1200/3600 sprite frames at 30 Hz differ from a Fraction oracle (float 1341), whole-tick integer math 0; phase accumulation capped a rate-change jump at 1 vs 540 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E378
- `[determinism][testing][gamedev][algorithm]` Per-entity RNG streams keyed by seed plus unique id left a fight roll unchanged by an unrelated spawn in 8/8 seeds (shared stream changed 8/8), but colliding ids bring the perturbation back; assert id uniqueness → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E379
- `[testing][harness][timing][determinism]` Poll-for-state passed a 16.7%-slow tick scheduler 3/3 like a correct one; a separate elapsed budget (+5%) failed only the slow one (11666 vs 10500 ms) and tolerated a 3%-slow control (modelled, margin chosen) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E380
- `[gamedev][testing][determinism]` Rounding slack hides a missing jitter term: with L=100, ceil(L/q) passes at q=15,16,19,33 but drops 15 valid events at q=20 where q divides L; always add jitter before ceil → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E381
- `[gamedev][testing][determinism][input]` An age gate is exact only if the valid/invalid lag gap exceeds twice the jitter; at gap = 2j the best gate still errs once (j=2,4) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E382
- `[gamedev][testing][determinism][input]` Quantized overlap gate hits the 10*W floor only when a frame edge lies in the W+1 optimal plateau; 28 of 60 alignments inflated the error → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E383
- `[gamedev][testing][determinism][input]` Age-gate error equals the sum of per-age minimum counts over the overlap only if valid-heavy ages precede invalid-heavy ones; reversed weights cost 66 vs 12 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E384
- `[gamedesign][balance][testing][method]` A flat ability cost against a level-scaled pool had binding rate 0.40 at level 1 then exactly 0.00 at levels 2-4 (spam endgame) while a cost scaled with the pool held 0.40 throughout; log binding rate per level and read a pinned 0 or 1 as a non-binding cost → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E385
- `[gamedev][input][testing]` Leak/loss duality at a quantized boundary: the two cutoff roundings trade L mod q lost valid events against (q - L mod q) leaked invalid ones, so pick the rounding by which error is cheaper, zero only if q divides L → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E386
- `[algorithm][vfx][determinism]` Half-open [lo,hi) edges conserve counts for phase buckets and polygon vertices (12 of 12 vs inclusive 15; PIP 0 vs 2 of 4 wrong) but float tau edges still misassign 18/295 cases, so pair them with exact compares → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E387
- `[vfx][animation][particles][gamedev]` Ki-blast sandbox: additive blend is order-free (1 vs 3 distinct results) and the charge telegraph is speed-independent but total window is not (collapses at speed 64); ramping charge emission in index order is lopsided (0.99) where bit-reversed (0.20) or golden (0.27) stays balanced → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E388
- `[comedy][humor][process][testing][model][sandbox]` A harm-ceiling card alone left 6 points per distance unused at distances 4 and 5 (15 and 17 against 21 and 23), tied a ceiling-plus-floor card below that, and an always-on floor lost 18 at distance 1, so add the mild-beat floor only for distant framings → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E389
- `[comedy][humor][process][testing][model][sandbox]` A rule-card harm ceiling matched the framing distance and scored at least the better of go-big or mild at 5 of 5 distances (70 total), but set 2 too high it collapsed to go-big (12) and 1 too low gave up 14 points, so verify the ceiling against the distance → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E390
- `[game-ads][film][testing][model][sandbox]` Ranking cuts on IPM times D1 flipped the pick in 83 percent of trials once holds under a 1.0 s read time counted as unreadable (0 at read time 0 or 0.3 s), so floor holds at read time before ranking → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E391
- `[game-ads][comedy][testing][model][sandbox]` The top IPM of 8 same-engine reskins looked 0.415 better than it was at 300 impressions for a real gain of 0.015 (diverse engines gained 0.237), so treat a reskin winner as unproven below about 1000 impressions per variant → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E392
- `[dive-planning][testing][model][sandbox]` In a tank-fraction toy the rule of thirds left exactly 0.0 spare gas for a shared exit at equal breathing rates and went to minus 0.167 at a 1.25 times rate (halves minus 0.5, quarters 0.25 and non-negative to 1.5), so treat thirds as the floor and turn earlier under stress → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E393
- `[comedy][humor][testing][model][sandbox]` On an 8-beat mixed ramp a safe cue beat the best distance (23 against 19) only while it muted under about 20 percent of severe-beat funniness; at 30 percent severe mute and half mild mute it fell to 15.3 and lost 16 of 25 grid cells, so test the cue's mute before swapping it for distance → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E394
- `[determinism][gamedev][balance][timing]` A reset-to-STEP cooldown made one fixed ability cost bind at 0.000/0.029/0.074 at 30/60/144 fps (fires 450/515/540) while a carry-overshoot loop gave 0.101 at all three (556 fires); fix cadence before tuning costs → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E395
- `[triage][testing][model][sandbox]` In a START-style fail-fast card all 6 orders of the three checks gave identical categories (0 of 32 differ) while mean checks ranged 2.634 to 2.766, but stopping at the first pass changed 6 of 32 types, so order by failure rate and never change the stop rule → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E396
- `[game-ads][comedy][testing][model][sandbox]` A second-game X gate on ad variants lifted an IPM-only pick from 0.374 to 0.997 of oracle value (any-new-noun form only 0.603), but added 0.000 once D1 was multiplied in and the naive form lost 0.034, so gate only when D1 is unseen and only on the derived-link check → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E397
- `[ui][input][state][gamedev][testing]` Sweeping an age threshold 0-8 on a ramp-dwell schedule only slid errors between stale executions (0 to 600) and dropped valid presses (1000 to 0), crossing at 265/265 of 1600; no threshold reaches the 0/0 that a state-unchanged check gives → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E398
- `[ui][input][state][gamedev][testing]` Swapping a uniform lag histogram for a skewed one left the best age gate at the tolerance (4) under every wrong-press weight, because each lag is decided independently; only the cost of a bad gate changed (8/80/800 at gate 12) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E399
- `[gamedev][physics][simulation][testing]` Particle-liquid pin puzzle: cap speed under one radius per tick (water tunneled 42->7 through 10px walls), make reaction stone sink by density not freeze (frozen crust stranded lava), and never use max particle speed as "settled" in a position-based stack; prove levels with a stepped completability test → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E38
- `[fitness][research]` Grip-strength mortality kernel (HR 1.16 per 5 kg, Lancet PURE) reproduces from the source abstract, but only for death/CVD: diabetes, falls, fracture are null, cancer reversed, and the per-5-kg HR cannot be stretched to fitness-tier contrasts or read as a training effect → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E206
- `[research][attribution]` Quote-attribution kernels: the Eisenhower urgent/important wording checks out against the 1954 transcript (no "seldom", unnamed college president, 0 primary texts for the popular form), but narrow it to "not in the documented text"; count roots with untraceable paraphrases kept separate, and check scope apart from wording because a verbatim match passes the misused Parkinson phrase → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E207
- `[game-ads][testing][model][sandbox]` A Bayer-ordered rotation of ad variants through delivery slots fixed the period-4 cycle (1.00 against 5.00 for round-robin) but gave 2.78 imbalance at period 8, so vary the rotation each cycle rather than fix one → projects/local__bayer-ad-schedule-aliases-at-period-eight.md#E1
- `[game-ads][testing][model][sandbox]` A film-style cold open is ranked to maximum promise by IPM (1.0) but to the promise the game delivers (0.5) by IPM times D1 in linear, steep and cliff penalty cases, so cap promise at delivery before recutting → projects/local__cold-open-promise-matches-delivery.md#E1
- `[curing][sandbox][numerics][model]` Cure dose percent needs its weight base: 0.25 percent of meat is 156 ppm nitrite but the same percent of meat plus brine is 195 to 312 ppm, and a trimming mismatch gives 195 or 125, so weigh the meat at cure time → projects/local__curing-dose-base-meat-weight-not-total.md#E1
- `[lab-dilution][sandbox][numerics][model]` The best serial-dilution step count rises with the pipette small-volume constant (2 steps to a=0.2 uL, 3 at 0.5, 4 at 1.0, 6 at 2.0), so two steps is a regime not a law → projects/local__dilution-best-step-count-grows-with-pipette-constant.md#E1
- `[lab-dilution][sandbox][numerics][model]` The best two-step dilution split is a plateau (1.97 percent for 20x50 to 50x20 at a=0.1 uL), and the balanced 31.6x31.6 plan loses to volume-grid rounding (4.31 percent at 1 uL), so pick a plan with whole-microlitre transfers → projects/local__dilution-optimum-is-a-plateau-pick-grid-friendly-plan.md#E1
- `[game-ads][testing][model][sandbox]` Spending extra impressions on the union of the IPM and IPM times D1 top variants lost 1.7 points when true scores were within 1 percent and gained 4.7 only with a clear winner, so check the top-score gap before targeting → projects/local__disagreement-targeting-does-not-help-flat-score-landscape.md#E1
- `[game-ads][testing][process][sandbox]` A hand-typed D1 sidecar keyed by cut name flipped the IPM times D1 winner in 1 of 8 single renames (only when the winner was the renamed cut) and was silent in all 8, so key on a stable id and assert the join count → projects/local__sidecar-drift-flips-ipm-d1-winner-only-when-winner-renamed.md#E1
- `[soldering][sandbox][numerics][model]` A solder joint melts only if its steady state exceeds liquidus: a ground-plane pad tops out at 187.5 C with a 350 C tip and 212.5 C at 400 C, so dwell is irrelevant and preheat (225 C steady state) or a bigger tip is the fix → projects/local__solder-joint-steady-state-decides-not-dwell-time.md#E1
- `[canning][food-safety][numerics][model]` altitude time additions are linear patches on an exponential: in a z=10 model they leave lethality at 0.74 of sea level at 3000 ft and 0.29 at 10000 ft, so treat the table as margin-based → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E35
- `[distribution][gamedev][model]` contain promotion, do not avoid it: dedicated thread, give-first and weekly spacing each guard a different community rule (1080k reach with all three, 880k without the thread, 280k without give-first, 20k dripping) in an assumed-rule model → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E36
- `[lab-dilution][curing][numerics][model]` fix the cure dose weight base (10 to 25 percent error) before tuning dilution steps (1 to 3 percent at the best n), but a single step at a coarse pipette gives 20 to 50 percent so still pick n → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E37
- `[plumbing][drainage][numerics][model]` the standard drain-slope table clears a 2 ft/s scour velocity by a thin margin at 1.5 in (1.77) and 3 in (1.99) and a wide one at 6 in (3.16); rougher pipe cuts 20 percent, so lay small runs above table → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E38
- `[game-ads][testing][model][sandbox]` a hook element that lifts IPM more for low-fidelity ads erodes the IPM times D1 correction (winner fidelity 1.0 at K=0, 0.7 at K=1, 0.4 at K=4), so hold frame-zero sound equal across variants before ranking → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E39
- `[lab-dilution][soldering][numerics][model]` raise a knob until a notch gains under 1 percent then check the target: dilution steps landed within 0.007 percent of optimum, a ground-plane solder joint stopped at 185.0 C of 217 C so change lever not dwell → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E40
- `[ai-video][compliance][disclosure][model]` gate AI product video on product-shot source then label by platform: real product plus labelled AI scenes passed everywhere, reference image at fidelity 1.0 still left 2 violations → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E41
- `[comedy][humor][testing]` a gag rule card lets a go-big sketch stay funny only when its harm ceiling equals the frame's distance (grounded 2/6 to 6/6 funny); a frame-blind ceiling gave 0 gain → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E42
- `[card-magic][permutation][exhaustive]` two-card faro goals: a 16-card deck reaches only 32/240 joint positions for a card pair (52 cards: 2600/2652) and top/bottom always mirror; generate multi-card goals from the BFS reachable set → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E39
- `[film][narration][timing]` Reaction cut on a real 2:00 captioned film: snapping pauses to the trigger caption's end split 0 lines (max lag 4.26 s) while 15 s intervals split 7 of 7 and running every line back replayed 38% of the source; run back only an early trigger in a long caption (12 s: lag 9.88 s) → projects/local__reaction-cut-pause-placement.md#E1
- `[film][tooling][testing]` Playwright recordVideo drifted from the driving script's wall clock (+3 s to +12 s over 100 s); place cuts from a timestamped contact sheet, not from log times → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E40
- `[gamedesign][input][mobile]` two-thumb defense: map heavy->block pad, swift->parry pad, lock a pad on early taps; masher won 23% vs casual 81%, parry beat block-only even with 10% misreads → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E41
- `[queueing][staffing][gamedesign]` patient queues need fewer servers than square-root staffing: with 24-42 s patience and 12 s service, net peaked at 6 servers for a load of 5.5, not the rule's 8 → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E42
- `[elevator][dispatch][gamedesign]` parking lifts ahead of a known one-way surge made the longest wait worse (21 s unparked vs 24-30 s parked); when capacity is the bottleneck keep cars moving, pre-position only for response-time peaks → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E43
- `[simulation][determinism][testing]` a gap drawn at the quiet rate skipped a 15 s surge entirely; redraw the pending gap on a rate change, and compare strict booleans (an undefined flag re-fired every frame and shifted every level) → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E44
- `[pitch][gamedesign][onboarding]` pitch a controls/feel change as the playable game (first screen demands the new input, lessons run on live stats, end card shows the player's own numbers); a recorded film of it was rejected as weak → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E45
- `[plumbing][geometry][sandbox]` a gravity drain's fall is run x slope, so max run = void depth / 0.25 in per ft (37 ft in 9.25 in); long runs need a vent, cleanout or re-route, never a flatter slope → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E400
- `[ladder-safety][geometry][sandbox]` the 4-to-1 ladder rule is a fixed 75.96 degree angle; size the ladder as the hypotenuse of landing height plus 3 ft and height over 4, so a 16 ft ladder safely serves only 12 ft landings → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E401
- `[comedy][humor][narrative][testing]` err low on a rule card's harm ceiling: one step under cost 44 to 32 in score but one step over cost 44 to 19 and half the funny beats → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E402
- `[comedy][humor][narrative][testing]` a rule card's harm ceiling must equal the framing distance and come first: at d=3 it scored 15 vs 6 for uncarded go-big, while ceiling d+1 scored 6 and a late card 14 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E403
- `[comedy][humor][narrative][testing]` a rule card's harm ceiling must be rewritten when the framing distance shifts: fixed at 2 on a rise it scored 11 vs 17, fixed at 4 on a drop only 3 of 6 beats were funny → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E404
- `[game-ads][testing][model][sandbox]` a two-stage recut test (IPM screen then top half on IPM times D1) matched the large-budget pick at 14400 vs 24000 impressions, while a top-2 confirmation gave 96 percent, barely over a one-stage 95 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E405
- `[gamedev][ai][racing]` a racing AI that took its drift direction from its steering error spent 52% of a lap on grass (no-drift: 10%); choose a committed manoeuvre's direction from the path ahead, not the control error → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E46
- `[gamedev][racing][state]` a lap counter that only increments turned a kart bumped back over the line into a whole lap of lost progress (12 rescues for one kart); decrement on backward crossings symmetrically → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E47
- `[gamedesign][racing][level-design]` judge an off-road shortcut by cut+boost vs main+boost, not vs the plain line; guessed layouts gave a 2.6 s dominant alley and boosted cuts no faster than boosting on road → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E48
- `[gamedesign][balance][racing]` weight in a zero-sum kart stat budget only acts in bumps, so solo time trials made it free points and one combo won all 3 tracks; keep stats a measurement cannot price out of the budget → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E49
- `[cribbage][exhaustive][testing][sandbox]` Cribbage hand totals 19, 25, 26 and 27 cannot occur in 12,994,800 hand-starter combinations, 29 occurs only 4 times and the modal score is 4, so enumerate a scoring space exhaustively and cross-check the scorer on 29, 24 and 0 first → projects/local__cribbage-hand-scores-19-25-26-27-are-impossible.md#E1
- `[lab-dilution][sandbox][numerics][model]` Peak and decline: on a 0.001 grid to 20 uL the optimal equal-step dilution count peaks at 6 for a from 1.93 to 3.69 uL then drops to 5, with switch points 0.015, 0.30, 0.77, 1.2 uL, so a ladder rule needs a ceiling → projects/local__dilution-step-thresholds-are-not-one-per-doubling.md#E1
- `[distribution][platform-etiquette][sandbox][model]` In a toy effort budget the 9:1 give-first ratio is a price not a ban: a standalone post costs R+2 actions and beats a 2-action thread post only above (R+2)/2 times its reach (flip between 5.4 and 5.6 at R=9), so compare reach per action → projects/local__give-first-ratio-binds-standalone-posts-not-thread-slots.md#E1
- `[game-ads][film][testing][sandbox]` Hand-edited cue tables left 9 cues off across four toy recuts and flipped the IPM-times-D1 winner from beat 3 to beat 0, where a time remap left 0 off, so remap recuts and check cue times before ranking them → projects/local__hand-edited-cue-tables-skew-recut-ranking-remap-does-not.md#E1
- `[game-ads][film][testing][sandbox]` A silent first second moved the IPM-only winning cold-open promise from 1.0 to 0.6 when only high-promise recuts were hit, yet IPM times D1 held at 0.5 in 3 of 3 toy scenarios, so gate every recut on a frame-0 audio hit before ranking → projects/local__hook-frame0-audio-vs-promise-cap-recut-rank.md#E1
- `[game-ads][testing][model][sandbox]` A hand-kept promise sidecar under IPM-times-D1 recut ranking picked the wrong recut in 2592 of 4096 toy drift cases (63.3 percent, 29.2 percent with one stale entry), far above the under-25-percent I expected, so derive promise from the cut at ranking time → projects/local__stale-promise-sidecar-picks-wrong-recut-only-when-contender.md#E1
- `[ux][interaction][testing][sandbox]` In a two-slide stream-then-gate demo model, leaving at a pending gate stranded 6 of 6 times without a lock reset on slide change and 0 of 6 with it, and uncancelled stream timers leaked 2 ghost lines, so reset lock, gate and timers together on navigation → projects/local__stream-gate-lock-reset-and-scrollback-carry-state-machine.md#E1
- `[bookkeeping][testing][checksum][sandbox]` A trial balance saw 2000/2000 one-sided errors and 1620/1620 transpositions in a toy ledger but 0/20 omissions, 0/20 wrong-account postings and missed 18/324 cancelling pairs, so add an account check digit and an entry count before trusting a balanced book → projects/local__trial-balance-sees-only-the-unbalanced-error.md#E1
- `[bookbinding][imposition][model][sandbox]` Simulate the folds and read the stack top-down to get a signature imposition (16 pages: back 16 1 4 13 / 9 8 5 12), rather than a memorised table; page 1 sits beside page N on the back, not the front → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E406
- `[comedy][humor][model][sandbox]` A rule-card harm ceiling equal to the framing's distance kept distant victims safe (0 unsafe beats) but left 582 unsafe beats for close victims in a toy model; make the ceiling depend on victim closeness too → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E407
- `[gear-trains][mechanics][model][sandbox]` Pick coprime tooth counts so a chipped tooth meets every mate: with gcd g it meets only b/g gear teeth (2 of 40 at 20 and 40, 40 of 40 at 17 and 40) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E408
- `[comedy][humor][model][sandbox]` A closeness-penalised harm ceiling stayed safe but kept 32% less value than a two-sided card (4302.7 vs 6307.2) once mild harm wants closeness; gate severe harm by distance and score mild harm by closeness → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E409
- `[game-ads][testing][model][sandbox]` Capping cold-open promise at delivery with an any-noun check column kept 0.84-0.88 of the best value on decorated sheets, against 1.00 for a derived-from-game column, so count only mechanic-linked elements → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E410
- `[game-ads][film][model][sandbox]` A promise cap with IPM-only ranking is no cheaper guard against hand-edited cue errors: it disagreed with IPM times D1 in 606 of 2000 clean sheets and lost value in 960 hand-edited ones, so keep remap and the D1 term → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E411
- `[game-ads][testing][model][sandbox]` A hand-kept D1 sidecar for IPM-times-D1 ranking flips the pick only when the winner is renamed (69 of 69 such trials, 0 of 331 others, default zero or mean alike), so join D1 from the IPM record or assert the keys match → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E412
- `[game-ads][comedy][model][sandbox]` Counting engine repeats through a stale sidecar hides fatigue: one missing reskin name of eight misleads the reskin-versus-diverse pick at 37 of 300 fatigue levels, so count from a field on each variant → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E413
- `[pitch][process][gamedesign]` before showing a rework or pitch of an existing game, audit it against the original's full feature list and robot-play the whole flow; the owner found 3 missing systems one prompt at a time → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E50
- `[onboarding][softlock][testing]` a tutorial arrow mapped separately from the step's pass test pointed at STRIKE for a GUARD lesson (endless repeat, all state tests green); derive both from one table, add an N-wrong-tries escape, test with a press-what-is-drawn robot → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E51
- `[javascript][input][gamedev]` early-return branches prepended to an event handler skipped its up/cancel cleanup and read consts before declaration (TDZ), both suite-green; declare and clean up first, dispatch after, and test sequential taps and every key path → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E52
- `[gamedev][state][combat]` a parry counter killed the attacker mid-string and its already-scheduled next hit still landed (dead wolf, 4 dmg); cancel an entity's scheduled events at death, and floor a tutorial foe's HP until its lessons finish → projects/pappydapimp69__demo-disc.md#E53
- `[narrative][structure][storytelling]` Out-and-back errand (Grimm KHM 29, The Luck Child): the return leg makes the suspense (tension 12 vs 0 with answers on arrival) while the reverse order adds nothing (forward order also 12); answering the ferryman before crossing traps the teller, so hold that answer until past it and the trap passes to the villain → projects/local__luck-child-out-and-back.md#E1
- `[game-ads][film][testing][sandbox]` A caption-warp cap of 1.5 before IPM times D1 ranking only pays when slowed footage really costs retention: it won from a slope of 0.05 (11.14 vs 8.65 at 0.2) and lost at 0.005 (11.14 vs 12.01), so measure the slope first → projects/local__caption-warp-cap-before-ipm-d1-ranking.md#E1
- `[cribbage][exhaustive][testing][sandbox]` In restricted cribbage decks the impossible-total set only grows (ace to eight: 10 gaps versus 4 in the full deck), so use small-space gaps to rule totals out, never in → projects/local__cribbage-subdeck-impossible-scores-grow-monotonically.md#E1
- `[game-ads][testing][model][sandbox]` Ranking ad recuts on IPM times D1 needs a derived-from-game fidelity link: it picked the true best in 5 of 6 toy sheets versus 3 of 6 for a new-noun check, and a stock flag cost one sheet so keep it a warning → projects/local__ipm-d1-rank-needs-derived-link-not-new-noun-check.md#E1
- `[bitset][javascript][testing][sandbox]` A JavaScript 64-flag bitset in one number gave 2 false negatives and 31 false positives (sign bit plus shift wrap), and greater-than-zero tests still failed 2 of 64 in two words; use 32-bit words with a not-equal-zero test → projects/local__js-bitset-bit-31-and-shift-wrap.md#E1
- `[scheduling][calendar][testing][sandbox]` A monthly repeat on the 29th to 31st must be computed from a stored anchor day: carry-forward drifted to day 28 for days 29, 30 and 31, skip fires 21 of 36 months on day 31 and clamp fires all 36 → projects/local__monthly-recurrence-day-31-skips-months-vs-clamp.md#E1
- `[gamedev][determinism][testing][sandbox]` Per-consumer RNG substreams isolate edits only when keyed by stable name, not slot: an insert changed 0 of 6 consumers by name versus 5 of 6 by index, and a 2-bit name hash shared streams for 3 of 6 names, so keep it wide and assert distinct seeds → projects/local__name-keyed-substreams-survive-reorder-index-keyed-do-not.md#E1
- `[comedy][humor][testing][sandbox]` A rule card that adds benign cover flipped a toy escalation from mild winning (1.40 vs 0.70) to go-big winning (2.20 vs 1.75), while also capping violation cost 0.53 of the go-big total, so the card should raise cover and not cap violation → projects/local__rule-card-lifts-benign-ceiling-so-go-big-escalation-works.md#E1
- `[comedy][humor][testing][sandbox]` A severity cap at mild harm made the distance dial forgiving instead of flipping it: 9 of 11 distances within 95 percent of best versus 1 of 11 for go-big, and only the closest framing lost, so tune distance only when gags reach severe harm → projects/local__rule-card-severity-cap-flattens-the-distance-dial.md#E1
- `[narrative][content][cadence]` A story-a-day quota (The StoryTeller's A Story Short): a 300 stock runs out on day 300, repeats fill the year at 18% duplicates, telling the day covers 365 with none, but idle days break it on day 300 → projects/local__storyteller-story-quota.md#E1
- `[narrative][game-design][curse]` A cure with a term (The StoryTeller's Hans My Hedgehog, The Three Ravens): acting 3 days early only loses when the penalty beats the days saved (1-3 pay or tie, 30 and 300 lose), and one missing sleeve restores 5 of 6 plus a wing under partial credit but 0 under all-or-nothing → projects/local__storyteller-term-cure.md#E1
- `[narrative][economy][simulation]` Trap a necessary sink (The StoryTeller's The Soldier and Death): with Death in the sack coverage falls 1.00 to 0.00 and 200 wait by year 59, cleared on release; with no aging the trap costs nothing, so keep decline running and show the backlog → projects/local__storyteller-trapped-death.md#E1
- `[narrative][stakes][simulation]` Stakes for a hero who cannot lose a fight (The StoryTeller's Fearnot): monsters up to size 1000 and a guarded sweetheart score 0, a parting he cannot fight scores 3.0 and her alone 10.0; cap his immunity at 100 and a size-1000 monster scores 9.0 again → projects/local__storyteller-fearnot.md#E1
- `[narrative][quest-design][simulation]` Nest a villain's heart one solver per layer (The StoryTeller's The Heartless Giant): 5 layers give 5 beats, 3 boxes on a reused key add 0, a missing salmon stops the quest at the well unless it has an OR route, one universal key gives 1 beat → projects/local__storyteller-heartless-giant.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][difficulty][simulation]` Fly the middle course (The StoryTeller's Daedalus and Icarus): with spray below and sun above, a stable or absent guide falls on the drift side and tolerated drift falls 13 to 2 as the band narrows 60 to 16; a guide stronger than its delay allows fails sooner than none, on whichever wall its swing reaches → projects/local__storyteller-icarus.md#E1
- `[narrative][antagonist][simulation]` Villain's failed attacks as the hero's progression (The StoryTeller's The Luck Child): a threat-driven king doubles 1,2,4,8 and hands the boy the goal in 4 attacks; a flat or deterred king stalls him (P 6 or 3), and a strong flat king arrives with half the response (7 vs 15), so tie the villain's drive to each gain → projects/local__storyteller-luck-child.md#E1
- `[narrative][pacing][simulation]` Test the one fragile condition at the threshold (The StoryTeller's Orpheus and Eurydice): with eagerness rising toward the exit, 1-3 silent steps late break the condition where 7-14 are needed early (resolve 10-40); with flat pull the exit adds nothing → projects/local__storyteller-orpheus.md#E1
- `[narrative][ai][simulation]` Flee a stale sighting (The StoryTeller's Perseus and the Gorgon): when the threat departs toward the avoider, fleeing its old spot gives 8 contact turns vs 2 for staying (6 vs 2 on a longer line), worst when its destination ends the flight; live tracking 2; harmless if it departs away → projects/local__storyteller-perseus-flight.md#E1
- `[narrative][worldbuilding][simulation]` A law keyed to the last holder picks her kin (The StoryTeller's Sapsorrow): daughters fit the dead queen's ring 1.15x to 21.98x as often as strangers as she gets rarer, 1.00x with no inheritance, 3.30x once a few strangers share her size, and win a crowd mainly by trying first (0.316 vs 0.0126) → projects/local__storyteller-sapsorrow.md#E1
- `[narrative][oral-storytelling][simulation]` A listener who voices the audience's doubt (The StoryTeller's dog) helps at the doubt beat (1.27x) and hurts mid-climax (0.89x) when the story answers later; at the climax only a large doubt is worth answering (9/9 cells), a small one is a toss-up (6/9), so ask early → projects/local__storyteller-dog.md#E1
- `[narrative][signals][simulation]` Forgotten sail (The StoryTeller's Theseus and the Minotaur): a forgotten update sends the default's meaning, so black-by-default cost 1400 vs 120 white-by-default vs 20 three-state; swap costs and black wins 280 vs 3000; no-news state worst (2000) if the receiver cannot wait → projects/local__storyteller-theseus-sail.md#E1
- `[narrative][structure][simulation]` Rule of three that teaches (The StoryTeller's The True Bride): fail twice for the same visible reason and change one visible thing on try three; as told gives 1 confirmed cause, two changes give 2, an off-screen blocker gives 0, and a hidden build-up makes the shown change look causal when it is not → projects/local__storyteller-true-bride.md#E1
- `[narrative][stakes][simulation]` Fearless hero, greedy guide (The StoryTeller's Fearnot): hero alone 0 tension; a guide paid only on a scare carries 4 scenes / 38.9 tension until cost beats hope, a fast learner 2 / 17.1; flat pay, a guide who knows, or one doubtful from the start (D0=128) gives 0 scenes → projects/local__storyteller-fearnot-tinker.md#E1
- `[narrative][bargains][simulation]` A blind "first thing to greet me" promise (The StoryTeller's Hans My Hedgehog) costs 67.67 against the pictured dog's 1 when the king has been lost for days and his daughter watches for him; naming the dog caps it at 1, "first person" still costs 68.33, and only a dog always at the gate makes it cheap → projects/local__storyteller-hans.md#E1
- `[narrative][concurrency][simulation]` A choice over a shared item is settled by who can reach it, not by intent (The StoryTeller's The Heartless Giant): shown-then-given kills in 2 of 4 orderings, a hand-to-hand give 0 of 5, a set-down handoff 1 of 5, a re-showing can meet a rival already on his way → projects/local__storyteller-heartless-giant-custody.md#E1
- `[narrative][security][simulation]` Death order carried by its subject (The StoryTeller's The Luck Child, Grimm KHM 29): the subject carrier gets 56/64 silent rewrites, a neutral courier 0/64; a seal cuts the subject case only to 37/64 while forgers exist, and an ally acting by name (37/64) needs the seal too → projects/local__storyteller-luck-child-letter.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][combat][simulation]` Striking a hazard you cannot look at (The StoryTeller's Perseus and the Gorgon, mirror shield): direct view dies 13/13; a corrected reflection hits 13/13 once tolerance covers rounding + (lag + 1) steps of target motion (coarse image 7/13 exact); an uncorrected reflection is worse than blind (1 vs 3); at wide tolerance blind ties it → projects/local__storyteller-perseus-mirror.md#E1
- `[narrative][negotiation][simulation]` Stall with escalating demands only while they outgrow his capacity and stay under his ceiling (The StoryTeller's Sapsorrow): d>g bought 70 days, d=g 30 (short of 40), d<g capped at 20 as the price hit 1133; largest-first bought 114.4 with no ceiling but escalating won 37.1 to 33.4 over an unseen one → projects/local__storyteller-sapsorrow-stall.md#E1
- `[narrative][endings][simulation]` A trickster who beats grudge-holding gatekeepers loses one exit per win (The StoryTeller's Soldier and Death: 2, 1, 0 exits after 0-2 wins); his last route, a sack handed to a soul who forgets at heaven's gate, fails for 1 or 250 holders and works only if the key fires by itself → projects/local__storyteller-soldier.md#E1
- `[narrative][persuasion][simulation]` Ask for small toppings to a pot already cooking, cheapest first (The StoryTeller's A Story Short, Stone Soup): stone plus cheap asks filled 12/12 where a full ask or no stone got 0, but only while each next item fit the willingness built so far; a ladder gap stalled at 2/12, other constants at 2 and 6, a meat above top willingness never came → projects/local__storyteller-stone-soup.md#E1
- `[pathfinding][navigation][simulation]` Ariadne's thread as a tether (The StoryTeller's Theseus and the Minotaur): wound in at dead ends it got out of all 56 mazes, at shortest cost only without loops; memory of 4 junctions overran 7 of 8 at 21x21; a cut traps only where a loop left a free-standing wall → projects/local__storyteller-theseus-thread.md#E1
- `[narrative][level-design][simulation]` One trap, two villains (The StoryTeller's The True Bride): a standing trap on the lured chase path took both with 1 setup and 0 added steps; one-shot needed 2 setups, off-path a 2-step detour; a chaser who saw the first fall escaped → projects/local__storyteller-true-bride-room.md#E1
- `[process][verification][design]` Enforcing a separate check with a digest of What + Rule + Prediction: it holds claim edits made after the check and ignores other edits, but anyone can compute it, so it proves freshness, not who wrote the check → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E208
- `[tooling][testing][parsing]` A steward rule keyed on `- Picked-by:` never fired because FIELD_RE admits no hyphen; the positive test passed vacuously and only the must-fire controls caught it (5/10 failed) — read such labels from raw text and always test the firing cases → projects/pappydapimp69__memory.md#E12
- `[process][tooling][brain]` `brain sync` pushes local kernels only from .brain/ideas/ itself; one saved in .brain/ideas/incoming/ (mirroring memory's layout) was skipped with no warning — file kernels in the ideas root and confirm the 'ideas N' count → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E209
- `[audio][testing][process]` Hunting spikes in a film score with long silences: ratio-to-preceding-second flagged every return from silence (3000x) while p99 thresholds plus joining loud 50 ms windows to scheduled marks named the culprit (a stamp effect at 2.5x p99) → projects/local__brain-films.md#E1
- `[audio][music][state]` A gliding score engine can't mute by zeroing levels (a ~2 s fade, then a near-silent first chord held for a bar); a separate mute path that releases pads fast and snaps levels back on resume as a key change cuts and restores cleanly → projects/local__brain-films.md#E2
- `[audio][film][testing]` Scene configs named music modes ('phrygian','locrian') missing from the engine's tables: the offline export threw at once while the timer-driven live player just dropped notes silently — check every config key against the tables at build time → projects/local__brain-films.md#E3
- `[narrative][explainer][process]` An allegory film's voiceover that retold the on-screen tale was rejected as too literal; keep the tale in the picture and put the mapping to the real system (what each prop is) in the voice, with cutaways for the narrator's premise checks → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E28
- `[film][captions][timing]` A caption window sized to reading time still ran 7 of 20 captions short by exactly its two 0.3 s fades (sweep at opacity above 0.9); add both fades to the window and measure visible time, not window length → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E29
- `[artifact][testing][browser]` Screenshotting an artifact page from file:// kept a hidden splash in all 24 stills because the host skeleton's [hidden] reset is absent locally; remove overlays (or mirror the reset) in the harness before reading the sheet → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E30
- `[film][animation][audio][timing]` A per-segment film-to-story time map stretched a hard-coded 2:56 film to 4:03 and parked the story during three cutaways with no tale literal changed; convert story-anchored events once with the inverse map (skip the flash-forward), and never derive the warp from caption reading time alone → projects/pappydapimp69__zanegpt.md#E31
- `[game-languages][performance][gc]` GC in a 60 fps loop (Unity): full collection spiked 6 frames to 26 ms, incremental with idle time kept all under 16.7 ms, but heavy reference churn fell back to full and a loop with no idle time went over every frame on barrier cost; pooling the hot path removed collections → projects/local__game-languages-gc.md#E1
- `[game-languages][gdscript][performance]` Typed GDScript gets optimized opcodes only where types are known: in a toy interpreter one untyped value at the head of a 10-step dependent chain made all 10 ops generic (0% of the typed gain kept) while at the tail or among independent statements it cost one op (90% kept) — type chain heads first → projects/local__game-languages-gdscript.md#E1
- `[gamedesign][hallucination]` When hallucinations fake specific objects, set dressing must never share their silhouette (no twig litter beside phantom sticks, no real moon beside a wrong one) → projects/pappydapimp69__sevin.md#E18
- `[threejs][rendering]` A sky dome behind fog must copy the final fog colour into its horizon every frame and include the tonemapping/colorspace chunks, or fogged trees seam against the sky → projects/pappydapimp69__sevin.md#E19
- `[threejs][rendering]` A DataTexture filled from 0-255 colour bytes renders washed out until colorSpace = SRGBColorSpace; fixing that (or adding tone mapping) darkens linear albedo, so re-tune every light, night ambient most → projects/pappydapimp69__sevin.md#E20
- `[gamedev][state][testing]` A dt countdown ends just below zero (-0.0167), so `!timer` reads "still running" forever after its first use; compare with `<= 0` or clamp at expiry → projects/pappydapimp69__sweety.md#E16
- `[gamedev][level-design][coop][testing]` A hazard corridor is a gate only if no gap between hazards fits a body (spacing under 2 kill radii plus a body width); a wait-in-the-gap hopper got through 20/24 where straight-line runners all died → projects/pappydapimp69__sweety.md#E17
- `[gamedev][level-design][coop][physics]` Size a gap the partner's help must bridge from MEASURED reaches (solo 17.5, early help 20.6, perfect help 25.9 tiles); helper timing moved reach 25%, so sit between solo and worst-timed help → projects/pappydapimp69__sweety.md#E18
- `[continued-fractions][calendar][testing][sandbox]` Leap rules are rational approximations of the year fraction 0.2421897: its convergents 1/4, 7/29, 8/33, 31/128 are each the best fraction at their size, and the Gregorian 97/400 is not one (drift 3,223 years against 4,264 for 8/33) → projects/local__calendar-leap-rules-are-continued-fraction-convergents.md#E1
- `[comedy][humor][testing][sandbox]` A safe cue that habituates still licenses going big down to 0.6 of its strength per beat (flat wins by only 0.06) and fails by 0.3 (mild wins); restating it every second beat widens the flat margin to 0.80 → projects/local__decaying-safe-cue-flips-go-big-back-to-mild.md#E1
- `[game-ads][testing][model][sandbox]` A fidelity check column on recuts is redundant under IPM times D1 (clickbait won 2 of 40) but needed under IPM alone (25 of 40 to 0), and it must test a derived-from-game link, since a bare new-noun check blocked the legitimate recut → projects/local__fidelity-check-column-guards-ipm-d1-recut-rank.md#E1
- `[comedy][humor][testing][sandbox]` A severity cap keeps the distance dial forgiving only if it lands before escalation leaves the mild zone: 9, 9, 9, 7 of 11 distances within 95 percent for caps arriving at beats 0 to 3, then 4, 2, 1 for beats 4 to 6 → projects/local__late-severity-cap-leaves-the-distance-dial-sharp.md#E1
- `[comedy][humor][testing][sandbox]` Matched distance beats any decaying safe cue at fixed framing (flat 5.11 against 3.58, 1.55, 0.97 as the cue fades); a cue only earns its keep on leftover mismatch (2.70 to 4.39 when matching was 0.2 off) → projects/local__matched-distance-beats-any-decaying-cue-at-fixed-framing.md#E1
- `[comedy][humor][testing][sandbox]` A rule card's benign cover substitutes for distance at both ends in a toy escalation: it flips fixed-close d 0.3 from mild to go-big and lifts a mild schedule at far d 0.9 from 0.03 to 0.51 of ungated, but is inert under severity-matched framing → projects/local__rule-card-cover-needs-closeness-floor-for-mild-beats.md#E1
- `[game-ads][testing][sandbox][process]` A stale hand-typed label sidecar drops the true best variant from an IPM-times-D1 ranking with certainty (0 of 40 picks, any budget) while no error fires; compare tracked and ranked counts, since budget only helps the close-race noise (10, 18, 22 of 40 at 100, 2000, 20000) → projects/local__sidecar-drift-silently-drops-ipm-d1-contender.md#E1
- `[tessellation][geometry][testing][sandbox]` Angle sums over regular polygons give 17 multisets and 21 vertex types but only 11 tile; an equal-neighbours test on odd polygons wrongly kept 8, while a closure walk round each odd polygon found exactly the 11 → projects/local__tessellation-vertex-enumeration-then-odd-neighbour-filter.md#E1
- `[algorithm][testing][puzzles]` Filling an open 5x5 word grid, fewest-matches-first slot order needed 5x to over 50x fewer search nodes than reading order as the list grew from 45 to 495 words, so choose the most constrained slot and cap the node budget → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E414
- `[comedy][humor][sandbox]` With severity-matched distance a safe cue adds nothing (0.0000), but under fixed far framing mild beats collapse to 0.05 and a decaying cue lifts them 11.5x yet stays far below going big, so match distance first and keep mild beats close → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E415
- `[algorithm][testing][ergonomics]` Over a prose and a code text, home-row share ranked an alphabetical layout above QWERTY (0.467 vs 0.303) while same-finger bigram rate ranked it last (0.123 vs 0.040), so score a keyboard layout on both numbers from your own text → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E416
- `[ai-video][compliance][tiktok-shop]` A reference image lowers product drift (180 to 14 alterations over 60 seeds with 9 refs) but never to zero and never makes an AI product shot compliant; gate on the real/AI source of each product shot, not on visible drift → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E417
- `[game-ads][testing][sandbox]` A drifted warp-map sidecar before cap-then-rank on IPM times D1 changed the pick only in a narrow slope band (0.003 to 0.01) and not at high slopes, so its silence proves nothing; derive the map from source or diff it every run → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E418
- `[game-ads][film][sandbox]` A silent first second on only the delivery-matched recut moves the IPM times D1 pick off 0.5 (to 0.6 or 0.4) in 2 of 6 scenarios, so gate every recut from its current audio rather than a remembered list → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E419
- `[songwriting][rhyme][sandbox]` Against 3752 designed four-line stanzas a one-pass rhyme labeller mislabelled 724 of 1552 XXAA stanzas while two-pass (uppercase resolved, lowercase assonance) matched every design, though lowercase marks 284 filler pairs sharing a vowel → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E420
- `[coin-weighing][search][testing][sandbox]` Exhaustive search confirms an odd coin of unknown direction needs (3^k - 3)/2 coins at most (12 in 3 weighings, 13 impossible) and one spare genuine coin buys exactly one more, so budget the reference coin first → projects/local__counterfeit-coin-weighings-need-a-spare-genuine-coin-for-the-last-coin.md#E1
- `[game-ads][testing][model][sandbox]` A derived-from-game fidelity link beats a drifting hand sidecar for IPM times D1 ranking only while it misreads under roughly 20 to 50 percent of variants (100% vs 67% exact at 0 noise, level at 20%, 36% vs 66% at 50%), and a new-noun flag stays at chance, so check the link on a few known variants first → projects/local__derived-link-beats-sidecar-and-noun-check-until-link-noise-passes-a-threshold.md#E1
- `[comedy][humor][testing][sandbox]` A gag rule card pays off in proportion to the severe beats it covers (flat beats mild from about 40% coverage, 2.56 vs 1.61 at 50%), and a safe cue on the uncovered beats makes go-big win even at zero coverage (3.58 vs 1.67) → projects/local__incomplete-rule-card-keeps-go-big-only-with-a-safe-cue-backstop.md#E1
- `[comedy][humor][testing][sandbox]` The late severity-cap deadline for keeping the distance dial forgiving is about the schedule midpoint (beat 3 of 6, 4 of 9, 6 of 12) and did not move with the mild split, so state the cap before the escalation is half done → projects/local__late-severity-cap-deadline-is-the-schedule-midpoint-not-the-mild-split.md#E1
- `[comedy][humor][testing][sandbox]` Matched distance stops beating a permanent safe cue once the severity estimate is off by about 0.2 (flat 2.70 vs 3.58) or lags a ramp (2.85 to 1.89 at one beat), so default to match plus cue, not match alone → projects/local__matched-distance-loses-to-a-permanent-cue-at-small-severity-error.md#E1
- `[origami][geometry][testing][sandbox]` Mountain-minus-valley equal to two is necessary but not sufficient for a flat-foldable vertex: only 4 of 8 count-satisfying assignments fold at the unequal 30, 60, 150, 120 vertex (8 of 8 at the square one), so test layer order on each candidate → projects/local__origami-vertex-maekawa-count-is-necessary-not-sufficient-to-fold-flat.md#E1
- `[comedy][humor][testing][sandbox]` Restating a decaying safe cue every second beat keeps go-big ahead in 9 of 9 cue-strength and distance cells at a 20% restatement cost; only a weak starting cue (0.4) flips, at a 50% cost → projects/local__restating-the-safe-cue-survives-a-costly-restatement-unless-the-cue-is-weak.md#E1
- `[game-ads][testing][model][sandbox]` Hand-kept fidelity sidecars cost nothing on reskin-only batches but a third of the score on engine-varied ones (64% exact at 25% drift, 41% at 50%), so derive the column whenever the engines differ → projects/local__sidecar-drift-costs-nothing-on-reskins-and-most-on-engine-varied-batches.md#E1
- `[comedy][humor][model][sandbox]` A second benign-cover source (safe cue or rule card) added 53% of the first source's go-big gain, not under half as predicted, and saturation only appeared at total cover 1.0 (far framing: 3.60 then 3.60), so plan two sources before a third → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E421
- `[comedy][humor][model][sandbox]` A half-covering rule card alone left the distance dial as fragile as no card (2 of 11 distances within 95% of best); a full card gave 8 and half plus a safe cue 6, and flattening costs peak score (6.00 to 3.00) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E422
- `[comedy][humor][model][sandbox]` Setting distance per beat by harm level (severe far, mild close) beat the best single global distance by the same 0.36 at 0%, 50% and 100% rule-card coverage, with or without a cue, and by 0.00 when mild beats reward no closeness → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E423
- `[comedy][humor][model][sandbox]` A per-beat distance split (severe far, mild close) is not flipped by a habituating safe cue, as go-big is: its gain over the best global distance fell from +0.240 to +0.040 at decay 0.2, and set to 0.8 without the cue it held +0.420 at decay 0.5 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E424
- `[comedy][humor][model][sandbox]` A fixed rule-card ceiling stops a go-big schedule collapsing under a habituating cue (0.29 to 0.43 at decay 0.8) but flattens it, so flat or mild still wins in all 6 cells; after the card pick flat at the ceiling → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E425
- `[plumbing][drainage][testing][sandbox]` Compute drain fall from the per-diameter minimum slope, not a quarter inch per foot everywhere: the uniform rule rejected 5 of 7 runs, all 3 in or larger, that the code minimum accepts, while pipes up to 2.5 in agreed → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E426
- `[game-ads][comedy][model][sandbox]` Ranking recuts on IPM times D1 lets one comic engine take 2+ of the top 3 in 14% of batches, 28% when engine strength drives both rates, so cap one per engine after ranking at about 2% value cost (6% on a skewed batch) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E427
- `[roofing][estimating][testing][sandbox]` Order roofing from footprint times the slope factor, then add waste: footprint plus 10 percent was short at all 7 pitches (1 bundle at 2/12, 7 at 6/12, 25 at 12/12 on 1800 sq ft), since waste never absorbs the slope → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E428
- `[verification][model][sandbox]` A-series paper is generated exactly by floor-halving 841 x 1189 mm (9 of 9 ISO sizes, 141.4 and 70.7 percent steps) while US Letter needs two folds to repeat its shape (19.4 percent drift after one) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E429
- `[comedy][humor][testing][sandbox]` the severity-cap deadline is beat 3, 4, 6, 9 of 6, 9, 12, 18 at a 7-of-11 bar but moves to 8 or 4 at n 12 for a loose or strict bar, so it is set by how forgiving the dial must be, not by the escalation → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E430
- `[comedy][humor][testing][sandbox]` on an escalating schedule cover the severe beats first (up to 1.30 points better than mild first, 0.39 with a cue of 0.7), and a safe cue of about 0.5 lets go-big win at zero coverage (2.56 vs 1.61) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E431
- `[game-ads][testing][model][sandbox]` a per-engine cap on a top-4 ad batch beat no cap (4.10 vs 3.65) under partial label drift and failed only when stale labels shared one unknown bucket (1.40), so give missing entries unique labels → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E432
- `[ai-video][compliance][disclosure][sandbox][model]` a reference image never makes an AI product shot safe under a source rule, and under a deviation rule only a near-perfect reference or an animated shot of two hops or fewer (about 15 s) passes; shoot the real product → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E433
- `[comedy][humor][testing][sandbox]` the late severity cap does not make the dial forgiving under a peaked matching-distance gate (1 to 3 of 11 distances near best vs 9 of 11 under a one-sided cover), so with a matched-distance gate set the distance to the cap level → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E434
- `[research][testing][model][sandbox]` giving each source one owner cuts the multi-root double count (8 to 5) but still ranks 3 aggregators over 2 roots above 4 independent sources (5 vs 4), so rank corroboration on distinct roots and credit joined sources to all roots → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E435
- `[accessibility][checksum][testing][sandbox]` An alt-attribute presence check flagged only missing alts (20/20) and a non-empty check falsely flagged all 8 correct decorative empties, while a subject-word check missed shared-word wrong content (0/12), so combine checks and sample by hand → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E210
- `[film][narration][timing][sandbox]` Absorbing a 2 s reaction pause into a caption window warps it to (w+2)/w, so windows under 4 s break the 1.5 cap (2 of 8, worst 1.80); hold instead unless the measured D1 slope is under about 0.01 → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E211
- `[game-ads][testing][model][sandbox]` The derived-link gate cut distinct comic engines to 4.7 of 8 but only by halving the pool (flat control 4.9) and did not raise top-3 engine repeats (26.2% vs 24.9%), so widen the recut batch before gating instead of adding engine quotas → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E212
- `[seismology][statistics][testing][sandbox]` The largest magnitude bin holds the biggest share of a region's quake energy while the frequency-law b is under 1.5 and a majority only near b 1.2 or lower (68.4% at b 1.0), so counting small quakes says little about released energy → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E213
- `[ladder-safety][statics][testing][sandbox]` The 4-to-1 ladder angle (76.0 degrees) needs floor friction of about 0.23 with a person at the top, so it holds at 0.25 and slips at 0.2; secure the foot on slick floors instead of trusting the angle → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E214
- `[comedy][humor][testing][sandbox]` A restated safe cue kept the go-big peak (3.06 vs 3.20) but widened its good-distance window only from 1 to 2 of 11 against 9 for a severity cap, so cap to forgive a wrong distance and cue to keep the peak at the right one → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E215
- `[error-correction][signals][testing][sandbox]` An overall parity bit paid only when the detected state got its own cheap reading (0.06 vs SEC 0.43 at p 0.02); read as fail it cost 1.82, so design the detected-state response before adding the bit → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E216
- `[comedy][humor][testing][sandbox]` A severity cap on a go-big ramp paid only with no card and no cue (+0.47 if started by beat 3, +0.16 at beat 6); with any card coverage or cue it lost score at every start beat, so the cap-by-midpoint rule is for an unsupported distance dial only → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E217
- `[parachute][physics][numerics][sandbox]` Under quadratic drag doubling canopy area from 25 to 50 to 100 square metres cut an 80 kg load's steady speed by 1.414 each time (5.845, 4.133, 2.923 m/s), not 2: halving descent speed needs four times the area → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E436
- `[film][narration][timing]` Pauses derived before the 1.5 caption-warp cap missed 8 of 8 capped caption ends when raw warp was 2.4/1.8 and 1.9/2.4, derived after the cap 0 of 8, and with raw warp at or under 1.5 order did not matter: cap first, then derive → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E437
- `[film][narration][timing]` A reaction-cut pause list stored beside the captions missed 8 of 8 caption ends after a 1.2-1.5 warp while pauses recomputed from the warped captions missed 0, and with no warp both agreed: derive pauses at build time → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E438
- `[film][captions][timing][sandbox]` At 5 words per 3 s a hook caption of 8 or more words needs warp over the 1.5 cap (7 words is the ceiling) and a pause fixed at 3 s lands 0.6 s late at 6 words and 3.0 s late at 10: trim words first, pause at the caption end → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E439
- `[error-correction][testing][checksum][sandbox]` Hamming(7,4) miscorrected 336 of 336 double errors; one overall parity bit flagged 448 of 448 doubles but still miscorrected 896 of 896 triples, so an added check moves the blind spot out one error weight: name the first weight it still miscorrects → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E440
- `[electronics][numerics][simulation][sandbox]` Explicit Euler on an RC discharge holds magnitude 1.0 at dt = 2*RC and grows to 5.06 at 2.5*RC over 10 time constants while backward Euler at 2.5*RC ends at 6.7e-03: keep the explicit step under 2*RC and far smaller for accuracy, or use the exact exponential → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E441
- `[audio][film][testing][sandbox]` Scanning a rendered score against the note schedule found 10 windows lost to an unknown mode and 35 to a never-released voice cap with 0 false alarms, while a config-key check saw only the mode and a median-relative silence scan reported 0 for the cap loss: compare to the schedule → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E442
- `[error-correction][reliability][checksum][sandbox]` Digit sum mod 10 plus a weighted sum mod 11 gave a joint miss 0.38 times the product of the single rates, but digit sum mod 10 plus mod 5 gave 4.76 times with no gain over mod 10 alone: make the second check unlike the first and enumerate the joint rate → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E443
- `[pitch][adoption][funnel][probability]` a better tool with five setup steps adopted 366 vs 535 for a one-command rival, but 721 vs 600 once the audience felt the problem first; multiply step pass rates only for independent gates → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E444
- `[film][tooling][game-ads][model]` derive reaction-pause times from the current captions since a one-time sidecar split 3 of 4 lines after a retime; the right recut won IPM times D1 13 of 40 at 100 impressions, 35 at 1000 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E445
- `[error-correction][compression][checksum]` apply SECDED after run-length compression (20 bytes vs 144 the other way); a sum check missed 13 of 80 single flips where the code fixed all 160, and same-nibble triples were wrong 56 of 56 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E446
- `[rate-limiting][testing][timing]` a fixed-window limit of 10 per 10 s admitted 20 across the boundary where a sliding log admitted 10 and a full token bucket admitted 19 of a steady 20; replay boundary bursts → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E447
- `[interval-arithmetic][testing][state]` an inclusive-end overlap test flagged 7 false conflicts on a back-to-back day where half-open flagged 0, both caught all 7 real overlaps; a zero-length marker at a meeting start is ignored by half-open → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E448
