- `[debugging][isolation]` ddmin minimal repro finds the full cause set for a conjunction but returns only one of two independent causes, so delete the culprit and re-run, and re-run the reduced repro as a positive control → projects/local__ddmin-minimal-repro-shrinks-single-cause-but-stalls-on-jointly-sufficient-and-needs-positive-control.md#E1
- `[ads][batching]` engine-first versus topic-first moved ad batch similarity by only 0.0053 while rotating 3 kit assets moved it by 0.17, so fix the kit before the engine mix → projects/local__engine-first-assignment-barely-moves-ad-batch-similarity-the-fixed-kit-dominates-and-a-mislabelled-axis-rides-along.md#E1
- `[simulation][gate]` the zero invalid presses at multiples of the period need exact periodicity; a Fibonacci-word signal never reached 0 (lowest 3 at lag 89) and a nominal P=13 formula missed 103 of 120 lags → projects/local__lag-mod-period-zero-at-multiples-holds-only-for-periodic-legality-fibonacci-word-has-no-zero-lags.md#E1
- `[caching][invalidation]` writes left the loop-sized cache at size 5 but late fills served 4 to 6 stale reads once writes outpaced fill latency, and a bigger cache did not help, so version the fills → projects/local__loop-sized-cache-with-writes-stays-size-5-but-late-fills-serve-stale-until-fills-are-versioned.md#E1
- `[slide-rule][logarithms]` a 1000-division log scale has a flat 0.23 percent relative error, three correct digits near 1 but only 31-59 percent of products starting 5-9, so quote relative error and track the decade by hand → projects/local__slide-rule-relative-error-is-flat-across-the-scale-so-three-digits-holds-above-3-and-fails-near-1-and-the-decade-must-be-tracked-by-hand.md#E1
- `[simulation][gate]` cumulative symmetric mismatch over a cutoff is not twice the gate-only total even at whole periods (245 vs 280 on period 12 width 5), so count it directly or bound it by the sum of lags → projects/local__symmetric-mismatch-cumulative-over-cutoff-is-exactly-twice-gate-count-at-whole-periods-and-drifts-within-lag-sum-on-a-tail.md#E1
- `[songwriting][rhyme]` two-pass rhyme letters matched perfect groups in all 1296 four-line stanzas (one pass merged 64.4%) but hid an assonance link to a resolved group in 24.1%, so list leftover assonant lines separately → projects/local__two-pass-rhyme-letters-never-merge-perfect-groups-but-hide-assonance-to-a-resolved-group-exhaustive-4-line-stanzas.md#E1
- `[performance][caching][invalidation]` A one-slot-short loop cache recovers hits once entries are invalidated (LRU4 12-39, FIFO4 ~50 of 100 vs 0), so size for the loop plus invalidation holes → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E466
- `[performance][caching][gc]` In a toy generational GC, invalidation raised exact-fit loop-cache cost (60 to 270 work) but cut the one-short cache (2490 to 544), so measure GC with invalidation included → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E467
- `[songwriting][rhyme][testing]` One-pass rhyme lettering changed the rhyme shape in 69% and the stable-versus-open reading in 35% of 1296 toy stanzas, so drive emotional-shape advice from perfect-rhyme letters → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E468
- `[performance][caching][gc]` With invalidation every 10 accesses 8 of 62 sizes earn hits on a 60-loop (up from 2) yet exact fit stays the cheapest under the toy GC (60 to 270 work), so keep sizing to the loop → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E469
- `[locksmithing][combinatorics][security]` Each master-keyed chamber doubles the keys that open a pin-tumbler lock (1, 4, 32 openers for 0, 2, 5 master chambers of 7776), so add master pins only where the hierarchy needs them → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E470
- `[morse-code][coding]` Morse is not prefix-free (56 letter-code prefix pairs; SOS has 192 gap-free parses, PARIS 5304), so keep the letter gap as a symbol when storing or sending it → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E471
- `[ladder-safety][statics][testing]` The 4-to-1 ladder rule is a friction demand of 17/72 (0.236) at the top rung for any length, rising to 0.246 for heavy climbers; grip 0.20 slips from 84 percent of the way up and needs a 1-to-4.72 lean, so size the lean from grip not the ratio → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E472
- `[songwriting][rhyme][tooling]` Hyphenated pacing syllables (beau-ti-ful) fooled a last-fragment rhyme reader into a false rhyme with pull in 1 of 4 stanzas (xAxA to AAxA); rejoining hyphens before lookup matched plain lettering in 4 of 4 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E473
- `[songwriting][rhyme][testing]` Two-pass rhyme letters (resolved first, then assonance) stopped a stray assonance steal in 856 of 4096 stanzas where one-pass failed, but folded truck/mud/blood/flood into AAAA; near-rhymes need their own letter and keys must be pronunciation, not spelling (spelled gave AABB) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E474
- `[caching][performance][testing]` A cache with write invalidation and delayed fills served 10 stale reads under both LRU and FIFO until a version guard cut them to 0 (hits 86 apparent, 76 real); even a fill landing on the next event raced, only an atomic install avoided it → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E475
- `[caching][performance][gc]` Write invalidation of the item just read cut a loop-sized cache's GC crossover from 985 to 493 per slot (W=2) and moved the best size from the loop length 5 to 4 and 3, refuting sizes 1 to 4 never best; re-sweep size and crossover under the real write pattern → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E476
- `[performance][caching][gc]` In a toy generational GC with an invalidating cache, throughput peaked at an interior young size (Y=200 at T=500) while max minor pause rose 30 to 100, entries invalidated before the next minor GC removed the pause cost, and overflow came only at Y=700; sweep young size with cache lifetime and heap limit together → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E477
- `[performance][caching][gc]` allocate-before-check on late versioned fills moved the size-5 versus no-cache break-even pause in 9 of 35 write cells (up to 1.5x) and never without writes; partial caches won in 0 of 45 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E478
- `[billiards][geometry][sandbox]` mirror-unfold a bank shot on the ball-centre inset box (nominal table missed by 4.85) and check the planned cushion order, which held in only 750 of 840 one-cushion plans → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E479
- `[rpg][combat][balance]` a percent-HP damage cap fraction needs a computed window: burst niche floor 0.40 to two-hit-kill ceiling 0.50 left only caps 0.45 to 0.49 contested, so check both edges → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E480
- `[documentation][doctest]` executable doc examples catch API drift only on the parameters they exercise; prose reference and untouched defaults rot silently → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E481
- `[songwriting][rhyme][sandbox]` front-cut a rushed line by syllables but never below the end word's stressed tail: the naive cut kept all 2401 stanzas at budget 2 and lost letters only at budget 1 (513 of 2401 kept) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E482
- `[hanoi][puzzles][algorithm]` 2^n - 1 is the Hanoi optimum for three pegs only: BFS gave 255 at 8 discs on three pegs but 25 at 7 discs on four pegs (formula says 127), so key move counts by peg count → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E483
- `[performance][caching][invalidation]` version late cache fills and check before allocating: 0 stale reads in 48 of 48 write cells, and allocate-first added GC work in 13 of 48 (180 vs 140 at worst), none without writes → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E484
- `[meteorology][humidity][simulation][method]` The Td = T - (100-RH)/5 dew-point shortcut is within 1 C only from 79% humidity up (1.97 C off at 50%, 18 C at 10%); guard it by humidity, else use Magnus → projects/local__dew-point-rule-of-five-holds-above-half-humidity-and-fails-below.md#E1
- `[caching][invalidation][simulation][tradeoff]` Versioned fills fix stale reads (0 of 36 cells vs 36) but dropped fills cost more than 2% extra in 32 of 36 random-write cells, up to 1.35x; measure the dropped-fill price → projects/local__dropped-fills-cost-of-versioned-install-on-random-writes.md#E1
- `[electronics][standards][simulation][method]` Resistor tolerance bands tile a decade only for E6; E12 leaves 1.2%, E24 4.1% and E96 16.9% of targets outside tolerance of any stocked value, so trim with a second resistor → projects/local__e-series-tolerance-bands-leave-gaps-from-e12-up-only-e6-tiles.md#E1
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][method]` Hyphenating end words is safe for two-pass rhyme lettering only if the judge keys the whole word: a last-segment judge mislettered 27.3% of 6561 stanzas → projects/local__hyphenating-end-words-breaks-two-pass-letters-only-under-a-last-segment-judge.md#E1
- `[songwriting][syllables][simulation][method]` True-break hyphens make a vowel-group syllable counter exact (30 of 30) and re-arm the budget warning (36 of 36 vs 0) but misplaced hyphens leave 12 of 30 wrong; the check is circular → projects/local__hyphenating-hiatus-words-heals-a-vowel-group-syllable-counter.md#E1
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][method]` Hyphenating end words makes a spelling rhyme judge much worse (wrong in 26.3% of stanzas plain, 58.4% hyphenated, repairing none); strip hyphens before judging → projects/local__hyphenation-shortens-the-text-a-spelling-rhyme-judge-reads.md#E1
- `[numeral-systems][canonical-form][simulation][method]` Re-encode-and-compare is exact on a soroban (1000 of 1000 states) but on a suanpan keeps 1000 of 5832 states and leaves 666 values with no canonical spelling; compare rods by value → projects/local__re-encode-and-compare-on-abacus-rods-finds-the-canonical-states-and-the-uncanonicalizable-values.md#E1
- `[seafaring][wind-scale][testing]` Inverting the Beaufort formula matched the published band table on 67 of 71 integer knots, every miss one band off; use the table for exact labels → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E485
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][method]` A last-segment judge on hyphenated end words mislettered 55% of toy stanzas but flipped the stable/open shape verdict in only 9%, always toward false closure; strip hyphens and distrust a stable verdict otherwise → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E486
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][method]` A last-segment judge on hyphenated end words changed the two-pass hidden-assonance list in 39% of toy stanzas (all losses), 432 with unchanged letter case; strip hyphens and diff the list, not only the letters → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E487
- `[astronautics][rocket-equation][testing]` A numeric rocket burn matched the Tsiolkovsky equation within 5 m/s and a vertical burn lost g times burn time (196 m/s at 20 s), so size propellant from delta-v plus gravity and drag loss → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E488
- `[documentation][survey]` A multi-select survey lead (docs 84% vs Stack Overflow 80%) is reach only; modelled time share can favour the runner-up, so do not read it as where learning happens → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E489
- `[documentation][doctest][testing][structure]` Gate doctest attempted above zero per diataxis quadrant, exempting explanation: the flat per-file gate false-flagged 2 of 4 flagged pages, the quadrant-aware gate caught the 2 real defects → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E490
- `[ballistics][physics][simulation][sandbox]` Quadratic drag drops the best launch angle below 45 degrees (41.0 at k 0.01 and 30 m/s, 36.5 at 60 m/s) and vacuum range overshoots 1.6x to 3.1x, so integrate and search the angle → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E491
- `[bookbinding][geometry][model][testing]` Facing-page sums are N+1 only per signature (three 8-page signatures give 9, 25, 41 globally), so impose each with its page offset; greedy sizing is minimal only for divisibility-chain sizes → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E492
- `[songwriting][syllables][rhyme][simulation][method]` Naive vowel-group hyphenation fixed 5 and broke 5 of 28 counter results (net zero, silent-e words worse); use lexicon breaks, and a whole-word lettering judge kept 168/168 stanzas against 126/168 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E493
- `[electronics][standards][simulation][method]` One same-series series or parallel partner closed all 438 single-value tolerance gaps in E12, E24 and E96 over 10 to 100 ohm, even at a quarter of tolerance → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E494
- `[photovoltaics][electrical][simulation][sandbox]` Size PV strings by cold Voc and hot Vmp, not the STC label: 14 modules read 652 V at minus 20 C against a 600 V limit; the safe window was 9 to 12 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E495
- `[songwriting][rhyme][suno-prompting][testing]` Trigger the hyphenate-to-slow hint on true syllables (recall 1.00 vs 0.61 for slack-3 vowel groups) and rejoin end words before lettering: whole-word judge kept 2000/2000 stanzas, last-segment 1302/2000 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E496
- `[documentation][doctest][testing][api-docs]` Run examples caught 2 of 3 API drifts and lifted developer copy success from 3/6 to 5/6; the drifted default with no example still misled, so give every documented default its own example → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E497
- `[songwriting][rhyme][testing]` Max closure distance separates AABB 1, AAAA 1, ABAB 2, ABBA 3 but open-rhyme count collides for 14 of 15 four-line schemes (10 after oldest-wait), so keep the per-line closure trace → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E498
- `[testing][metrics][mocking]` Two assertion-free or not-None suites hit 100 percent line coverage and killed 0 of 7 mutants, exact-value asserts killed 6 of 7, and a mock asserting an internal call broke on a safe refactor: read coverage as a gap map → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E499
- `[songwriting][rhyme][lyrics][testing]` Split hyphens before ad-lib exclusion and rejoin them before keying rhymes: a last-segment key lost 1 of 2 rhyme pairs and an unsplit ad-lib check kept oh-oh-oh as a line (gaps 1,1 became 2,1) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E500
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][testing]` Hyphenating just one end word under a last-segment judge flipped 360 of 1024 toy shape verdicts (stable AABB read open); the whole-word judge flipped 0 of 4096, so rejoin before scoring shape → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E501
- `[empathy][helping][model][testing]` Lab per-person rates 0.85/0.62/0.31 still give a 0.85/0.95/0.89 chance that someone acts, and the group chance only falls once per-person willingness drops faster than n^-0.7, so an individual bystander effect does not imply crowd inaction → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E502
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][testing]` A last-segment judge on hyphenated end words with shared suffix tails made false rhyme groups in 4736 of 20736 toy stanzas and lost none, the reverse of the unique-tail result; the error direction depends on the tails, so rejoin first → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E503
- `[numerics][model][sandbox][lab-dilution][fastener-torque]` A nominal-constant optimum hides a tail: 3 dilution steps (best at a=0.5) had 6.00% worst error over a 0.05 to 2.0 against 3.76% for 7 steps, and the K=0.20 torque hit 167% of proof at K=0.12, so carry the constant or pick by worst case → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E504
- `[distribution][platform-etiquette][sandbox][model]` If give actions earn reach g, the standalone-post flip point drops from (R+2)/2 to (R+2)/2 - R*g/Y (5.5 to 3.7 at g=0.2, R=9; capped gives only to 4.9), so measure g before declaring the ratio a price → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E505
- `[songwriting][rhyme][lyrics][testing]` Hyphen-join and ad-lib-exclusion faults did not stack: with both unfixed only 1 of 3 stanzas gave a wrong rhyme gap (single fault 1 of 6), each bites only when placed on the rhyme path, so give each fault its own fixture → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E506
- `[testing][mocking][model][method]` Exact-call mocks broke on 1 of 3 safe refactors (only the one changing the collaborator call shape) and stayed green under collaborator drift where real-repo behaviour tests went red 3 of 3, so pair each mock with a contract test → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E507
- `[songwriting][lyrics][rhyme][testing]` A hyphen-rejoin cleans rhyme lettering (4/4 pairs vs 2/4 by last fragment) but loses 0/4 stutter titles, split finds them but loses 0/4 timing titles, so give each check its own view and match titles on either → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E508
- `[baking][formula][model][sandbox]` Dough-only hydration misstates total hydration by p*(Hl-Hd): a 100% levain at 10/20/30% of flour moves 75% to 77.5/80.0/82.5%, a stiff 50% levain drops it to 70.0%, so quote total hydration and solve the dough water → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E509
- `[teaching][feedback][model][testing]` In a spacing model, self-focused feedback cut delayed retention at all 8 gaps and also moved the best gap (task-focused 8 at 0.3945, self-focused 4 at 0.0355), so pick spacing and feedback style together, not as independent scale factors → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E510
- `[tire-pressure][physics][model][sandbox]` Tire temperature drop scales with absolute pressure: 70F to 20F is 3.28/4.69/8.94 psi at 20/35/80 psi gauge, a gauge-ratio shortcut runs 42/30/16% low and the 1 psi per 10F rule is +7% at 35 but -44% at 80 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E511
- `[curling][sandbox][scoring]` With 8 stones per team and all rank orders equally likely, a curling end scores 2 or more for a given team 7/30 of the time (23.3%), 1 or more 1/2, mean 8/9 points; use 7/30 as the luck baseline for 2-plus ends → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E512
- `[darts][sandbox][checkout]` Double-out darts: 21 one-dart and 162 of 169 three-dart finishes (impossible: 159, 162, 163, 165, 166, 168, 169); one trusted double (D16) still finishes 112, not under 90 as predicted, so search reachability instead of assuming a collapse → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E513
- `[lab-dilution][curing][numerics][model]` A 25% hidden dose-base error left 26-27% total error under 1- or 2-step dilution while step choice moved it only 1 point, so scale the spec by assumed/actual base first and pick steps only at ~1.5% tolerance → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E514
- `[songwriting][rhyme][testing]` A last-segment rhyme judge broke 0/8855 stanzas with boundary hyphens (to-night) but 56.3% with hyphens inside the rime (fi-re, lo-ve), mostly in the perfect pass, so the hyphen position is the risk and rejoining is the safe default → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E515
- `[songwriting][rhyme][lyrics][testing]` For reduced line ends, letter rhymes on the rejoined sung form: written full forms (to, of) lettered 73.5% of 495 stanzas wrong and a last-segment hyphen read 93.9%, against 19.2% for gonna-style sung forms (wanna spelled -anna still misses) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E516
- `[songwriting][rhyme][lyrics][testing]` Blanket-rejoining hyphens before rhyme lettering broke stutter end words (0/96 right, run-run-run no longer rhymes sun) while last-fragment broke timing hyphens (48/192); collapse identical-fragment stutters, then rejoin → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E517
- `[cheesemaking][sandbox][numerics][model]` In a Van Slyke-style yield forecast one point of moisture is worth 0.10 milk-fat points for cheddar (1.61% yield) but 4.17% yield at 75% moisture, so measure fat and casein first for hard cheese, moisture first for fresh → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E518
- `[gamedev][determinism][testing][sandbox]` Counter-keyed rolls flipped 0 of 200 outcomes under extra consumers and reversed order versus 48.5% for a shared stream, but an omitted purpose key made hit and crit joint 49.0% not 29.0% → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E519
- `[numerics][sandbox][model][tolerance]` Hidden-parameter error budgets were solder 2.8%, bolt nut factor 6.2% and cure base 28.0%, so verify the tightest first and recompute when a knob moves the margin (solder 15.7% at 400 C) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E520
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][method]` A last-segment rhyme judge broke 0 of 6561 stanzas with prefix hyphens (re-turn) but 54.9% with suffix tails (burn-ing), so rejoin hyphenated end words before two-pass lettering → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E521
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][method]` Reduced forms (gotta, lotta) flipped two-pass rhyme letters in 84.6% of 4096 stanzas and a last-segment judge broke 45.8% after hyphenation, so letter the reduced form with hyphens stripped and re-letter after every pass → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E522
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][method]` Two-pass letters fit a named shape (AABB, ABAB, ABBA, XXAA) in only 24.7% of 4096 stanzas and a one-pass labeller renames 30.5% of those, so read shape from two-pass uppercase and leave other stanzas unnamed → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E523
- `[model][numerics][sandbox]` Halving a thermostat band cut swing 41 percent then 8 percent as it neared the 1.1 K lag floor, and dilution at a=0.2 uL is best at 2 steps and worse at 4, so measure the floor first and stop where the knob term equals it → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E43
- `[coin-weighing][search][testing]` Exhaustive search over count states shows only 4 v 4 opens a 12-coin, 3-weighing puzzle (6 v 6 fails) and 13 coins need a spare genuine coin with a 5 v 4 opening, so tabulate surviving first moves as well as capacity → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E44
- `[empathy][bias][model]` In a grid model a positive in-group coefficient beside a negative out-group one (+0.5, -0.5, trait 0) marks a gap story and a general empathy raise leaves harm at 0.0 (out-group-only: -0.5), so check the in-group coefficient before blaming low empathy → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E45
- `[songwriting][suno-prompting][testing]` Reducing going to to gonna before a bare verb cleared the rushed-line hint on 9 of 16 lines exactly one over and 0 of 16 two-over (a vowel-group counter saw no change), rhyme letters kept 12/12, so reduce first then hyphenate only leftovers → projects/pappydapimp69__brain-auto-training.md#E46
- `[determinism][rng][state][testing][sandbox]` Name-keyed state-word saves kept 3/3 old streams exact when a stream was inserted (index-keyed 2/3) but the new stream diverged from a from-scratch run; counter-keyed draws matched with 0 words saved, so test migration against both → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E524
- `[game-ads][testing][model][sandbox]` A copied fail-ending tag stale on a clock cut made IPM x D1 identical to IPM-only in 441/441 cells (wrong in 213) while a beats-derived tag matched measured D1, so derive the flag from the cut or assert it varies → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E525
- `[songwriting][rhyme][testing][sandbox]` Rewriting end words to their sung reduced form changed two-pass rhyme letters in 68.6% of 4096 toy stanzas (hyphens harmless under a whole-word judge, 38.2% wrong under a last-segment one), so letter the scheme from the sung form with hyphens stripped → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E526
- `[rating][elo][model][sandbox]` Games-played Elo K (40/20/10) matched best fixed K on steady error (32.4 vs 33.2 RMS) but took 502 games to recover a +400 veteran versus 223 at K 32; reopening K after a net residual of 3 over 10 games cut it to 286 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E527
- `[paper-sizes][numerics][model][sandbox]` The ISO 216 A0 to A10 mm sizes are rounded A0 (841 by 1189) then chained halving with round-down (11 of 11); rounding the exact size per level matches only 5 of 11, so define a foldable ladder by a chained integer rule → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E528
- `[empathy][helping][model][testing][sandbox]` A general empathy raise leaves crowd harm unchanged only if harm depends on the in-group minus out-group gap (12/12 cells); under a ratio form it moved harm -0.026 and under a saturating form +0.031, and an out-group-only raise gained 0.091 at n=3 but 0.002 at n=12 without diffusion → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E529
- `[songwriting][rhyme][testing][sandbox]` A last-segment rhyme judge on real hyphenated words changed two-pass letters in 8.21% of 65536 stanzas (vs 27.3% abstract), all containing a compound like someone or anywhere whose segment tail differs, always as resolved-versus-assonance errors, so key the whole word → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E530
- `[lab-dilution][thermostat-control][curing][model][sandbox]` Narrowing a bath thermostat band 1.0 to 0.25 K moved a dilution-plus-cure error budget by 0.0155 points of 11.0 percent (water-density term 0.045 to 0.030, under 0.18 at any 4 to 60 C), so rank all error terms and weigh the meat base first → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E531
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][method]` A legibility check (one letter, one class) cannot catch a last-segment rhyme judge, wrong in 1024 of 4096 stanzas yet 0 illegible; check identity against unhyphenated lettering and judge whole words → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E532
- `[radio-horizon][propagation][simulation][method]` For radio line of sight use 4.12 times root height in metres for km (within 0.08% to 12 km); the optical 3.57 is 13.4% short for radio, and a two-antenna link is the sum of the two horizons → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E533
- `[songwriting][lyrics][suno-prompting][simulation]` Hyphenation and gonna-reduction commute (6 of 7 lines reduced in either order, 6 syllables saved) and only reduction cuts the counted length (hyphenating alone saved 0), so run them independently → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E534
- `[road-grade][geometry][simulation][method]` Grade percent over 1.7455 gives degrees within 1% only to a 17% grade, and slope length equals horizontal run within 1% only to 14% grade; past those use atan and the square root → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E535
- `[numerics][sandbox][model][measurement]` Closed-form floors predicted knob sweeps in dilution (41 of 41), thermostat (18 of 18) and solder (27 of 27, once a dwell term is added to steady state); dilution crossover is 0.0147 uL → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E218
- `[darts][sandbox][numerics][model]` Best dart aim leaves treble 20 as spread grows: treble 20 at 10 mm, treble 19 at 25 mm (+0.8 points), lower-left at 40 mm (+1.6 points); the ring neighbour sum is 198 against 38 sorted → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E219
- `[paper-sizes][sandbox][numerics][standards]` ISO 216 A-series sizes match 11 of 11 only when derived from the rounded size above with floor halving; half-up gives 1 of 11 and exact root two 5 of 11 → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E220
- `[numerics][sandbox][model]` A hidden-base spec error that scales (dose 33 percent, torque 125 percent of proof) is fixed exactly by a ratio, but a lag overshoot is an additive 1.20 K floor at every band, so measure it → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E221
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][method]` Hyphenated end words under a final-fragment judge changed two-pass letters and case in 40.1 percent of stanzas but the open/stable verdict in only 1.7 percent, so audit letters, not verdicts → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E222
- `[lab-dilution][curing][numerics][sandbox]` A ratio rescale removes the 33 to 38 percent dose-base error from a diluted cure but leaves the pipette floor, so choose the step count (2 at a=0.02, 1 at a=0) separately → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E223
- `[songwriting][rhyme][lyrics][simulation]` Guarded gonna reduction and rejoined hyphens leave two-pass letters unchanged (38416 of 38416) and save 1 syllable per reduced line; a last-fragment judge still broke 10.5% → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E224
- `[fastener-torque][numerics][sandbox][measurement]` Variable-first torque failed 0 of 7 cases against 3 of 7 for the quoted torque, but only while the friction estimate is within the band half-width (20 percent), at 30 percent every case failed → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E225
- `[tooling][testing]` Derive a ranker's input map from the real artifact and test against it: a silently dropped D1 field flipped 184 of 300 IPM-times-D1 picks and none of the IPM-only picks → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E536
- `[lab-dilution][measurement]` A pipette-floor estimate off by 2x cost under 5% in 16 of 18 dilution cells but doubled the error at the 1-step/2-step crossover, so re-measure the floor near that point → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E537
- `[numerics][measurement]` Measure the hidden floor or base before setting the knob, but under deployed conditions: knob-first failed 11 of 12 cells, variable-first 0 of 12, and a floor measured at half the real lag failed 6 of 9 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E538
- `[depth-of-field][optics]` Focusing at the hyperfocal distance keeps H/2 to infinity sharp only while diffraction stays under the circle of confusion: 0.011 mm at f/8 but 0.043 mm at f/32 against c of 0.030 mm → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E539
- `[songwriting][rhyme]` Rejoin hyphenated end words before two-pass lettering: a last-segment judge got 59.4% of 2401 stanzas wrong on perfect letters and 14.8% of the rest wrong on the leftover-assonance list → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E540
- `[gamedev][determinism]` Save RNG state words and split one stream per system from hash(seed, name): one inserted roll shifted 98% of later combat rolls on a shared stream and 0 on split streams → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E541
- `[analog-timing][rc-timer]` Check capacitor leakage against R before tolerance in a 2/3-Vcc RC timer: Rl of 2R or less never fires, Rl of 3R runs 50% long, while a 20% capacitor only moves the time 21% → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E542
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][method]` A rhyme-shape motion label (AABB, ABAB, ABBA) read from letters on rejoined whole words never changed under hyphenation (0/10000); a last-segment judge corrupted it in 23.0% of stanzas → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E543
- `[ladder-safety][statics][simulation][method]` The 4:1 ladder angle (75.96 deg) holds at the top step only if floor friction is at least 0.236 (0.125 mid-ladder); a 1500 N climber at mu 0.15 needs 81.2 deg, so secure the foot on slick floors → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E544
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][method]` Reducing going to to gonna mid-line then hyphenating end words kept two-pass letters in 1296 of 1296 stanzas under a whole-word judge and saved 1.0 syllable per line; the guard leaves line-final going to alone, so end words never change → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E545
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][method]` Rejoined hyphenated end words keep two-pass lettering identical to plain (38416/38416) while one-pass regrouped lines in 44.4% of mixed stanzas; score lettering by line grouping, since letter counts hid the damage (2.1%) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E546
- `[tire-rotation][maintenance][simulation][method]` A cross tire rotation evens four tires only if the diagonal wear pair sums match (spread 0 vs straight swap 2.0 vs none 12.0 over 10 intervals); with non-additive gaps it left 1.0 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E547
- `[debugging][isolation][testing][method]` ddmin returns one cause of an OR failure (66/66 pairs) and the whole set of an AND (66/66); delete the culprit and rerun until the failure vanishes (66/66 pairs, 220/220 triples), with a positive control that carries every field the predicate reads → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E226
- `[roofing][sandbox][numerics][model]` Shingle waste follows cut-edge geometry (gable 1.3%, valley 3.6%, hip 7.4%), a 5.7x spread a flat 10% factor hides; it over-orders every case here only because starter and ridge material are missing from the model → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E227
- `[ladders][sandbox][numerics][model]` The 4-to-1 ladder angle needs about 0.24 floor grip at the top of the climb (80 kg climber): enough for a wet floor, not ice (min angle 72.4 wet, 84.0 iced), so check grip as well as angle → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E228
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][method]` A last-segment judge flipped the open/closed shape verdict in 240 of 6561 stanzas (3.7%), all via suffix end words (-ly, -ful), whole-word judge 0, so judge shape on rejoined words → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E229
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][method]` Reduce and hyphenate mid-line freely: whole-word lettering held 0/4 changes, but a naive going-to rewrite changed 2/4 stanzas where it ended a line and a last-segment judge 1/4, so never reduce a line-end word without re-lettering → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E230
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][method]` Undo hyphens back to a spelling judge's tail window: reading only the last segment added 3952 to 4710 wrong stanzas of 65536, rejoining two segments fully repaired tails 3 to 5 on short-segment words, full rejoin is the safe default → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E231
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][method]` Reading the last segment of silent-e hyphenated words (de-si-re, lo-ve) changed two-pass letters in 3858 of 6561 stanzas (62.4% of mixed silent-e ones), false rhymes across unrelated words, so letter on the rejoined word → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E232
- `[game-ads][testing][model][sandbox]` Confirming an IPM times D1 winner at two impression budgets is not enough: the lower-bound winner differed from the point estimate at 20 of 40 budgets and 42 of 780 agreeing pairs named the wrong one, so also check the point estimate or a margin → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E233
- `[ads][batching][simulation][method]` A fake-ad batch is min(16, 6 x min(trope cap, usable axes)): one lost axis changed nothing, two capped it at 12, and filling to 16 added 0.0073 pair similarity versus 0.188 saved by rotating 3 kit slots → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E548
- `[rpg][combat][balance][testing]` In a toy duel model a per-hit percent-HP cap erased the burst skill strong-band win at every cap under 1.0 and a divided cap made 3v1 take 10 turns like 1v1, but the aggregate burst-win count also fell (7 to 5 at cap 0.45), so tabulate wins per band → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E549
- `[debugging][isolation][testing][method]` ddmin returned one of two independent sufficient causes (P then S after delete and re-run), and a positive control missing a field the predicate reads made a healthy predicate fail, so build controls from the real upstream fields → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E550
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][method]` A last-segment rhyme judge mislettered 2256 of 4096 stanzas, all from feminine endings with stress before the last segment (0 once removed), so key the full stressed tail and still list assonance separately → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E551
- `[ladder-safety][statics][simulation][method]` At the 4-to-1 ladder setup (75.96 degrees) floor friction 0.24 is needed with the climber at the top versus 0.011 at the foot, so it holds on dry or wet tile (0.3) with a 1.26x margin but slips on a 0.1 surface; check top-of-climb demand → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E552
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][method]` A last-segment rhyme judge changed the stop count of 1248 of 4096 stanzas (0 without feminine endings), and stop count with mean gap cannot separate ABBA from ABAB, so judge whole words and add a nesting measure → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E553
- `[gamedev][determinism][testing][state]` Saving sfc32 four state words restored with 0 replayed rolls versus 100000 for seed plus count with identical output, sorted-key hashing made equal states hash equal, and one extra roll desynced the stream → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E554
- `[testing][sandbox][simulation][method]` capped fake-ad batches of 6, 12 and 16 still held a one-syllable-apart name pair in 28, 76 and 91 percent of batches while exact duplicates were 0.5 to 6 percent, so screen lookalikes at every size → projects/local__capped-fake-ad-batches-still-need-the-lookalike-name-screen.md#E1
- `[pottery][numerics][sandbox][testing]` throw size = target / (1 - shrinkage), not target * (1 + shrinkage): 2.88 mm short on 200 mm at 12 percent, and clay volume needs the cube (1.467 L for 1 L, naive 23.7 percent under) → projects/local__clay-shrinkage-oversize-by-division-and-volume-by-cube.md#E1
- `[gamedev][input][state][testing]` staircase bound N*p*(1-p) per lag is a mean, not a ceiling, on a Fibonacci-word signal: Fibonacci lags cost 0.42 of it, lags far from integer multiples of phi cost 1.62, so scan per lag → projects/local__fibonacci-lag-sparsity-compounds-gate-only-cost-drop-at-the-lossless-cutoff.md#E1
- `[lockpicking][combinatorics][sandbox][testing]` five-pin ten-depth keyspace with a MACS of 7 is 79666 of 100000 (20.3% removed), and a master-keyed lock opens at most 2^(differing chambers) combinations: 32, or 16 in two sampled cases → projects/local__macs-rule-and-master-keying-shrink-pin-tumbler-keyspace.md#E1
- `[songwriting][rhyme][suno][simulation][method]` Cutting rushed lines from the front kept two-pass rhyme lettering exact in 4096 of 4096 stanzas, end truncation broke 91.2%, and hyphenation shortened nothing in word count → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E555
- `[songwriting][rhyme][diction][simulation][method]` Spelling-judged two-pass lettering mislettered 57.9% of 1296 stanzas with full-form endings (going to, want to) vs 23.3% with sung reduced forms; hyphenation did not matter → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E556
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][method]` Stripping hyphens did not fix a spelling rhyme judge for two-pass lettering: 21.2% of 20736 stanzas wrong under strip, keep and last-segment alike; hyphens were not the variable → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E557
- `[cron-scheduling][calendar][testing]` A cron line restricting both day fields runs on either match: 0 0 13 * 5 fires 61 days in 2026 against 3 for the both reading (58 surprise runs), and 0 0 31 * 0 fires 58 against 1, so restrict one field and guard the other in the command → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E558
- `[songwriting][suno-prompting][testing]` End-word hyphens left a vowel-group syllable warning silent (0 to 0 on 12-syllable lines) and whole-word letters unchanged (0 of 2963), while a last-segment judge changed 10.1 percent and a hiatus word shifted its count, so key the hyphenate hint off true syllables → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E559
- `[songwriting][rhyme][testing]` Rhyme lettering mostly needs only the join view of hyphenated ends (8 of 8 stanzas against 6 of 8 for last-segment), unlike the title matcher, but a repeated-syllable laugh chain (ha-ha-ha) reads ABCD under join and AABB under split, so collapse repeats first → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E560
- `[songwriting][rhyme][testing]` Reduced ends (gonna, wanna) keep AABB where spelled-out ends fall to AaAx, and hyphenating them (gon-na) holds AABB under a whole-token judge but collapses to AAAA under a last-segment judge, so judge rhyme on the joined word → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E561
- `[songwriting][rhyme][testing]` A hyphen split that fixes a stutter-hook title matcher (2 of 8 found hyphen-kept, 8 of 8 split) did nothing for two-pass lettering (0 of 8 stanzas changed); only a first-segment end-word judge changed letters (2 of 8), so test each consumer separately → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E562
- `[songwriting][suno-prompting][testing]` A vowel-group syllable warning with slack 3 missed 48.4 percent of 13 to 15 syllable lines and 57.9 percent when the end word was an undercounted hiatus word (gap 9.5 points under the 10 predicted), so the miss follows the words, not the rhyme letters → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E563
- `[prompt-engineering][reasoning][model][testing]` In a serial-step toy, reasoning before the answer gained +0.64 at L but 0.00 at XL (depth 6) and reasoning after it gained 0, so the benefit is a hump bounded by chain depth over per-pass capacity, and shared-root votes reach only 0.68 vs 0.76 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E564
- `[empathy][helping][model][testing]` A gap score correlates 0.70 with harm even when only one level drives it, so claim the gap matters only if it beats both components (0.95 vs 0.67 here), and crowd help still rises 0.62 to 0.89 with n=10 → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E565
- `[espresso][extraction][numerics][sandbox]` Extraction yield from TDS times water in instead of beverage mass reads 1.72 to 2.20 times high (2.80 at 1:1) because the puck keeps ~2 g water per gram of coffee, so weigh the cup; out/water-in rescale fixes it exactly → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E566
- `[numerics][sandbox][model][measurement]` Assumed-over-actual rescale is exact (1e-16) for cure base and nut factor but leaves 0.29 to 1.59 percent error for starter water in dough, so rescale only proportional hidden terms and subtract additive ones → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E567
- `[songwriting][rhyme][tooling][testing]` Running the real suno-builder, hyphenated end words left rhyme letters identical in 38416/38416 stanzas (rhymeKey strips hyphens) but changed syllable counts on 7.1 percent of lines, so strip hyphens before counting syllables → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E568
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][testing]` A last-segment rhyme judge missed 68.6% of full-form stanzas but 14.4% of reduced-form ones, so lettering errors come from spelling-different rhymes, not hyphens or gonna-style reductions; key the whole word → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E569
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][testing]` Two-pass lettering under a spelling judge missed 71.9% of 20736 stanzas (one-pass 69.8%, hyphenated 71.9%), so the judge key, not passes or hyphens, sets the error; key on sound → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E570
- `[sourdough][hydration][numerics][sandbox]` A 20 percent starter shifts a 70 percent dough +2.7 points at 100 percent hydration and -2.4 at 50 percent (sign follows starter minus recipe hydration), so sum starter flour and water in before quoting hydration → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E571
- `[scheduling][calendar]` Overdue age flag T=30 on a day-31 clamp monthly schedule flagged 20 of 35 valid gaps (0 lapses); choose T between longest legal gap 31 and shortest lapse 59 → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E234
- `[fuel-economy][numerics]` Average mpg as total miles over total gallons: 30 and 60 mpg over equal miles is 40.00 not 45, over equal fuel is 45.00, and mile-weighted arithmetic is up to 12.5% high → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E235
- `[songwriting][rhyme]` Fixing an undercounting hyphenate hint exposed a last-segment judge: 1454 of 20736 stanzas relettered (7.0%) versus 0 before; strip hyphens before judging, then fix the hint → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E236
- `[ladder-safety][geometry]` Ladder foot at length/4 instead of height/4 sits 3.1% too far out and leans only 0.44 degrees shallower (75.52 vs 75.96) at 8 to 24 ft, so the shortcut is acceptable → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E237
- `[calendar][scheduling]` A day-29 monthly repeat in skip mode loses 75.00 to 75.76 percent of Februaries under Julian, Gregorian and 8/33 leap rules alike, so fix the policy (clamp), not the leap rule → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E238
- `[breadmaking][numerics]` A stiff 50% starter fed 1:5:5 shrinks its hydration error about 11x per feed (not 6x): dough error 5.08 to 0.47 to 0.043 points, so two feeds make the 100% assumption safe → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E239
- `[songwriting][rhyme]` fixing the syllable hint to true counts flags 2x lines vs vowel-group counting yet left two-pass letters unchanged in 20736/20736 stanzas, so the two fixes are independent → projects/pappydapimp69__brain.md#E240
- `[gamedev][shaders][state]` Sim-driven shader uniform arrays must alias the sim's typed arrays (a separate copy left the skin effect dead while every state probe passed); check a rendered frame, not state → projects/pappydapimp69__dlist.md#E1
- `[locomotion][animation][gamedev]` Schedule a biped gait's duty factor on Froude number v^2/(g*leg) crossing 0.5 near Fr 0.5, not on fraction of top speed; a rigid-leg walk fails below Fr 1 once the stance arc is real (leg force goes negative at stance ends, Fr_mid <= 3cos(th0)-2, 0.67 at 27 deg) → projects/pappydapimp69__dlist.md#E2
- `[locomotion][animation][gamedev]` When Alexander's law sets stride (stride/h = 2.3 Fr^0.3, 4.33 m vs a linear 3.34 m at 7.4 m/s), check stance length duty*stride against IK reach at toe-off; a fixed metatarsus overreaches first (duty cap 0.225), stance heel lift restores it (cap 0.375) → projects/pappydapimp69__dlist.md#E3
- `[locomotion][animation][physics]` In a level-bodied biped put acceleration lean in the feet: plant support h*a/g behind the CoM (0.34 m at 3 m/s^2) and cap a by leg reach; trunk pitch, neck and tail poses move the CoM under 2 cm (style only), and tail straightening rebalances only a well-drooped tail → projects/pappydapimp69__dlist.md#E4
- `[locomotion][animation][physics]` A passive spring tail lags INSIDE a turn only briefly then swings OUTSIDE at speed (1.23 m at 7.4 m/s, 1.35 rad/s), while a path-following tail sits inside (0.73 m); drive outward swing from v*yawRate and tune damping on the whole chain (per-joint critical damping still overshot 24%) → projects/pappydapimp69__dlist.md#E5
- `[locomotion][animation][gamedev]` An eased swing foot lands with its target's world velocity: velocity*remaining prediction stops skid only on straight paths; predict heading too (else omega*|offset| skid, 1.05 m/s in a 1.35 rad/s sprint turn) and freeze the stance-centring speed at lift-off (else 0.5*a*duty*T) → projects/pappydapimp69__dlist.md#E6
- `[songwriting][rhyme][testing][sandbox]` the vowel-group syllable warning and two-pass assonance hiding are independent defects (assonance hidden 32.5% with a crowded line vs 31.6% without), and the warning's miss rate swung 3% to 36% with hiatus-word share, so check them separately → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E572
- `[lab-dilution][curing][numerics][sandbox]` fix the cure percentage base before choosing a dilution plan: with a 25% wrong base the best plan was under 9% of the combined error, but with a 2% base a single 1000x step was 91% of it at a=0.2 uL → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E573
- `[empathy][helping][model][testing]` independent bystanders overshoot the field numbers (99.4% someone helps at 8 people vs 90.9% with mean 3.76); a no-help class and a smooth beta spread both fit, and every model overshot the 1.10 per-bystander odds ratio (1.17 to 1.32) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E574
- `[songwriting][rhyme][simulation][sandbox]` hyphenating hiatus words healed the vowel-group syllable warning (miss 82% to 0%) and is safe for two-pass letters only with a whole-word judge (0.0% wrong) versus a last-segment judge (9.7% wrong) → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E575
- `[curing][thermostat-control][breadmaking][sandbox]` fix the percentage base before tightening a control band: wrong cure base cost 25 percent dose error vs 2.8 points from narrowing 1.0 to 0.25 K; hidden starter water was 6.1 percent at 100 percent hydration but 2.0 percent at 50 percent → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E576
- `[pressure-cooking][numerics][sandbox][model]` a gauge-pressure cooker drops from 120.8 C at sea level to 115.9 C at 3000 m, and the extra time is 29 to 77 percent for z of 45 to 20, so the flat 5-percent-per-1000-ft rule (39 percent) fits only mid-range foods → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E577
- `[fastener-torque][curing][lab-dilution][sandbox]` a ratio correction from a guessed category helps most of the time (8/9 torque, 16/25 dilution) but only 2/4 for cure base, where options sit on both sides of nominal, so measure before scaling → projects/pappydapimp69__brainwork.md#E578
- `[locomotion][animation][physics]` An actively balancing tail should hold the INSIDE of a turn at asin(min(sin 0.5, m*h*v*|yawRate|/(m_t*d*g))), swung in no faster than the body yaws through a critically damped filter: steady roll -11% at sprint, -40% at walk vs straight; outside holds and flicks cost 2-4x peak leg yaw torque → projects/pappydapimp69__dlist.md#E7
